Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.19

Chapter 1801
As the power poured out, the golden light in Luo Zheng's eyes kept flashing!
A magnificent and sharp sword intent condensed from the Chiliocosm Heavy Sword …
"Anatta Sword!"
"Yi God's One Sword! Cut! "
"Swish!"
The blazing sword intent almost cut off all of Luo Zheng's emotions. At this moment, Luo Zheng forgot everything. The sword intent kept swallowing and spitting, forming a sharp edge!
Luo Zheng was the only one here, so he could use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao without any scruples.
Luo Zheng didn't release the sword intent. His purpose was only to cut off these stalagmites.
Although the color of the stalagmites had faded, they were still as fragile as paper when Luo Zheng's power and the Emotion Severing Divine Dao burst out at the same time.
"Bang, bang, bang, bang …"
Along with a burst of cracking sounds, a large part of the stalagmites that imprisoned Luo Zheng suddenly broke!
"Whoosh!"
After cutting off the stalagmites, Luo Zheng jumped up. Although he couldn't fly, his great strength combined with his strong legs made him turn into a sharp sword and shoot toward the black mud!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng couldn't define what this strange life form was, but the trap of this cave was obviously the work of this mud!
The mud was still wriggling slowly on the wall. Wherever it passed, the liquid it secreted had a strong attraction to the huge shadow in the wall. The more liquid it secreted, the faster the shadow in the wall moved.
This "mud" probably thought that Luo Zheng couldn't get out of the cage, so it kept a very slow speed. But after a loud explosion, it saw Luo Zheng jump over at a terrifying speed!
Its five tentacles suddenly stretched into the layer of gangue, and the whole cave was connected with it in an instant. Under the control of this mud, translucent stalagmites began to spread again like thousands of tentacles, determined to stop Luo Zheng!
However, the speed and strength that Luo Zheng displayed at this moment far exceeded the mud's expectations!
Before this, this piece of mud obviously didn't put Luo Zheng in its eyes. After all, after it was trapped, it had swallowed a lot of True Gods. How could a mere Proof of Divinity martial artist do anything to it?
Now, this piece of mud finally realized how extraordinary Luo Zheng was, but it was obviously too late!
Those crazily growing stalagmites were simply not enough to stop Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's expression didn't change in the slightest. After falling into the state of "Anatta Sword", not only could he fully exert the power of the Emotion-Severing Divine Way, but it also allowed Luo Zheng to be in a state of absolute calmness. He could clearly consider all the threats around him!
"Dong dong dong dong …"
The Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword surrounded Luo Zheng and slashed crazily. Any stalagmite that dared to approach him was cut down by his sword. There was no exception!
The entire process only lasted for a single breath of time. During this time, Luo Zheng slashed out more than a hundred times and destroyed more than a hundred stalagmites. Finally, he rushed towards the mud!
"Chi!"
A strange sound came from this piece of mud.
At this moment, this piece of mud actually split into pieces, turning into palm-sized pieces that drilled into the wall behind it!
"This thing can really drill into the gangue layer." Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. This time, Luo Zheng could clearly see that this piece of mud was divided into six pieces. The other five pieces were just tentacles of this piece of mud. The middle piece was the largest, and there was a bulge on the surface of this piece of mud.
Almost without any hesitation, he heavily smashed the Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword into the bulge in the middle of the piece of mud!
"Pop!"
He didn't expect this bulge to be so sturdy. Luo Zheng's Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword didn't break it. However, the bulge bounced out of the mud and wanted to jump along the wall of the cave.
Luo Zheng glanced at it and found that it was a pure and flawless transparent bead. In the center of the bead, there seemed to be an "eye" slowly rotating. It seemed to be the eye of some kind of creature!
No matter what kind of creature it was, the eye was the most fragile part. This was the law of evolution for most creatures. However, this eye obviously surpassed this law and was actually so sturdy.
Although Luo Zheng was very surprised, he didn't stop at all.
After losing the eye, the six pieces of mud scattered on the ground were motionless like dead objects. Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed the six pieces of mud and put them into his Sumeru Ring. At the same time, he stepped back and stretched out his left hand to grab the eye!
Luo Zheng's original target was the mud on the ground. However, after seeing the strange eye, he faintly had a premonition that these things should be related to the Chaos Secret Skill he cultivated. Otherwise, his Inner World wouldn't have given such a strong reaction.
However, at the moment when he reached for the eye, the huge black shadows on the side of the wall were getting closer and closer to the wall. At this moment, a skinny long arm suddenly stretched out from the wall and directly grabbed at Luo Zheng!
"This arm …"
The arm didn't look like the arm of a creature. It seemed to be carved out of some special jade. Luo Zheng couldn't feel any blood Qi from the arm. It was like a puppet's body.
If he allowed the eyeball to escape, Luo Zheng would not be willing to accept it!
He clenched his teeth and directly slashed to the right with the Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword …
"Clang!"
Sparks flew everywhere …
Luo Zheng had used 70% of his strength to activate the One Sword of God Yi in the State of Selflessness. Even an ordinary True God would find it difficult to resist the power of this sword. However, when it slashed at the jade arm, it only created sparks and didn't even leave a mark on it!
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's heart sank. His body suddenly floated. He clenched his teeth and increased his speed by 30%, almost pushing himself to his limit! His left hand suddenly reached out, and he finally grabbed the eyeball in his hand …
"Luo Zheng, come out, come out …" Evil Old Man shouted in his mind.
On the surrounding walls of the cave, countless one-zhang-long arms stretched out and continuously grabbed at the air. On the side of the wall where Luo Zheng was, six or seven arms stretched out and grabbed at Luo Zheng!
When Luo Zheng slashed with his sword just now, he had already tested the strength of these arms. With his current cultivation, if he was grabbed by these arms, he would definitely die. Even if he activated all his strength, it would be difficult for him to break free.
"Eight-Zigzag Flying Smoke!"
At the moment of danger, Luo Zheng quickly activated his movement skill.
These black giant shadows only extended their arms out of the layer of gangue. They were also grabbing randomly based on their senses. After Luo Zheng used the Eight-Zigzag Flying Smoke, his entire body was like a piece of paper, floating along with these randomly grabbing arms. After floating for a while, he finally left the range of these arms.
Luo Zheng finally heaved a sigh of relief. He stopped using the Eight-Zigzag Flying Smoke and panted heavily …
Looking at the densely packed arms that were constantly grabbing at him, Luo Zheng's scalp went numb. The longer he stayed here, the more danger he would be in. Now, he had to find an exit!
Just now, Luo Zheng had drilled out from the top of this cave. However, the tunnel he created with his own hands had mysteriously disappeared.
He couldn't figure out the reason behind it. What's more, he didn't have time to figure it out. He almost relied on his own body to slash at the top of the cave.
With this slash, the slender sword beam directly tore a huge crack in the ceiling of the cave. Just as Luo Zheng was about to rush out of the hole, he saw the scene in the hole. His legs immediately went soft and he almost fell to the ground!
Outside that huge crack was another huge cave. In that cave, countless small pieces of mud were constantly jumping. In the center of these mud was a small protrusion. On each protrusion, there was a pupil that was staring at Luo Zheng.
If it were only these "mud", Luo Zheng should be happy. If he could get his hands on these "mud", it would be very easy for him to condense his godhood. However, on one side of these "mud", there were seven or eight huge heads sticking out of the crack. These heads were like skinless monsters. Pairs of millstone-sized eyes were looking down at Luo Zheng with awe-inspiring gazes.
Being stared at by these eyes, Luo Zheng almost couldn't breathe!

Chapter 1802
At this moment, Luo Zheng suspected that he had fallen into some kind of illusion.
He had clearly drilled his way down from the top of the cave. Why had the scene changed?
It was impossible for another huge cave to appear in the gangue layer above him in such a short period of time … What he saw could no longer be explained by common sense.
"Luo Zheng, below!" the Evil Old Man shouted in his mind.
The Evil Old Man could feel Luo Zheng's depression, and he could understand Luo Zheng's feelings.
Anyone who encountered such a situation would suspect that they had gone mad …
The Evil Old Man was naturally very shocked, but he was only a remnant soul after all. He was not as nervous as Luo Zheng. The scene before him was too absurd, but he still hoped that Luo Zheng could find a way out.
Since a cave had appeared above him, he might as well take another path. Thus, he had Luo Zheng cut a path from below.
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly, realizing that he had lost his composure.
This had to do with the change in Luo Zheng's mentality. Back in the Great Evolution Universe, Luo Zheng had been on a meteoric rise. His confidence had been like the rising sun in the morning, and his hot-bloodedness could burn everything. Now, Luo Zheng had become cautious and sharp. The environment in the divine territory was different. Luo Zheng had to adjust his mentality to deal with it!
After the Evil Old Man's shout, Luo Zheng's survival instincts rose in his heart. Even if the path above no longer existed, he could still find another way. It was better than being trapped here!
The arms around the cave grew longer and longer, and the shadows were about to burst out from the walls. The huge heads above also poked out from the cracks in the walls at the same time …
"Anatta Sword …"
In this situation, any negative emotion could be fatal. Luo Zheng once again suppressed his emotions with his understanding of the way of the sword. At the same time, he used the Emotion Severing Divine Dao!
With a flip of his right hand, the Great Chiliocosm Sword suddenly stabbed downwards. His body abruptly retreated, and the sword intent emanating from the Great Chiliocosm Sword tore a hole in the ground. The gangue on the surface of the cave had turned dark green, while the normal gangue was as black as charcoal!
Through the hole, Luo Zheng saw a patch of black gangue. Whether in terms of hardness or toughness, this kind of "normal" gangue was far inferior to the dark green gangue in the cave!
"Finally normal!" Luo Zheng was delighted.
After falling into the Emotion Severing Divine Path, Luo Zheng's mind should not have any emotional fluctuations, but Luo Zheng ultimately did not cultivate this Divine Path to the extreme. Seeing these black gangue, he suddenly had a feeling that he had escaped death. His originally "emotionless" soul was still somewhat excited in the end!
"Boom, boom, boom …"
. Now Luo Zheng did not hold back at all. With his absolute strength and the Yi Shen Sword, this sword was able to break a tunnel that was more than 30 meters long. After three or four sword strikes, the tunnel was already 50 to 60 meters long!
After thrusting out these sword strikes, Luo Zheng jumped up and fell straight down like a vertical wooden stick.
At the same time, Luo Zheng heard dense footsteps. Those huge black shadows finally broke out of the walls. However, Luo Zheng did not even have time to take a look at what those huge shadows were before he fell into the tunnel. When he raised his head again, Luo Zheng saw dozens of arms stretching out and filling up the tunnel he had created …
After taking a look, he did not raise his head again. He only focused on digging down. After digging about four to five hundred meters in this layer of gangue, he raised his head again. The tunnel above his head was only a blurry dot. With Luo Zheng's eyesight, he could still see that those arms were still tightly stuffed at the entrance of the tunnel.
At this moment, he let out a sigh of relief. It seemed like those fellows were unable to leave the cave. Then, he released the Anatta Sword.
Although he did not know what had happened inside and outside the cave, he guessed that he had dug down from the Tie Clan's pit. After passing through that cave, he continued to dig down several hundred meters.
It was unrealistic to keep digging down. There were many strange things underground in the Divine Domain. Who knew what else he could dig up?
Luo Zheng discussed with the Extreme Evil Old Man in his mind for a while. In the end, he decided to dig horizontally. Although the cave above his head was not small, he could still go around it if he dug horizontally.
As he dug horizontally, he used the Da Qian Heavy Sword to dig another ten thousand feet long tunnel. At this point, Luo Zheng changed his direction again and started to dig diagonally upwards.
To create such a long passageway in the gangue layer, even for a middle true god, it was quite tiring. Luo Zheng's strength was boundless. If some lower true gods with average talent were placed here, they might not have the ability to break through the thick gangue layer and climb up.
As he dug upwards, Luo Zheng was very careful. He was afraid that he would accidentally dig into that cave again.
As he dug, he analyzed the situation with the Extreme Evil Old Man. They came up with the closest answer. The strange cave had indeed moved. As for how the cave moved in the layer of gangue, the two of them could not figure out the reason.
However, they managed to escape without any mishap. More importantly, he obtained six pieces of "mud." Among the six pieces of mud, five of them were similar in size to the ones sold to him by the Tie Clan. The piece in the middle was four to five times larger than the other pieces.
Other than these pieces of mud, he also caught the eyeball. As for what the eyeball was, he could only study it when he returned.
Luo Zheng maintained his vigilance as he dug diagonally upwards. Naturally, his speed was not fast. After digging for about eight hours, he suddenly felt the Da Qian Heavy Sword lighten. At the same time, he heard the sound of sand rubbing against each other.
"I have dug out the layer of gangue!" Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. After passing through the layer of gangue, there was an ordinary layer of mud. This layer of mud was much easier to dig. Moreover, this proved that Luo Zheng had already bypassed that cave.
Following the soil layer, Luo Zheng continued to dig for the time it took for an incense stick to burn. He discovered that the soil above was getting wetter and wetter. Luo Zheng already had a vague guess in his heart. The moment he dug out the soil layer, a raging stream of water flowed down from above. He had dug into a pond.
This pond was located in one of the courtyards of the Tie Clan. Although the Tie Clan was not ranked high in the Six Ren Divine City, they had been in business for many years. Therefore, they were far superior to ordinary clans. The courtyard of the Tie Clan occupied a large area and was built in a disorderly manner.
The maidservants in the courtyard suddenly noticed a whirlpool in the middle of the pond. Their faces were filled with curiosity. The water in the pond quickly sank into the tunnel that Luo Zheng dug. Soon, a soaked Luo Zheng appeared in the middle of the pond. Immediately, the maidservants of the Tie Clan screamed …
Not long after, Luo Zheng was invited back to the main hall of the Tie Clan.
Tie Yan's Second Uncle and Third Uncle were extremely puzzled. Naturally, they had to ask Luo Zheng in detail.
Not only did they fail to find Luo Zheng, but they also didn't know how Luo Zheng left. Moreover, the huge pit was quite a distance away from the Tie Clan. After Luo Zheng disappeared mysteriously for a few hours, he actually reappeared in the pond of the Tie Clan. This made the whole matter even more strange.
Luo Zheng didn't tell them the truth. If he had only dug out a few pieces of mud, there was no need to hide it. However, when he encountered that strange cave and saw the man and woman on the stone, he knew that this matter wasn't simple. Therefore, he naturally didn't reveal anything.
Luo Zheng told the Tie Clan that he had discovered something strange while he was digging. He couldn't find his way back and could only dig aimlessly. After being trapped for a few hours, he finally dug his way out. He didn't expect to find himself in the Tie Clan's pond.
The elders of the Tie Clan didn't comment on Luo Zheng's words. It seemed that they didn't believe him at all. In fact, they had already made up their minds. After Luo Zheng left, they would send people to explore the passage of the pond … …
Luo Zheng was well aware of the plans of the Tie Clan. However, whether or not the Tie Clan could find that strange cave was another matter altogether. According to the location where he appeared, Luo Zheng was certain that the cave could be moved. This was probably the reason why the cave hadn't been discovered for so many years.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1803
After spending a day in the Tie Family, Luo Zheng finally took his leave. The Tie Family naturally did not ask him to stay. They were also in a hurry to explore the passage under the pool.
Luo Zheng chose to go into seclusion as soon as he returned to the Heaven-Containing Mansion.
Luo Zheng had already figured out the purpose of the mud.
Although the world in his body kept urging Luo Zheng to devour the "mud" as soon as possible, Luo Zheng still ignored it.
In addition to the "mud", Luo Zheng also obtained the transparent bead this time.
When Luo Zheng took out the eyeball, the mud lost its spirituality and became ordinary mud.
In other words, when Tie Lin's Second Uncle caught the first piece of mud, it was actually the black thing that abandoned a part of its body. The real core should be this eyeball!
Luo Zheng put the Sumeru Ring on his finger and took out the eyeball with a flip of his right hand.
Luo Zheng did not observe it too carefully because he was trying to escape. Now that he took a closer look, he found that the surface of the bead was cracked and broken. Fine cracks covered the surface of the transparent bead.
The bead was indeed sturdy, but it could not withstand the power of Luo Zheng's sword.
"This is a kind of soul container. There must be some kind of living soul inside the bead." The Evil Old Man looked at the bead in his mind and analyzed it for Luo Zheng. "Generally speaking, only the most advanced puppet techniques can seal a soul into a bead. It's said that some Saints like to do this."
"Seal a soul into a bead …" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. Afo in the Holy Realm Dwellers' Mansion was a puppet, but his soul had lived for countless years. He had never seen Afo's soul container and wondered if Afo's soul was sealed in a small bead by Gu Bei.
Just as Luo Zheng was looking at the bead, the pupil in the transparent bead suddenly turned and rolled along Luo Zheng's finger. It wanted to escape from Luo Zheng's hand!
Although Luo Zheng didn't expect this, how agile was his reaction? With a flip of his hand, he easily pinched the bead in his hand, and a smile appeared on his face. "There is indeed a soul in it, and it wants to escape …"
When the bead was caught by Luo Zheng once again, the pupil in the bead stopped moving and lost all signs of life once again...
"Hehe, the soul in this bead has been playing dead all this time." Old Man Extreme Evil laughed.
If he wanted to find out the secrets of the cave, he had to start with this small bead. After holding the small bead tightly, Luo Zheng's mind emitted an invisible Divine Sense. The Divine Sense slowly extended from between his eyebrows and poured into the small bead.
The moment Luo Zheng poured his divine sense into the small bead, a clear image was projected into his mind. At this moment, Luo Zheng's pupils suddenly dilated!
"This, this is …"
It was a hellish scene. Blood-red crows circled in the air, and a few slender black figures carried curved sickles as they walked aimlessly in the underground cave. In the middle of these black figures was a large cauldron that was thousands of feet tall. Under the constant burning of blood-red flames, the large cauldron was also boiling. And in the center of the cauldron, there was something moving up and down.
The thing in the cauldron was actually a small head!
This head's hair seemed to be hundreds of feet long. The black hair covered the cauldron and floated along with the boiling water in the cauldron …
After a while, the hair slowly parted like a curtain, revealing an exquisite and elegant face. It was actually the head of a young girl.
When Luo Zheng saw this young girl's appearance, his soul suddenly trembled!
"What's wrong with you?" Old Man Extreme Evil asked anxiously in his mind. What exactly did Luo Zheng discover for his soul to tremble to such an extent?
"This, this is Luo Yan?"
The young girl's appearance was almost exactly the same as Luo Yan's!
"No, this isn't her …"
Although the appearance was exactly the same, the temperament was completely different!
Under Luo Zheng's gaze, the young girl's head in the cauldron suddenly opened her eyes.
After the light purple eyelids opened, a pair of blood-red pupils were revealed. Those pupils emitted a boundless demonic aura, giving people an indescribable feeling of bewitchment. That pair of red pupils actually looked in Luo Zheng's direction. At the same time, her thin lips slightly curled up, containing a trace of a smile.
That trace of a smile had a profound meaning …
Then, he saw her lips move slightly, as if she was saying a word!
Luo Zheng's heart shook violently. He also stared at the young girl's head, wanting to know what she wanted to say.
But at this moment …
The pupil in the transparent bead in Luo Zheng's hand suddenly spun. Then, a flame flashed in the pupil. With a muffled bang, the originally clear pupil suddenly turned turbid. The soul in the soul container had chosen to self-destruct.
At the same time, the scene in Luo Zheng's mind suddenly collapsed. That world shattered and disappeared without a trace …
Luo Zheng seemed to have lost his soul. He sat there in a daze, not moving an inch.
After a while, Old Man Extreme Evil asked, "What did you see? Why are you so shocked? "
In Old Man Extreme Evil's eyes, for Luo Zheng to be qualified to be Gu Bei's disciple, he must have seen many people of the world. He found it hard to imagine what could make Luo Zheng lose his soul.
…
Somewhere in the Divine Realm …
Above the cauldron, the young girl's head slowly floated up.
Her long, slender hair continued to grow as her head floated up. It was like an inverted black waterfall, slowly being pulled out of the cauldron.
Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …
Not far away, a group of black things crawled over.
These black things were the "mud" that Luo Zheng had been searching for. The mud quickly intertwined and constantly transformed into a curvaceous body shape. At the same time, the color of the "mud" also rapidly changed, forming a layer of snow-white skin.
Finally, the mud transformed into a near-perfect headless female body!
The young girl's head slowly floated to the headless female body's neck, quickly fusing with it …
Her hair, which was hundreds of meters long, dragged on the ground like black silk. The girl's eyes, which were filled with demonic nature, flashed with a hint of gloominess. She used an ethereal and special voice to say, "In any case, let me finish what I want to say …"
…
…
Luo Zheng was still staring at the wall in a daze, tightly holding the transparent bead in his hand.
Logically speaking …
He didn't need to worry about Luo Yan's safety. Startail had told him that Luo Yan was under the protection of Yu Taibai. As the chief disciple of his father, Yu Taibai's strength was at the peak of the Divine Realm, only slightly weaker than those Saints. Yu Taibai wouldn't allow Luo Yan to fall into such a state.
But why did the young girl in the cauldron look exactly the same as Luo Yan?!
The image of the cauldron kept replaying in Luo Zheng's mind, unable to be erased. Although he denied his thoughts at first, he was still faintly worried. If that was really Luo Yan, what should he do?
"She's definitely not Luo Yan!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp light, quickly cutting off this thought.
Once this thought emerged, it would grow crazily, constantly shaking Luo Zheng's state of mind. If this thought was allowed to grow, it would eventually evolve into his heart's devil, and the gains wouldn't make up for the losses!
At the last moment, that woman seemed to want to say something, but after her lips moved slightly, the soul in the transparent bead self-destructed. He didn't see the following scene.
Luo Zheng interpreted it with his lip-reading technique. He saw that she said the word "I". What did she want to tell him?
After standing blankly for a long time, Luo Zheng finally decided to put this matter to the back of his mind. At the same time, he stuffed the transparent bead back into the sumeru ring.
No matter how he thought about it, he couldn't come up with an answer. If he had enough strength in the future, he might explore that strange cave again. But at this stage, it was clearly not the time.
After dealing with the transparent bead, Luo Zheng took out the "mud" from the sumeru ring.
A piece of mud could only condense a trace of godhood crystal. He didn't know how many pieces of mud were needed to condense a complete godhood. But at this stage, this was the only method Luo Zheng could find. He had no other choice.

Chapter 1804
Under the urging of his inner world, Luo Zheng threw this piece of "mud" into his inner world once again.
Just like last time, Luo Zheng let this piece of "mud" fall freely.
When this piece of "mud" fell into the Chaotic Ocean, it started to bubble violently. Very soon, it turned into bits of brown mist above the ocean …
At the sight of this scene, Luo Zheng slightly raised the corner of his mouth. Indeed, the color of this piece of mud was slightly different from the one that Tie Lin had sold him. However, the effect was the same. Perhaps that piece of mud from Tie Clan had been stored for too long and had turned brown. However, these pieces of mud in Luo Zheng's hand were black.
Soon after, he threw the remaining pieces of "mud" into his inner world!
A large area of the ocean started to boil. Huge bubbles rapidly spread out as if the entire ocean was being boiled. The surface of the ocean was constantly rolling.
Not long after, a huge brown cloud formed above the ocean. This huge brown cloud first swallowed the small cloud formed by the piece of "mud" that Luo Zheng had thrown in before slowly drifting towards Luo Zheng's godlattice plate …
While the brown cloud was drifting, Luo Zheng also integrated his comprehension of the "Emotion Severing Divine Dao" into the cloud. Sharp sword lights started to faintly refract from the cloud, which slowly turned into a "sword cloud".
"Huff, huff, huff …"
Luo Zheng's godlattice plate was like a huge whirlpool, which gathered all the sword clouds into the godlattice plate. As the sword clouds shuttled back and forth in the godlattice plate, they gradually started to solidify. Finally, they turned into a triangular crystal, which was embedded in the center of the godlattice plate.
The previous crystal was only a tiny bit, which was the size of a fine needle. However, this triangular crystal was more than 10 times larger than the previous one.
The size of the "mud" thrown into his inner world also determined the size of the "cloud" formed. The larger the cloud, the larger the crystal that could be condensed. If there was enough "mud", Luo Zheng's condensing of a Godhead would be just around the corner.
When Luo Zheng escaped from that strange cave, he cut through the ceiling of the cave and saw another cave connected to it. Luo Zheng saw many black things crawling inside, but that cave was too dangerous.
With Luo Zheng's current strength, he might not be able to return if he rashly entered again. This matter could only be discussed at length …
During this period of time in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture, Luo Zheng also often listened to the preaching of those True Gods. The strength of these True Gods was not worth mentioning — a considerable portion of the True Gods were not born to fight. The purpose of these True Gods was only to explore the Divine Dao, so their fighting strength was rather limited.
However, the teaching true gods did have extraordinary understanding of the Divine Path. They often had very unique understandings of the Divine Path and could even translate it into simple and easy to understand language for these students.
Luo Zheng used the Infinity Ruler to obtain the acknowledgement of many Divine Daos. After absorbing the Dao of those Dao tablets, he relied on the Infinity Ruler to even release the power of those Divine Daos. However, his method was to swallow them all, and he himself did not understand those Divine Daos.
Now, Luo Zheng also benefited greatly from listening to the preaching of these True Gods every day. According to Luo Zheng's thinking, spending a year or so in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture was not a bad thing. At least he would be able to understand more about the Divine Realm and have a deeper understanding of the Divine Dao.
However, these days only lasted for a month.
A month later, people from the Han Family's main family came …
Every time the main family sent someone, it would shake the entire Six Ren Divine City.
The Heaven-Containing Prefecture included the disciples of various second and third tier clans. They all had high hopes for their own disciples, hoping that one day they would be chosen by the main family to enter the floating island.
However, entering the Heaven-Containing Prefecture was difficult, and entering the floating island was even more difficult.
Regardless of whether it was the Han Family or the other major clans' floating islands, they were all considered rare resources in the Divine Realm. Countless True Gods dreamed of squeezing into a floating island. Just like the Extreme Evil Old Man, who had been famous for a long time, he was also fascinated by the floating island.
Logically speaking, the main family of the Han Family should only come next year to select the most outstanding people in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture and send them to the floating island.
However, this time, the Han Family branch used their special privilege to recommend Luo Zheng to the Heaven-Containing Prefecture!
In history, the Han Family branch rarely used this special privilege. Most of the time, those who had the right to use this privilege to recommend were all extremely outstanding people. When this news spread, it naturally attracted countless envious looks. Just like the rumors from before, Luo Zheng's entry into the floating island was set in stone.
However, there were also those who were jealous. They cursed the Han Family branch for making the wrong choice. Who knew, they might end up making a big joke out of themselves …
In the Han Family's main pavilion, an old man wearing a golden-threaded robe sat boldly at the head of the table. His white mustache was curled up and down, and his expression was rather impatient.
"I remember that the 'Han Yang' you all recommended last time was really 'top quality.' I still remember his performance after he went to the floating island. I wonder how that kid is doing in your Han Family branch?" the old man asked in a mocking tone.
Today, in order to welcome this old man, all the important figures of the Han Family branch were gathered here.
When this old man brought up the old matter again and mentioned "Han Yang," everyone in the Han Family branch revealed an unnatural expression, especially Han Daozhi. His face was originally full of flattery, but when the old man said the two words "Han Yang," Han Daozhi's brows furrowed slightly. A hint of anger flashed in his eyes, but that anger was quickly covered up.
Han Yang was Han Daozhi's youngest son. As the son of the head of a Han Family branch, Han Yang wasn't tainted by the bad habits of the other members of the family. He worked very hard from a young age, and his talent was top notch. Even in the main family, he wouldn't lose out.
When Han Yang became a certified divinity expert, there were still thirteen years before the main family would take him in. So, after a discussion with the other branch members, Han Daozhi used a special recommendation right to recommend Han Yang to the floating island.
Who would've thought that Han Yang would be sent back after only three years on the floating island? By the time he returned, Han Yang had already gone insane.
It was said that after Han Yang went to the floating island, he was bullied by the other members of the Han branch. In the end, he couldn't take it anymore, and became like this.
Now, Han Yang was still being raised in the Han Family branch. With the Han Family's ability, raising a madman wasn't a burden. However, Han Yang had become an eternal pain in Han Daozhi's heart. Even though this old man's position was extraordinary, when he personally mentioned Han Yang, Han Daozhi couldn't help but feel a surge of anger.
"Yang 'er is still in our branch …" Han Daozhi said in a low voice, suppressing the anger in his heart.
The leaders of the other branches all knew of Han Yang's position in Han Daozhi's heart. Even if they didn't get along with Han Daozhi, they definitely wouldn't touch Han Yang. That would only infuriate him.
"Haha, that's good. I hope this time your branch won't recommend trash like Han Yang," the old man said with a laugh.
As soon as the old man finished speaking, Han Daozhi finally couldn't take it anymore. His face filled with grief and anger. "Han Yang isn't trash!"
This old man's position on the floating island wasn't bad, but as the head of the branch, Han Daozhi's position wasn't any lower than his. It was just that the old man held an important position on the floating island, so he had to suppress the branch.
Moreover, when the branch recommended geniuses to the floating island, they always had to go through the old man. As a result, everyone in the Han Family treated the old man with great respect. Although Han Daozhi's personality was a bit strange, he wasn't an exception. He always thought of ways to make the old man happy. Every time the old man came to the Han Family branch, he would naturally have a haughty attitude.
Unexpectedly, this time, Han Daozhi didn't give him any face at all. Not only did his face darken, but his tone was also very unfriendly.
Although the old man knew he'd misspoken, he still felt like he'd lost face. "That Han Yang's talent is average, his performance is average, but he's very arrogant. He couldn't stand the competition between the main branch's disciples. In the end, his mental state became chaotic and he lost his mind. Of course he's trash. What's there to argue about?"
"Back then, Han Yang was personally tested by you, Han Yaozhi. If he was trash, how could he have passed your test?" Han Daozhi said coldly. He was incomparably irritable right now. He even wanted to fight Han Yaozhi!

Chapter 1805
Han Yaozhi's words made the faces of the Han Family branch, including the Mansion Lord Han Meng, turn ugly.
The Han Family branch had a special recommendation right, and they treasured it greatly. They rarely used it, but when they did, it was guaranteed to be an extremely outstanding person.
Although Han Yaozhi was in charge of the test, he was just going through the motions every time he came to pick someone up. He wouldn't take it too seriously. After all, the Han Family branch wouldn't randomly recommend someone, or they'd be exposed the moment they stepped onto the floating island.
In all fairness, Han Daozhi's youngest son, Han Yang, wasn't a piece of trash. It was just that he'd encountered some major issues after stepping onto the floating island.
If Han Yaozhi really wanted to take the test seriously, then Luo Tianxing would have a hard time passing!
The Heaven Mansion's people were naturally nervous …
Seeing the faces of the Han Family branch, Han Yaozhi was immensely pleased. These old fogeys wanted to compete with him? They weren't qualified at all. He raised his voice and sneered, "Why isn't that brat here yet? He's been dragging on for too long. Don't blame this old man for not waiting!"
"He's here, he's here!"
A Heaven Mansion guard's voice rang out from outside the main pavilion.
Luo Zheng was then led into the main pavilion by two guards.
Luo Zheng was a little excited. His plan was going smoothly. After all, entering the floating island was a rather important step.
As soon as he entered, he felt that the atmosphere was a little strange.
The heads of the Han Family branch present, including Han Daozhi and Han Meng, all had gloomy expressions. Han Daozhi, in particular, was furious. He was the Han Family branch's head, an upper true god. Even in the entire divine realm, he was an existence that couldn't be ignored. What could have happened to make him so emotional?
Han Yaozhi glanced indifferently at Luo Zheng, then said in a strange voice, "And here I thought you were going to recommend some precious person. It turns out you're going to shove a piece of trash onto the floating island!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly. His eyes flashed with a trace of undetectable coldness. Han Yaozhi sat at the head of the main pavilion with a dignified and arrogant expression. Luo Zheng could tell at a glance that this person was the key person who brought him to the floating island.
He didn't expect the old man's words to be so unpleasant, calling him a piece of trash.
The Han Family branch didn't say anything, and Luo Zheng didn't act out either. However, the flame of excitement in his heart was instantly extinguished, and his face became a bit colder.
"Ahem, ahem." Han Meng couldn't help but cough. Although Han Yao Zhi and Han Dao Zhi had some disagreements, they still had to look at the big picture. "This Luo Tianxing was recognized by hundreds of Dao steles during the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination. He is no small matter. That's why we made an exception and recommended him to the main family. He's definitely not trash."
A strange light flashed in Han Yaozhi's eyes. Although he was surprised in his heart, he snorted and said, "Being recognized by hundreds of Dao tablets is just a good plasticity. Moreover, biting off more than one can chew! True geniuses with a bright future only need to cultivate one Divine Dao to achieve great things! "
In order to attack Luo Tianxing, Han Yaozhi made these words absolute. In reality, many True Gods didn't cultivate just one Divine Dao. Sometimes, several Divine Daos could be used together to unleash even greater power. However, his words were somewhat reasonable. Being recognized by hundreds of Dao tablets was indeed shocking. Luo Zheng had even used the Infinite Ruler to grasp the Dao of those Divine Daos, but in essence, it didn't increase Luo Zheng's strength by much.
When facing a strong enemy, Luo Zheng still had to rely on the Emotion-Severing Divine Dao.
Han Meng didn't argue with Han Yaozhi. He understood that Luo Zheng's monstrous talent wasn't just being recognized by the Dao tablets. This little guy with mysterious origins hadn't revealed many tricks. He was like an iceberg, his true strength hidden beneath the surface of the sea.
"Whether or not Luo Tianxing has the ability to enter the floating island depends on you." Han Meng smiled faintly, appearing very confident.
Seeing Han Meng's expression, Han Yaozhi became even more displeased. In his heart, he made up his mind to make things difficult for the Han Family branch.
As for Luo Zheng, seeing this development, he felt somewhat depressed. He didn't know what kind of dispute the Han Family branch had with that old man. In the end, the unlucky one would be him!
"Good! Then let me see what this little guy is capable of! " Han Yaozhi laughed loudly. Suddenly, he took a deep breath, and a powerful aura erupted, reverberating throughout the entire main pavilion!
That aura contained the Dao of the Five Elements Divine Daos. Han Yaozhi had clearly activated his Divine Dao.
The branch masters' expressions became even more unsightly. Han Yaozhi was a grand upper True God, yet he needed to activate his Divine Dao to test a Divine Dao?
Previously, Han Daozhi had wanted to test Luo Zheng, but he'd only relied on his strength to play with Luo Zheng. Unexpectedly, he'd been thrown out by Luo Zheng. However, Han Daozhi wasn't angry at all. A grand upper True God should have some bearing.
Han Yaozhi was going all out. Could it be that he wanted to kill someone?
Everyone cursed in their hearts, but they didn't dare to say anything. They knew that if they interfered now, it would only add fuel to the fire!
"What is that old man trying to do? A grand upper True God using the Five Elements Divine Dao to suppress you? Luo Zheng, you'd better run … "The Extreme Evil Old Man was also dumbfounded. The Extreme Evil Old Man cultivated the Extreme Evil Dao, so he didn't differentiate between good and evil. However, this was the first time he'd seen such a shameless old man …
"Who knows?" Luo Zheng was filled with depression. Although he was extremely displeased, he still focused 120% on dealing with this old man. After all, this was a chance to enter the floating island. Luo Zheng didn't want to be negligent. Perhaps the old man had other intentions?
"Little fellow, I, Han Yaozhi, have been immersed in the Five Elements Divine Dao for 12 Divine Eras. I already have my own understanding of it. I'll use the Five Elements Divine Dao to create the Five Elements Illusory Destruction. If you can last for half an hour, I'll consider you a pass!" Han Yaozhi laughed coldly. He didn't have any ill feelings towards Luo Zheng. However, since he wanted to suppress the Han Family, he wouldn't show any mercy!
As soon as he finished speaking, the Dao in the main pavilion started to shrink, turning into the power of the five laws of metal, wood, water, fire, and earth. They quickly condensed around Luo Zheng …
This "Five Elements Illusory Destruction" was an ability that Han Yaozhi had comprehended from the Five Elements Divine Dao.
With the power of the five elements, it was actually enough to construct a world. However, it wasn't complete to construct a world purely with the power of the five elements. Instead, it contained the power of the five elements and formed a slightly incomplete world of disillusionment. It was quite innovative in this Dao!
Very quickly, the Dao continued to evolve, turning into a brilliant world. However, this world was only a few feet wide, and it enveloped Luo Zheng within!
"Huala …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng seemed to have entered an illusory world. Everything around him disappeared, and he fell into a world formed from the power of the five elements. The ground beneath his feet, the golden light in the sky, the trees in the distance, and the tsunami in the distance. Clouds of fire interweaved to form a wonderful world.
However, this world was rapidly collapsing, becoming illusory …
Under the illusory destruction, the power of the five elements collided and exploded, attacking Luo Zheng at the same time!
Luo Zheng raised his eyes slightly. The attack of an upper True God was indeed extraordinary. The energy released by the interweaving power of the five elements was extremely complex. This kind of energy was completely opposite to the energy contained in the Divine Essencefused Mountain!
After condensing the power of the five elements, the Divine Essencefused Mountain became extremely heterogeneous. Therefore, it was also very stable.
However, this Han Yaozhi worked in the opposite direction, using two opposing forces to destroy each other, erupting with extremely unstable illusory destruction energy. Because of this instability, its power could be multiplied several times!
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng was enveloped by the illusory destruction energy.
Sharp golden light crazily cut Luo Zheng's body. Raging flames burned fiercely, roasting Luo Zheng … Luo Zheng could still endure the power of these laws, but when the illusory destruction energy erupted, Luo Zheng felt a great threat!
"Rumble, rumble …"
The illusory destruction energy easily pierced through the concentric robes and acted on Luo Zheng's body. Even though Luo Zheng's body was as tough as steel, the illusory destruction energy still tore open wounds on his body!
Han Yaozhi still had a cold smile on his face. "If you can't block it, remember not to force yourself. If you can't go to the floating island, there will be other chances in the future. Dying here is too much of a pity!"
Han Meng and Han Daozhi both stared coldly at Han Yaozhi!
The people of the Han Family branch all sighed in their hearts. A Proof of Divinity cultivator was blocking the self-created divine ability of an upper True God. This Han Yaozhi was truly too shameless.

Chapter 1806
In truth, Han Yaozhi hadn't used his full strength either.
If the full strength of an upper True God erupted, half of Six Ren Divine City would be destroyed.

Han Yaozhi was the one who recommended the candidates to the floating island. Because it was only for one person, the test wasn't very serious. It was completely based on Han Yaozhi's preferences. However, the main family's selection next year would be different. They would have to go through a strict selection process before they could enter the floating island.
Han Meng was also clear that no matter how strict the main family's selection process was, it would still be much easier than Han Yaozhi's method. If Han Yaozhi wanted to make things difficult for Luo Zheng, there was no way he could pass his test. Enduring the Five Elements Vanishing Technique for five minutes? Only someone as shameless as Han Yaozhi could think of such a thing!
Han Daozhi had already decided that if he had the chance to visit the floating island in the future, he would definitely ask the main family about Han Yaozhi's method.
Luo Zheng didn't listen to Han Meng's advice. He didn't want to waste this hard-won opportunity. He wanted to try this challenge for five minutes.
Whoosh whoosh …
The sound of the earth cracking, metal clashing, flames burning … all of these sounds echoed in Luo Zheng's ears.
That Vanishing Technique was truly powerful!
Luo Zheng could sense that Han Yaozhi was holding back. However, an Upper True God's level of comprehension was much higher than his. Even though the Vanishing Technique wasn't fierce, it was still as sharp as a knife, easily cutting through Luo Zheng's body.
At this moment, Luo Zheng also used several methods in an attempt to avoid the power of disillusionment. For example, he used "Eight Paths of Flying Smoke". However, he was unable to avoid it at all. This kind of movement technique was very effective against martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods, and even against some lower true gods. However, against an upper true god, it could not help but be reduced to an insignificant skill …
Luo Zheng could only grit his teeth and endure!
Although Han Yaozhi's expression was cold, he was actually quite surprised.
In the beginning, he didn't plan on using the power of 'disillusionment'. In his opinion, just the power of the five elements was enough. But in the blink of an eye, he understood that the power of the five elements alone wouldn't be able to harm Luo Zheng in the slightest. Thus, he decisively used the power of the five elements to mutually destroy each other!
He originally thought that Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to hold on for more than two or three breaths, and would definitely beg for mercy and give up. He didn't expect that Luo Zheng would be able to hold on for so long, which was really beyond his expectations.
In these ten breaths of time, that brat had not been injured at all. How could a martial artist who had proven divinity cultivate such a monstrous body?
Han Yaozhi was also hesitating whether or not he should use more of his' Shi '.
However, he still had some self-awareness. As an upper True God, investing more than 10% of his strength in the examination of a Proof of Divinity martial artist was already very excessive. If he strengthened this "Five Elements Vanish" even more, it would not be good for his reputation in the future …
"An incense stick's worth of time. I don't think you'll be able to hold on," Han Yaozhi thought to himself.
The more the power of the five elements intertwined, the more the power of disillusionment would appear. If he wanted to resist this power, the more difficult it would be!
Right now, Luo Zheng was indeed in a very difficult situation. Under the constant erosion of the power of disillusionment, more and more cracks were appearing on Luo Zheng's body. Even if he really could hold on, his body would suffer great damage.
Luo Zheng was also conflicted. Han Yaozhi had already given him another path. If worst came to worst, he could give up now or wait until next year. A year later would not affect the situation.
But when he thought of Han Yaozhi's expression, Luo Zheng once again clenched his teeth!
"Pu!"
Several strands of the power of disillusionment cut towards Luo Zheng's legs. This power directly passed through the concentric robe, cutting into the sides of his legs. Golden blood spurted out.
When Luo Zheng's golden blood flowed out, it attracted the attention of some members of the Han Family. But the golden bloodline was not particularly rare in the Realm of the Gods. Everyone only felt that it was strange and did not take it to heart.
"Give up, little guy." Han Yaozhi smiled faintly. "The power of disillusionment is not something you can resist. If it enters your head and damages your soul, you will lose more than you gain."
Faced with Han Yaozhi's warning, Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. He still stood in place, unmoving!
Old Man Evil sighed in his mind. He deeply felt how terrifying this kid was. Once he decided on something, no matter who tried to dissuade him, it was useless. For example, Han Meng had advised him out of good intentions, while Han Yaozhi had advised him with a mocking tone. But no matter what, Luo Zheng simply turned a deaf ear to it!
While Luo Zheng allowed the power of disillusionment to cut his body, he began to pay attention to the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind.
The Immeasurable Ruler could only be activated by using Dao Accumulation. Other than that, it floated quietly in his mind. It looked ordinary on the outside, almost as if it did not exist.
The Five Elements Divine Dao had already been recorded by the Immeasurable Ruler. Luo Zheng could easily use one or two of the Five Elements Divine Dao's Divine Dao's Divine Dao. However, the "Five Elements Disillusionment" that Han Yaozhi used was a Divine Dao that he had comprehended himself. It was similar to Sage Jiu's Ice Soul Devil Flame. Even the true Dao tablet of the Five Elements Divine Dao did not have this Divine Dao's Five Elements Disillusionment.
Would the Immeasurable Ruler be able to absorb this Divine Dao?
A bold idea welled up in his mind. Should he allow a portion of the Dao Accumulation to rush into his mind for the Immeasurable Ruler to absorb?
When facing the Dao tablet, Luo Zheng could bear the Dao Accumulation on it.
After all, the Dao tablet was an ownerless object. The Dao Accumulation within it was naturally emitted. Han Yaozhi was not a Dao tablet that could not think. Doing this was extremely risky!
Luo Zheng did not have much time to think, and he did not intend to think too much. The moment he made his decision, Luo Zheng began to draw the Dao Accumulation into his mind.
Any Divine Dao's Divine Dao's Divine Dao's Divine Dao would emit an invisible Dao Accumulation, and the Disillusionment Force was no exception! Luo Zheng drew the Dao Accumulation into his mind, so he naturally had to bear a considerable risk. After all, the mind and Dantian were Luo Zheng's two vital points.
Seeing that Luo Zheng suddenly gave up protecting his head and even tried to take the initiative to approach the Disillusionment Force, Han Yaozhi was dumbfounded. He had just warned Luo Zheng to be careful not to damage his soul. This guy was going to use his head to resist? He had seen people who did not care about their lives, but he had never seen someone who did not care about his life like this. He even suspected that Luo Zheng was provoking him.
The others in the main pavilion also looked at each other. They had no idea what Luo Zheng was doing. He was simply courting death!
The Five Elements' Divine Dao collided continuously. Every time two conflicting Divine Dao collided, a Disillusionment Force would erupt. This Disillusionment Force was like an invisible sharp knife, hovering around Luo Zheng. If Han Yaozhi deliberately controlled this Disillusionment Force, Luo Zheng naturally would not be able to resist at all.
However, Han Yaozhi did not treat Luo Zheng as a real opponent. He only used the Five Elements Disillusionment Force to test Luo Zheng. This gave Luo Zheng a sliver of a chance!
Luo Zheng carefully drew the Dao Accumulation from the Disillusionment Force into his mind. The Dao Accumulation was too strong, and the Evil Old Man's soul had already hidden away. Luo Zheng also hid his soul in the depths of his mind. It would not be fun to lose one's soul if he was not careful.
However, the Immeasurable Ruler was still floating quietly in his mind, showing no reaction …
"Did I guess wrong? Or did I not accumulate enough Dao Accumulation? "
Even though he was taking such a big risk, the Immeasurable Ruler still did not move. Luo Zheng was very anxious.
"Puff!"
At this time, a Disillusionment Force cut between Luo Zheng's eyebrows. Although this Disillusionment Force did not cut open Luo Zheng's skull, it left a terrible wound on his face. Blood immediately flowed down his face!
Cries of alarm came from the main pavilion!
Even Han Yaozhi felt a little embarrassed. He did not intend to let Luo Zheng pass his test, but if he really did let this brat die in his hands, things would become troublesome. Although Luo Zheng asked for it, he could not justify it.
"Stop, Han Yaozhi," Han Daozhi said with a gloomy face. At the same time, he slowly released the Dao Accumulation in his body. He could not watch Luo Zheng die in the Heaven-containing Mansion without doing anything. Although he could not persuade Luo Zheng to give up, he could stop Han Yaozhi. Besides, he had long wanted to fight Han Yaozhi.
"It's that brat himself …."
Han Yaozhi was helpless. When he saw that Han Daozhi was getting serious, he could only hurriedly explain. He and Han Daozhi were both upper true gods, but in terms of absolute strength, Han Daozhi was much stronger than him. Now that Han Daozhi was about to attack him, he naturally did not want to lose out.
"No matter what the reason is, stop." There was no hesitation in Han Daozhi's eyes. It seemed that if Han Yaozhi did not stop, he would immediately attack!
Two upper true gods of the Han Clan fighting in the Heaven-containing Mansion was probably a big deal. Han Meng and the others all tried to calm them down.

Chapter 1807
Han Meng raised an eyebrow. "It's those eyes again!"
The key to Luo Zheng's quick acceptance by the dao steles in the Divine Inquiry Palace should be his eyes!
At the time, some people had thought that Luo Zheng possessed the Fire God's Eyes.
Han Daozhi hadn't been there in person, but he'd heard of Luo Zheng's golden eyes. Now that he'd seen them in person, he was truly amazed.
Han Yaozhi's face was also full of surprise. He didn't know how this kid would struggle against his Five Elements Vanishing with those golden eyes.
The scale of the Five Elements Divine Dao in the Infinite Ruler blossomed with intense golden light!
The dao that poured into Luo Zheng's mind gathered on that scale once more!
The dao that the Infinite Ruler absorbed wasn't the original Five Elements Divine Dao, but the dao of the Five Elements Vanishing that Han Yaozhi had comprehended on his own. As the Infinite Ruler absorbed the dao, a clear current seemed to flow into Luo Zheng's mind as well.
"In the nature of heaven and earth, many prevail over few. Therefore, water prevails over fire. Essence prevails over firmness. Therefore, fire prevails over metal. Hardness prevails over softness. Therefore, metal prevails over wood … "
In that moment, Luo Zheng actually understood Han Yaozhi's Five Elements Vanishing through the Infinite Ruler!
"Since the Five Elements Vanishing is based on two opposing forces, I can use two opposing forces to create perfection. That way, I can break through it!"
It was clearly unrealistic for Luo Zheng to break through an upper true god's combat art.
However, although Han Yaozhi had used his unique technique, he hadn't used his full strength, giving Luo Zheng a sliver of a chance!
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly swayed, and chaotic Qi rapidly circulated in his inner world. The chaotic Qi immediately turned into the power of the five elements, metal, wood, water, fire, and earth, and surged out of Luo Zheng's body like five rivers.
"Five Elements Divine Dao?" One of the branch heads frowned.
"He's using the Five Elements Divine Dao, but what is he trying to do?" Another branch head stared intently at Luo Zheng.
The Five Elements Divine Dao was the most common divine dao in the divine realm. Countless people cultivated it. For Han Yaozhi to have his own understanding of the Five Elements Divine Dao, he was undoubtedly a grandmaster. Luo Zheng's use of the Five Elements Divine Dao was the same as showing off in front of an expert.
"Luo Zheng intends to use the Five Elements Divine Dao to break … Five Elements Vanishing? Isn't his train of thought? " Han Meng's eyes were also filled with confusion. If he couldn't see through the foundation of the Five Elemental Destruction, not only would his Five Elemental Divine Dao be unable to break through the Five Elemental Destruction, it would instead be used by Han Yaozhi. After all, Luo Zheng's Five Elemental Divine Dao was also a pair of opposites. Han Yaozhi could easily disrupt it and turn it into the Five Elemental Destruction!
"Not necessarily." Han Daozhi shook his head. In his eyes, Luo Zheng's strength might not be worth mentioning, but he definitely wasn't so stupid. There had to be a reason for him to use the Five Elements Divine Dao. He just didn't know if this reason could reverse his situation.
Han Yaozhi's face was slightly startled, then he sneered, "Ha, is this brat courting death?"
In front of everyone's eyes, Luo Zheng waved his hands, and the power of the five elements surged out. Just based on his control of the Five Elements Divine Dao, Luo Zheng had far surpassed the Proof Divinity experts who mainly cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao!
After all, the power of the five elements was constructed from chaos energy, but in terms of power, it wasn't any weaker than the power of a True Divinity condensed from godhood!
"The Five Elements Vanishing are opposites. That's why such a powerful Vanishing force can be formed. If I hadn't used the Five Elements Divine Dao, I wouldn't have been able to create such a powerful Vanishing force …"
"Hualala …"
Behind Luo Zheng, a water dragon spiraled and rushed towards a golden fire bird. This was the moment of fire and water, and under the suppression of each other, the power of Vanishing was produced!
"Go!"
Luo Zheng flicked his hands, and a golden light suddenly appeared in his left hand, and a faint shadow of a giant tree appeared in his right hand!
Before the water dragon and fire bird restrained each other, Luo Zheng released the power of metal and the power of wood in advance. Metal and water coexisted, wood and fire coexisted.
Under the fusion of these powers, the foundation of the Five Elements Divine Dao changed from Vanishing to Perfection, and they canceled each other out!
"Success!"
Luo Zheng smiled, and his hands didn't stop moving. He immediately entered a state of concentration.
As long as there were two opposing forces of the Five Elements in this area, Luo Zheng would display the corresponding force of the Five Elements, so that the two forces would coexist with each other …
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know that in the main pavilion of Heaven Containing Mansion, there was already silence.
The middle-level true gods and upper true gods present all stared at this scene in amazement!
Although Luo Zheng didn't cancel out all of the power of Vanishing, he still managed to cancel out 90% of it. The remaining layer of power of Vanishing didn't pose much of a threat to Luo Zheng. This was equivalent to Luo Zheng unraveling the Five Elements Divine Dao that Han Yaozhi had comprehended!
Han Yaozhi's true strength was much weaker than Han Daozhi's, but he was still an upper True God, and the Five Elements Divine Dao he comprehended was rather famous in the Realm of the Gods. There were many True Gods who cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao who wished to cultivate this Divine Dao, but Han Yaozhi had never taught it to a second person, not even his own descendants!
But now, Luo Zheng had actually seen through the Five Elements Vanishing, and at the same time used an extremely simple and crude method to break through the Five Elements Vanishing …
"Hehe, hehe …"
A silly smile finally appeared on Han Daozhi's gloomy old face.
Han Meng and the others stared fixedly at Luo Zheng, a myriad of thoughts running through their minds. For Luo Zheng to be able to do this, there was only one explanation, and that was that he had comprehended the Five Elements Vanishing.
But this Five Elements Vanishing was not like those Dao tablets!
The Dao on the Dao Stele was open for people to observe and comprehend!
But the Dao in the Five Elements Vanishing was sealed. Besides Han Yaozhi himself, no one else could see through the mechanism within …
Let alone Proving Divinity martial artists, even Han Meng and Han Daozhi couldn't do this. Han Meng even suspected that even a Saint wouldn't be able to decipher the complete Dao in the Five Elements Vanishing in such a short time.
But this sort of unreasonable thing still happened in the end.
Han Meng naturally didn't know that the Infinite Ruler was an unreasonable treasure when it came to deciphering Dao.
Any Dao that was absorbed by this jade ruler would be recorded by the Infinite Ruler, and at the same time transformed into a small scale on the Infinite Ruler. Any Dao held no secrets in front of the Infinite Ruler, and could be easily broken through by the Infinite Ruler …
Han Yaozhi stood there in a daze, as if he couldn't recover from his daze.
He was the one who created the Five Elements Vanishing, so he was well aware of the intricacies within. This Five Elements Vanishing was a unique technique that he, Han Yaozhi, could only use in the entire Divine Realm.
Because he spent most of his time on the floating island, Han Yaozhi rarely fought with others, and in these past few years, no one had been able to break through the Five Elements Vanishing. But that didn't mean that the Five Elements Vanishing was an invincible divine ability. There were many divine abilities more powerful than the Five Elements Vanishing, and there were even more upper True Divinities that could defeat Han Yaozhi. But there wasn't a single person that could break through this divine ability!
"This brat actually understood the Dao of the Five Elements Vanishing! Just how did he do it … Those who break through my Dao cannot be allowed to live! "
Among upper True Divinities, Han Yaozhi wasn't considered a powerhouse. Not to mention the Divine Realm, he wasn't even ranked in Han Jia.
The more he was like this, the more he cherished his wings. The Five Elements Vanishing was his proudest creation. He didn't care how Luo Zheng had broken through the Dao, but he absolutely couldn't tolerate Luo Zheng doing it!
Once evil thoughts arose, it was hard to suppress them!
At this moment, all the living beings in Han Yaozhi's inner world were silently offering up their power of faith. That power of faith interweaved into thin threads that gathered in his godhead. The 'force' that Han Yaozhi had used before could be said to be negligible, but now that he had activated his killing intent, it was naturally different.
It was simply too easy for an upper True Divinity to kill Luo Zheng.
At the instant that aura surged out, Luo Zheng had already noticed that something was off …
In this illusory space, the power of the Five Elements revolving around him had undergone a fundamental change.
If Luo Zheng had been facing a small snake before, then now he was facing an enormous dragon that could cover the sky. There was no need for the Five Elements Vanishing. Just the power of the Five Elements Vanishing was enough to crush Luo Zheng to smithereens. This was the power of an upper True Divinity!
"This …" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed. He hadn't expected Han Yaozhi to be so serious!

Chapter 1808
Luo Zheng didn't need Old Man Evil's reminder.
Although he was well aware of the situation, he didn't have the means to fight Han Yaozhi. He could only place his hopes on the Han Family branch.
He was betting that the Han Family branch would take action. He was betting that they wouldn't watch him die at Han Yaozhi's hands.
In the Great Evolution Universe, Luo Zheng rarely faced this kind of risk. Back then, Luo Zheng wouldn't place his hopes on others, because if he miscalculated, he'd die.
However, this was the divine realm, and Luo Zheng was a second-class lifeform unfamiliar with this place. There were some risks he had to take.
As the Five Elements power surged around him, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a violent collision, followed by a muffled groan.
The illusionary realm around him had completely disappeared …
A relieved smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face.
Not far away, Han Daozhi clenched his fists, his face filled with rage. Meanwhile, Han Yaozhi was clutching his chest, his entire body slumped against a wall. He coughed nonstop, blood trickling from the corner of his mouth.
No one saw how Han Daozhi had attacked. He'd moved as fast as he could.
"Han Daozhi, you dare attack this old man?!" Han Yaozhi spat.
Han Daozhi smiled sinisterly. "Why wouldn't I dare? You and I might be of the same generation, but I'm still the head of a branch family. Come to think of it … You're nothing more than a mere support on the floating island. Even if I killed you, the main family wouldn't do anything to me, let alone beat you up! "
"You!" Han Yaozhi was both frightened and furious.
Although Han Yaozhi was a member of the main family and an upper true god, his innate talent was mediocre. His strength was average among upper true gods, so he wasn't given an important position in the main family.
His' support 'position was actually just an idle job. However, due to his seniority and cultivation, everyone treated him with great respect. In truth, his position was far inferior to Han Daozhi's.
Though Han Daozhi had an eccentric personality and often joked around with the younger generation, he was still the patriarch of a branch family and was highly valued by the main family. Over the years, the main family had wanted to transfer him to an important position on the floating island, but he'd refused every time they brought it up.
Even if Han Daozhi did kill Han Yaozhi, the main family would punish him, but they wouldn't punish him too severely. Besides, Han Daozhi was in the right. After all, Han Yaozhi was the one who had been recommended by the Heaven-Containing Prefecture. Just because he had comprehended Han Yaozhi's Five Elements Vanish Divine Art, Han Yaozhi wanted to silence him.
Luo Zheng stood in the center of the main pavilion and didn't say anything. This old man had almost killed him just now, so he naturally didn't have a good impression of him.
As for the other branch leaders of the Han Family, they might have been scheming against each other and fighting for the benefits of their own branch, but when they saw Han Daozhi teach Han Yaozhi a lesson, they were all delighted!
"This person … I refuse to bring him to the floating island!" Han Yaozhi spat. "When the main family comes next year, I'll definitely go with them. As long as I'm here, this kid can forget about going to the floating island!"
Han Daozhi shrugged. "Do as you wish!" He then turned to Luo Tianxing. "There's no shortage of people like him who can't find a place to belong. Let alone the Han Family, the Jiang Family, the Mu Family, the Li Family … Which one of the major clans on the floating island wouldn't want him? If you wish to ruin the main family's order, then do as you please! When Luo Tianxing is in the limelight one day, I'll naturally go to the main family and humiliate you! "
Luo Zheng smiled faintly at Han Daozhi's words.
The others in the main pavilion agreed. As they saw it, Luo Zheng's performance couldn't even be described as stunning. He'd seen through and comprehended a divine art in less than an incense stick's worth of time. His talent was frightening enough.
Even if they didn't know where he came from, which major clan wouldn't fight to have him?
Han Daozhi's words were actually meant to rope in Luo Zheng. He knew Luo Zheng wasn't an ordinary genius. People like him should either be killed in their infancy or have a good relationship with him. As the head of a branch of the Han Family, Han Daozhi had the awareness and responsibility to do so.
After holding back for a long time, Han Yaozhi glared fiercely at Han Daozhi and snorted. "Just you wait!"
With that, he floated into the air and prepared to leave the Heaven-Containing Prefecture.
But before he could leave, a purple shadow floated over from the horizon. It was a huge purple flower.
It emitted brilliant lights and vibrant colors, dazzling to the eye.
The purple flower floated above the Heaven-Containing Prefecture and stopped there. One of its petals gently fell off, and a woman floated down from it.
She wore a purple muslin robe, her sleeves fluttering in the wind. She looked graceful and elegant.
Before long, the woman stood firmly in the main pavilion with a gentle smile on her face. She glanced at Han Yaozhi in the air, then back at Han Daozhi. "We're all family. Why are you fighting?"
"Han Meng also came today." Han Daozhi didn't answer her question. He merely cupped his hands calmly.
Han Meng smiled faintly. "What? Am I not welcome? "
"We're honored by your presence, Fairy Han Meng. How could we not welcome you?" Han Daozhi smiled.
In truth, Han Daozhi didn't welcome Han Meng's arrival because she was a disciple of Aunt Han Jiu.
After the Heaven-Containing Prefecture's examination, Luo Zheng had displayed astonishing talent and been acknowledged by the Purple Cloud Divine Dao's dao stele. The six branches of the Han Family had secretly sent people to the floating island to notify Aunt Han Jiu.
Han Daozhi had rushed ahead, wanting to recommend Luo Zheng to the floating island first. Thus, he'd invited Han Yaozhi.
However, he hadn't expected that Han Yaozhi and Aunt Han Jiu would act so quickly. Han Yaozhi hadn't left yet, but Han Meng had already arrived.
Previously, he hadn't wanted Luo Zheng to be taken away by Aunt Han Jiu. That way, the merit of the recommendation would have nothing to do with his branch.
But now that he'd gotten into such a big conflict with Han Yaozhi, it seemed impossible to send Luo Zheng to the floating island through normal channels. Why not go with the flow and sell Aunt Han Jiu a favor?
All kinds of thoughts intertwined, and Han Daozhi's smile grew even wider.
"I've come this time for a little fellow called Luo Tianxing. After Aunt Han Jiu heard about him, she wanted to bring him to the floating island." Han Meng went straight to the point. Her phoenix eyes had already landed on Luo Zheng.
The people standing in the main pavilion were all branch heads of the Han Family. Practically all of them were middle true gods. Only Luo Zheng was a Proof of Divinity cultivator. With Han Yaozhi here, she guessed that this young man was most likely Luo Tianxing.
Han Yaozhi had just personally cut off Luo Zheng's hopes of entering the floating island. Now that Aunt Han Jiu had sent people to bring Luo Zheng to the floating island, it was inevitable that it would be somewhat ironic.
Han Daozhi smiled and said, "Aunt Han Jiu's eyes aren't bad. This person is indeed a rare genius. Even among the disciples of the main family on the floating island, it's hard to find someone like him."
Hearing Han Daozhi's words, Han Meng felt that it was a bit far-fetched.
The Heaven-containing Palace was usually very wary of Aunt Han Jiu. Why was Han Daozhi so cooperative today, as if he'd eaten the wrong medicine?
Han Meng lowered her head and sighed softly. The only thing she could do now was to hand Luo Tianxing over to Aunt Han Jiu.
Han Yaozhi, who'd been planning on leaving, suddenly spoke up. "Aunt Han Jiu has always been a disciple who can only cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Dao. This kid doesn't have an Extreme Purple Yin Body, so how could he have caught her eye? How ridiculous!"
Of course, Han Yaozhi knew that Luo Zheng had been acknowledged by hundreds of dao steles, but he hadn't expected that there would also be dao steles of the purple qi divine dao among them. He'd never heard of someone who didn't possess the Extreme Amethyst Yin Physique but could cultivate the purple qi divine dao.
Han Daozhi laughed. "Luo Tianxing, why don't you show some frogs at the bottom of a well?"
Luo Zheng was quite cooperative. As soon as Han Daozhi finished speaking, Luo Zheng waved his hand lightly, and a wisp of purple energy shot into the sky.
The purple energy transformed into a phoenix that circled around the Heaven-containing Palace, then obediently landed on Luo Zheng's shoulder.
"Purple Qi! He's already so proficient in it, but he still hasn't condensed a godhead. What a strange little fellow. "Han Meng looked at Luo Zheng with a strange gaze. Luo Zheng didn't have an Extreme Purple Yin Body, so the fact that he could cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Dao was already very ridiculous.
She found it even more difficult to understand why he couldn't condense a godhead even after controlling it.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1809
Luo Zheng naturally couldn't explain the problem of godhood to Han Meng.
If he walked the path of cultivation, condensing godhood would be easy.
He could choose any type of Divine Dao and condense the Dao he comprehended into godhood.
However, cultivating the Secret Primal Chaos Arts was extremely difficult.
He'd taken a huge risk to obtain that strange mud, but he'd only managed to condense a small portion of it. He didn't know when he'd be able to find more of it again, so Luo Zheng was very anxious.
Han Yaozhi saw Luo Zheng casually use the Purple Cloud Divine Dao and didn't know what to feel. He didn't say anything else, only coldly glaring at Luo Zheng. He wanted to firmly remember this youth's appearance, then turned and shot into the sky.
Han Meng saw Han Yaozhi angrily leave and smiled, then asked Han Daozhi, "You must have some conflict with him, otherwise your Heaven-Containing Manor wouldn't have let him come so easily."
Han Daozhi shook his head, "Don't mention it. Fairy Han Meng came all the way to the branch family, so we naturally have to treat her well …"
"That's only a small matter," Han Meng focused all of her attention on Luo Zheng.
She lightly walked over and looked Luo Zheng up and down, then continued, "Among Master's disciples, 99% are women, with only two or three men. I didn't think I'd have another junior brother this time."
Luo Zheng raised an eyebrow. Master?
Although he wanted to enter the floating island, he didn't have any plans of becoming a disciple. He already had a master.
Han Meng's observation of the situation was extremely meticulous. "What? You don't want to? "
Aunt Han Jiu was a famous "Secondary Sage" in the Divine Realm. Her position on the floating island was second only to the Han Family's Saint Emperor. Moreover, she planned on establishing her own floating island! Being accepted as a disciple by Aunt Han Jiu was definitely a shortcut for many True Gods.
Han Meng naturally didn't know that Luo Zheng didn't have much interest in becoming a disciple.
At his current stage, even if Gu Bei personally came, he wouldn't be able to teach Luo Zheng anything. He could only rely on himself to figure things out, not to mention Aunt Han Jiu cultivated the Purple Cloud Divine Dao.
"No, I already have a master," Luo Zheng said, shaking his head.
Hearing this, Han Meng smiled.
Admittedly, Luo Zheng's innate talent was extraordinary, but he was still a martial artist struggling to prove divinity in a small divine city. How could his master compare to Secondary Sage Han Jiu?
But from Han Meng's point of view, this little fellow's persistence could be considered a kind of virtue.
Han Meng immediately explained, "Becoming Master's disciple doesn't necessarily mean that you'll be accepted as a disciple. Becoming Master's disciple isn't that easy. When the time comes, whether or not Master will accept you is another matter altogether." She believed that Luo Zheng would eventually understand his own path after entering the floating island. As long as Luo Tianxing wasn't stupid, he would eventually make the right choice.
Since Han Meng had given such a promise, Luo Zheng didn't pursue the matter any further. He would first enter the floating island.
Han Meng stayed in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture for two days. Naturally, the prefecture treated her well.
This time, Han Daozhi very generously offered Luo Zheng to Aunt Han Jiu. He and Han Meng seemed to have reached some kind of agreement. As for what it was, Luo Zheng didn't know. However, the Han Family's branch had suffered a loss in the open, so they had to find a way to earn back some of it.
Two days later, Luo Zheng packed up and left Six Ren Divine City with Aunt Han Jiu.
Luo Zheng had only stayed in the Heaven-Containing Prefecture for a few short months before entering the floating island. When news of this spread, the name "Luo Tianxing" became the focal point of Six Ren Divine City. However, when people discussed his identity, it seemed that they were still shrouded in mystery.
The Han Family's branch understood that Luo Zheng's background couldn't be investigated, so they simply helped Luo Zheng forge a more detailed identity. This way, no matter how others investigated, the Han Family and Luo Zheng would be able to justify themselves. Thus, the issue of where he came from before entering the floating island was completely resolved. It could be said that they had helped Luo Zheng clear his identity as a "stowaway".
…
…
