After Luo Zheng was invited onto the purple treasure flower by Han Meng, they set off for the sea of time …
This purple treasure flower was a spatial treasure created by Han Meng using the Purple Cloud Divine Dao. It contained a huge amount of space and could be considered a world. However, it was still very far from the level of the Purple Sky Decimating Earth.
Within this treasure flower, Luo Zheng couldn't see the outside world. He could only feel the treasure flower shuttling through at an extremely fast speed.
After entering the treasure flower, Han Meng rarely showed herself. In this empty world, Luo Zheng was bored to death.
Pure cultivation was already useless to Luo Zheng's cultivation. He had already reached the limit of a Proof of Divinity cultivator. All that was left was to condense his Godhead. However, the Godhead was his greatest bottleneck …
During this time, Luo Zheng repeatedly practiced the Divine Daos in the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind. There were many types of Divine Daos, each with their own unique characteristics. Under certain circumstances, they could produce unexpected results. If used skillfully, they could achieve extraordinary results.
These days lasted for about two months.
Two months later, Luo Zheng suddenly felt the treasure flower descending. Not long after, it stopped. Then, he heard Han Meng's voice faintly floating over, "Okay, we're here."
After Luo Zheng left the purple treasure flower, what entered his eyes was a boundless mottled color. These colors interweaved together to form a boundless sea. This sea contained an ancient aura that encompassed the desolation of the world.
"Sea …"
This was a magnificent sea of colors …
This sea couldn't see the end. Moreover, what was churning in the sea wasn't the usual "sea water". It was a kind of colorful liquid. Wave after wave swept over, creating a rainbow-like radiance. It was a feast for the eyes and made people gasp in amazement.
"This is the Sea of Time?" Luo Zheng looked into the distance and asked.
Han Meng nodded.
The Sea of Time was very common to the people of the floating islands. However, many Proof of Divinity cultivators and even True Divinities had never seen it in their entire lives.
People who saw it for the first time would naturally be very shocked.
However, to Han Meng who was born on the floating island, she was already used to seeing it.
"What a beautiful sea …"
Luo Zheng sighed softly. This Sea of Time seemed to contain some kind of mysterious aura that had a strong attraction to Luo Zheng.
After staring for a long time, he seemed to have lost his mind. He actually couldn't help but walk towards the sea.
Seeing Luo Zheng's action, Han Meng's figure flashed and blocked in front of Luo Zheng. "What are you doing?"
"I want to see this sea …" Luo Zheng was still staring at the Sea of Time. His heart was filled with a strong sense of belonging. His subconscious seemed to be constantly reminding him that he came from this sea and would also go to this sea.
"The Sea of Time is fatal to you." Han Meng faintly smiled. She didn't notice Luo Zheng's strange behavior. She only thought that Luo Zheng was curious about the Sea of Time. "Let alone you, even if I fell into the Sea of Time, I wouldn't be able to survive."
Seeing Luo Zheng's confused expression, Han Meng could only patiently explain, "Look at this sea when it surges, it turns into various colored fragments. In fact, these things aren't as beautiful as they look on the surface. They aren't some kind of material. These fragments are actually fragments of time! The Primordial Age, the Middle Age, and every divine era has been recorded by the Sea of Time, turning into 'countless' fragments gathered here. If you touch one of these fragments, you will fall into one of those eras. "
Luo Zheng was stunned. "In other words, through the Sea of Time, I can freely travel to any era in the Divine Realm?"
"In theory, that is indeed the case. However, as I said before, these are all fragments of time. They aren't complete time passages. When you fall into the sea, your body will also split into countless fragments and fall into different eras. Therefore, the moment you fall in, it also means death." Han Meng smiled.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng suddenly broke out in a cold sweat. Looking at the waves that were constantly surging not too far away, Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a cautious expression. "Since that's the case, why would so many rich and powerful families establish floating islands in such a dangerous place?"
Han Meng shook her head. "There is only one reason. The Governing Slate is here."

Chapter 1810
After waiting at the seaside for more than an hour …
Luo Zheng saw a small black dot appear in the distance.
The small black dot continued to enlarge. After reaching a certain distance, Luo Zheng was able to clearly see that it was a small boat sailing on the Sea of Time.
Luo Zheng was too embarrassed to ask Han Meng, so he could only ask the Extreme Evil Old Man in his mind, "This Han Meng said that anyone who falls into the Sea of Time will be torn to pieces. Why is there a boat sailing on the Sea of Time?"
The Extreme Evil Old Man didn't say anything in Luo Zheng's mind. He had been to the Sea of Time once before, and this time, he was revisiting the place. He was also very emotional. When he suddenly heard Luo Zheng's question, he immediately laughed and said, "Hehe, there will always be a way. The material of that boat is very special. To a certain extent, it can isolate the temporal laws of the Sea of Time … I have never been to the floating island, but from what I know, these boats come from a very mysterious race called the Sun Blind Race. All the Gods of this race have never opened their eyes to see the world. They are all blind.
They have been sailing on and off the Sea of Time. "There were many legends about the Sun Blind Race in the Divine Domain.
This was a low-key and mysterious ancient race. Just as the Extreme Evil Old Man said, everyone in the Sun Blind Race was blind. However, this wasn't the main point. The main point was that this race had been cruising on the Sea of Time before the various great clans occupied it.
The True Gods of the Divine Domain didn't know much about this race. Many of their customs and strange clan rules were often puzzling to outsiders. It was also a headache for many descendants of the great clans. These great clans were the pinnacle forces of the Divine Domain. They were usually extremely overbearing, but they were very tolerant of this weak race. Because of this, the people of the Divine Domain had many conjectures and speculations about this race.
That small boat kept drifting on the Sea of Time. It looked like it was moving very slowly, but in fact, it was moving very quickly.
Luo Zheng could see a distance of a thousand miles, but the small boat only took an incense stick's worth of time to travel from that distance …
As the boat approached, Luo Zheng saw that the ferryman was a strong man with red skin. This strong man seemed to be blind. His eyes were closed, and a pair of thick and powerful arms were moving a pair of strange oars — which seemed to be the spine of some large fish.
After waiting for the boat to reach the shore, the burly man listened for a moment before asking, "Is Fairy Han Meng going to ferry us?"
"That's right," Han Meng softly replied.
The ferryman nodded. After securing the boat, he said, "Please board the boat."
Han Meng glanced at Luo Zheng and reached out to grab Luo Zheng's arm. With a light jump, the two of them landed firmly on the small boat.
When the ferryman heard this, he immediately asked, "Fairy Han Meng brought another person with her?"
"Two people is enough," Han Meng indifferently replied.
The ferryman's eyes were still closed, but he was facing Luo Zheng. He couldn't see anything with his eyes, but he seemed to be using his divine sense or some other means to size up Luo Zheng. He didn't move for a long time.
Han Meng wrinkled her brow.
The rules of the Sea of Time were very complicated. One couldn't fly in the entire Sea of Time, nor could they use Space Transmission.
Whether it was an Upper True Divinity or a Peak True Divinity, one had to rely on these ferrymen to enter and exit the Sea of Time. Generally, it wasn't so troublesome. She didn't know what was wrong with this guy today. After enduring for a while, Han Meng asked, "Why haven't you started the boat yet?"
The ferryman didn't speak. He still faced Luo Zheng motionlessly.
Although these ferrymen didn't have high cultivations, they were indispensable existences in the Sea of Time. None of the great forces dared to provoke them casually. After all, the Sea of Time was too dangerous. Boarding the boat was equivalent to handing over one's life to them. If the ferrymen capsized the boat and fell into the Sea of Time, their fate could be foreseen.
Therefore, Han Meng patiently waited.
After facing Luo Zheng with his eyes closed for a while, the ferryman suddenly opened his eyes.
There were no pupils in his eyes. His whole body was pure white, looking very frightening. Moreover, the ferryman put his face in front of Luo Zheng, only two or three inches away from Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng was also baffled. He didn't know what was so good about him.
At this time, the ferryman suddenly said, "I won't ferry you. You can go down."
"What do you mean?" Han Meng's expression suddenly sank.
These ferrymen had always been rather docile. On the Sea of Time, they were like hardworking and down-to-earth servants. For countless years, they had been quietly dispatched by the major clans. Except for special periods, they would never go on strike, nor would they delay matters. They were on call at any time.
Had the ferryman taken the wrong medicine today?
Luo Zheng also frowned, but didn't say anything.
"It's nothing. In any case, I can't ride him." The ferryman still stubbornly shook his head. He retreated from the front of the boat to the back of the boat. At the same time, he placed his two oars to the side. His meaning was that if Luo Zheng didn't get off, he wouldn't sail.
"Our Han Clan pays enough shenwu coins to your Sun Blind Tribe every year. Today, I have an important matter to attend to on the floating island. Are you not willing to ferry me?" Han Meng finally couldn't take it anymore.
The Sun Blind Tribe's ferryman still shook his head. "This Sea of Time is very dangerous. Therefore, our Sun Blind Tribe has many taboos. Fairy Han Meng should also know."
The Sea of Time was boundless. It was beautiful and mysterious, but if one ignored the gorgeous splendor of this sea, it was a typical sea of death.
Any living thing that fell into the Sea of Time would be torn to shreds. Then, their corporeal body would fall into a certain period of time.
To true gods, the Sea of Time was even more terrifying than many great forbidden lands. Those forbidden lands that made one's expression change might be terrifying, but there were still many ways to survive. If one was careless and fell into the Sea of Time, only death would await them. There were no exceptions.
Apart from the Saints, if other True Gods wanted to enter the floating island, they had to obediently let the Sun Blind Tribe ferry them through the sea. Only the Sun Blind Tribe could rely on a small boat to "conquer" the Sea of Time, which was full of taboos.
The Sun Blind Tribe also had many strange rules. These rules were passed down from their ancestors and couldn't be violated. They were called the "Four Nots."
The first was midnight. However, the Sun Blind Tribe wouldn't ferry people between midnight and noon.
The second was wind and rain. However, if the weather in the Sea of Time wasn't good, they wouldn't ferry people either. Naturally, they didn't fear ordinary storms. Once there was a storm in the Sea of Time, it would be destructive to all living things on the sea.
The third was the transit. However, the transit was a grand festival for the Sun Blind Tribe. On this day, there were no boats on the Sea of Time. Even if they wanted to, they couldn't cross.
The fourth was fear. If there was someone on the boat that the ferryman was afraid of, they wouldn't let them cross.
Han Meng naturally heard of the "Four Nots." She pondered for a moment, then asked, "Right now, it's not midnight. The weather is clear and there aren't any storms. It's also not the transit. Why don't you let him cross?"
"Because I'm afraid of him," the Sun Blind Tribe ferryman said.
"Afraid? Luo Tianxing is just a Proof of Divinity cultivator. What are you afraid of? "Han Meng asked curiously. These Sun Blind Tribe fellows were especially serious when they were tangled up.
Luo Zheng's face was full of doubt. At the same time, he began to be vigilant.
This Sun Blind Tribe was so strange. They seemed to have an extraordinary sense of smell. If they found out about his origins, it would be troublesome.
The ferryman shook his head. "I'm afraid of him. His aura isn't right."
This time, Han Meng was completely helpless.
She couldn't use force against the Sun Blind Tribe. No floating island would provoke this mysterious tribe. If he said not to let Luo Tianxing cross, Luo Tianxing really couldn't enter the floating island!
"Then how can we let him enter the floating island?" Han Meng asked again.
If this Sun Blind Tribe member wasn't willing to ferry Luo Tianxing, then the only way was to ask her master. Her master had the ability to send Luo Tianxing in, but for this kind of matter, it was impossible. Luo Tianxing didn't have the qualifications.
"Let my father come. My father and big brother might not be afraid of him," the ferryman said.
The Sun Blind Tribe was divided into three, six, and nine ranks. However, because this tribe was mysterious, they rarely interacted with outsiders. Ordinary people wouldn't be able to understand the differences. Although Han Meng had entered and exited the Sea of Time many times, she didn't understand. She even felt that all the Sun Blind Tribe members looked the same.

Chapter 1811
As the ferryman said that, Han Meng's expression relaxed.
She flicked her hand and 2 shenwu coins were thrown towards the ferryman.
However, the ferryman didn't take the two coins. Instead, he gently flicked them with his hand and the two coins flew back to Han Meng. He then shook his head, "Han Jia has already paid. We won't accept it a second time."
Following which, the ferryman suddenly stood up. His eyes were still closed as he faced the sea of time. He muttered something but no sound came out. No one knew what he was saying.
The Sun Blind race had a special ability.
No matter where you were in the sea of time, as long as you stood on the shore, you did not need to send out any message or inform anyone. They would always arrive in the shortest time possible. They would not waste any time!
In ancient times, the All Saints Hall was not established and there were no floating islands in the sea of time. At that time, some true gods only needed to bring 2 or 3 shenwu coins to explore the sea of time. These ferrymen would ask if they wanted to enter the sea of time.
Later on, the sea of time was occupied by various big clans. After the All Saints Hall was established and the rules were set, they reached an agreement with the leader of the Sun Blind race. Other than the people of the big clans, they would not ferry any true gods.
Hence, when Han Meng brought Luo Zheng to the seaside for a while, this Sun Blind race member rowed the boat over.
Luo Zheng and Han Meng waited on the boat for a long time …
Not long after, two small black dots appeared on the colorful sea surface. Two small boats, one in front and one behind, were rapidly approaching.
The leader was a tall and sturdy man. On the boat behind was a stooped old man. Clearly, they were the father and brother of the ferryman.
The two people slowly rowed the boat to the back. The three Sun Blind clansmen quietly communicated with each other for a while. They didn't even make a sound, only their lips trembled slightly. Luo Zheng and Han Meng naturally didn't know what they were saying.
After the communication, the stooped old man said to Luo Zheng, "Get on my boat. I'll take you over."
Seeing that the old man agreed to ferry Luo Zheng, Han Meng let out a sigh of relief. She nodded to Luo Zheng and said, "Go, we'll meet on the floating island."
Luo Zheng then jumps up and stands firmly on the old man's boat.
After Luo Zheng sat down, the old man put down the two huge bone oars. Waving his arms, he gently swung the bone oars. Like a flood dragon entering the sea, the boat split the colorful seawater and moved forward.
"Creak, creak …"
The pair of bone oars continued to make ear-piercing noises, stirring up waves on both sides of the ship. Even if it was just a wave, it still gave off a strong power of the Laws of Time.
These time laws were not condensed from true essence. They were the real 'time', the 'history' of the divine realm. It was just like how Luo Zheng had boarded the Sun Blind Race's small boat on the shore. It had only happened an hour ago, but it had also become a historical record in the sea of time.
In theory, the Sea of Time recorded everything that had happened since the birth of the Divine Domain. However, everything had turned into fragments of time and merged into the Sea of Time. Wanting to accurately find a certain period of time in such a huge sea of time was like looking for a needle in a haystack. It was almost impossible.
As the boat moved further and further away, its speed also increased.
At this time, Luo Zheng noticed that the Sun Blind Race's bone oars were starting to emit a faint green light. Every time he paddled, the green light would dissipate and revolve around the bottom of the boat. It was this faint green light that allowed the boat to travel through the Sea of Time as though it was flying!
The Sun Blind Race had been in the Sea of Time for so many years, so they definitely had their own merits. Otherwise, given the personalities of the wealthy families of the floating islands, why would they rely on this race?
The old man was only focused on rowing the boat. He had no intention of communicating with Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng could not help but ask, "Just now, your son said that he was very afraid of me. That's why he wasn't willing to give me a ride. May I know what he is afraid of?"
"Creak, creak …"
The bone oars continued to move. The old man's thick and thick arms continued to paddle. After a long silence, the old man said, "To people like you, the Sea of Time is a sea of death."
Luo Zheng nodded. "I heard that any living thing that falls into the Sea of Time will be torn to pieces by the laws of time and then travel to an unknown time …"
The Sun Blind Race's old man chuckled and continued to paddle the bone oars. He then said, "To people like you, that is indeed the case. Even the top existences among the True Gods and Paragons will also die if they fall into the Sea of Time … However, to the living things that live in the Sea of Time, that is not the case."
"Living in the Sea of Time? You mean there are living things in the Sea of Time? "Luo Zheng's face was full of surprise.
"Otherwise, how do you think the Sun Blind Race conquered this Sea of Time? Where did our boat and bone oars come from? "the Sun Blind Race's old man asked in return.
Luo Zheng had asked the Extreme Evil Old Man this question. The Extreme Evil Old Man had only told Luo Zheng that this was a special method of the Sun Blind Race. Luo Zheng did not think too much about it.
With the Sun Blind Race's old man's reminder, Luo Zheng immediately understood. "You mean this boat and bone oars came from the living things in the Sea of Time?"
"Yes," the Sun Blind Race's old man replied.
This pair of bone oars was taken from the spine of the Ghost Swimming Fish. The Sun Blind Race had used a special method to capture the Ghost Swimming Fish. They skinned the fish alive and took out the spine in the middle. Then, they used a method passed down from their ancestors to refine it into a pair of special oars.
The boat used for ferrying was made from the skin of the Thousand-faced Arowana Fish. The bottom of the boat was covered with the skin of the Thousand-faced Arowana Fish. This way, the boat could sail through the Sea of Time without any worries.
Both the Ghost Swimming Fish and the Thousand-faced Arowana Fish were living things in the Sea of Time. After countless years of evolution, these living things could freely travel through time and were not afraid of the tearing force of the Sea of Time.
"So the Sea of Time is not a dead zone." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. The evolution of nature was indeed mysterious. He immediately asked, "But what does this have to do with being afraid of me?"
The old man paused for a moment and then said, "There are many unknown powerful living things in the Sea of Time. Among them, there is one kind of sea monster that is the most powerful. Our Sun Blind Race calls it the 'Emperor's Hatred'. You have a strange aura that is very similar to the Emperor's Hatred, very similar …"
The Sun Blind Race's journey through the Sea of Time was not smooth sailing. There were many hidden dangers in this quiet and beautiful sea. If they were not careful, the boat would capsize and people would die. Thus, the race had many taboos and rules.
However, as long as they followed these rules, it was very safe to travel through the Sea of Time. There was one situation that was unavoidable, and that was encountering a sea monster like the 'Emperor's Hatred'.
The legend of the Emperor's Hatred did not only exist in the Sun Blind Race. Even the Divine Realm knew of the existence of this monster.
However, the Emperor's Hatred was too mysterious. So far, no one had seen its complete form. Once this kind of sea monster hunted in the Sea of Time, there was no possibility of survival for creatures below the Sage Realm!
In the Middle Ages, there was even an Emperor's Hatred in the Sea of Time that dragged a Sage into the Sea of Time, wanting to kill that Sage. However, after the Sage transformed into the universe, as long as the universe was not destroyed, his body was immortal and indestructible. That Sage finally escaped from the Sea of Time after being entangled with the Emperor's Hatred for several years. Although the Sage was not captured by the Emperor's Hatred, he was very embarrassed.
Some people listed the Emperor's Hatred as a True Spirit, but many people did not agree. The Emperor's Hatred had strength that far exceeded that of an ordinary True Spirit. Even a Sage could not do anything to it!
"I have the aura of the Emperor's Hatred on my body?" Hearing the old man's words, Luo Zheng was also confused. He did not know that he had such a strange thing on his body.
"It's just very similar," the old man said. "My son is young and has never seen the Emperor's Hatred. However, I saw the Emperor's Hatred a Divine Era ago. I saw one of its hands."
Speaking of this, his face showed fear and sadness. It seemed to evoke a sad memory.
The appearance of the Emperor's Hatred was bound to bring a devastating disaster. It could be imagined that the Emperor's Hatred at that time brought a painful blow to the old man. Even after a Divine Era, he was still immersed in sadness.
＝＝＝＝＝

Chapter 1812
This Sunblind old man didn't reveal what had happened back then, nor did he tell Luo Zheng what the Emperor's Hatred looked like.
In fact, the entire Sunblind race was a race of few words. They constantly sailed on the Sea of Time, but they almost never spoke to their guests.
It was already very "abnormal" that the old man was willing to say so much to Luo Zheng.
However, in the end, the old man said that he would invite Luo Zheng to their race as a guest during the transit of the sun.
Luo Zheng was also curious about the Sunblind race, so he agreed and didn't notice anything strange. However, after arriving at the floating island, Luo Zheng realized that even if one was a member of the floating island, ordinary people wouldn't have the qualifications to be invited by the Sunblind race.
After the boat sailed for two hours, the surrounding scenery disappeared.
It seemed like there was only the boundless Sea of Time in this world. Other than that, there was only this Sunblind old man and this small boat …
Unknowingly, Luo Zheng fell into a state of extreme fatigue. He actually felt drowsy and couldn't help but yawn.
When he paid attention to the Extreme Evil Old Man's soul in his mind, he realized that he had actually fallen into a deep sleep.
Just as Luo Zheng was feeling strange, the Sunblind old man said indifferently, "Many people will lose their ability to judge time while cruising on the Sea of Time. Although I have only brought you here for two hours, your body will feel like a long time has passed. Naturally, you will be tired. If you feel tired, you can sleep."
"I see …"
The old man's words seemed to have a magical power. Luo Zheng felt his eyelids fighting and finally closed. He didn't even realize when he had fallen asleep.
"Creak, creak …"
After sleeping for an unknown period of time, Luo Zheng heard the sound of a bone paddle. When he opened his eyes in a daze, he saw huge objects floating in the sky!
"These are floating islands!"
Luo Zheng instantly woke up.
At the same time, the Extreme Evil Old Man said lazily, "These should be the floating islands of those rich and powerful families. This is the first time I have seen them."
Those floating islands were rather high above the sea of time, and each of them was incomparably huge!
These floating islands were like the Cloud Hall of the Central Domain, but the scale was a thousand times, no, ten thousand times larger. Each floating island was a huge city, quietly floating in the sky …
The floating islands were not parallel to each other. Instead, they were in a spiral state, extending all the way into the sky.
The top of the floating islands was shrouded in thick clouds, so Luo Zheng couldn't see how high they were.
"Yes, we're here," the old man replied calmly.
As he spoke, he rowed the small boat to the center of the sea. In front of them was a huge floating disk. The entire disk was also floating in the air, but it was only four feet above the sea level. This disk was the public dock below the floating island.
Han Meng stood at the side of the dock. She had already arrived ahead of time, and after seeing Luo Zheng, she waved her hand.
The old man moved the small boat closer to the dock. After Luo Zheng expressed his thanks, he jumped towards the dock and landed on the disk.
The old man shook the bone oar and waved his hand at Luo Zheng. "When the time comes, if you come to participate in my clan's gathering, you only need to wait here." After saying this, he turned the small boat around and slowly rowed away.
After hearing this, Han Meng's bright and beautiful eyes flashed with a strange light. She stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Her expression confused Luo Zheng. Although the other party was an upper True God and was also an elder, Luo Zheng could not help but tease her. "Is there something written on my face …"
Han Meng shook her head and asked, "Did I mishear? This old man from the Sun Blind Clan invited you to participate in the gathering?"
"En, what's wrong?" Luo Zheng blinked. He did not understand why Han Meng was so serious.
"Hu …" Han Meng took a deep breath and looked at Luo Zheng with a meaningful gaze, "I really don't know what kind of secret a little fellow like you has to actually be invited to participate in the Great Gathering of the Sun …"
As far as Han Meng knew, although there were no Saints among the people invited by the Sun Blind Clan, they were still patriarchs of the various large clans. Most of them were at the Perfection stage, and even Quasi-Saints would participate. Aunt Han Jiu had also received an invitation before!
The Sun Blind Clan was a very low-key clan, but they were the clan that understood the Sea of Time the best. The various large clans had been fighting with each other for so many years, but they had all chosen to build good relations with the Sun Blind Clan.
Han Meng was only an upper True God. Although she was the closest person to Aunt Han Jiu and didn't know much, Luo Zheng being invited still greatly exceeded her expectations.
"Let's go." Han Meng saw that Luo Zheng was also confused. She sighed in her heart. It seemed that helping her master bring Luo Tianxing back was the right choice. She wondered what kind of storm this little fellow would stir up once he entered the floating island …
On this disc-like dock, there were many small discs arranged in a row. After the two of them stepped onto one of the small discs, the discs began to slowly rise, steadily floating towards the sky.
Luo Zheng felt that everything here was new and fresh, so he naturally looked all around.
Not long after, he approached the first floating island, which was also the lowest floating island. On one side of this floating island was a giant 'Liu' character. This' Liu 'character was likely a million feet tall and a million feet wide. It was incomparably large.
"Liu Family?" Luo Zheng asked in a low voice as he saw the character.
Han Meng faintly smiled. If it were some other Proving Divinity martial artist, she probably wouldn't have bothered to explain anything to them. But, she had a good opinion of this little fellow, so she patiently explained. "Mm, this is the floating island's Liu Family. They are also the lowest ranked noble clan of the floating island. The Liu Family was once ranked in the top ten noble clans of the floating island, but 60 god eras ago, a great change occurred in the Liu Family, causing them to fall to their current state. They even have the risk of being squeezed out of the ranks of the noble clans."
Luo Zheng nodded. Even the peak noble clans of the Realm of the Gods weren't able to hold the greatest advantage forever. In the long river of time, even the slightest bit of laziness would cause them to fall from their pedestal.
The discs beneath their feet didn't rise too quickly, giving Luo Zheng time to look around. These floating islands were divided into large cities. These cities weren't any smaller than Six Ren Divine City. It was just that the Liu Family's floating island seemed a bit dilapidated, and the entire island was covered in a dusky haze.
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw the second floating island.
"The Xie Family, the second lowest ranked noble clan in the Sea of Time. The Xie Family has been on this floating island for 110 god eras, but it has always been ranked second from the bottom. It hasn't risen, nor has it fallen," Han Meng directly explained before Luo Zheng could ask.
The Xie Family's floating island was completely different from the Liu Family's. The entire floating island was covered in lush green trees. Luo Zheng couldn't even find a single building from the outside.
Moving up once more …
This time, Luo Zheng saw a pitch-black floating island.
This floating island looked as if it had been burned. The outside of the floating island was charred, as if it was a piece of pure black charcoal floating in the air. The inside of the floating island didn't have the slightest bit of vitality. It had a lifeless appearance. As for the large characters engraved on the outside of the floating island, they were already badly damaged.
Luo Zheng revealed a strange expression. Through the blurry and damaged characters, he tried his best to distinguish between them. "The Ling Family … or the Meng Family? What happened to this floating island? It seems to have been burned by someone? "
Han Meng faintly smiled. "Neither. That's the Luo Family. The Luo Family used to be one of the top ten noble clans, but it has been destroyed. Every now and then, the floating island's ranking will drop by one level. There's no one left on this island, so the ranking will continue to drop. I believe that it won't be long before the Luo Family's floating island falls into the Sea of Time."
The speaker didn't mean it, but the listener took it seriously.
Hearing Han Meng's words, Luo Zheng's entire body stiffened.
"The Luo Family!"
At the same time, Old Man Evil asked in his mind, "This should be your father's floating island, right?"

Chapter 1813
As the son of a Sage, Luo Zheng could be considered a member of a wealthy family.
In the Great Yan Universe, Luo Zheng had heard of the Noble Floating Island, but he had never imagined what the real Luo Family was like.
He had never imagined that the floating island owned by the Luo Family would appear before him in such a state.
Such a huge floating island, just how flourishing had it been?
How many members of the Luo Family, how many True Gods, how many geniuses had there been …
Yet it had turned into ashes, devoid of life.
Han Meng was an expert at observing people's expressions. Even the slightest change couldn't escape her eyes. Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, Han Meng smiled. "I forgot that your surname is also Luo. You aren't a member of the Luo Family, are you?"
"Luo Family?" Luo Zheng stared blankly for a moment, then smiled. "Of course not. It's just that my surname is also Luo. Seeing the Luo Family fall to such a state, I feel a bit rueful."
Although Luo Zheng's heart was in turmoil, he did his best to restrain himself.
Now that the Luo Family had lost the support of a Sage, it had already fallen to such a state. These wealthy families were afraid of drawing a clear line between themselves and him. If he admitted that he was a member of the Luo Family now, it would only bring him more trouble.
"That's true. If you were a member of the Luo Family, there's no way you wouldn't know what happened on this floating island," Han Meng said indifferently. There were countless people surnamed Luo in the Divine Realm. Han Meng wouldn't suspect that Luo Zheng was a member of the Luo Family just because Luo Tianxing's surname was Luo.
"What happened on the Luo Family's floating island? How did it fall to such a state?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
He was truly curious.
Now Luo Zheng only knew that his father had offended the Sage Halls and had a great enmity with Mu Hai. However, he still didn't know the real reason.
"This is the punishment of the Governing Slate. It's a specially refined divine lightning that fell on the floating island. It's enough to baptize the entire island," Han Meng said.
Luo Zheng nodded. "Why did this divine lightning fall? What did the Luo Family do? Who did they offend?"
Taking advantage of this opportunity, Luo Zheng also wanted to inquire about more information and eliminate Han Meng's suspicions of him. After all, he really didn't know anything about the Luo Family.
But Han Meng's answer made Luo Zheng very disappointed.
"These battles involve games between Saints. Us True Divinities naturally don't know about them." Han Meng shook her head. In the past, Luo Xiao seemed to have betrayed the Holy Temple. However, Luo Xiao's motive and reason for his betrayal had never been announced. Instead, it had been sealed up as a secret.
After so many years, the Luo Clan had already been forgotten by the gods. Only this floating island was left for people to occasionally think of.
It wouldn't be long before the Luo Family's floating island completely fell into the sea of time. At that time, the last trace of the Luo Family would be completely erased.
Since Han Meng was not clear, Luo Zheng did not ask.
As the disc beneath his feet continued to rise, Luo Zheng saw more and more floating islands …
Gongsun Clan, Yang Clan, Xue Clan …
These floating islands were connected to each other in a spiral shape, as if they were the stairs of a heavenly pagoda, and disappeared into the horizon.
The scenery on each floating island was different. Some floating islands were covered in lush greenery, and some floating islands were covered in exquisite buildings. There was even a floating island that had a giant lake …
As Luo Zheng rose higher and higher, there were also many discs passing by Luo Zheng and Han Meng. Between floating islands, it seemed that these small discs were used as a means of transportation. And those who rode these small discs were mostly True Divinities. It was very rare to see a Proof of Divinity martial artist.
"Hahahaha …"
A burst of hearty laughter sounded out. It was as if there was no one else present, laughing and joking.
Not too far away, there was a disc that was moving in the same direction as the one beneath Luo Zheng's feet.
On that disc stood a group of young True Divinities. The laughter just now had come from these True Divinities. As for the seven people on the disc, they were all lower True Divinities. They should have just become Divinities a few years ago.
"Mm?"
One of them suddenly revealed a puzzled expression. He looked over from several dozen feet away.
Luo Zheng was also sizing them up. Their gazes suddenly collided.
"Qin Tianze …" Luo Zheng's face was stupefied, but his heart was slightly startled.
Back in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, in order to help Ji Luoxue obtain a cultivation platform, he had fought with this Qin Tianze. This Qin Tianze was the same as Han Liusu. He was also a member of the Han Family. Luo Zheng didn't expect to meet him here!
Although Luo Zheng's appearance had changed, in these few months, there had been continuous and subtle changes. This Qin Tianze shouldn't be able to recognize him.
But the most frightening thing was that in order to defeat him, Qin Tianze had used the Great Wish Technique.
According to the rules of the Great Wish Technique, as long as Qin Tianze didn't kill him, his cultivation would never improve …
And after making a wish, once he got close to the target of his wish, his heart would sense it! Thus, Qin Tianze's face was filled with confusion as he stared at him from afar.
"Tianze, what do you see?" A lower True Divinity beside Qin Tianze asked.
Qin Tianze stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Nothing …"
"You know that guy?" Someone asked.
"I don't." Qin Tianze still shook his head. "It's just that he gives me a strange feeling."
"Hahaha, are you being oversensitive? Your body has just recovered and you still need to rest. Don't think too much …" Someone comforted him.
Han Meng also felt it was strange. She asked Luo Zheng, "An old friend of yours?"
Luo Zheng hurriedly denied, "I don't know him … I'm just curious why he keeps staring at me."
"That's the young master of the Qin Family. It's said that he was injured in a battle in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement." Han Meng lowered her voice and said, "Moreover, he stupidly made a wish. In the end, the brat who defeated him ran away without a trace. It's said that the brat is a secondary life form in some universe. Right now, even the Sages of the Qin Family can't find him …"
"Since the young master of the Qin Family entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he should have been a Proof of Divinity expert, right? He didn't fulfill his wish, so how did he break through the World Lord Realm and step into the True Divinity Realm? "Luo Zheng asked in confusion.
Luo Zheng also cultivated the Great Wish Divine Dao. If he couldn't fulfill his wish, his cultivation would be permanently restricted.
For Qin Tianze to be able to break through this restriction, it was too miraculous …
Back then, Luo Zheng and Qin Tianze both had the same cultivation. Now, not only did Qin Tianze break through this restriction, he even became a lower True Divinity. Luo Zheng had fallen behind by quite a bit.
"No one can avoid the restriction of the Great Wish. Even if Qin Tianze is an extremely important person in the Qin Family, the Sage Emperor of the Qin Family personally acted and temporarily blocked his Great Wish Restriction. However, it's only a temporary measure. If we want to solve the restriction of the Great Wish Restriction, we still have to find that secondary life form," Han Meng said in a low voice.
The difference between the Han Family and the Qin Family wasn't that great. The two families were in a competitive relationship. As for the matters of the Qin Family, Han Meng was very familiar with them …
"Losing to a secondary life form … it seems it'll be difficult to find him," Luo Zheng said in agreement. He had a strange feeling in his heart. He didn't know what Han Meng would think if she knew that the secondary life form Qin Tianze was looking for was him.
"We definitely won't be able to find him," Han Meng said with a smile.
Qin Tianze's Great Wish Restriction had been blocked by the Sage Emperor of the Qin Family. As a result, the detection of Luo Zheng had become extremely vague, which was why Qin Tianze had felt so strange.
This could be considered a freak combination of factors. If the Sage of the Qin Family hadn't acted, Qin Tianze would have discovered Luo Zheng's existence. After all, no matter how Luo Zheng changed his appearance, he couldn't escape the Great Wish Technique.
After this block, Qin Tianze's cultivation smoothly stepped into the True God Realm. However, he had lost an excellent opportunity to "fulfill a wish".
Not long after, the small disk Qin Tianze was on suddenly turned and floated towards a huge floating island in the distance. That floating island was the Qin Family's floating island, ranked eleventh in the sea of time.
As the small disk floated away, Qin Tianze couldn't help but look back at Luo Zheng again. He felt that his mind wasn't clear.
Han Jia's floating island was one place higher than Qin Tianze in the sea of time, ranked tenth.
The small disk under Luo Zheng's feet continued to rise. Not long after, Han Jia's huge floating island finally appeared in front of Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1814
Right now, Luo Zheng was standing on the disc and had already risen high into the sky.
The surrounding clouds and mist gave Han Jia's floating island an ethereal feeling.
Looking down at the sea of time from this angle, the floating islands below were like a pearl necklace, connected to the magnificent sea.
Luo Zheng could see the center of the divine realm from here!
However, he didn't know where the Saint's Hall was. The sky was covered by thick clouds. He didn't know what kind of power the clouds contained, but he couldn't see through them.
As Luo Zheng continued to look around, the disk under his feet finally approached Han Jia's floating island.
When the disk stopped on the floating island, Han Meng jumped onto the floating island first. Luo Zheng also jumped lightly. The two of them left the disk, and the disk automatically flew out of the floating island, returning to the "dock" at the bottom.
After getting on the floating island, Han Meng continued to lead Luo Zheng forward. At the same time, she said, "You entered Han Jia's floating island because of the Extreme Purple Yin Body, so I'll take you to the Purple Soul Palace first."
Han Jia's floating island was divided into two parts. Han Jia's main family took up the majority of the island, but one sixth of the island was given to Aunt Han Jiu. Aunt Han Jiu was Han Jia's secondary saint, so she built the Purple Soul Palace on the floating island, which was independent of the main family.
Luo Zheng hadn't joined Han Jia's main family. He was actually chosen by Aunt Han Jiu to enter the Purple Soul Palace.
"I don't have the Extreme Purple Yin Body …" Luo Zheng didn't know whether to laugh or cry.
"I know. However, to me, if you can cultivate the Extreme Purple Yin Body, then you must have it," Han Meng said indifferently.
Since Han Meng said this, Luo Zheng had nothing to say. He naturally wouldn't explain about the Immeasurable Ruler. If she thought he had the Extreme Purple Yin Body, then let her think so …
Han Meng didn't take him into the main city. Instead, she took him along a small path.
Not long after, they finally reached their final destination, which was Aunt Han Jiu's territory — the Purple Soul Palace.
The Purple Soul Palace's style was extraordinary. Purple light slowly flowed through the complicated patterns. The light emitted its own aura. Luo Zheng even suspected that the entire palace was created by the Great Purple Qi Technique.
"Big Sister Meng is back?"
Just as they arrived in front of the Purple Soul Palace's door, a blue-clothed woman quickly stepped forward.
This blue robed woman was a Lower True God. Facing Han Meng who was an Upper True God, she didn't have any distinction of seniority. She very casually called out. At the same time, the blue robed woman sized up Luo Zheng who was behind Han Meng. Her face immediately revealed a pleasantly surprised expression. She immediately called out loudly: "Sisters, quickly come and take a look. Big sister Meng has found another junior brother for us!"
At the call of the blue-clothed woman, over ten women rushed out from the Purple Soul Temple. Among these women, there were Divine Realm martial artists as well as lower True Divinities. Each of them had their own merits. They all thought that male disciples were rare, so they all came forward to take a look …
As Han Meng saw this, she couldn't help but roll her eyes, "Honorable Master's discipline is lacking. These fellows have always been like this, you will slowly get used to it …"
Although there were many people in the Realm of the Gods who possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body and could cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Way, compared to the vast number of Gods, they were still a drop in the ocean. To be able to gather in the Purple Soul Palace could be considered a type of fate.
In addition, Aunt Han Jiu herself didn't like the distinction of seniority. So, in the Purple Soul Palace, besides Aunt Han Jiu's Honorable Master, all the other disciples, regardless of their strength or cultivation, called each other sisters. Over time, this kind of relaxed atmosphere formed.
There were very few male disciples in the Purple Soul Palace. Every time a male disciple appeared, these women would naturally find it strange and wanted to surround him to see what was going on.
So, not long after, over ten gorgeously dressed women tightly surrounded Luo Zheng. At the same time, they pointed and whispered …
"Is he from a poor family? He's only wearing a long gown, and he's wearing it so plainly? "
"I don't think I can feel the aura of the Extreme Purple Yin Body from his body. Can he really cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Way?"
"He's still very handsome. Is he really a genius recommended by the Heaven Containing Palace?"
"… …"
Hearing these chattering voices, Luo Zheng's forehead was covered in sweat. He really had never seen such a scene before. He even suspected that entering the floating island this way was a mistake … …
"Alright, little junior brother has just entered the floating island and is tired from the journey. Let's settle him down first. Don't make so much noise … …" Han Meng had already anticipated this situation, but she didn't have a way to resolve it.
In the Purple Soul Palace, there were actually two kinds of disciples. One was the disciples personally accepted by Aunt Han Jiu. The other was the ordinary disciples. Han Meng was Aunt Han Jiu's disciple, but she didn't dare casually scold these female disciples. If she did, these female disciples would tear up. If this were to spread to Honorable Master, not only would Honorable Master not order these disciples who didn't follow the rules, she would instead reprimand Han Meng. So, there were no rules in the Purple Soul Palace, only sisters.
"That … … I shouldn't be some little junior brother, right?" Luo Zheng corrected Han Meng's words.
Han Meng faintly smiled, "Although you haven't been accepted as a disciple, you still have to call me junior brother. Our Purple Soul Palace's male disciples are very popular … …"
"… …" Luo Zheng was speechless again. This Fairy Han Meng had been very normal the entire way, but after returning to the Purple Soul Palace, she seemed to have become somewhat abnormal. It seemed that the environment really could change people.
"Little junior brother, quickly call senior sister Qian!"
"Little junior brother, your bone age doesn't seem to be a hundred years old, right? So young … … "
"Senior sister Han Meng, let me arrange little junior brother's living quarters. Also, I'll just tell him the rules of the Purple Soul Palace … …"
With the "enthusiastic" help of these senior sisters, Luo Zheng finally settled down.
The Purple Soul Palace occupied one-sixth of the Han Clan's floating island, but the number of female disciples with the Extreme Violet Yin Body was only one in ten thousand of the main family's disciples. Thus, the Purple Soul Palace was relatively spacious, and Luo Zheng's living quarters was actually a single small palace.
This was where Aunt Han Jiu's tyranny came from. Although the floating island was large, its surface area was limited. This place couldn't be compared to Liuren Divine City. If the floating island's land could be traded, it would probably be the most expensive place in the entire Divine Realm.
Even the main family's disciples, if not for their special status and lofty status, could only be allocated a small and cramped living quarters. Normally, the outer family's disciples who could enter the floating island would be squeezed into a single room, and True Gods were no exception!
However, Aunt Han Jiu was a very protective person. As long as it was someone who followed her, she would definitely fight for the greatest benefits. Thus, she had forcibly asked for such a large piece of land from the Han Clan's Saint Emperor. Moreover, the number of disciples she had was limited, so she was naturally very well-off. Luo Zheng, who had been allocated a small palace the moment he entered, had always been envied by the other members of the Han Clan. Unfortunately, they didn't have the Extreme Violet Yin Body, so they couldn't be accepted into the Purple Soul Palace.
After nightfall …
The thick fog above the floating island still hadn't dissipated, but it had become much lighter.
Through the hazy moonlight, Luo Zheng could vaguely make out the huge objects above his head. Those were the floating islands of the other great clans.
"The Luo Clan has their own floating island. As a Saint, Master should have his own as well. Along the way, I haven't seen the Gu Clan's floating island. Why is that?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked the Extreme Evil Old Man.
Although it was the Extreme Evil Old Man's first time entering the floating island, he had been in the Divine Realm for a long time. He should have heard of these things.
"Hehe, although the North Sage's clan isn't very powerful, Gu Bei himself is very powerful! Although he only took in two disciples, they're both at the peak of the Perfection Paragon level! The entire Gu Clan relied on those two freaks to get their floating island into the top five … This was before I was captured by Gu Bei. I don't know what the Gu Clan's floating island is now, but it's definitely above the Han Clan's! " Said the Extreme Evil Old Man. He didn't know much, but this was just common knowledge.
"In other words, the Gu Clan's floating island is above them …" Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Hearing the Extreme Evil Old Man mention his master's two disciples, Luo Zheng immediately thought of the "Second Senior Brother" on the Path of Extinction. This Second Senior Brother had given him some pointers back then. Cultivating "Eight Zigzag Smoke," "Thunder Kill," and "Ten Thousand Spell Rival Book" had all been his suggestions.
"I still don't know their names," Luo Zheng said.

Chapter 1815
"Jing Wuhuan? Yu Shenfeng? "
Luo Zheng had never heard of these two names.
Old Man Evil nodded. "Yes. Although Gu Bei has become a Saint, he doesn't have many descendants. The Gu family has very few people, but his two disciples are very powerful! Especially the eldest senior brother, Jing Wuhuan. He has the potential to become a Saint! "
Since Jing Wuhuan was the eldest senior brother, then the second senior brother he had met before should be called Yu Shenfeng.
"With these two protecting you on the Sea of Time, hehe, you can do whatever you want," Old Man Evil joked. "Unfortunately …"
In terms of background, Luo Zheng had a higher starting point than many of the core disciples of the wealthy families.
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng's opponents were too powerful …
The first one he had to face was Mu Haiji, and even the entire Holy Church!
Luo Zheng's two senior brothers were indeed powerful, but if Luo Zheng casually showed up now, they might not be able to protect him.
"It's not suitable for me to look for them now." Luo Zheng shook his head.
The relationship between Luo Xiao, Gu Bei, and Jiu Sheng was probably not a secret in the Holy Church. Luo Xiao had been dealt with, and Gu Bei was not in the divine territory. Luo Zheng did not know about Jiu Sheng's situation, but those around Gu Bei would definitely be under Mu Haiji's surveillance. Luo Zheng was not stupid enough to show up.
"It's not suitable for you to look for them on the floating island, but with this relationship, hehe …" Old Man Evil smiled mysteriously. "You will have a chance elsewhere!"
"Elsewhere?" Luo Zheng was startled.
Old Man Evil continued, "You still don't know much about the divine territory. Hehe, remember you asked me about the use of Shenwu coins?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "Right, you said you would tell me about the use of Shenwu coins when we reach the floating island. Can you tell me now?"
Ever since Luo Zheng came into contact with the local customs of the divine territory, he had been very suspicious about Shenwu coins.
This black disc itself seemed to be worthless, but the purchasing power of Shenwu coins was quite strong. Even if a martial artist who had proven himself to be a god worked hard for many years, it would still be difficult to exchange for the value of a single Shenwu coin. Such a currency system was unimaginable in the universe!
From this point of view, Shenwu Coin was definitely not a currency issued by a certain clan. This could only mean that Shenwu Coin had a very important function. It was so important that all the third-tier, second-tier, and even first-tier wealthy families could not ignore it.
"Hehe, do you know what's the biggest thing in the divine territory?" Old Man Extreme Evil laughed. At this point, he was still planning to keep Luo Zheng in suspense.
"Sage?" Luo Zheng asked tentatively.
Old Man Evil shook his head.
Luo Zheng pondered for a moment and then said, "If it's not the Sage, then it should be that stone slab."
The Ten Thousand Commandments Slate, also known as the Governing Slate.
Even a Sage could not defy this slate. Just based on its strength alone, this slate deserved to be called the Ruling Slate.
"That's right." The Evil Old Man nodded. "There are all kinds of rules on the Governance Slate. These rules have been accumulated over countless divine epochs. They are also the product of constant compromises and negotiations between the Saints of the Noble Clans. Have you ever wondered how these rules were written?"
"From time to time, they write by hand …" Luo Zheng was puzzled.
The Ten Commandments Slate and the Hundred Commandments Slate in the Central Divine City were all like this. Luo Zheng also thought that the Governing Slate was the same.
Old Man Extreme Evil did not know whether to laugh or cry. "Do you think the Governing Slate is an ordinary slate? The slates you see are nothing compared to the Governing Slate. If you could casually write rules on them, the divine territory would be in chaos … "
The Ten Thousand Commandments Slate in Six Ren Divine City was already very powerful. However, it could only defend against ordinary upper-level true gods. If it was a Sage's disciple or a slightly more powerful upper-level true god, the Ten Thousand Commandments Slate's rules would not be able to do anything to them.
However, the Governing Slate was different.
Sages were already at the pinnacle of this world, but they were still trapped by the Governing Slate.
There had once been a Sage who tried to resist the Governing Slate but was severely punished in the end. In history, there were also some Sages who had violated the Governing Slate's rules and had no choice but to escape from the divine territory. They wandered in the chaos and did not dare to return to the divine territory …
Hence, the Governing Slate's rules were ironclad rules. Other than some small loopholes, no one could go against them.
As a stowaway, Luo Zheng did not go through the Jiang Family's reception.
In fact, he had violated the Governing Slate's rules. However, he had the Golden Bloodline. Luo Xiao had meticulously searched for loopholes in the Governing Slate to avoid the Governing Slate's punishment. Hence, Luo Zheng could be considered to have taken advantage of the Governing Slate's loophole this time.
After all, no matter how powerful the Governing Slate was, it was just a slate. It would only act according to the rules. However, rules that were set always had some flaws and loopholes that could be studied.
"Then, what's the connection between the Governing Slate and Shenwu Coins?" Luo Zheng found it difficult to connect the two, so he was still puzzled.
Old Man Evil explained, "It's very simple. Whether it's changing the rules on the Governing Slate or writing new rules, it requires Shenwu Coins."
"Using Shenwu Coins to write?" Luo Zheng blinked.
Old Man Evil's eyes widened. "After grinding Shenwu Coins, the essence is extracted to make Shenwu Ink!"
Controlling the Governing Slate was equivalent to controlling the divine territory.
And the Divine Martial Ink was the only material that could leave behind words on the Command Slate!
"Using Shenwu Coins to make Shenwu Ink can write the rules of the entire divine territory." Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. He asked again, "Since that's the case, the Noble Families will naturally collect Shenwu Coins and set rules that are beneficial to their families …"
The conflict between Mu Haiji and Luo Xiao was also a conflict over the rules of the Governing Slate.
Before Luo Xiao's death, the rules on the Governing Slate could only allow one True God to participate in the Universal War.
In order to be foolproof, Mu Haiji changed the rules on the Governing Slate.
Changing the rules was much cheaper than writing new ones. Changing this rule only required two strokes on the Governing Slate. In other words, changing "one" to "three". With this change in the rules, Mu Haiji believed that conquering the Dayan Universe was a foregone conclusion.
However, Luo Zheng had lured Zhan Beihai and Shi Xiaoqiao out of the Immortal Abode. Whether this was Gu Bei's countermeasure was still unknown, but it had also ruined Mu Haiji's plan.
"That's not right …" Luo Zheng thought for a while. "If I have enough Shenwu Coins, I can write a rule on the Governing Slate that forbids anyone in the divine territory from attacking me. Won't that mean that even the Saints can't do anything to me?"
Old Man Evil's eyes widened again. "Why don't you just write it and kill everyone in the Holy Church? Won't this world be peaceful?"
"That's possible," Luo Zheng said seriously.
"Hehe, unfortunately, do you think those Saints are idiots? What you can think of, they can't think of? So many Divine Epochs have passed. Why hasn't anyone written a rule like that? "Old Man Evil retorted.
Luo Zheng naturally didn't think that those Saints were dumber than him. There must be a reason.
"The amount of Shenwu Coins needed to write a rule on the Governing Slate is an astronomical figure. Even the Grand Families find it difficult to accumulate enough Shenwu Coins to write a rule once. Moreover, the rules written on the Governing Slate are divided into many levels! A rule like the one you mentioned is too heaven-defying. The amount of Shenwu Coins needed is unimaginable. I'm afraid that even if all the Grand Families' Shenwu Coins were added together, it would still be impossible to write it! "Old Man Evil shook his head.
"According to the different rules, the amount of Shenwu Coins needed is also different." Luo Zheng nodded. This explanation was correct. Otherwise, if a Saint was the first to write an invincible rule, he would have no opponents in the Divine Territory to speak of. The current Divine Territory wouldn't be in such a state as Luo Zheng saw.
Old Man Evil's explanation enlightened Luo Zheng. It was no wonder that the Grand Families valued Shenwu Coins so much. Shenwu Coins were indeed irreplaceable in the Divine Territory. At the same time, Luo Zheng had new doubts …
"These Shenwu Coins don't look like ores. Where did they come from?" Luo Zheng asked.
In the Central Domain, those square crystals were excavated from ore veins. True Yuan Stones and Zhen Yuanyu were the same. They were all condensed from Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi. Could it be that Shenwu Coins were also excavated from ore veins?

Chapter 1816
The wealthy families took up 80% of the resources in the Divine Dominion.
The countless second and third tier clans below the Noble Clans divided up the remaining 15% …
Even if the majority of the Divine Dominion's citizens were added together, they could only take up the remaining 5%.
If any of the divine citizens wanted to achieve something, they could only go to the various divine cities and slowly climb up through the various divine residences. Among them, those with exceptional talent had a slight chance of entering the floating islands, and only then would they be able to obtain a certain amount of resources and support from the floating islands!
"The most important resources here are the forbidden lands," the Extreme Evil Old Man emphasized. "All the Shenwu Coins are obtained from the forbidden lands. Apart from the vast amount of Shenwu Coins, there are also all sorts of precious treasures and rare materials. However, basically all the entrances to the forbidden lands are controlled by the wealthy families. Not to mention the citizens of the Divine Dominion, even True Gods have almost no chance of entering!"
"Back then, I was lucky enough to fish in troubled waters and enter the Soul Wilderness once." The Extreme Evil Old Man shook his head, his face filled with emotion. "Because of that one opportunity, the rewards were equivalent to several Divine Epochs worth of hard work …"
At that time, the Extreme Evil Old Man had just become a middle-level True God. However, regardless of whether it was his strength or influence, he was not ranked highly in the Vast Sky Domain. Occasionally, he would sneak into a wealthy family's team and experience life and death in the Soul Wilderness. After obtaining a considerable amount of rewards, he proudly pushed his cultivation to the peak of middle-level True God.
Since then, the Extreme Evil Old Man had made a name for himself and established his own territory in the north of the Vast Sky Domain.
With the Extreme Evil Old Man's strength, he could have created a third-tier family. However, with his eccentric personality, he naturally did not have such plans. He planned to use all his strength to become an upper True God!
To the True Gods on the floating island, they had almost unlimited resources. Even so, it was still relatively difficult for them to become upper True Gods. For a True God like the Extreme Evil Old Man, who was born in the Vast Sky Domain, every step forward was as difficult as crossing a natural chasm!
The Extreme Evil Old Man originally had a slight chance with that Stone Forest Rat, but in the end, he ended up in such a state.
"Those forbidden areas are so important …" Luo Zheng was lost in thought.
The Extreme Evil Old Man chuckled. "Although I have never been to the floating island, I believe that it won't be long before you have the opportunity to go to those forbidden areas. When the time comes, you will naturally know. Sigh …" The Extreme Evil Old Man sighed again. He was extremely envious of Luo Zheng for being able to enter the floating island. Now that he had entered the floating island, it was a pity that he was only left with this remnant soul.
The night passed …
Early the next morning, a plain-clothed woman came to inform Luo Zheng to head to the Purple Soul Palace's Dream Ending Pavilion.
Luo Zheng followed behind this plain-clothed woman. Along the way, he bumped into many women from the Purple Soul Palace. These women all stopped to look at him curiously.
Although there was no lack of men on Han Jia's floating island, Luo Zheng, as a male disciple who had just joined the Purple Soul Palace, naturally received a lot of attention. Naturally, there was no lack of pointing and whispering. Hearing these women's words, Luo Zheng's head was filled with black lines …
He wondered if "Aunt Han Jiu" had summoned him. After arriving at the Dream Ending Pavilion, he realized that he was thinking too much.
In the Dream Ending Pavilion, Luo Zheng saw a group of Proof of Divinity experts. Of course, they were all women as well. Other than Luo Zheng …
This group of Proof of Divinity experts had clearly not joined the Purple Soul Palace for long. Compared to the True Gods outside, they were much more inexperienced. When they sized up Luo Zheng, they were much more restrained. However, their eyes were filled with astonishment. Could it be that they were wondering why a man had come in?
After the plain-clothed woman brought Luo Zheng into the Dream Ending Pavilion, she said to everyone present in a deep voice, "In a moment, Grandma Yue Ying has something to say. You all know her temper. Everyone, be quiet!"
Hearing the plain-clothed woman's words, the originally chattering women suddenly quietened down. It was as if they all knew how terrifying Grandma Yue Ying was.
Not long after the plain-clothed woman left, a person slowly walked in from the back door of the Dream Ending Pavilion. It was a plump old woman. Her body looked very old, but her face was as delicate as a baby's. It looked very strange.
This should be the Grandma Yue Ying that the plain-clothed woman spoke of. Luo Zheng thought to himself.
After Grandma Yue Ying entered the Dream Ending Pavilion, the atmosphere in the entire Dream Ending Pavilion froze. The bodies of the women were all stiff. They were like statues. Some of the women even had expressions of fear on their faces.
Luo Zheng was very curious. What did Grandma Yue Ying do to make these women so afraid? Han Meng and Grandma Yue Ying were both Upper True Gods. However, Han Meng's temper was much better. Furthermore, Han Meng had said that in the Purple Soul Palace, other than Aunt Han Jiu, there was no hierarchy.
After Grandma Yue Ying stood still, her gaze swept around. Finally, it landed on Luo Zheng. She then said, "I heard that you were personally chosen by Master? Why don't I feel the aura of the Extreme Purple Yin Body from your body? "
Luo Zheng had only been brought into the Purple Soul Palace by Han Meng for a day. Grandma Yue Ying probably had something to tell him, so she asked this question.
"I do not have the Extreme Purple Yin Body." Luo Zheng cupped his hands.
Grandma Yue Ying frowned. "Not the Extreme Purple Yin Body? Why did Master summon you into the Purple Soul Palace? "
"Because I can also cultivate the Purple Cloud Divine Dao." Luo Zheng calmly explained.
"Look!" Grandma Yue Ying scolded.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly. He was slightly displeased. This old woman's temper was really bad. However, he had just entered the Purple Soul Palace and was unfamiliar with the place. He didn't want to bicker with her.
Thus, he stretched out an arm and gently flipped it. He condensed a purple cloud. The purple Dao circled once before quickly dissipating.
Granny Yue Ying's eyes revealed a trace of surprise before she nodded indifferently. "Yes. Although I don't understand how you did it, since you've already infiltrated the Purple Soul Palace, you're naturally a member of our Purple Soul Palace! Take it! "
After saying this, she stretched out her hand and threw a green jade card at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng reached out his hand and caught the jade card. He took a closer look at it. The top of the jade card was engraved with a large "Han" character, and the bottom was engraved with the words "Luo Tianxing". There was a faint and complex aura coming out of this nameplate. Presumably, this jade card was also made using some kind of secret method that couldn't be easily imitated.
Grandma Yue Ying said, "This is Han Jia's tablet. You can't do anything without this tablet on the floating island. Don't lose it."
"Thank you." Luo Zheng calmly replied.
"Although you've just entered the floating island, since you're a Proof of Divinity cultivator, there are some rules that I have to repeat …" Grandma Yue Ying then explained the basic rules of the floating island.
The old woman's words were very cumbersome. She often emphasized a sentence several times. Luo Zheng was very impatient, but he couldn't do anything. He could only brace himself and listen.
After a while, Grandma Yue Ying finally finished speaking. She then turned to face the group of women. When Grandma Yue Ying spoke again, Luo Zheng realized that she was already treating him very "kindly".
"Those who are ranked below the bottom three hundred this month, step forward!" Grandma Yue Ying shrieked. Her voice was so sharp that it could pierce eardrums.
When three of the women heard this, their bodies suddenly trembled.
"Step forward!" Grandma Yue Ying continued shrieking.
The three women had no choice but to take a few steps forward. The others also looked at them with pity.
Grandma Yue Ying suddenly waved her hand. Strands of pure purple qi quickly condensed. Seeing the purple qi, Luo Zheng's eyebrows raised slightly. Grandma Yue Ying's attainments in the Purple Qi Divine Dao were very high. The Dao within it was incomparably condensed.
The purple qi continued to condense, turning into a purple vine with thorns.
"Grandma Yue Ying …"
"We'll try to rush out of the bottom three hundred next month …"
"…"
The three women actually began to beg for mercy with a sobbing tone.
But Grandma Yue Ying was unmoved. With a wave of her hand, the purple vine coiled out. In an instant, Luo Zheng heard the crisp sound of falling hailstones.
"Pa pa pa pa pa …"
In that instant, Grandma Yue Ying pulled out the vine hundreds of times.

Chapter 1817
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly when he saw the three beautiful women being beaten so miserably.
Han Meng had said that the Purple Soul Palace had a relaxed atmosphere. It seemed like there was a cruel side to it.
Although he wasn't sure what Granny Yue Ying meant by "ranking below 300", it was probably a mission given by the Purple Soul Palace. These three women had failed to complete it and were thus punished.
After Granny Yue Ying finished whipping them, she patted them lightly!
A woman carrying a porcelain plate came in from the Dream Ending Pavilion. There were three blood-red pills on the porcelain plate.
Granny Yue Ying sneered, "Swallow these Blood Muscle Pills to heal your wounds!"
The healing panacea bestowed by the Purple Soul Hall was naturally not an ordinary item. Even from such a distance, Luo Zheng could smell the rich pill fragrance coming from the Blood Muscle Pill. Presumably, this pill had been refined by an extremely powerful alchemist.
However, for some unknown reason, when the three women saw the medicinal pill, their complexions turned deathly pale. They were actually extremely fearful of the medicinal pill.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind.
"Heh, this old hag has some tricks up her sleeve. Although the Blood Muscle Pill is a panacea for healing injuries, its effects are strange, and it can also increase the strength of their physical bodies. However, swallowing this pill is extremely painful." The Extreme Evil Old Man laughed. This method was in line with what the Extreme Evil Old Man had done in the past. When punishing subordinates who were strong but disobedient, the Extreme Evil Old Man would often give this kind of "reward".
The three women's injuries looked terrible, but they were only superficial. After cultivating to the Sector Lord realm, these injuries would heal on their own. There was no need to use the Blood Muscle Pill.
Granny Yue Ying had given them these pills to increase their strength. On the other hand, it was another form of punishment.
Although the three women weren't willing to swallow the Blood Muscle Pill, under Granny Yue Ying's pressure, they each plucked one and placed it in their mouths.
After swallowing the three pills, the three of them began to violently tremble. Large amounts of blood sprayed out from their originally open wounds. At the same time, they let out shocking screams. Their originally beautiful faces became ferocious. This Blood Muscle Pill was clearly giving them intense pain!
The blood started to coagulate on the surface of their skin. Layer by layer, it formed a blood cocoon that completely wrapped them up. It was only at this moment that their screams stopped. When they broke out of the cocoon, all the wounds on their bodies would disappear.
These female disciples weren't old and hadn't been on the floating island for long. Their mental states hadn't been tempered. Such a punishment would definitely leave a deep impression on them.
Luo Zheng looked at all this indifferently.
Although he didn't like Granny Yue Ying's temper, after experiencing a thousand tribulations, he had a clear understanding of the cruelness of time. The competition on the floating island would only get more intense.. if he wanted to become stronger, if he wanted to obtain Han Jia's, if he wanted to obtain more resources, he would have to be all.
At this time, Granny Yue Ying faced Luo Zheng and asked, "Luo Tianxing, do you know why the three of them are being punished?"
"I don't know," Luo Zheng shook his head.
Granny Yue Ying then said, "According to the rules of the floating island, everyone who enters the floating island, from Paragons to Proving Gods, has to fight at least three battles in the Pantheon Jade Wall every month. According to our Purple Soul Palace's rules, the last three hundred people must be punished."
"Fight in the Pantheon Jade Wall? What's the Pantheon Jade Wall? "Luo Zheng was stunned. He had never heard of this thing before.
"You'll know soon enough. Everyone, follow me!" After saying that, Granny Yue Ying walked towards the main entrance of the Dream Ending Pavilion. The female disciples present glanced at the three blood cocoons with fear in their eyes before following closely behind Granny Yue Ying.
Luo Zheng curled his lips and followed.
Granny Yue Ying led this large group of people through the enormous Purple Soul Palace.
Granny Yue Ying was probably the only one in the Purple Soul Palace with a bad temper. Along the way, all the women, be it Proving Gods, Lower True Gods, or even Middle True Gods, quietly avoided her …
"Old Man Extreme Evil, have you heard of the Pantheon Jade Wall?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind.
"I've heard of it, but I don't know much about it. It seems that this thing can determine the floating island's ranking, but as for the specifics … I'm not sure," Old Man Extreme Evil said, shaking his head.
"Determine the floating island's ranking?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed. "That's not right. If the Pantheon Jade Wall can determine the floating island's ranking, why would it let us participate?"
The floating island's ranking was very important. It was definitely a contest between the Sages. How could mere Proving Gods like them participate?
"That's unknown. Let's wait and see." Old Man Extreme Evil shook his head.
Not long after …
Granny Yue Ying led the group into a nameless hall.
When Luo Zheng entered the nameless hall, he saw a huge jade wall erected in the middle of the hall!
This jade wall was two hundred feet tall and three hundred feet wide. It was dark green in color.
This time, Luo Zheng didn't need Granny Yue Ying to explain. He already knew that this was the so-called "Pantheon Jade Wall." However, what was the use of this Pantheon Jade Wall? How did it determine the floating island's ranking?
Granny Yue Ying walked toward the jade wall, stretched out her shriveled right hand, and formed a mysterious hand seal. Then, she patted the jade wall lightly.
"Weng …"
A soft sound was transmitted from the jade wall.
A ray of light slowly rose from the bottom of the jade wall and slowly moved upward. It continued to grow branches and leaves until it finally opened completely.
"This is …"
The jade wall's green light reflected in Luo Zheng's eyes. The green light continued to outline, revealing one name after another …
At the bottom of the jade wall, the names were densely packed together. They were as small as mosquitoes and flies. At a glance, there were probably millions of names. The higher up the jade wall, the larger the names outlined by the green light. The names were also more conspicuous!
At the top of the jade wall, the names were as tall as a person. Moreover, the characters at the top of the jade wall were all different. They seemed to have been personally written!
"Fang Henshao! Tang Wan! Mu Xuerong! Heavens! These are all first-rate characters in the Divine Realm! "Before Luo Zheng could size them up, Old Man Extreme Evil's voice had already reverberated in his mind.
In addition to the people mentioned by Old Man Extreme Evil, Luo Zheng also saw some familiar names at the top of the jade wall. Yu Taibai, Yu Shenfeng, and Jing Wuhuan were also there.
The people at the top of the jade wall were all at the Perfection Realm. Their cultivations were second only to the Sages. They were also the group of talents in the Divine Realm most likely to become Sages.
"Could it be that the Pantheon Jade Wall is just a ranking of strength?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind.
Old Man Extreme Evil shook his head. "It's not that simple. Look at the densely packed names at the bottom of the jade wall. Some of them seem to be constantly jumping."
Luo Zheng looked in the direction Old Man Extreme Evil was pointing. Indeed, it was as he said. Some of the names inadvertently jumped down a notch, while others suddenly jumped up.
"Could it be that the Pantheon Jade Wall has a mystical ability to automatically rank everyone on the floating island? Isn't this a little too mystical? " Luo Zheng revealed a trace of disbelief. Perhaps the cultivation of living beings could be measured, but aside from actual combat, there was almost no way to measure one's strength. Having a high cultivation did not necessarily mean that one was powerful. This was something Luo Zheng had already verified countless times.
"Impossible. Let alone this jade wall, even the Governing Slate cannot do this." Old Man Extreme Evil was also in disbelief.
While Luo Zheng and Old Man Extreme Evil were discussing the use of the Pantheon Jade Wall, Grandma Yue Ying had already turned around. She stared at Luo Zheng as if she wanted to see through him. "Master said that the geniuses specially recommended by the Heaven-Containing Prefecture do not need to be tested by the Purple Soul Palace. She seems to be very confident in you. However, I am somewhat doubtful of Master's confidence. I don't care what methods you used to deceive everyone by using the Purple Clouds Divine Dao, but you still need to prove your strength. If you can't even defeat the most basic of opponents, I will definitely report this to Master and expel you from the floating island! "

Chapter 1818
Granny Yue Ying didn't know Luo Zheng's background.
She wasn't well-liked in the Purple Soul Palace, but she was someone that the Purple Soul Palace couldn't possibly lack.
