Although the Purple Soul Palace didn't follow seniority and all the female disciples were like sisters, there was always a need for someone to maintain the basic rules.
This was why Aunt Hanjiu had given Granny Yue Ying so much power. Her position was similar to that of a sect's law enforcement elder. It was also because of her many years of experience that she had developed a suspicious personality.
Although Luo Zheng had been personally selected by Aunt Hanjiu to enter the Han Clan's floating island, and Han Meng had also personally visited the ancestral land, Granny Yue Ying believed that she had to help her master check.
"Your opponents?" Granny Yue Ying sneered. "There are too many! In this Divine Jade Wall, you can find your opponent at will! "
"How do I find my opponents?" Luo Zheng looked at the huge Pantheon Jade Wall again. Although he didn't understand, he had a guess.
"Place the jade tablet I gave you before on the jade wall." Granny Yue Ying pointed at the jade wall.
Under Granny Yue Ying's guidance, Luo Zheng walked over to the jade wall. He realized that the bottom of the jade wall wasn't completely flat. There were small grooves on it. Luo Zheng took out the jade tablet Granny Yue Ying gave him and placed it in one of the grooves. At the same time, he activated the jade tablet.
"Buzz buzz …"
The moment the tablet was activated, the jade wall in front of Luo Zheng rippled with water. At the same time, the words "Luo Tianxing" appeared.
Luo Zheng had used the alias "Luo Tianxing" to enter the floating island. After the Purple Soul Palace helped him register, he naturally used this name as well. In addition to his name, the number of battles he fought and his points were also recorded, but the words "0" were displayed behind them.
Right at this moment, a glimmer of light shot out from the jade disc and enveloped Luo Zheng within it.
"This light …"
Luo Zheng's eyes lit up. He seemed to have understood something.
Luo Zheng had seen this light before. When the Great Era Battle was held in the Great Era of the Great Era, it was nominally to choose a great era's genius. In reality, it was to train ten Dao Children out of a trillion cultivators!
When he entered the Illusory Battlefield, he had relied on this kind of light.
It was very likely that his father had used the Divine Jade Annulus on the floating island as a reference when he created the Illusory War. The Dayan Universe was his father's world after all, so the Illusory War he created was even larger and more complicated.
"Buzz …"
At the same time, Luo Zheng felt his mind buzzing.
Everything around him began to collapse rapidly. He was quickly immersed in an illusory world. At the same time, Luo Zheng could no longer sense the existence of the Evil Old Man!
"As expected!" Standing between heaven and earth, Luo Zheng roughly understood the purpose of the Deities Jade Annulus.
There must be more than one Divine Jade Annulus like this. There should be quite a number of them on the floating islands above the Sea of Time. Through the Divine Jade Annulus, all the True Gods could be projected into this illusory world to fight.
The Extreme Evil Old Man had just said that the Pantheon Jade Annulus could determine the ranking of the floating islands. Luo Zheng finally understood.
Just now, when Luo Zheng placed his jade tablet in front of the Divine Jade Annulus, he could see that his points were zero. That was because it was his first time entering the Divine Jade Annulus.
As a member of Han Jia, he represented Han Jia in battle. Every time he won a battle, he would gain a small amount of points. The Proof of Divinity cultivators on the floating island, True Gods and even Paragons, had to choose their own battles to accumulate points.
According to the points of everyone on the floating island, the floating island's ranking would be determined.
In this competition for the rankings, the Sages did not directly participate in the process. Their main disciples would fight on their behalf.
"The stronger the person, such as Yu Taibai and Yu Shenfeng, the more terrifying the number of points they would gain. The Proof of Divinity cultivators' points should be pitifully low. However, even if it's low, the big families will still pay attention to it. That's why when the three ladies in the Dream Ending Pavilion fall to the bottom three hundred, they will be severely punished …" Luo Zheng stood in place and thought to himself. "I wonder who my first opponent will be after entering the Divine Jade Annulus this time?"
At that moment, a ray of light slowly flashed in front of Luo Zheng. Bits of dust floated out from that ray of light. As the dust continued to condense, it quickly formed a human figure. A man in brown brocade clothes slowly appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
That man sized up Luo Zheng. His eyes flashed with a hint of mockery as he looked at Luo Zheng. "You're also entering the Arena of Divinity for the first time? According to the rules of the Arena of Divinity, my opponent should also be entering for the first time. "
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. It turned out that the world that one entered through the Divine Jade Annulus was called the Arena of Divinity. This was the place where the gods competed.
It was also that old granny's fault for not being bothered to explain it clearly. She did not even tell Luo Zheng the rules. She only allowed him to come in for a challenge. Grandma Yue Ying probably thought that he was a swindler, so she directly threw him into the Arena of Divinity. If he lost, he was afraid that she would really chase him off the floating island …
Seeing Luo Zheng nod, that man's mocking smile became even more intense. "Then your luck really isn't too good." As he said that, he shook his head and sighed, as if he was silently mourning for Luo Zheng.
"Why isn't my luck too good?" Luo Zheng asked in astonishment.
That man laughed heartily. "Because your first time is destined to be defeated by me! Remember my name. My surname is Tang, and my name is Tang Yu! It should be a great honor for you to be able to fight with me! "
Tang Yu was very confident.
As a descendant of one of the top ten great families on the floating island, Tang Yu was also the son of the Tang Family's secondary saint. After painstakingly cultivating for seventeen years, he had finally stepped onto his own path!
Tang Yu's reputation on the floating island was not small. At least many true gods knew that another little monster had appeared in the Tang Family.
This was Tang Yu's first time entering the Arena of Divinity. At the level of Proof Divinity, he planned to continuously win his way up. After all, his strength had far surpassed other Proof Divinity experts of the same level. It could even be said that the majority of Proof Divinity experts were not even worthy of fighting with him!
Seeing Tang Yu's spirited appearance, Luo Zheng responded indifferently, "Oh, can we start now?"
"Of course …" Tang Yu laughed wildly as his left and right hands began to form two completely different hand seals.
However, Tang Yu would never have thought that his first time entering the Arena of Divinity would be to encounter Luo Zheng. It was definitely his misfortune …
Compared to the complicated methods that Tang Yu was about to use, Luo Zheng's methods were clean and neat.
His figure suddenly slid along the ground and approached Tang Yu at an extremely fast speed. The golden light in his eyes flashed faintly, and a strange smile appeared on his face.
"Soul attack? Stupid … "
Tang Yu felt the soul pressure bursting out from Luo Zheng's eyes. For a moment, he felt disdain.
However, this disdainful thought only lasted for a moment. When Luo Zheng turned that wisp of soul pressure into a sharp arrow and completely rushed into his mind, he realized that this soul pressure was much more terrifying than he had imagined.
The stupid one wasn't his opponent, but himself!
The unlucky one wasn't his opponent, but himself …
In the eyes of others, Tang Yu was considered the new little monster of the Tang Family. He also boasted that among the Proof Divinity experts, he was almost invincible. However, he never thought that he would encounter a monster with such a powerful soul in his first battle.
As the son of a Quasi-sage, Tang Yu's reaction was quick-witted. Those Proof Divinity experts from the Floating Island were not comparable to him. In fact, the Proof Divinity experts on the floating island were more than one level stronger than the Proof Divinity experts among the major Proof Divinity experts. This was because all the geniuses in the entire Divine Domain were gathered here, above the Sea of Time!
After feeling Luo Zheng's extremely powerful soul pressure, Tang Yu also made a countermeasure. He knew that after his opponent stabbed his soul, he would immediately follow up with a killing move.
Previously, Luo Zheng had used this move to defeat Hulan Zhuo in the Hanyue Building.
However, Tang Yu was ultimately much more outstanding than Hulan Zhuo. He suddenly clenched his teeth and used all his strength to retreat backward, wanting to avoid Luo Zheng's killing move!
Unfortunately, although he realized this, he still couldn't avoid Luo Zheng's pursuit!
"Pu!"
This punch landed on Tang Yu's body. It didn't contain much skill, but the power in this punch was far from what he could withstand.
"Regardless of speed, power, or soul, they are all far beyond my imagination. Is this guy really a Proof Divinity expert?"

Chapter 1819
On the Tang Family's floating island …
A young man in his early twenties came out from the jade wall.
This young man was Tang Yu, who lost to Luo Zheng.
Outside the Divine Jade Wall, there were a dozen people from the Tang Family.
In the center was a gray-haired, middle-aged old man. He had a long beard and long eyebrows. He had a square face and round eyes. There was a faint flame flowing between his eyebrows. He unconsciously emitted a powerful aura.
This person was Tang Yu's father, the Tang Family's Second Sage, Tang Jianchou!
After he sent Tang Yu to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement to cultivate, he returned to the Tang Family's floating island. Today was Tang Yu's first time entering the Arena of Gods.
Tang Jianchou had spent a lot of effort on Tang Yu. Naturally, he wanted to see his son's performance. Therefore, he accompanied Tang Yu to the Divine Jade Wall.
Tang Jianchou didn't doubt Tang Yu's strength.
Even the Proof of Divinity martial artists on the floating island weren't weak, but Tang Yu had just stepped into the Arena of Gods. The opponents he would encounter wouldn't be too strong, so Tang Yu should win. According to Tang Jianchou's estimation, Tang Yu would be able to achieve at least ten consecutive victories within four hours, or even twenty consecutive victories before he would encounter a true opponent.
However, the moment Tang Yu exited the Divine Jade Wall, green words flowed slowly below the Divine Jade Wall. It actually showed that Tang Yu had lost!
"Tang Yu lost?"
"Did Nephew Tang purposely go easy on him?"
"Or did he encounter a genius from another wealthy family?"
The many True Gods present looked at each other. They didn't understand what had happened.
Even the Second Sage, Tang Jianchou, was surprised. "Yu 'er's Yang Body is invincible among those of the same level. How could he lose in the first round?"
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The Divine Jade Wall's light that enveloped Tang Yu's body dissipated. Tang Yu climbed up from the ground with a depressed expression. His eyes were still in a daze.
"Yu 'er, what kind of opponent did you encounter? Why did you lose in the first round? "Tang Jianchou asked.
Although Tang Jianchou was a Second Sage, he didn't have many children. Tang Yu was his most talented son in the past few decades. Naturally, he would protect and care for him.
The people around Tang Jianchou also stared at Tang Yu, wanting to hear his explanation.
Tang Yu pouted and said rather helplessly, "Dad, your son's luck isn't good. I actually met a monster in the first round …"
"Monster?" Tang Jianchou and the other Real Gods were stunned. "Is the opponent very strong? Can he break your Yang Body? "
Tang Yu's face was full of resentment. "I didn't have time to activate my Yang Body before that guy attacked. His soul is ridiculously strong, and it seems to be even stronger than an ordinary lower True God …"
"What?"
"The soul strength of a Divine Warrior is comparable to a True God?"
"Which rich and powerful family raised this little guy to be so capable?"
Actually, the people present had some doubts about Tang Yu's words, but since it was Tang Yu who said it, they didn't dare to refute.
On the floating island, the number of outstanding Proof of Divinity martial artists was not large. Almost every family had a few heaven-defying little fellows, but cultivating a Golden Battle Soul was already the limit. Even Tang Jianchou had not seen any family's child who could cultivate a True God Soul …
"Just having a strong soul isn't enough to defeat you." Tang Jianchou shook his head in disbelief.
"It's not just a strong soul. Whether it's speed or power, it's far beyond the scope of a Proof of Divinity martial artist. He only used one move to defeat me, and he didn't use any martial arts or Divine Path. I suspect that guy used cheating methods. He's definitely at the True God Realm! It must be! "Tang Yu said hurriedly.
The gods present didn't say anything. They knew that it was impossible …
Looking at Tang Yu's embarrassed and anxious expression, he smiled faintly. "That's Yu 'er's bad luck. Maybe he really met a genius raised by a rich and powerful family. Don't be discouraged, Yu' er. Since ancient times, no one has been able to keep winning in the Arena of Gods. If you lose, just fight again!"
"I understand." Tang Yu nodded.
In the first round of the Arena of Gods challenge, he had suffered a blow to the head. To him, this wasn't necessarily a bad thing. It would also let Tang Yu know his own strength!
"But what's the name of your opponent? Which rich and powerful family does he belong to? "Tang Jianchou asked.
"That guy's name is Luo Tianxing. He's from Han Jia!" The Pantheon Jade Wall wouldn't show Tang Yu's opponent's information, but before Tang Yu entered the Arena, he could see his opponent's name and clan.
"Surname Luo … Han Jia's external martial artist," Tang Jianchou murmured. "And it's his first time entering the Arena of Gods. Looks like that guy didn't grow up on the floating island …"
Although Tang Yu gave very little information, Tang Jianchou still made a fairly accurate judgment.
However, Tang Jianchou didn't pay too much attention to it. Geniuses in the Sea of Time would appear every day. Most of them were like meteors streaking across the sky before disappearing in the Sea of Time. A mere Proof of Divinity martial artist wasn't enough to attract the attention of a quasi-sage.
On the floating island of Han Jia …
Luo Zheng also left the Pantheon Jade Wall cleanly.
After the light enveloping his body dissipated, he leaped up with a relaxed expression. On the Pantheon Jade Wall behind Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's victory was already displayed. After his victory, a small "1" was written.
Granny Yue Ying naturally saw this scene. Her face, which didn't match her bloated figure at all, revealed a hint of surprise. She wasn't surprised that Luo Zheng had defeated his opponent. Instead, she was surprised that Luo Zheng could finish the battle in such a short time …
"Fortunately, I didn't let you down. I've won my first match," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile as he cupped his hands.
Unexpectedly, Granny Yue Ying shook her head. "You were just lucky. Your first opponent was also an opponent who had just stepped into the Arena of Gods. Such an opponent is too weak to count, right?"
Luo Zheng shrugged and said disapprovingly, "Indeed, he's too weak."
If Tang Jianchou and Tang Yu heard the conversation between Granny Yue Ying and Luo Zheng, they would probably vomit blood in anger.
Granny Yue Ying didn't know who Luo Zheng's opponent was. If she knew that Luo Zheng's opponent was the son of a quasi-saint, she wouldn't have said such words. As for Luo Zheng, he really felt that Tang Yu's strength was very ordinary …
"Go and challenge him again. You have to win at least five matches in a row to prove your strength," Granny Yue Ying said calmly.
Hearing Granny Yue Ying's words, the female disciples present looked at each other. Some of them were also silently worried for Luo Zheng.
There were strong and weak opponents in the Arena of Gods. Even if you were lucky enough to win this match, there was no guarantee that you would be able to defeat your next opponent. You might encounter some genius from a powerful family. If you met such a genius, you would almost certainly lose. Granny Yue Ying was deliberately making things difficult for Luo Zheng by letting him win five matches in a row.
Of course, these female disciples felt indignant for Luo Zheng in their hearts, but no one dared to jump out and say a word.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate at all. To Luo Zheng, this kind of battle couldn't even be considered a warm-up. After turning around, he activated the jade tablet once again and entered the Pantheon Jade Wall for the second time.
In the misty and illusory world, Luo Zheng's second opponent had appeared.
It was a burly man with a rough appearance. His face was full of valiance and his appearance was abnormally ferocious. This burly man had just appeared in the Arena of Gods when he started speaking ill of Luo Zheng, wanting to gain the upper hand by a show of strength and suppress Luo Zheng's aura.
However, just as he opened his mouth, he felt his vision blur, and he was stepped on by Luo Zheng. "You're far inferior to the previous guy. The disciples of powerful families are far less powerful than I imagined," Luo Zheng said as he shook his head.
"You, you …" The burly man under his feet kept struggling.
Luo Zheng looked down at the burly man under his feet and smiled slightly. "Let me remind you. Aura isn't produced by talking. Aura … is the embodiment of one's strength!" After saying this, Luo Zheng lightly exerted force with the tip of his foot. Immediately, there was the sound of bones breaking. The second match was over.
In the first match against Tang Yu, Luo Zheng had only spent about twenty breaths of time from the moment he entered the Arena of Gods until the end, including the time when Tang Yu was talking nonsense.
The second match had taken nine breaths of time.

Chapter 1820
He had ended the battle in such a short time and even won.
The female disciples in the nameless hall were all puzzled.
Given their strength, it was difficult for them to understand how Luo Zheng had won the battle. Could it be that Luo Zheng's opponent had not been able to hold out against him in such a short time?
"Luo Tianxing's strength can't be stronger than Senior Sister Lan's, right?"
"There's no comparison now. Senior Sister Lan has already attained the Divine Dao. Moreover, Senior Sister Lan's points were high at that time, so her opponents were naturally stronger …"
"I also feel that they can't be compared. After all, Luo Tianxing has just entered the Divine Jade Wall. His opponents can't be too strong."
The female disciples finally could not help but discuss in extremely soft voices.
The Senior Sister Lan they were talking about was once the number one Divine Dao martial artist in the Purple Soul Palace. She had displayed her talent not long after entering the Purple Soul Palace. After entering the Arena of Gods, she had won all the way …
However, not long ago, Senior Sister Lan had ascended to the True Immortal Realm.
Now that they saw this newcomer's outstanding performance, they could not help but compare him to Senior Sister Lan.
Granny Yue Ying had not expected such a situation at all. She had always been suspicious of everything, so she had thought that there was something wrong with the Pantheon Jade Wall. Two consecutive battles. One took twenty breaths, while the other took less than ten breaths.
Even if Luo Tianxing's opponents were weak, they should not have been weak to this extent. After all, this was the floating island. Luo Tianxing's opponents were not the grassroots Gods outside the Sea of Time.
This could only mean one thing. Luo Tianxing's strength was too strong. The difference in strength was so great that it was enough to crush him!
Granny Yue Ying had already acknowledged Luo Zheng in her heart. However, she did not relax at all. Her face was still tense as she said, "Again!"
Luo Zheng did not speak. He turned around and activated the jade pendant again.
As Luo Zheng was enveloped in a gentle light, Granny Yue began to calculate the time in her heart.
She wanted to see if this kid was really that amazing. Could it be that he only needed a few breaths to finish off each opponent?
One breath, two breaths, three breaths …
In fact, it was not only Granny Yue Ying who was doing this. The female disciples were also curious about Luo Zheng.
When they counted to six breaths, the words on the Divine Jade Wall changed. The gentle light that enveloped Luo Zheng dissipated. Luo Zheng had already exited the Arena of Gods.
"Six breaths …"
"He's won another match. He's already accumulated 12 points."
"His points has already surpassed mine!"
The points in the Arena of Gods's points.
Generally speaking, the higher the points a martial artist had, the more victories they would have, and the higher their ranking and strength would be.
For example, the three female disciples who were punished by Granny Yue Ying had negative points, so they were ranked in the bottom three hundred of the floating island.
To be exact, they were the last three hundred of the Divine Warriors.
In the Arena of the Gods, there were a total of five levels of battles. The Proving God Martial Artists were in the first level, while the True God Realm's upper, middle, and lower levels were in the second level.
The last level was called "Battle of the Heavenly Kings." The people who participated in this level were experts like Yu Taibai and Yu Shenfeng...
...... Luo Zheng's opponents would actually become stronger and stronger. However, in the Proof of Gods' level, Luo Zheng's first opponent, Tang Yu, was actually qualified to stand at the very front.
Luo Zheng's strength was already enough to completely crush this level. It was up to the people with the highest points to see if they had the qualifications to stop Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng's points were too low. Naturally, it was difficult for him to foresee those people.
After three matches, the way Granny Yue Ying looked at Luo Zheng had also changed a lot.
Although Granny Yue Ying was extremely strict and was disliked by many Proof of Gods and True Gods, she was extremely loyal. Everything she did was for the benefit of her master, Aunt Han Jiu!
Now that she understood Luo Zheng's value, she naturally wouldn't mention banishing him from Han Jia Floating Island.
This time, before Granny Yue Ying could say anything, Luo Zheng activated the jade tablet once again...
However, the duration of the fourth battle was even shorter than the previous one.
From the time he entered the Arena of Gods to the time he came out, it was only two breaths of time.
This didn't make sense. There wasn't enough time to make a move. However, Luo Zheng's victory increased by another round. His points also increased by eight. He had a total of twenty points.
After the light dissipated, Luo Zheng looked at everyone's puzzled expressions. He smiled awkwardly. "I met my first opponent. The other party directly admitted defeat."
It turned out that Luo Zheng met his first opponent in the Arena of Gods, Tang Yu.
After Tang Yu lost to Luo Zheng in the first match, he won two consecutive matches. With Tang Yu's strength, he could also crush ordinary Proof of Gods and quickly accumulate points. He didn't expect to meet Luo Zheng again after winning two consecutive matches. He could only blame himself for being unlucky.
Tang Yu was also an understanding person. Although he was extremely confident in his own strength, he had fully analyzed "Luo Tianxing's" strength. He knew very well that he had no chance of winning. He might as well just admit defeat.
Therefore, in the eyes of Granny Yue Ying and the others, Luo Zheng had miraculously created a new record.
Not long after, the five matches Luo Zheng agreed to with Granny Yue Ying ended. Granny Yue Ying didn't ask Luo Zheng to continue. Instead, she asked Luo Zheng to take back the jade tablet. At the same time, she said to the female disciples present, "Enter the Arena of Gods on your own."
Hearing Granny Yue Ying's order, the female disciples neatly took out their jade tablets and stepped forward to place them into the grooves at the bottom of the jade wall.
This jade wall was three hundred meters wide and could accommodate three to four hundred people entering the Arena of Gods at the same time. There were probably quite a few jade walls like this on the floating island.
"For Master to be able to make Han Meng personally come, Master's judgment can't be wrong. Luo Tianxing, you are very outstanding," Granny Yue Ying said with a faint smile.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng cursed inwardly. She had just said that she was suspicious of Master and wanted to expel him from Han Jia's floating island. Now, she had changed her words. This old woman was really …
"This large hall is specially provided for your use. If you encounter any problems in your cultivation, everyone in the Purple Soul Hall can be your master. If you have time, I suggest you come here more often and enter the Arena of Gods to earn points," Granny Yue Ying said.
"What are the benefits of earning points?" Luo Zheng asked.
To be honest, Luo Zheng wasn't very interested in entering the Arena of Gods every day to challenge those Proving Divinity cultivators.
Currently, Luo Zheng was in a hurry to break through his bottleneck and condense his godhood …
If entering the Arena of Gods to earn points was only to help Han Jia's floating island increase its ranking, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't be too motivated.
"The use of points?" A strange smile appeared on Granny Yue Ying's face. She then said in a lecturing tone, "Don't you know that all the floating islands in the Sea of Time, all the clans, and everyone's status is measured by the Jade Annulus of the Gods?"
Just as the Evil Old Man had said, the only reason they wanted to enter the floating islands was because all the entrances to the forbidden areas were occupied by the floating island's rich and powerful families. For grass-roots True Gods like them, it was extremely difficult to enter the forbidden areas. It was a once-in-a-lifetime opportunity.
As for the warriors and True Gods on the floating islands, if they wanted to enter the floating islands, they had to rely on themselves to fight for it. Points were one of the criteria. Each forbidden area had different requirements for points.
In addition to obtaining the right to enter the forbidden areas, Han Jia would also provide extremely attractive rewards and even special privileges based on the ranking of the points.
It could be said that the Arena of Gods' points were second only to Shenwu Coins to the people on the floating islands. Sometimes, they were even more important than Shenwu Coins.
Granny Yue Ying was actually patient enough to explain so much to Luo Zheng …
"Understood." Luo Zheng nodded.
He had to admit that after countless Divine Epochs, the rich and powerful families on the floating islands had indeed found an effective way to motivate everyone to work hard.
Although the Arena of Gods didn't involve the Sages, if the strength of their own True Gods declined, the floating island's ranking would also decline. This also forced the Sages to put in all their effort to nurture the disciples on the floating islands and their own followers.

Chapter 1821
After passing Granny Yue Ying's test, Luo Zheng regained his freedom.
The female disciples who entered the Purple Soul Palace were all focused on cultivating the Purple Cloud Divine Dao, hoping to attain the Divine Dao as soon as possible.
Luo Zheng could no longer raise his strength, and the Purple Soul Palace's restrictions were very lax, so he naturally had nothing to do.
Fortunately, there was no one in the Purple Soul Palace to control Luo Zheng. These days, he had been roaming around the Purple Soul Palace and had a rough understanding of the status of the rich and powerful disciples.
There were a total of seven Pantheon Jade Annulus in the nameless hall in the Purple Soul Palace, and one could enter the Arena of the Gods to challenge each of them. Everyone had to challenge three opponents every month. If they left during the month and returned to the floating island, they had to make up for it within a limited time. These records were all recorded.
However, most true gods weren't willing to challenge the Arena of Gods. They were more willing to explore the various forbidden lands and earn Shenwu Coins.
There were just too many experts in the Arena of Gods, and the opponents they chose based on their points were usually on par with them.
If they were careless and lost, a portion of their points would be deducted, making it difficult for them to obtain the qualifications to enter certain forbidden lands. This was also why the floating island forced everyone to undergo three battles.
Apart from this, Luo Zheng was also carefully collecting information on Mu Haiji.
The Mu Family led by Mu Haiji was ranked seventh, three ranks higher than Han Jia's floating island. The entire floating island's overall strength was definitely stronger. On the Pantheon Jade Annulus, the woman called Mu Xuerong was ranked third at the very top. In terms of ranking, she was higher than Yu Taibai, Yu Shenfeng, and Jing Wuhuan!
However, Yu Taibai was his father's chief disciple, and the Luo Family's floating island had already been destroyed. How did Yu Taibai maintain his ranking?
He had some doubts, but he couldn't find an answer in the Purple Soul Palace. One day, he simply left the Purple Soul Palace and headed for the Han Family's Saint Emperor City.
The floating island of the Han Family was divided into three parts. The Purple Soul Palace was the territory of Secondary Sage Han Jiu Yi. Apart from that, the floating island was also divided into Saint Emperor City and Spirit Heart Palace. Spirit Heart Palace was where the direct descendants of the Han Family's Saint Emperor lived. Ordinary people couldn't enter.
Saint Royal City was the place with the most people on the entire floating island. Most of the main family disciples and disciples with different surnames were gathered there.
Before entering the Saint Emperor City, Luo Zheng had to go through a round of inspection by the guards. Fortunately, he already had his own jade card.
The two guards saw Luo Zheng's jade tablet and sized up Luo Zheng. Their faces revealed a trace of ridicule. "A man from the Purple Soul Palace? That's rare … "
Luo Zheng silently took back the jade token and entered the Saint Emperor City.
When he entered Saint Royal City, he finally understood what a sea of people meant. He also understood why the Han Family disciples envied the Purple Soul Palace.
From the moment they stepped through the city gate, the road was packed with True Gods from all walks of life. There was a clamor everywhere. This was no different from a market set up by mortals. However, these people were all reputable True Gods, living beings with endless lifespans. Squeezing here and there like this could be considered tiring.
"Tomorrow, a third-level forbidden land will open. They are here to recruit twelve people. They require middle-stage True Gods and two thousand points …"
"Gather three lower-stage True Gods and require five hundred points. We will enter the Demon-Killing Forest together …"
"…"
On the plaza to the right of Luo Zheng, hundreds of True Gods were shouting loudly. They were all inviting other True Gods of the same cultivation level to explore the forbidden land together.
Just like what Luo Zheng understood previously, forbidden lands were linked to points. In order to enter certain forbidden lands, one had to work hard to raise their points …
"Demon-Killing Forest, tsk tsk …" The Evil Old Man in Luo Zheng's mind revealed a look of envy. "These people can enter the Demon-Killing Forest as they wish. This world is so unfair!"
"What kind of place is the Demon-Killing Forest?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Evil Old Man replied emotionally, "It is a second-level forbidden land. However, this forbidden land is very small. Before the Demon-Killing Forest was controlled by the wealthy families, many grassroots True Gods would enter it. I was also a frequent visitor. Later on, even the Demon-Killing Forest was taken over by the wealthy families. Sigh …"
"Second-level forbidden lands. How many of these forbidden lands are there in the Divine Plane?" Luo Zheng asked.
Hearing Luo Zheng's question, the Evil Old Man explained the division of forbidden lands in the Divine Plane to Luo Zheng.
After the Holy Hall was established, it had been monopolizing the resources of the entire Divine Plane. Among them, the most important were these forbidden lands.
When the Holy Hall was first established, it announced that the four forbidden lands belonged to the Holy Hall. No True God was allowed to enter without the permission of the Holy Hall!
Before this, all the forbidden lands in the Divine Plane could be entered at will.
This action caused dissatisfaction in the entire Divine Plane. However, dissatisfaction aside, who dared to challenge the authority of the Holy Hall?
In the beginning, they controlled the four forbidden lands. Later on, they controlled all the forbidden lands …
The forbidden lands in the Divine Plane were divided into six levels according to their danger level. The four forbidden lands were all level-six forbidden lands and were also the most dangerous places in the Divine Plane. These four forbidden lands were the Soul Wilderness, Buzhou Spiritual Mountain, Takixuan Forest, and the Abyss Demon Territory.
Until now, the wealthy families had explored the four forbidden lands countless times. However, they had never fully understood these forbidden lands.
If one were to explore these four forbidden lands, even a True God at the peak of the Great Circle of True Gods would perish if they were not careful!
"Takixuan Forest is also one of the four forbidden lands?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
The Evil Old Man nodded. "That's right."
"Then how did you escape …"
Gu Bei's immortal abode stood in Takixuan Forest. When the immortal abode's boundary was opened, the forest had left a deep impression on Luo Zheng.
"That's because the Northern Sage's residence didn't go too deep into the forest, and we were quite lucky that day …" Old Man Malevolence still had a lingering fear in his heart at the mention of this. The ferocious creatures in the forest had swarmed into the residence as if they'd gone mad, and hadn't bothered them at all.
At this moment, someone suddenly shouted from the side of the plaza, "Don't expect to go to Buzhou Spiritual Mountain during this period of time. The Jiang family and Yu Taibai are fighting there. It's best to be careful!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows shot up.
After entering Han Jia's floating island, Luo Zheng had been looking for such news.
However, most of the women in the Purple Soul Palace were focused on cultivation. They didn't care about the bigger picture in the Divine Plane. He didn't expect to hear news of Yu Taibai as soon as he arrived at Han Jia's Saint Emperor City!
"Yu Taibai and the Jiang family are fighting again?"
"Does Yu Taibai control Buzhou Spiritual Mountain now? With his strength alone, he dares to cause trouble for the Jiang family again and again. I wonder what he's planning …"
"Haha, if Yu Taibai really angered the Jiang family's Saint Emperor, he won't be able to escape death. After all, even Firmament Saint died!"
"Not necessarily. Yu Taibai has been operating in Buzhou Spiritual Mountain for so many years. If he really does control Buzhou Spiritual Mountain, even the Jiang family's Saint Emperor won't be able to do anything to him …"
Luo Zheng's eyes burned. He passed through the bustling crowd and walked towards the person who spoke.
This person had the cultivation of a middle true god. He wore a strangely shaped headscarf and had a kind face.
"Friend, how did Yu Taibai and the Jiang family fight? Can you tell me more about it? "Luo Zheng asked.
That person didn't notice Luo Zheng at first, but after hearing Luo Zheng's words, he casually glanced at him with a strange expression. "Proving divinity? What are you doing here? "
The true gods in this square would exchange information about the forbidden area with each other. At the same time, they would choose suitable candidates to explore the forbidden area …
Normally, proving divinity martial artists would wholeheartedly strive to prove divinity after entering the floating island. Who would have the time to come here?
"My cultivation isn't important. I want to know what you just said," Luo Zheng asked seriously.
He didn't care about Yu Taibai. Although Yu Taibai was his father's disciple, he had never met Luo Zheng. The problem was that Luo Yan was with Yu Taibai!
"Go away, what's a trifling proving divinity martial artist doing here! Go back and cultivate! "That person waved his hand impatiently, wanting to send Luo Zheng away.
"Hahaha, little guy, your cultivation is ordinary, but you like to gossip?"
"It's not easy to enter Han Jia's floating island. It's better to improve your cultivation …"
There were also a few true gods nearby who mocked Luo Zheng. They were quite surprised that a proving divinity martial artist would appear here.
"Hualala …"

Chapter 1822
The True Gods on the floating island were naturally different from the True Gods in Liuren Sacred City.
They could use their points to explore the forbidden grounds and earn Shenwu Coins. These people were much wealthier than the True Gods outside the Sea of Time.
Even so, the few hundred Shenwu Coins that Luo Zheng threw out was not a small sum to them!
They were on Han Jia Floating Island, so Luo Zheng knew that these people would not dare to act recklessly. He was in a hurry to get information about Yu Taibai and Luo Yan. What was a mere Shenwu Coin worth?
"Telling you this information, these Shenwu Coins are all mine?" The other party licked his lips.
A Proof of Divinity expert could casually throw out a few hundred Shenwu Coins. Seeing this scene, the True Gods began to guess Luo Zheng's identity. After all, they would only get a few dozen Shenwu Coins each time they explored the forbidden grounds. Even if they were especially lucky, they would only get a few hundred Shenwu Coins. Moreover, they had to risk their lives for it.
Now, just by telling this guy some information, they could earn such a large sum of money. Who wouldn't be tempted?
"Isn't it about the conflict between Yu Taibai and the Jiang Family? I know about it too. How about I tell you? "Another True God came up to him.
"Haha, Yu Taibai should be in control of Buzhou Spiritual Mountain now, right? He has the capital to fight against the Saints. They have already caused trouble for the Jiang Family a few days ago …" Another True God chimed in, but his eyes were fixed on the Shenwu Coins under Luo Zheng's feet.
Although there was a lot of Shenwu Coins, it was not enough for these True Gods to fight over them. Although there were no rules on the floating island, no one dared to act recklessly. Besides, Luo Zheng had thrown out so many Shenwu Coins in one go. Everyone suspected that he had a big background!
The matter of Yu Taibai was not a big secret. The middle True God warned everyone to be careful when they went to Buzhou Spirit Mountain. He did not expect to suddenly attract a windfall. Seeing that several other True Gods wanted to compete with him, his face suddenly changed. He immediately stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Don't listen to them. I got the news first-hand. Ordinary people don't know the inside story …"
"Take the shenwu coins. Let's talk at the side," Luo Zheng said calmly. He did not want to attract too much attention. Although he used the alias "Luo Tianxing", his surname was still Luo. If he were to ask about Luo Xiao's disciples in such a big way, it would inevitably arouse suspicion.
Although Luo Zheng was only a Proof of God martial artist, his aura did not lose to these middle True Gods at all. Money made the world go round. These middle True Gods were not disgusted by his attitude at all. Instead, they quickly picked up the Shenwu Coins on the ground and followed Luo Zheng to the side.
The remaining few Real Gods had disappointed expressions. They had lost an opportunity to make a fortune, so they were all grumbling.
"Is there something wrong with that kid?"
"He's willing to spend a few hundred shenwu coins just for some worthless information …"
"Sigh, too bad he didn't look for me."
Luo Zheng brought the middle True God to a secluded place with great difficulty. Then, he said, "You can speak now."
The middle True God laughed. "I'm very curious. You're just a Proof of God martial artist. Where did you get so many shenwu coins? Are you from the Han Family? "
"Don't ask what you shouldn't ask." Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
"Digression, digression." He was afraid that he would make Luo Zheng unhappy. After all, he had taken Luo Zheng's money. Therefore, he said, "Yu Taibai made a move last time. Actually, I found it quite strange. Yu Taibai is Luo Xiao's eldest disciple. Even if he wanted to take revenge, he should look for the Mu Family. However, not long ago, Yu Taibai made a move against the Jiang Family … However, Yu Taibai did not kill anyone. At that time, the Jiang Family was sending emissaries to various universes, but those emissaries were forced back by Yu Taibai."
Luo Zheng nodded. He knew about this.
Xingwei had helped him, Hua Tianming, and the others to sneak into the divine territory to avoid the Mu Family's surveillance. As long as Jiang Yu 'er was in the Great Yan Universe, he would not be able to hide from the Jiang Family. Later, Jiang Yu' er returned to the divine territory and never appeared again.
When Luo Zheng was sneaking in, Xingwei told him that it was his eldest senior brother who had stopped the Jiang Family.
"Yu Taibai's current strength is enough to fight against a Saint?" Luo Zheng asked.
The middle-level True God looked longing. Then, he shook his head. "In terms of true strength, he is naturally inferior to a Saint. After all, a Saint created the universe. As long as the universe is not destroyed, the Saint will not die. Even Luo Xiao did not die!"
"If the Jiang Family's Saint makes a move, won't Yu Taibai be unable to deal with him?" Luo Zheng asked.
The middle-level True God looked strange. He stared at Luo Zheng and continued asking, "Do you really not know, or are you pretending not to know?"
Luo Zheng was about to shake his head when the Evil Old Man spoke in his mind. "Luo Zheng, if Yu Taibai really controls Buzhou Spiritual Mountain, he will not be afraid of a Saint. Just like Shi Xiaoqiao, she controls the Soul Wilderness. Relying on the Soul Wilderness, she is also not afraid of a Saint."
"Shi Xiaoqiao?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"The woman on the second floor of the dungeon …" The Evil Old Man was very reluctant to talk about that woman. The three of them had accompanied each other in the dungeon for several epochs. But when that woman was mentioned, the Evil Old Man still looked terrified.
Not only was Shi Xiaoqiao not afraid of a Saint, but the Saint would also be afraid of her.
Ever since Shi Xiaoqiao entered the Soul Wilderness and obtained the Momentary Silence of the Gods Staff, the Saints were no longer as arrogant as they were before.
When she first obtained this staff, Shi Xiaoqiao had used it once. It was also at that time that the entire Divine Realm, from the Saints to the Gods, had their inner worlds sealed for a breath's time!
In that breath's time, the Saints were shocked to discover that the close connection between them and the universe had strangely disappeared!
A breath's time was not long, but it could be fatal at critical moments.
The strength of Saints could often reach a delicate balance. If two Saints fought and Shi Xiaoqiao used the Momentary Silence of the Gods at the most critical moment, the result could be imagined!
Therefore, the vast majority of Saints were very wary of Shi Xiaoqiao. There were also some Saints who wanted to subdue this extraordinary woman. Unfortunately, Shi Xiaoqiao had already disappeared for nine divine epochs. She had only suddenly appeared in the Great Evolution Universe recently, which was also something that many people did not expect.
After the Evil Old Man's reminder, Luo Zheng asked again, "If Yu Taibai really isn't afraid of the Jiang Family, why did they find trouble with him this time?"
The middle-level True God chuckled. "That's the key. Most people only know what's on the surface. From what I know, although Yu Taibai has operated in Buzhou Spirit Mountain for many years, he hasn't completely controlled the mountain. The Jiang Family also learned of this secret, so they took action at the critical moment! This way, Yu Taibai might die. Not only will he die, but the descendants of the Luo Family who followed him will also die! "
"What …" Luo Zheng was startled.
Seeing Luo Zheng's expression, the middle-level True God shook his head. "This is normal. No one who goes against the Templars has a good ending. When the Luo Family was defeated, Yu Taibai should have chosen a tree to live in. With his talent, he might even have become a Saint by now. How could he have fallen to such a state …"
The middle-level True God probably felt apologetic for taking so many Shenwu coins from Luo Zheng. While he was lamenting about this matter, Luo Zheng had already turned to leave. However, before he could take a step, he turned back and asked, "How many points does it cost to enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain?"
"Points to enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain? You want to enter? "The middle-level True God was amused.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Haha, Buzhou Spirit Mountain is a sixth-level forbidden area. Normally, it costs more than eight thousand points. Moreover, middle-level True Gods like us can only wander around Buzhou Spirit Mountain. We don't dare to enter. Even if an upper-level True God can fly, it's difficult to survive in Buzhou Spirit Mountain. As for you … it's better for you to prove your godhood first!" The middle-level True God advised.
Luo Zheng didn't take the middle-level True God's warning to heart.
His father didn't seem to have made many arrangements for him when he sent him to the divine territory. It was as if he had allowed Luo Zheng to climb up on his own. There were too many coincidences involved in this.

Chapter 1823
Han Family's floating island, Spirit Heart Palace …
Over a hundred important members of Han Family were gathered in a row in front of the palace.
Sitting at the head of the table was the current head of the Han Family, Han Tianxiao. Sitting below him was the Han Family's Second Saint, Han Jiu Yi.
Aunt Han Jiu sat on a thick blanket. She wore an elegant dress that made her look dignified and beautiful. However, her eyes wandered, seeming somewhat absent-minded.
"Aunt Han Jiu, this time it's clearly your Purple Soul Palace that's dragging us down. If our Han Family doesn't handle this well, our floating island's ranking will drop by two places," Han Tianxiao said loudly as he stared at Aunt Han Jiu. His voice was full of blame.
The entire palace was silent.
Aunt Han Jiu was the Han Family's Second Sage. Her strength was second only to the Han Family's Saint Emperor.
The head of the Han Family, Han Tianxiao, was only at the peak of the Saint Emperor. However, the Saints behind the various large families of the floating island no longer dealt with specific matters. As long as the Han Family didn't encounter a life or death crisis, the Han Family's Saint Emperor wouldn't interfere. Instead, he would leave it to the head of the family, Han Tianxiao, to handle.
However, as a member of the same family, everyone knew Aunt Han Jiu's temper. She had always been unreasonable on the floating island. Now that Han Tianxiao didn't give her any face and bluntly criticized her, no one knew how she would respond.
"If it's dropped, it's dropped. What's the big deal?" Aunt Han Jiu said indifferently.
Han Tianxiao seemed to have expected her to say this. His expression immediately became serious. "Dropping out of the top ten will cause our Han Family to lose many opportunities. Han Family's disciples will also lose even more. How can we just brush it off with a 'what's the big deal'?"
In the past, Han Tianxiao had tolerated Aunt Han Jiu, which was why he gave the Purple Soul Palace so many privileges.
If Han Family's floating island dropped by two places, it would truly be a huge blow. Han Tianxiao had specifically asked for the Saint Emperor's permission, which was why he opposed her.
Hearing this, Aunt Han Jiu shook her head. "Even so, my Purple Soul Palace is powerless. Those women's talents are originally weaker than the main family's disciples. It's not realistic to expect them to earn more points."
The Han Family Floating Island recruited martial artists based on merit, especially after going through the selection process of the Heaven-containing Palace. Almost all of them were the best Proving Divinity martial artists in the Han Family Ancestral Land. Their talents couldn't be too weak.
But Aunt Hanjiu was different. As long as one possessed the Extreme Violet Yin Body and was recognized by the Dao Tablet of the Violet Cloud Divine Dao, she would accept them all.
After all, the number of people in this world who possessed the Extreme Purple Yin Body was in the minority. The Purple Soul Palace could not select geniuses like the main family.
In this way, the Purple Soul Hall's overall strength was on the weaker side. They did not have an advantage in the Arena of Gods. Almost every time, the Purple Soul Hall would drag the Main House down.
Aunt Hanjiu also realized this. Although she herself was powerful, she wasn't good at managing her own disciples. She often indulged and spoiled the disciples of the Purple Soul Palace, so she had no choice but to let Granny Yue Ying go out and punish the disciples who were dragging them down …
But the ones who were dragging them down weren't the Proving Divinity martial artists. Looking at it from an overall perspective, they were missing a lot of points. So what needed to be improved was not the Proving Divinity martial artists, but the entire Purple Soul Palace.
"No, there's a way." Han Tianxiao said in front of the entire Han Family. He had already calculated all of Aunt Hanjiu's excuses. "Expel all the Proving Divinity martial artists from the Purple Soul Palace. Expel the bottom eighty percent of the True Divinities as well. That way, our Han Family will have the confidence to pull the floating island's points up at the last moment!"
The floating island's points were calculated from the entire island. If a large portion of the Purple Soul Palace's disciples were expelled and their jade tablets removed, the Purple Soul Palace's people who were dragging them down naturally wouldn't be counted. The floating island's points would immediately soar by a large amount!
"Expel eighty percent of the Purple Soul Palace's disciples!"
Aunt Hanjiu had already guessed that Han Tianxiao would want her to expel the disciples. If this was the only way, Aunt Hanjiu would have compromised. This was the Han Family after all, and she was the Han Family's secondary sage. Everything had to be done in consideration of the big picture.
But Aunt Hanjiu only wanted to expel the bottom ten percent of the disciples. She didn't expect Han Tianxiao to make such a ridiculous request. Expel all the Proving Divinity martial artists and eighty percent of the True Divinities …
This was equivalent to having her shut down the Purple Soul Palace!
Aunt Hanjiu wasn't the only one who was surprised. The important members of the Han Family were also surprised.
Aunt Hanjiu had hopes of becoming a sage. If the Han Family could have two sages, the Han Family's status would naturally soar again. Wasn't Han Tianxiao going too far by treating her like this?
"This request of yours is impossible." Aunt Hanjiu shook her head, her charming face darkening.
"This isn't my request." Han Tianxiao continued, "This is the Sage Emperor's order. If you have any objections, you can go directly to the Sage Emperor. Also, let me remind you that the Sage Emperor places great importance on this matter of the floating island's demotion. He definitely won't allow the Han Family to fall outside of its real name. There's no point in you going to the Sage Emperor … unless you can make the Purple Soul Palace's points rise every day in three months!"
The floating island rankings were calculated once a year. After three months, all the floating islands in the Sea of Time would be recalculated to determine the floating island's ranking.
Making the entire Purple Soul Palace change in three months was something even Aunt Hanjiu, a secondary sage, couldn't do. Secondary sages weren't allowed to participate in the Arena of the Gods. No matter how strong Aunt Hanjiu was, she couldn't affect the Purple Soul Palace's points …
"The Sage Emperor also said this?" Aunt Hanjiu coldly glared at Han Tianxiao. Her body suddenly floated up and disappeared. She really was going to find the Han Family's Sage Emperor to verify it …
"Master, expelling eighty percent of the Purple Soul Palace's disciples, isn't that too much?" A Han Family member asked.
The Extreme Purple Yin Body in the Purple Soul Palace wasn't easy to obtain. It was the resources that Aunt Hanjiu had accumulated over the years. Besides, the Extreme Purple Yin Body was indeed a bit weak in the beginning, but once it rose up, it would be extremely powerful. Aunt Hanjiu herself was a living exception.
"We can't wait until then." Han Tianxiao's eyes were filled with determination. "The Sage Emperor already has a new plan. We must maintain our ranking within the top ten!"
Aunt Hanjiu went to find the Han Family's Sage Emperor. Outsiders didn't know what the result was, but everyone knew that Aunt Hanjiu returned to the Purple Soul Palace and, for the first time, gathered all the disciples of the Purple Soul Palace.
In front of a large platform inside the Purple Soul Palace, all the disciples were gathered. Luo Zheng was naturally among them.
As far as the eye could see, there were probably thirty to forty thousand female disciples present.
Although Luo Zheng had heard that there were men with the Extreme Purple Yin Body in the Purple Soul Palace, Luo Zheng hadn't seen a single one of them since entering the Purple Soul Palace.
Today, after searching through the crowd for a long time, Luo Zheng finally found two "men" with the Extreme Purple Yin Body. However, the movements of these two men were extremely similar to women. They were like eunuchs in a mortal emperor's palace …
In short, the only real man in the entire Purple Soul Palace was probably Luo Zheng.
The thirty to forty thousand people waited here for a long time. A large door behind the platform was suddenly pushed open. A group of upper True Gods poured out. Luo Zheng saw Grandma Yue Ying and Han Meng in the middle.
These upper True Gods stood neatly on both sides of the platform.
Then, another woman walked out …
With Luo Zheng's current vision, he could clearly see the difference between lower True Gods, middle True Gods, and upper True Gods.
However, the aura emitted by these two women was unfathomable and hard to grasp in Luo Zheng's eyes!
"Is this Aunt Hanjiu?" Luo Zheng asked in his mind. "A Secondary Sage's aura is indeed extraordinary!"
Who knew that after Luo Zheng praised her, Old Man Extreme Evil couldn't help but say, "This woman isn't Aunt Hanjiu. Aunt Hanjiu is a Secondary Sage of the Han Family. Her cultivation is second only to a Sage. This woman is obviously a Perfected True God …"
"Cough cough …" Luo Zheng was slightly embarrassed.
A Perfected True God had already cultivated their own world to perfection. They only lacked nine stars to create a universe. Their strength was indeed unfathomable.
"This woman should be Aunt Hanjiu's disciple," Old Man Extreme Evil deduced.

Chapter 1824
The moment this woman appeared.
The inner worlds of all the Purple Soul Palace disciples began to tremble. Of course, other than Luo Zheng …
Aunt Hanjiu had already cultivated her Purple Cloud Divine Dao to the "Purple Sky Decimating Earth" level, so her every movement had a strong effect on the other Extreme Purple Yin Physique disciples.
Only Luo Zheng was an exception. His dantian did not move at all.
In front of the platform, there was silence.
A few well-informed female disciples had already heard the exact news.
Under Han Tianxiao's pressure, the Purple Soul Palace had no choice but to expel the majority of their disciples.
To the disciples of the Saint Royal City, this was a piece of great news.
The disciples of Saint Royal City had long been envious of the Purple Soul Palace. At the same time, they felt it was unfair. Why were those weak women of the Purple Soul Palace able to obtain more resources from the Han Clan?
But with Aunt Hanjiu's support, these disciples did not dare say anything.
Now that the clan leader had personally ordered Aunt Hanjiu to expel eighty percent of the female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace, they all felt extremely satisfied.
In the past few days, these female disciples had been ridiculed by the other disciples in the Saint Emperor City. When they first heard the news, they didn't dare to believe it. Only after a while did they believe it to be true!
Now that they saw Aunt Hanjiu's expression, they were even more certain of this matter.
Some of the women's eyes immediately turned red, as if they were about to cry …
Some of the female disciples had stayed in the Purple Soul Palace for many years. Although it was difficult for their strength to increase, they had deep feelings for the people of the Purple Soul Palace. How could they bear to leave?
That sorrowful mood spread among them. Very quickly, some people began to cry. Even Han Meng and the others on the platform were affected. Their eyes were red.
"It seems that everyone already knows." Aunt Hanjiu said softly. "The majority of you must leave within three months …"
The Saint Royal City had given a time limit of three months. When three months were up, the majority of people had to cancel their jade tablets on the floating island. These people naturally had to be expelled from the floating island!
"All of the Proof of Divinity cultivators must leave. Even the True Divinity Realm, eighty percent will be expelled …" Aunt Hanjiu said indifferently.
Luo Zheng had also heard about this matter. Now that he suddenly heard this quasi-sage announce it personally, he was also rather disappointed …
He had just entered the floating island and hadn't even had the chance to show his talent, yet he was going to be chased out?
They didn't even give him a chance. Luo Zheng naturally felt wronged. If the last 80% of the Proof of Divinity martial artists were expelled, Luo Zheng had a high chance of entering the top 20%. But Han Jia didn't even care about the Proof of Divinity martial artists. It directly killed all the Proof of Divinity martial artists in the Purple Soul Palace!
Aunty Han Jiu looked very capable. Outwardly, she was swift and decisive, but in reality, she was outwardly hard and outwardly soft. Although the expulsion of the Purple Soul Palace disciples didn't affect Aunty Han Jiu much, she still felt very pained.
But she had already argued with the Saint King once. This time, the Saint King's attitude was very clear. He didn't care what method Han Jiu used. As long as the Han Clan's floating island remained within the top ten, the female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace would be safe.
But this was an almost impossible mission. How could Aunty Han Jiu greatly increase the strength of the Purple Soul Palace disciples in such a short time?
Just as the girls were in grief, Luo Zheng suddenly took a deep breath and stood out …
Luo Zheng was a mere Proof of Divinity martial artist. It was clearly inappropriate for him to stand out in this kind of situation. And he didn't have the Extreme Purple Yin Body. He was more like an outsider than everyone else. So this Luo Zheng mustered up great courage.
"Honorable Master, I want to know how our Purple Soul Palace can survive this calamity!" Luo Zheng said clearly, as if he was throwing caution to the wind.
To the side, Grandma Yue Ying suddenly took a few steps forward. Just as she was about to scold him, Aunty Han Jiu reached out and gently stopped him. A kind expression appeared on her face as she smiled faintly. "You're the person Han Meng brought back from the ancestral land not long ago?"
Although Aunty Han Jiu didn't remember his name, she at least remembered that there was someone like him in the Purple Soul Palace. At the same time, Luo Zheng said, "I'm called Luo Tianxing."
Aunty Han Jiu nodded and continued to say, "Of course there is a way. I've calculated that in a short three months, each of you will have to win four matches in a row in the Arena of Gods. Is this possible?"
Having one of them win four matches in a row wasn't difficult.
But having thirty thousand people each win four matches in a row was somewhat inconceivable. It was simply an impossible mission. Everyone present couldn't help but sigh in their hearts when they heard their Honorable Master's words.
But at this time, Luo Zheng continued to ask, "What if we can do it? Will everyone be able to stay in the Purple Soul Palace? "
Hearing Luo Zheng's rhetorical question, Aunty Han Jiu smiled bitterly. As expected, the ignorant were fearless. She didn't know the difficulty of this at all. But Aunty Han Jiu still nodded. "In theory, it is indeed so …"
"There's still three months. Why don't we strive towards this goal?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a sharp light as he asked again. His tone was aggressive, as if he was blaming Aunty Han Jiu.
Although Luo Zheng was only a Proof of Divinity expert, when these words came out of his mouth, everyone felt their hearts heat up. What he said was right. This goal might be an impossible goal, but it didn't mean they should give up now!
"We can give it a try!"
"Although the chances are small, giving up like this is too unreconciled!"
"We … won't be a burden to Han Jia!"
"There's still time. We can work hard!"
Luo Zheng's words quickly resonated. The women present couldn't help but become restless.
Originally, everyone here had sunk into sorrow. But because Luo Zheng stood up and interrogated them, the fire in their hearts was ignited!
Looking at the hustle and bustle below, Aunty Han Jiu's charming eyes were fixed on Luo Zheng. Her eyes were full of admiration. At the same time, she thought to herself, "What a powerful little fellow …"
If it was an ordinary person who wanted to stir up depressed emotions, it wouldn't be an easy task.
Luo Zheng faced her without the slightest fear …
A Proof of Divinity expert dared to interrogate a quasi-sage. When had this ever happened on the floating island?
Since such a thing had happened, then it wasn't impossible for the Purple Soul Palace to raise their ranking in three months and reach the level of the main house disciples!
This was the effect of Luo Zheng's words!
"Since these little fellows are so crazy, I'll accompany you all this once …" Aunty Han Jiu thought to herself. Then she said, "Since that's the case, I announce that the Purple Soul Palace has entered a special period. During this period of time, the Purple Soul Palace will provide unlimited support to all disciples!"
After Aunty Han Jiu's words, the scene became even more heated. Luo Zheng took advantage of the chaos and quietly hid in the crowd. Only Aunty Han Jiu had unparalleled insight. She noticed Luo Zheng's actions, and in the depths of her eyes, there was a faint trace of doubt.
A few days later …
Luo Zheng quietly found a piece of Pantheon Jade Annulus. Below it, there were hundreds of people sitting cross-legged. Their bodies were completely immersed in the Arena of Deities. They were all focused on killing in the Arena of Deities, hoping to earn more points in a short period of time.
Many people were disgusted with the Arena of Deities. Other than completing the three matches set every month, they generally wouldn't step into the Arena of Deities again.
But now, in order to "save themselves", the disciples of the Purple Soul Palace could only frantically increase their points and rankings in the Arena of Deities!
For a time, the Pantheon Jade Annulus in the Purple Soul Palace was packed with people!
"I won another victory today. It's already two consecutive victories!"
"Be careful. When you win two consecutive battles, the opponent you meet in the next match will definitely not be weak!"
"Mm, I know!"
The rules of the Arena of Deities were very fair. If you continued to win consecutive battles, the opponents you met would only become stronger and stronger.
Luo Zheng waited for a long time. Finally, a woman put away her jade tablet and left the Arena of Deities. On her jade tablet, it showed that she had two more victories. Normally, people who won two consecutive battles would take a good rest before facing the third battle.
After the woman left, Luo Zheng placed his jade tablet into the groove below. The woman was a lower True God. She was still immersed in the joy of two consecutive victories. She looked at the jade tablet with a somewhat absent-minded expression. After all, Luo Zheng's cultivation was one level lower than hers.

Chapter 1825
During this period of time, Luo Zheng's winning streak had quietly increased by 26 matches.
According to the rules of the arena, the points gained from continuous winning streaks would be terrifying!
When Luo Zheng defeated Tang Yu in the first match, he only gained one point. But Luo Zheng won 120 points in the 26th match. Now, Luo Zheng's total points had reached a terrifying 2,200 points.
When a Proof God martial artist fought another Proof God martial artist to determine the victor, the most one could gain was three to six points. The reason why Luo Zheng was able to accumulate such a ridiculous amount of points was because he was in a state of continuous winning streak.
Although the woman behind Luo Zheng was a lower True Divinity, she had only accumulated 800 points. So when she saw Luo Zheng's winning streak and Luo Zheng's points, she felt as if everything was unreal.
The woman originally wanted to rest, but now she didn't want to leave. She wanted to see how this boy had accumulated over 2,000 points.
Generally speaking, a Proof God martial artist's points ranged from 0 to 600 points. A lower True Divinity's points ranged from 600 to 2,000 points. A middle True Divinity's points ranged from 2,000 to 8,000 points. The points of an upper True Divinity and a Great Perfection True Divinity's points were extremely ridiculous. Some Great Perfection True Divinities' points could reach hundreds of thousands, or even millions …
In the past, there were many geniuses from rich and powerful families who entered the Arena of Gods for the first time. They also used the method of winning streaks to rapidly accumulate points. Take Luo Zheng for example. After winning 26 matches in a row, the additional points he received were comparable to some middle True Divinities!
However, this kind of accumulation of points was very "virtual". This was because after winning streaks, one only needed to lose one match to lose a large amount of points. And in the Arena of Gods, no one could guarantee that they would never lose. Soon, such a person's points would slowly return to normal.
Luo Zheng didn't know that his points had attracted the attention of others. After activating his jade card, his entire person sank into the Divine Jade Wall!
"Ru Meng, what are you looking at?"
A woman beside her noticed her strange actions and asked curiously.
The woman called Ru Meng pointed blankly in front of Luo Zheng. The woman looked over and was immediately stunned. "My God, 26 consecutive wins, more than 2000 points!"
For a Proof of Divinity martial artist to have over 2000 points was indeed a very shocking matter. Moreover, Luo Zheng had just entered the Purple Soul Palace not long ago. Proof of Divinity martial artists like him had only accumulated a few dozen points!
Because of this woman's exclamation, it attracted even more people's attention. Therefore, even more people gathered behind Luo Zheng to "admire" Luo Zheng's almost abnormal battle record!
"Two thousand points is indeed a lot, but these points are just for show!"
Someone pointed this out. After all, the rules of the Arena of Gods were no secret on the floating island.
"Yes, because he hasn't tasted defeat yet, the points awarded for winning streaks are especially high. Once he loses, he will lose most of his points, and then he will be included in the rankings."
"I'm curious as to how long his winning streak will last. Logically speaking, the opponents he's facing now shouldn't be too weak!"
Even though Luo Zheng's points were so high, it didn't attract much attention in the entire Arena of Gods. The reason was simple: Luo Zheng still didn't have a ranking!
According to the rules of the Arena of Gods, continuous wins couldn't be included in the rankings. Luo Zheng hadn't lost yet, so the rankings of the Proof of Divinity martial artists didn't include him. Most people also didn't know that there was a Proof of Divinity martial artist in the Purple Soul Palace who had won 26 consecutive matches and accumulated over 2000 points …
At the current stage, Luo Zheng's opponents were already considered the top existences among the Proof of Divinity martial artists. There were almost no weaklings. Just based on Luo Zheng's current strength, it was almost impossible for the Proof of Divinity martial artists to contend with him. Therefore, Luo Zheng was still able to maintain a crushing momentum!
The 27th match …
"26 consecutive victories! Over 2000 points. Tsk tsk tsk. If I win against you, my points will double in one go, right? "A figure slowly descended from mid-air, dragging out his voice as he laughed lightly.
Luo Zheng's opponent looked to be around 26 or 27 years old. He had a pair of slender eyebrows and looked very delicate. However, between his eyebrows, there was a faint evil aura.
"Your surname is Mu? You're from the Mu Family? " In the previous matches, Luo Zheng rarely tried to find out about his opponent's information. However, this time, he took the initiative to ask.
This youth named Mu Chen had already won six consecutive matches and had accumulated over 1200 points. He was already an extremely outstanding existence among the Proof of Divinity martial artists. According to Luo Zheng's inference, Mu Chen should be a relatively important person in the Mu Family.
"I'm very curious. Since you're able to win 26 consecutive matches, why don't you know that I'm Mu Chen?" Mu Chen stared at Luo Zheng with a trace of dissatisfaction in his eyes. It seemed that Luo Zheng not knowing of his existence was a huge sin!
"Now I know," Luo Zheng replied with an indifferent expression. Because of Mu Haiji, he didn't have any good feelings towards the Mu Family. However, Luo Zheng wouldn't reveal his feelings here. His current goal was to earn more points.
"It's good that you know," Mu Chen laughed mischievously. Like a gust of wind, he floated towards Luo Zheng. With a light wave of his hands, two dim short swords fluttered out from between his hands. With a strange and tricky angle, they swept towards Luo Zheng's neck.
Since Luo Zheng won 20 consecutive matches, the opponents he encountered weren't simple.
Although these opponents hadn't proven their Divine Way or condensed their Divine Spark, they either possessed extremely powerful martial skills or could use their Divine Way Dao to the limit. In terms of strength alone, they had far surpassed the Realm Masters that Luo Zheng had come into contact with in the Great Yan Universe!
When Luo Zheng had just entered the Divine Realm, the Realm Masters that he had encountered were weak, almost causing Luo Zheng to be taken aback. However, these peak Proof of Divinity martial artists that he had encountered on the floating island had gradually changed Luo Zheng's impression. These Proof of Divinity martial artists were far more powerful than Luo Zheng had imagined. At the very least, they possessed strength and talent that weren't inferior to the "Dao Children".
Unfortunately, Luo Zheng's current strength couldn't be compared to the past. No matter how powerful these Proof of Divinity martial artists were, it was difficult for them to be his opponent …
Facing Mu Chen's two short swords that strangled towards him from the left and right, Luo Zheng actually didn't dodge. Instead, he rushed towards them!
"You're really confident, ah. It seems like you're only so-so …" Mu Chen cracked a smile.
At the same time, the two short swords in his hands suddenly began to dance rapidly. The originally dim short swords released a dazzling silver light at this moment. These silver lights constantly intertwined and spiraled, wanting to forcefully twist Luo Zheng's head in!
Silver Scale Twisting Slash …
This was a killing technique that Mu Haiji had created himself.
True Gods naturally didn't need this kind of martial skill. They could just use their Divine Way. The reason why Mu Haiji had created this set of martial skills was for Mu Chen!
Even Mu Chen's two-handed short swords were personally ordered by Mu Haiji. The short sword in his left hand was called "White Coat", and the short sword in his right hand was called "Silver Scale". These two short swords were grandmist treasures that were tailor-made for Mu Chen. From this, one could see how much Mu Haiji loved Mu Chen!
Mu Chen had also fought over a thousand battles in the Arena of Myriad Gods. He was the same as Luo Zheng. When he had just entered the Arena of Myriad Gods, he had experienced a continuous winning streak. He had won a total of "twenty-one battles" in a row and had only obtained his ranking after losing twenty-two battles.
In these over a thousand battles, none of the opponents who had been caught in the Silver Scale Twisting Slash had been lucky enough to escape.
Therefore, when Mu Chen saw that Luo Zheng didn't dodge, contempt immediately appeared in his eyes. If this Luo Tianxing was knowledgeable enough, he would know the power of this Silver Scale Twisting Slash. He wouldn't stand there like a dumb chicken and let himself be twisted.
However, just as the silver light that Mu Chen brandished was one foot away from Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng finally moved.
His figure was filled with tenacity at this moment. He instantly moved to the back. At the same time, he suddenly spread out his hands and grabbed at the constantly spinning white light!
"Ding, ding, ding, ding!"
The white light slashed at Luo Zheng's hands, producing crisp sounds. Although the short swords were extremely sharp, when they slashed at Luo Zheng's body, it was like slashing at an indestructible treasure. The Silver Scale Twisting Slash only left a few white marks on Luo Zheng's hands.
Luo Zheng blocked the two spinning short swords. Taking advantage of this opportunity, Luo Zheng also grabbed Mu Chen's hands …
Mu Chen didn't expect that Luo Zheng would be able to break his Silver Scale Twisting Slash with such a simple and overbearing method. Mu Chen's face was still in a daze.

Chapter 1826
Luo Zheng didn't give Mu Chen any time to react.
What he wanted was to end this fight cleanly and cleanly ….
The martial arts techniques that Mu Chen used were indeed profound and extraordinary. If Luo Zheng wanted to use clever techniques to break them apart, it would be impossible.
As the saying went, one person could defeat ten people. This was still applicable even in the divine territory.
Using his tyrannical body to forcefully break apart the opponent's martial arts techniques, then using absolute power to suppress the opponent. If necessary, Luo Zheng would supplement it with soul attacks. With his current strength, it would be bullying to fight against a Proof God expert.
In fact, in the Great Yan Universe, Luo Zheng could barely fight against Mu Han and the others.
Although Mu Han's cultivation was at the lower level of a True God, as Mu Haiji's disciple, his true strength was comparable to an intermediate True God!
In other words, with Luo Zheng's current strength, to a certain extent, he could fight against an ordinary intermediate True God …
At the current stage, Luo Zheng still didn't have the motivation to challenge those of a higher level. He realized that after consecutive victories, the points awarded were more than he imagined. Earning points at the Proof God level in the Arena of Gods seemed like a good choice.
The moment Luo Zheng grabbed Mu Chen's hands, a vast amount of power poured out, forming a powerful tearing force. It didn't allow for any resistance and cruelly tore Mu Chen into two pieces …
In the battle with Mu Chen, Luo Zheng won.
After winning this battle, Luo Zheng didn't think too much and directly left the Arena of Gods.
He didn't expect that as soon as he left the Arena of Gods, he would be frightened by the black mass of people behind him …
Behind him, there were hundreds of beautiful women of all shapes and sizes watching. It seemed like they were all watching him. At the same time, there were many people whispering in low voices.
"Twenty-seven consecutive victories …"
"His current opponent should be the most powerful Proof God experts of the various large Families."
"His points are rising so quickly. He actually gained more than two hundred points from this battle!"
In the eyes of these female disciples, Luo Zheng, who didn't have the Extreme Purple Yin Body, was originally an incomprehensible anomaly in the Purple Soul Palace. Not long ago, he had jumped out to encourage everyone when their master was speaking. Now, they realized that this guy's true strength was terrifying.
However, he did not know how long his winning streak would last.
Luo Zheng also realized that the battle with Mu Chen just now had directly increased his points by 240 points. If he continued to win like this, to what extent would his points increase?
Luo Zheng didn't pay too much attention to the hundreds of girls watching from behind. Instead, he continued to immerse himself in the Arena of Gods …
Among the wealthy families of the floating islands, there were people who were monitoring the movements of the Pantheon Jade Wall!
For example, if someone's ranking suddenly shot up during a certain period of time, all the rich and powerful families would pay close attention to it.
However, they were all observing according to the rankings on the Pantheon Jade Wall!
At this stage, among all the Proof of Divinity cultivators, the first was Jiang Family's Jiang Wenxuan, the second was Han Family's Han Chuyue, and the third was Lei Family's Lei Huohuo. Mu Chen, who had fought with Luo Zheng earlier, was previously ranked sixth.
