However, after being defeated by Luo Zheng, his points had decreased by another chunk, and his ranking had dropped from seventh to twelfth.
If Luo Zheng originally had a ranking, according to his current points, he would probably instantly surpass the first ranked Jiang Wenxuan.
However, Luo Zheng had fought 27 consecutive battles without a single defeat. According to the rules of the Arena of Gods, there was no way to list out the rankings. Hence, Luo Zheng was like an invisible person, constantly accumulating points without attracting too much attention.
Only those who had been defeated by Luo Zheng muttered. These people also had a continuous winning streak, and they felt that sooner or later, someone would defeat Luo Zheng. At that time, Luo Zheng's points would return to normal …
However, Luo Zheng's winning streak had never been stopped.
On that day, Luo Zheng won another three battles, accumulating a total of 31 victories, and his points also shot towards 3000 points.
In this way, Luo Zheng's target of 8000 points was not far away. Of course, the premise was that Luo Zheng kept winning. As long as he lost a battle, he could lose more than 90% of his points!
Just as everyone was working hard to accumulate points, the Purple Soul Palace once again announced the rewards for this time's Arena of Gods ranking!
Seeing everyone in the Purple Soul Palace doing their best to increase their points, Aunt Han Jiu was naturally very gratified. Everyone was working so hard, so she naturally had to take out some good things. Aunt Han Jiu had been the Han Family's secondary saint for so many years, so she naturally had many good things …
Luo Zheng also went to take a look. Aunt Han Jiu's rewards were not bad. Among them was a silver spider silk sword that was very eye-catching. Luo Zheng was very happy. According to Aunt Han Jiu's announcement, these treasures would be distributed according to how many points one gained. The person who earned the most points in three months would be the first to pick, and so on.
If Luo Zheng maintained his winning streak, his chances of being the first to pick a reward would be very high.
Following Aunt Han Jiu's announcement of the rewards, the Han Family also announced the rewards for this time's Arena of Gods!
It seemed like this time's Floating Island ranking was very important to the Han Family. Aunt Han Jiu's side took out treasures to encourage the Purple Soul Palace's female disciples, and the Han Family's side took out even more treasures as rewards.
Luo Zheng was curious and also went to the Saint Emperor City to take a look. While he was there, Luo Zheng's attention was attracted by one of the treasures.
No matter how rich Aunt Han Jiu was, she couldn't compare to the Han Family's main family!
This time, the main family could be considered to have put in a lot of capital. In order to raise the Floating Island's ranking, the Han Family was willing to sacrifice the Purple Soul Palace and take out the family's precious treasures.
Just the grade-grade grandmist treasures alone numbered more than thirty, and they were arranged in a row in the huge hall.
Almost all the True Gods who had entered the main hall had greed and shock in their eyes. However, these grandmist treasures weren't anything good in some people's eyes … For example, Luo Zheng didn't have much interest in them.
From the moment he entered the hall, his gaze was fixed on the highest point of the hall.
There were five treasures arranged in a row there. They were a jade sword, a pair of scissors, a mirror, and a palm-sized cauldron. At the center of the five treasures was a copper lion statue!
These five treasures all exuded a strange power. Everyone who stood below them felt even more shocked.
"No wonder the Saint Emperor requested for the Purple Soul Palace to be disbanded. In order to raise everyone's ranking, the Saint Emperor has really put in a lot of capital!"
"Is there a need to change some of the floating island's policies? In the past, the Han Family didn't pay much attention to the rankings …"
"The Guang Ling Mirror … I've always dreamed of having this treasure. I didn't expect the main family to be willing to take it out as a reward!"
Many middle True Gods and even upper True Gods stared at the five treasures with drool dripping down their faces.
However, what attracted Luo Zheng wasn't the five treasures themselves.
The copper lion statue at the center of the hall was crouching on a huge tray. The tray was about a zhang wide, and it was greenish-gray in color. From the looks of it, it was extremely old!
Although Luo Zheng didn't recognize the material of the tray, the world in his body accurately reflected a strong desire for the first time. It also allowed Luo Zheng to determine that the tray was made of that special mud!
Being unable to condense a godhead had always been Luo Zheng's sore point.
Although Luo Zheng had managed to grasp a chance in Six Ren Divine City by coincidence, he had almost lost his life in that strange cave. Wanting to find that special mud again was too difficult.
Luo Zheng felt extremely depressed in the depths of his heart. If he couldn't find that special mud, his cultivation would always be stuck at the World Lord Realm.
Who would have thought that he would be able to find it here! Although Luo Zheng was excited, he could only thank the God of Fate for his favor.
However, the status of these five treasures was originally above the thirty-plus grandmist treasures. Moreover, the copper lion statue was the head of the five treasures. The conditions to obtain the copper lion statue were probably quite harsh!
"Senior, may I know what conditions are required to obtain the main family's reward this year? For example, the copper lion statue at the top … "Luo Zheng raised his head and asked a middle True God beside him.

Chapter 1827
The disciples of Saint Emperor City also understood that while Han Jia was showing a certain level of sincerity, this' sincerity 'wasn't something they could grasp.
The disciples of Han Jia's floating island also had differences in rank and status. According to their birth, they could be divided into four classes.
The disciples of the floating island with a different surname were ranked fourth. Luo Zheng was probably one of them, but his situation was a little more special.
The third class were the disciples of the Han Family's side branches. Regardless of whether it was strength or talent, the difference between them and the disciples of the Han Family wasn't that great. However, their surname still had the word 'Han' in it. As the main branch of the Han Family, they would naturally be taken care of.
The ordinary disciples of the main branch of the Han Family belonged to the second class. These disciples of the main branch were born on the floating island and grew up there. Unfortunately, their talent was average, and their strength was too weak. The Han Family could only use a certain amount of resources to ensure that these disciples didn't fall behind. No matter what, they were still on the same side.
The first class were naturally the elites of the Han Family!
These people were the geniuses of the Han Family. Practically all the resources and effort were poured into them. As the elites of the next generation of the Han Family, they carried the hopes of the entire Han Family to a certain extent.
For example, there was Han Cangyan, who had been in the limelight these past few years, and Han Liusu, who had just become a lower true god.
Normally, these people rarely showed themselves, but in the Arena of Gods, they weren't the least bit careless. Among those at the same level, they were all ranked at the very top. Although this group of people didn't make up much of the Han Family, their points would often have a decisive impact on the floating island's ranking!
That middle-level True God explained very clearly that although these five treasures were the best of the best, the ordinary disciples on the floating island could only look at them and drool. In the future, those who could take out these treasures were destined to be the elite disciples of the main family. Only those monstrous geniuses would be able to accumulate enough points in a short period of time.
"Whether or not I have the qualifications, there needs to be a fair rule, right?" Luo Zheng asked with clear eyes.
Luo Zheng was very clear that there was no such thing as absolute fairness in this world. There was a huge gap between the strength of the Han Family disciples. These treasures could be fought for by anyone on the surface, but there were only a few people who had the strength to meet the requirements …
The middle-level True God glanced at Luo Zheng. He felt that Luo Zheng, a mere Proof God martial artist, was overestimating himself. Hence, he reported Han Jia's requirements. "The next 30 treasures require an increase of fifteen thousand points within three months. The five treasures above require fifty thousand points." Halfway through his sentence, he continued, "Based on the number of points a Proof God martial artist earns for winning a battle, if you wish to obtain one of the 30 treasures below, you would need to win around five thousand battles. If it's one of the five treasures above, you would need to win around fifteen thousand battles. Hehe …"
He originally thought that the number he gave would make Luo Zheng's face change.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng's expression remained as calm as water. There was no change in his expression. He only nodded slightly.
A Proof God martial artist would only obtain the most basic three points in a battle. However, Luo Zheng had maintained a winning streak. The additional points accumulated would become more and more terrifying. If Luo Zheng worked harder, it was still possible for him to win 15,000 points.
However, his goal was one of the five treasures, the "Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Luan Statue." More accurately, what he needed was the huge tray below the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Luan Statue. He wasn't too interested in the statue itself.
However, it would still be difficult for Luo Zheng to obtain 50,000 points in a short period of time …
At this moment, many people rushed in from the side of the hall.
These people were surrounded by guards in golden armor. Clearly, their status was not ordinary!
"Elder sister, elder sister, look! This time, father is finally willing to take out the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Luan Statue! " A girl who looked to be seventeen or eighteen years old was jumping up and down. She pointed at the treasures above the hall and said.
"This voice …" Luo Zheng's eyebrows rose slightly. His gaze focused slightly. He recognized the girl in front of him. She was Han Chuyue, whom he had met in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
This Han Chuyue was now a Proof God martial artist. Back in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, her strength was still relatively weak. However, she was now one of the most outstanding Proof God martial artists!
Beside Han Chuyue was Han Liusu.
The current Han Liusu looked much thinner. Her original cheerful and enthusiastic appearance had completely disappeared. All that was left was a melancholy expression. However, she had already proven her Divine Dao. Although she was only a Lower True God, her aura was abnormally strong. Luo Zheng felt that she was probably stronger than Mu Han, Mu Jiu, and Mu Tian.
Meeting the two Han Jia sisters here, Luo Zheng was very surprised. However, he had no intention of going forward to greet them.
In fact, Luo Zheng had asked the Extreme Evil Old Man to teach him how to change his appearance so that the two girls would not recognize him after he went to Han Jia's floating island.
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, the two Han Jia sisters had helped him solve his doubts about his origins. They were clear about his origins. If he were to rashly reveal his identity to them, it would bring about a lot of trouble.
It was not that Luo Zheng did not trust the two sisters. In fact, the two sisters were very loyal to each other. Luo Zheng's actions were like gauging the heart of a gentleman with one's own yardstick. However, right now, Luo Zheng had no choice but to be cautious.
"The Eldest Princess has graced us with her presence. Everyone else must leave the hall!" A guard loudly berated.
Luo Zheng also quietly sized up the guards. He did not see "He Zuowei" in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Presumably, the group of guards from Han Jia had always been in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement …
When everyone heard "The Eldest Princess has arrived," they could not help but be slightly surprised. The true gods in the hall naturally knew that these women were not ordinary people. However, they did not expect them to be the Eldest Princess of Han Jia.
"Let's go. There's nothing else to see here. Look, the elites of the main family have already reserved these treasures." The middle true god beside Luo Zheng shook his head. He even patted Luo Zheng's shoulder kindly, indicating that Luo Zheng should leave.
"Alright," Luo Zheng replied and was about to turn around and leave. At this moment, a clear and melodious female voice resounded, "Luo Tianxing! Wait a moment! "
When Luo Zheng heard this voice, he was slightly stunned. He turned his head and looked over. Originally, other than the two Han Jia sisters, there was also a little girl in green. It was the little girl he met on the day of the examination in the Heavenly Court.
At that time, the old man beside the little girl had only flashed a small jade sword. Everyone in the Heavenly Court had sunk into silence, keeping the identity of this little girl a secret.
Using the Extreme Evil Old Man's disguise technique, Luo Zheng's facial muscles and bones shifted. His appearance was completely different from before. The two Han Jia sisters did not recognize him, but the little girl in green had seen him once during the examination in the Heavenly Court. Naturally, she recognized him now.
The guards in charge of clearing the area also blocked Luo Zheng's path. When the little girl in green saw Luo Zheng, she was very happy. She then turned her head to Han Chuyue and laughed. "I already said that I did not make it up. A powerful monster has appeared in the Heavenly Court. He will come to our floating island sooner or later!"
After saying this, the little girl in green quickly ran over to Luo Zheng, her face filled with excitement.
"Luo Tianxing, since you passed the Heavenly Court's examination, why didn't Saint Emperor City send anyone to receive you?" the little girl in green suddenly asked.
This little girl in green held a high position in the Han Jia. Naturally, she had many conveniences. She knew that the Heavenly Court had already recommended Luo Tianxing to the floating island. Luo Tianxing would come to the floating island sooner or later. However, she did not expect that something would go wrong. Because of the conflict between Han Yaozhi and Han Daozhi, Luo Zheng was sent to the Purple Soul Palace in the end.
This was why the little girl in green had not heard any news of Luo Tianxing entering Saint Emperor City. This made her feel very strange.
The little girl in green had a good relationship with Han Chuyue and Han Liusu. However, these elites of the Han Jia were not only good friends, but also competitors. When the two Han Jia sisters entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and encountered Luo Zheng, it led to a series of fortuitous encounters. When the two sisters returned to the floating island, they told this story to the little girl in green.
The little girl in green had heard about how Luo Zheng dominated the Zhen Yuan from the beginning to the end, how he defeated a group of elites from rich families alone, and how he made Qin Tianze suffer a great loss. She was both envious and curious about how the two sisters met such a strange person in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.

Chapter 1828
After Han Chuyue and Han Liusu returned from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, they had been worried about Luo Zheng.
Han Liusu had even used her connections to inquire about the Great Yan Universe through the Jiang Family!
If it was in the past, Han Liusu would have been able to do so. After all, Han Liusu's position in the Han Family was quite high. The Saint Emperor's eldest daughter could have used the Jiang Family to investigate, but this time, she found nothing.
Because the Jiang Family had also lost control of the Great Yan Universe, the reason was naturally because of Yu Taibai.
Helpless, Han Liusu could only give up. She couldn't personally go to the Great Yan Universe, and her father would definitely not agree.
Previously, when she heard about Luo Tianxing from Han Biluo, she was slightly curious.
After the Luo Family was eradicated, there were fewer and fewer geniuses with the surname Luo. It was unavoidable that a genius with the surname Luo would suddenly appear. Moreover, if Luo Zheng really came to the Divine Realm, he definitely wouldn't use his real name.
But thinking about it, the Divine Realm was so big. Luo Zheng just happened to come to the Han Family's ancestral land, passed the Heavenly Palace's examination, and met Han Biluo in the Heavenly Palace. Han Biluo just happened to want to show off to her, and that person just happened to be Luo Zheng?
Was this possible? The probability of this happening was so small that it was negligible. After understanding this, Han Liusu didn't pay much attention to it.
As for Han Biluo, this was just a spur of the moment, completely out of her competitive nature. She had inquired about Luo Tianxing several times in the Saint Emperor City, but after failing to find anything, she simply gave up.
Until today, when she, Han Liusu, and Han Chuyue came together to observe the rewards given by her father, they just happened to meet Luo Tianxing.
Han Liusu was also quietly sizing up Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng's appearance was completely different, and his aura was much stronger than before, Han Liusu still faintly felt that he was very familiar. It was this familiarity that made Han Liusu very confused. This was clearly the first time she had met this person, so why did he give her such a familiar feeling?
"I didn't join the Saint Emperor City." Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "I joined the Purple Soul Palace."
"Purple … Purple Soul Palace." The corners of Han Bi Luo's mouth twitched as a strange smile appeared on her face, "How did you end up joining the Purple Soul Palace? I've heard that the Purple Soul Palace has men, but those men are all like women …"
Not far away, Han Chuyue also bounced over. She was about the same age as Han Biluo, so she also found it very amusing. "Biluo, is this the genius surnamed Luo from the Heaven Containing Mansion you were talking about? ... he doesn't have the Extreme Purple body? Purple Palace..........
. "Seeing the two girls laughing in front of him, Luo Zheng's face was full of helplessness." There's nothing to laugh about. Although I don't have the Extreme Purple Yin body, I can be recognized by the Purple Clouds Divine Dao. "Saying this, he lightly flicked his hand. A wisp of dark purple Qi revolved around Luo Zheng, from that Qi came a profound Dao.
Not far away, Han Liusu saw this scene and her eyes were full of surprise. She lightly asked, "The Purple Clouds Divine Dao uses Yin Qi to merge with the void. Only by using all one's strength can one master it. You can't understand these two points, but you can directly comprehend the Dao of the Purple Clouds Divine Dao. How did you do it?"
After a few years, Han Liusu had become more mature and melancholy. She also had the aura of someone who had been in a high position for a long time. When she opened her mouth to speak to Luo Zheng, she naturally had a commanding tone. This was also due to her position.
"Forgive me for not being able to reveal it," Luo Zheng directly refused.
Han Liusu didn't say anything, but the expression of the guard beside her had already darkened. This guard was an upper true god. Not only did he act as Han Liusu's personal guard, but he also had a very high position in Han Jia. Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, he coldly shouted, "Eldest Princess asked a question. How can you refuse?"
Although the other party was an upper true god and had an overbearing attitude, Luo Zheng still calmly replied, "I'm only joining Han Jia's floating island. I didn't sell my entire body to Han Jia, did I?"
This was the truth. The various large floating island clans didn't overly restrict their own disciples. Generally, they didn't have too many restrictions on disciples with other surnames.
However, in order to curry favor with these clans, countless disciples with other surnames were naturally extremely polite. There were even some who flattered them, hoping to make them happy in exchange for some opportunities and benefits.
However, this was what Luo Zheng didn't care about the most!
To Luo Zheng, Han Jia's floating island was just a passageway. Since it required eight thousand points to enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain, Luo Zheng would obediently accumulate eight thousand points. He didn't want to ask for help from others.
When the upper true god heard Luo Zheng's answer, he became even angrier. "You've got guts-"
"Say no more, Uncle Gong," Han Liusu calmly said.
When Han Liusu stopped him, this upper true god called "Uncle Gong" finally shut his mouth. He glared fiercely at Luo Zheng before retreating to the side.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, the confusion on Han Liusu's face gradually dissipated. In reality, the confusion in her heart was growing stronger.
Whether it was appearance or voice, this guy in front of her was completely different from Luo Zheng. However, this Luo Tianxing's nature was almost exactly the same as Luo Zheng's. She might not have seen any flaws, but a woman's unique intuition was constantly guiding her.
Previously, she was just a little curious about the familiarity Luo Tianxing's temperament gave her, but now she was trying to prove that "Luo Tianxing is Luo Zheng", trying to prove that "Luo Zheng is pretending not to know them".
Thinking of this, a trace of craftiness appeared in her eyes. She turned to Han Chuyue and asked, "Chuyue, didn't Bi Luo say before that there's a Proof of Divinity martial artist stronger than you?"
Han Chuyue's talent was even greater than Han Liusu's. Although she was only a Proof of Divinity martial artist, she was already second in the Arena of Gods. She was only second to the Jiang family's Jiang Wenxuan!
If the floating island could represent the highest standard in the Realm of Gods, then Han Chuyue's strength, in theory, was ranked second among all the Proof of Divinity martial artists in the Realm of Gods. This was already a considerable talent.
Han Bi Luo had indeed said something similar before, but Han Chuyue's temper was a little rash. She was not as meticulous and sensitive as Han Liusu. She had long forgotten about this matter. Han Chuyue did not see anything special about Luo Tianxing, and could not link Luo Tianxing to Luo Zheng.
However, when she suddenly heard her sister's question, she suddenly remembered. She clapped her hands together, "Oh right! Bi Luo, weren't you bragging about this guy's strength every day? You even said he was stronger than me? Now that we have the chance, let's exchange pointers once! Quick! Right here! "
Han Chuyue had the personality of doing whatever she wanted. She actually planned to exchange pointers with Luo Zheng here.
If Han Chuyue knew that Luo Zheng was Luo Tianxing, she definitely would not have made this choice. Although Han Chuyue's personality was a little rash, her brain was still top notch. After the two sisters of the Han family returned, they had secretly analyzed Luo Zheng's background. Chapter 1829

These upper True Divinities had always been the bodyguards of the core disciples of the rich and powerful families. They were already well aware of the rules. They knew that sometimes, they couldn't be too responsible. Otherwise, they would just be looking for trouble.

Chapter 1832
Although they were causing a ruckus in Han Jia's territory, they knew the severity of the situation.

At this time, he decided to get serious!
A silver light suddenly gushed out from the top of the silver flute. As the silver light spiraled, mysterious patterns spread out from it.
At the same time, there was something Luo Zheng was very familiar with — primal chaos qi!

At this moment, Luo Zheng's originally obscure eyes suddenly lit up. Not only did he not retreat, but he also charged toward Liu Chengtian.

However, Luo Zheng didn't have this psychological burden.
The toxicity of the primal chaos qi wasn't harmful to Luo Zheng. However, in order to not be too conspicuous, Luo Zheng gently twisted his body and found a gap to squeeze through.
"That kid plans to stop Liu Chengtian?"

"Does he really think he's a match for Liu Chengtian?"
Although Luo Zheng had just used several types of Shinto to toy with Liu Chengtian, no one thought that Luo Zheng would be stronger than a True God. Even among the Lower True Gods, Liu Chengtian was a peak figure. If Luo Zheng rushed up like this, it would be no different than seeking death.
When Liu Chengtian saw that Luo Zheng had taken the initiative to come to his door, he was overjoyed.
He had no choice but to activate the Sacred Worshipping Flute. The Sacred Worshipping Flute was one of the only two Chaos Treasures in the Liu family. His father had given it to him, but he was not allowed to take it out of the Sea of Time. Usually, he would have to return the Sacred Worshipping Flute before going to the Forbidden Area.
If the full power of the flute was unleashed, the entire hall would be destroyed. It would be bad if the elders of the Han Clan were alerted, and they would be severely punished.
Now that Luo Zheng had rushed up, Liu Chengtian's eyes flashed with an ominous glint. He reached out and gently pinched the Sacred Worshipping Flute. A whistling sound came from the Sacred Worshipping Flute, and the primal chaos qi was slowly retracted.
At the same time, Liu Chengtian waved his hands, and the Force in his inner world rushed toward his Godhead. Since this Proof of Divinity cultivator didn't know the immensity of heaven and earth and dared to toy with him, he would let him know what it meant to pay a miserable price.
Although Liu Chengtian had used Force, he still hadn't used his Shinto.
Pure use of Force was the use of the power of faith. However, the Godhead could use the power of faith to the extreme!
He wanted to use the most straightforward method to tear Luo Zheng to shreds!
Just as Luo Zheng rushed in front of him, the Force that was emitted from Liu Chengtian's body had already surged through every inch of his muscles. His arms swelled up high, as if they contained endless power. A small portion of this power came from his own body, but the majority of it came from the "Force".
His arms crisscrossed, and his hands turned into two tiger claws that shot toward Luo Zheng, grabbing Luo Zheng's arms as fast as lightning!
"Little brat, if you don't have the strength, then don't try to show off." Liu Chengtian stared at Luo Zheng and sneered. His actual age was actually younger than Luo Zheng, but he believed that his cultivation was higher than Luo Zheng's, so he naturally treated Luo Zheng as a junior. "But you won't have the chance to regret it. My warning came too late, ai …"
He sighed, wanting to tear Luo Zheng to shreds!
"Luo Tianxing …" In the distance, Han Biluo was startled. She wanted to stop him, but it was too late.
At this moment, Han Liusu's heart was pounding.
From the beginning, she had planned to have Han Chuyue test Luo Tianxing, but after seeing Luo Zheng use all kinds of Shinto, she began to doubt her own intuition.
But at this moment, the premonition in her heart became incomparably strong and sensitive.
Her judgment followed a certain logic. This Luo Tianxing was able to cultivate six or seven Shinto at the same time, he was definitely not a mediocre person.
Such an intelligent person would not seek his own death by charging at Liu Chengtian. He must have a certain level of confidence!
If it was Luo Zheng … then there was indeed a certain level of confidence.
She had seen Luo Zheng's terrifying strength. Back then, Luo Zheng's harvest from the Mountain Shattering Clan was quite shocking.
Whether Luo Tianxing was Luo Zheng or not, the next moment, the answer would be revealed.
The other rich playboys all had indifferent expressions. Only a portion of the Han Clan's people could not be touched. If the others were killed, then so be it. The Han Clan would at most reprimand them, and would not even inform their families!
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng suddenly revealed a relaxed expression, smiling at Liu Chengtian. "Little brat? You don't have the qualifications to call me that … "Saying so, his body suddenly twisted …
Under the rotation of the nine stars, Luo Zheng used an extremely large amount of strength while twisting.
Luo Zheng's movements were not fast, but because of the strength, they were very heavy. Liu Chengtian simply did not have time to react. He tightly grabbed Luo Zheng's arm and felt a huge force attacking him. That force was far greater than anyone he had ever fought in his life!
Very quickly, his entire body was pulled and thrown into the air, heavily smashing into the roof of the large hall …
"Crash …"
A milky white colored glass lamp was hanging on the roof of the large hall. Liu Chengtian's body shot up, instantly smashing that glass lamp into pieces. The glass fragments scattered all over the ground. At the same time, Liu Chengtian also heavily smashed down.
Before he could hit the ground, Luo Zheng had already lifted his leg and kicked out.
Since he had stood up for Han Chuyue and Han Biluo today, he might as well do it to the end. In any case, he had already offended this person. Moreover, this person had just wanted to kill him.
If he was not afraid of the rich and powerful family behind Liu Chengtian, he would not have kicked him. He would have already used the Great Chiliocosm Heavy Sword to cut him in two!
Following a rumbling sound, Liu Chengtian's body was like a broken gunnysack, slamming into the other side of the large hall and softly lying on the ground …

Chapter 1833
On the floating island, because of the existence of the Arena of Gods, as long as a strong person appeared, they would quickly be noticed by others.
For example, in a short two years, Han Chuyue had risen to second place in the Proof of Gods rankings. Those wealthy families knew that this girl was destined to be Han Jia's future star!
Strong or weak, they could find opponents at the same level. The Arena of Gods wasn't just a competition of strength, it was also a way to prove one's talent and future.
During this period of time, Luo Zheng's performance in the Arena of Gods was excellent, winning all the way!
However, he did not climb up the leaderboard. He was like a wandering ghost in the Arena of the Gods, constantly plundering points. Other than the dozens of Proof of Divinity martial artists who lost to Luo Zheng, the others did not know of his existence.
Han Chuyue's strength was second only to the Jiang family's little genius, Jiang Wenxuan. Facing Liu Chengtian, she could only be toyed with.
There was a huge gap between the Sector Lord realm and the True God realm. It couldn't be bridged by relying on talent. After all, a True God's "potential" released energy was a whole level higher than normal true essence!
Han Jia, this unknown young man, had used the ingenious combination of five to six types of divine Daos to toy with Liu Chengtian.
It wasn't difficult to do this. After careful planning, several Shinto would often have miraculous effects. It was just that this kind of trick was like a magic trick. In many people's eyes, it wasn't good enough.
But no one would have thought that this brat would suddenly explode with unimaginable speed and power, instantly defeating Liu Chengtian. This was a bit unbelievable.
It was normal for a lower True God genius to challenge a middle True God, but it was rare for a Proof of Gods to defeat a lower True God!
Han Liusu's mesmerizing eyes suddenly showed a faint smile.
Although this brat's disguise technique was profound, he could change his face, but he couldn't change his personality. She never would have dreamed that Luo Zheng would quietly appear on Han Jia's floating island …
"Hehe, I told you, isn't he stronger than the brat you mentioned?" Han Biluo said with a smile.
Han Chuyue pouted. "How could that be? He's just so-so … "
"So-so, hmph. Since Luo Tianxing is already on the floating island, I'll ask my father tomorrow to transfer him from the Purple Soul Palace …" Han Biluo said.
"This kind of genius naturally needs to be used by our Han Jia. But he's probably in trouble now." Han Chuyue's face showed worry.
Since Luo Zheng had defeated Liu Chengtian for them, these people wouldn't let this matter rest.
Liu Chengtian was still lying on the ground, and no one went up to help him up. Everyone's attention was on Luo Zheng. Soon, a long-haired youth walked out from the crowd. He had a small gem tattoo on his forehead, and a faint red glow surrounded the gem tattoo. He glanced at Luo Zheng coolly. "Such a person has never appeared on the rankings. Han Jia can keep its cool. What's your name?"
The long-haired youth was also a lower True Divinity, but his aura was even stronger than Liu Chengtian's.
. Everyone thought that Luo Zheng was a disciple of the Han Jia, and a genius that they had secretly raised.
"My surname isn't Han. I'm called Luo Tianxing," Luo Zheng said calmly. He pretended to be calm, but inside, he felt helpless. It would be hard to get away from this group of people. He could only hope that the elders of the Han Jia would quickly appear. Otherwise, it would really be troublesome.
Winning against Liu Cheng Tian just now was partly due to luck. Among the Lower True Gods, Liu Cheng Tian's strength was not bad, but he was definitely not an existence at the peak. If the person in front of him were to challenge him, Luo Zheng did not have the confidence to win. Moreover, there were not only Lower True Gods in this group, but also Middle True Gods.
"Surname Luo?" The long-haired youth was surprised.
The others had the same expression …
"So it's just a lowly outsider, haha." Someone in the crowd laughed.
"But the Han Jia seems to have picked up a treasure. A little monster that can rival a true god. How many years has it been since someone like that appeared?" someone else said.
"He was only able to defeat Liu Chengtian by luck. Liu Chengtian didn't activate his chaos treasure and fought with strength. He just happened to fall into that kid's trap …"
The most glorious thing about this group of rich kids was their family name.
Those that could appear in the sky above the Sea of Time were all legendary families. They were also the rulers of the entire divine territory.
As for the outsider disciples …
It didn't matter if you came from a second-tier or third-tier family. You didn't have the qualifications to be on the same level as them. No matter how strong you were, you wouldn't be able to stand on the stage because you didn't have a huge family behind you. You also didn't have a Saint to back you up!
So, when they talked about Luo Zheng, their words were even more impolite.
The long-haired youth grinned, his eyes filled with ferocity. "It's not easy for an outsider to cultivate to this level, but you dared to injure Liu Chengtian … your path has come to an end!"
"Aiya, Xie Jiao is angry!"
"Both the Xie and Liu Clans are at the bottom of the Floating Islands. They've always been close, and he has a good personal relationship with Liu Chengtian."
"What if Xie Jiao kills that kid in a fit of anger?"
"He's just an outsider. No matter how talented he is, Han Jia won't be able to pursue the matter …"
If Luo Zheng really was a member of Han Jia, they naturally wouldn't dare do anything to him. At most, they would teach him a lesson, but they wouldn't dare kill him.
Outsiders, however, were different.
Although outsiders were able to enter the Floating Islands through hard work, their status didn't change much. In many aspects, they would encounter double standards. So, there were people who criticized the rich families of the Floating Islands for "deciding one's fate by their family name."
As soon as Xie Jiao finished speaking, he pounced on Luo Zheng. He was ranked 40th among the lower true gods and was much stronger than Liu Chengtian!
Luo Zheng was already on high alert. He only needed to wait for Xie Jiao's figure to move. Then, he exploded with a powerful burst of force from the tip of his foot, and he quickly retreated.
Luo Zheng's speed was ridiculously fast. At the very least, no one among the Proof of Divinity cultivators could beat him.
A cold smile appeared on Xie Jiao's face. "Running?"
The red glow of the gemstone mark on his forehead flickered, and his entire body was enveloped in a red shadow. As he chased after Luo Zheng, he dragged countless blood-red figures behind him. It was as if there was a large group of people chasing after Luo Zheng. The momentum was enormous.
Xie Jiao was actually a bit faster than Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng had just retreated seven or eight meters when Xie Jiao appeared in front of Luo Zheng in the blink of an eye. Two slender fingers were placed in front of his forehead, touching the blood-red gemstone mark. At the same time, a ray of blood-red light flashed from the gemstone mark on his forehead and gathered at his fingertips.
"Whoosh!"
For some reason, the faint sound of metal could be heard from his fingertips. Those blood-red fingers seemed to have transformed into two sharp swords.
Xie Jiao gently flipped his right hand, and the two fingers pointed at Luo Zheng. His eyes were indifferent. Killing an outsider didn't give him any sense of accomplishment. On the contrary, Liu Chengtian's loss to this guy was a great humiliation. Therefore, he wanted to help Liu Chengtian.
Under Xie Jiao's finger, Luo Zheng's heart exploded with a strong sense of crisis!
"Eight Turns Flying Smoke!"
Faint wisps of white smoke rose from his body, and his entire body suddenly became as light as paper.
Seeing the two sonorous "swords" shot out from Xie Jiao's fingertips, Luo Zheng floated in a circle, as if he was completely unaffected, and slowly landed on the ground.
"It really is him …"
Han Liusu's face revealed a trace of relief. Her guess was finally confirmed.
She had seen Luo Zheng use Eight Turns Flying Smoke. Xie Jiao had forced Luo Zheng to use this life-saving technique, and this made Han Liusu even more certain of her guess!

Chapter 1834
After Luo Zheng used Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke, he instantly became weightless and floated like smoke!
Xie Jiao did not hold back at all.
The red light on his two fingers criss-crossed. Little red rings spun up and down around his fingertips. Every time those little red rings collided, there would be the sound of metal colliding. Then with a light tap of his finger, a unique red sword energy would shoot out from his fingertips!
"Woosh woosh woosh woosh woosh woosh!"
Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was very nimble in the Great Expansion World. Because there was not much pressure in the Great Expansion World, all matter was very light compared to the divine realm. But in the divine realm, this movement technique was greatly reduced.
Even more, Eight Zigzag Flying Smoke was just a movement technique. It was very difficult to face Xie Jiao's [Tian Huan Shen Dao]. Sometimes, Luo Zheng had to roll on the ground to barely avoid the red sword energies. He appeared very disheveled!
"Run this way … …"
"Retreat, retreat quickly!"
Han Chu Yue and Han Bi Luo naturally could not participate in this kind of battle. The two of them had nervous expressions as they cheered Luo Zheng on. Other than worry on their faces, there was a hint of excitement. They did not know how great the risk Luo Zheng was shouldering. They only thought that Luo Zheng would be able to make a successful counterattack.
Luo Zheng naturally wanted to do this.
Being suppressed by this guy, Luo Zheng had almost no power to fight back. It was good enough that he was able to dodge the sword energies. This could not continue.
"Woosh woosh … …"
A red sword light drew a long rainbow in the air and brushed past the side of Luo Zheng's face. It only left a thin scratch that was almost imperceptible to the naked eye, but a string of blood suddenly spurted out from that extremely thin scratch.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all, but his expression became colder.
The opponent's speed was much faster than his. He used his finger to attack him with a sharp sword energy. From start to finish, he maintained a distance of about three steps from him … …
He kept calculating in his mind.
Facing such an expert, Luo Zheng didn't have many tricks up his sleeves. Apart from all kinds of Divine Daos, the strongest killing move he could use was still his strength.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng suddenly turned around and ran to the other side.
A cold smile still hung on Xie Jiao's face as he followed Luo Zheng at an elegant pace. The Sword Qi on his fingertips was constantly shooting towards Luo Zheng.
However, just as Luo Zheng was rushing forward, he suddenly stopped. Even the solid ground was left with two small pits by Luo Zheng's feet. At the same time, the entire hall shook!
Luo Zheng could rely on his powerful strength and strong body to stop like this, but Xie Jiao couldn't do it. To suddenly stop at such a high speed, he needed to sprint for a certain distance. Experienced warriors could usually predict it in advance, but Luo Zheng didn't give him any time to do so.
If he used all his strength to stop like Luo Zheng, his body might not be able to bear it, and his legs might be fractured.
Therefore, although Luo Zheng's move looked ordinary, in fact, it required a lot of strength and physical strength. Even a True God would find it difficult to do … …
At this stage, Luo Zheng didn't have much of an advantage against a True God. He could only use his body to the limit.
Xie Jiao didn't expect Luo Zheng to be like a tree trunk. He suddenly plunged into the ground and stopped abruptly. Then, he rushed straight towards Luo Zheng. However, Xie Jiao didn't panic too much. After all, he wasn't Liu Chengtian. He had too many tricks up his sleeves against a True God warrior.
The moment he rushed towards Luo Zheng, he pointed with his two fingers and shot out three sword rays. They made an arc through his body. He wanted to go around Luo Zheng!
Luo Zheng had expected Xie Jiao to use this move. His eyes flashed, and he rolled up. His originally heavy body was as agile as a little monkey. He made a pose in the air, and the three sword rays passed through his armpit, crotch, and waist.
After avoiding the three sword rays, Luo Zheng rushed towards Xie Jiao to grab him.
However, the three sword rays Xie Jiao shot out forced Luo Zheng to dodge to the left, while Xie Jiao slid past him to the right. The two of them were three feet apart. Luo Zheng didn't have a chance to grab him.
"You're still too naive." Xie Jiao smiled faintly, as if everything was within his calculations.
However, at the same time, Xie Jiao also noticed that Luo Zheng was also grinning, revealing a strange smile.
What's this guy smiling about …
Just as this thought arose in Xie Jiao's mind, an invisible hand pressed down on Xie Jiao's body and slapped him to the ground.
"Boom!"
The entire hall began to tremble again.
Everyone's eyes were once again filled with shock!
"What happened?"
"How could Xie Jiao be pressed to the ground …"
"There seems to be an invisible force. Can this guy control his strength at will? Has he cultivated the Supreme Tao of Strength to the extreme? "
Everyone couldn't understand what had happened just now.
These people had good eyesight. Generally, there were only two ways to operate one's strength. One was to cultivate the Supreme Tao of Strength to the third stage. When one cultivated the Supreme Tao of Strength to the third stage, one could force the strength out of one's body and form a new body — the Body of Strength. This body could also become one of one's own protectors, which was very powerful.
The other method was the "Transcendence Divine Ability." With the "Transcendence Divine Ability," a Sage could mobilize the strength of the universe from anywhere.
Luo Zheng wasn't a Sage, so he naturally couldn't use the "Transcendence Divine Ability." However, even if he cultivated the Supreme Tao of Strength, this guy hadn't even condensed a Godhead. How could he cultivate it to the third stage?
Everyone's mind was spinning rapidly, trying to figure out how Luo Zheng had done it.
Even the guards brought by these people — the upper True Gods — looked at Luo Zheng with strange and astonished expressions!
However, before anyone could react, killing intent flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes. He grabbed the air, and the invisible hand lifted Xie Jiao up from the ground.
Xie Jiao had been slapped by the powerful hand just now, and he still hadn't reacted. Now that he was lifted up, his whole body hung limply in the air.
Luo Zheng suddenly attacked, his fingers constantly hitting Xie Jiao's limbs, chest, and spine!
"Ka Ka Ka Ka …"
His attack was so accurate that it instantly shattered the most important bones and joints in Xie Jiao's body. Xie Jiao had just reacted and didn't have time to scream before he lost all feeling in his body!
Then Luo Zheng suddenly dragged Xie Jiao to the ground and stepped on him. He looked around coldly and said, "I didn't mean to offend you today. It's just that the little girl from Han Jia is an old acquaintance of mine, so I had no choice but to attack. If anyone wants to teach me a lesson, I can accompany you to the end in the Divine Jade Wall. If anyone wants to fight here, I'm afraid the life of the person under my feet will be taken with me!"

Chapter 1835
Luo Zheng had already figured it out. There was no end to his entanglement with this group of people here.
Even if he defeated Xie Jiao, there would still be others who would appear …
With Luo Zheng's current abilities, it was already very strenuous for him to fight against a lower True God. If his opponent was an intermediate True God, he would most likely lose without a doubt.
His only chance was to hold Xie Jiao hostage and stabilize the situation first.
Although these descendants of wealthy families were very vicious, they cherished their lives very much. In their eyes, the lives of the people outside the floating island were cheap. Using their lives to exchange for Luo Zheng's was definitely not worth it.
As expected …
Seeing Luo Zheng's ferocious appearance, those people immediately quieted down.
A purple-haired youth cast a cold sidelong glance at Luo Zheng and warned, "Brat, do you know who you're stepping on?"
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Of course I do. He's just a member of the Xie family."
"You only know that his surname is Xie, but you don't know that he's the grandson of the Saint, Xie Fantian! Do you know the consequences of killing him? "The purple-haired youth sneered. He looked as though he knew that Luo Zheng wouldn't dare do anything to Xie Jiao.
After the purple-haired youth's words, the atmosphere immediately relaxed. They also felt that Luo Zheng wouldn't dare kill Xie Jiao.
Luo Zheng blinked. His face flashed with a hint of madness. "If I don't kill him today, will you let me go? Xie Jiao didn't hold back when he attacked just now. He tried to kill me time and time again. Why can't I kill him? If his life is precious, then exchanging my life for his is considered a bargain, right? "
After saying this, Luo Zheng stomped his foot again. Xie Jiao's chest instantly caved in. A huge pressure was exerted on Xie Jiao's body. Xie Jiao involuntarily groaned, as though he was struggling on his deathbed …
Many of the True Gods in the Divine Region had existed for a long time, so it was difficult for them to feel anything in the world. For example, the Evil Old Man probably wouldn't even bat an eyelid at such a scene.
However, these disciples of wealthy families were all rather young. They might have experienced countless battles in the Arena of Gods, but the Arena of Gods was ultimately not a real battle. Furthermore, when these disciples of wealthy families fought on the floating islands, they would often leave each other some leeway. Everyone's background was not bad. If they really killed each other, no one would have an easy time.
Now that they saw Luo Zheng's ruthlessness, it was beyond their expectations.
Those rich kids looked at each other and didn't know how to respond to Luo Zheng's words. The purple-haired boy who spoke before also fell into silence. He could see that if someone continued to attack, that kid would definitely kill Xie Jiao!
If they really killed Xie Jiao, that kid wouldn't be able to live, but they would most likely be implicated as well. Although they wouldn't receive a huge punishment, they would definitely be reprimanded. Moreover, they would be grounded by their respective families.
Han Biluo and Han Chuyue were full of smiles. They were quite pleased with themselves. The two girls only thought that it was fun. Luo Tianxing had suppressed this group of people by himself. If this matter spread out, it would bring honor to their Han Family!
Han Liusu's eyes were as clear and deep as the autumn lake. She stared at Luo Zheng, her heart filled with emotions. This guy had never retreated when he had the strength. In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he had relied on himself to crush everyone. Now that he was on her floating island, he still didn't back down in the face of such a disadvantage …
Leng Linyue stood by Han Liusu's side. He naturally noticed the expression of this' future wife 'of his. The way Han Liusu looked at Luo Zheng was an eyesore to him.
In fact, although Leng Linyue and Han Liusu had a marriage agreement, the two didn't want to be close. They had only met twice!
Leng Linyue had visited several times, but Han Liusu had refused to meet him. After being rejected several times, Leng Linyue naturally had a temper.
What kind of person was Leng Linyue?
Whether it was status, strength, or talent, he wasn't inferior to Han Liusu. How easy would it be for him to find a God Companion? Countless outstanding women wanted to marry into his Leng Family. Was Han Liusu the only one missing?
However, as a member of the Leng Family, Leng Linyue's considerations were naturally different from Han Liusu's. He knew why his father wanted to form a marriage alliance with the Han Family. He also knew that the Sea of Time was about to welcome a large disturbance. Considering the big picture, he was determined to get Han Liusu. Moreover, he did like Han Liusu.
Now that he saw Han Liusu's gaze, a flame of jealousy climbed up his heart like a scorpion. He raised his head and smiled, then walked towards Luo Zheng. "I've seen many people who are fierce in appearance but weak in heart! A life for a life? Do you dare? "
"Brother Yue?"
"That kid is a bit crazy. You should …"
"Don't force him. Let's just forget about today's matter."
These people saw that Luo Zheng was serious and didn't want to provoke Luo Zheng into doing something stupid. Now that Leng Linyue suddenly made such a move, they were naturally nervous.
Who knew that Leng Linyue wasn't moved at all. He still slowly walked towards Luo Zheng. "Hmph, this kind of person climbed up from the grass roots with great difficulty. He wouldn't have this kind of courage!" As he spoke, his eyes watched Luo Zheng's expression change.
In reality, Leng Linyue didn't care about Xie Jiao's life or death. He just wanted Luo Zheng to die.
Luo Zheng dared to kill Xie Jiao, so he was definitely dead. Even if Leng Linyue didn't deal with him, the Xie family would certainly not let him go. If Luo Zheng really looked fierce on the outside and didn't dare to kill at all, then things would be easier to deal with … This was Leng Linyue's plan.
Just as Leng Linyue stepped forward, Luo Zheng also saw through his thoughts. He smiled coldly, "Some people are so self-righteous that they can figure out other people's thoughts …" Halfway through his words, Luo Zheng suddenly exerted force on his feet!
At the same time, Leng Linyue suddenly accelerated towards Luo Zheng and shouted, "How dare you!"
The others also looked at Luo Zheng with a face full of disbelief. In their eyes, Xie Jiao's body seemed to be pressed down by an invisible sledgehammer. In an instant, his skin was squeezed in all directions, and at the same time, there were even cracks, from which red blood splashed out!
"Pu!"
Xie Jiao was crushed from head to toe by a huge force. His entire body completely collapsed, sticking to the ground like a piece of paper. Around his collapsed body, a huge blood flower splashed along the ground, turning into a huge blood flower. Looking at it, it was a ghastly sight!
"Ah!"
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo covered their mouths at the same time, staring blankly at the blood still flowing on the ground. Han Liusu also suddenly bit her lips, her face pale. Leng Linyue forced Luo Zheng to kill Xie Jiao, this was equivalent to putting Luo Zheng to death!
Leng Linyue's eyes twitched as he rushed forward. In his heart, he sighed. What a ruthless guy. If this person wasn't killed, he would become a hero in the future!
Luo Zheng's eyes bloomed with killing intent. At the same time, the surface of his soul began to flicker with a faint golden light.
However, this golden light didn't bloom …
If he wanted to use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, he would have to fight to the death!
Luo Zheng had been very restrained from the start. He didn't want to infuriate this group of aristocratic descendants. He had too many things on his shoulders. From the moment he entered the divine domain, he had been very careful. His previous temperament had also been much more restrained. After entering the floating island, he had become even more reserved.
But sometimes, the situation wasn't under his control!
"Whoosh …"
After Luo Zheng crushed Xie Jiao, he didn't stop at all and immediately retreated. At the same time, he drew out the Boundless Heaven Sword and slashed forward without even looking at what was in front of him!
Leng Linyue was ranked fifth in the Arena of Gods. It could be said that he was ranked fifth among all the mid-level True Gods in the divine realm …
The Extreme Evil Old Man was a peak mid-level True God. If he were to be compared to Leng Linyue, he was far inferior!
If Leng Linyue traveled outside the Sea of Time, he could easily kill some of the upper-level True Gods from second-tier clans!
Luo Zheng couldn't resist such an expert.
He didn't use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao when he swung his sword, but the boundless energy source was poured into it. The power was considerable!
"Whoosh!"
With the sound of a gust of wind, Luo Zheng's sword naturally missed its target.

Chapter 1836
After Luo Zheng missed his attack, he let go of the sword without any hesitation!
The Great Thousand Sword left Luo Zheng's hand and flew through the air. It pierced through the wall on one side of the hall and landed heavily on the ground outside.
The two lower true gods were shocked when they saw the sword stuck in the ground.
The people outside knew that there were important people in the hall, but they didn't know that a fight had broken out inside. Therefore, they were confused when they saw the Great Thousand Sword.
When Luo Zheng let go of the sword, his body suddenly shrank and he rolled to the side …
When fighting with such a powerful expert, Luo Zheng couldn't catch his opponent's figure. Both his eyesight and divine sense were useless at this moment.
Luo Zheng made a series of judgments based on his instincts!
Just as Luo Zheng rolled to the side, a faint figure suddenly appeared where Luo Zheng was standing. Together with the figure were three crimson claw prints!
"Swish swish swish!"
The three claw prints didn't catch Luo Zheng, but they strangely floated in the air and didn't dissipate for a long time.
Leng Linyue stood where he was with one hand behind his back, while the other hand glowed with a cold light. A hint of surprise appeared in his eyes. His attack just now was not something that Luo Zheng could have avoided, but at that moment, it was as if Luo Zheng had been helped by God. He rolled to the side and dodged Leng Linyue's attack.
"Interesting. What a strong fighting instinct." Leng Lin Yue smiled. "But can you avoid it this time?"
After saying that, Leng Lin Yue took another step forward. When he took the step, his figure became blurry. As a middle true god, Leng Lin Yue couldn't fly, but his speed was fast to a certain level!
Luo Zheng was lucky to avoid Leng Lin Yue's claw. Seeing Leng Lin Yue disappear again, Luo Zheng could only roll again …
But this time, Leng Lin Yue was prepared. After jumping in the air, he found the direction Luo Zheng was going to dodge and floated there. He still had one hand behind his back as if it was a great insult to himself to fight a Proof of Divinity expert with two hands.
However, just as he was about to grab Luo Zheng, his expression suddenly changed. His whole body abruptly rose up and somersaulted backwards like a spring onion on dry land. Only then did he stand firmly on the ground. There was a coarse wound on his forehead …
No matter what the situation was, Luo Zheng wouldn't sit still and wait for death.
Sages disdained to use the Transcendence Divine Ability to operate their power source, but now it was the only method Luo Zheng could use to fight against Leng Lin Yue..
He just figured out Leng Lin Yue's strategy. When he somersaulted, he drew out eighty percent of his power source and turned it into a long spear. Leng Lin Yue was too arrogant. No matter how excellent Luo Zheng was, he was only a Proof of Divinity expert. He wouldn't put a mere Proof of Divinity expert in his eyes.
Therefore, his two attacks were exactly the same. He leaped up at an extremely fast speed and swooped down from the sky like an eagle swooping down on a chick. Without any surprise, Leng Lin Yue's forehead smashed into the spear formed from the Origin of Power!
At the moment of impact, Luo Zheng suddenly pushed the long spear upwards. Unfortunately, Leng Lin Yue was still Leng Lin Yue. His reaction speed was much faster than Luo Zheng's. The long spear only scratched his forehead. He suddenly twisted his body and pushed back.
However, he was injured. This was an established fact …
"Leng Lin Yue … is injured."
"A peak middle true god was injured by a Proof of Divinity expert?"
"This … how is this possible …"
These people still hadn't recovered from Xie Jiao's death. Now that they saw that even Leng Lin Yue was injured, everyone was dumbfounded.
Luo Zheng was able to kill Xie Jiao with a bit of luck. His strength was indeed quite strong.
However, Xie Jiao was only a lower true god. It had been many years since a Proof of Divinity expert defeated a lower true god, but it didn't mean it had never happened in history …
Back then, the Leng family's Saint Emperor used his "Dao Heart" to defeat twelve lower true gods. At that time, the Leng family's Saint Emperor was also a Proof of Divinity expert.
Therefore, Luo Zheng was able to kill Xie Jiao. This showed that he had monstrous talent and strength.
However, when Leng Lin Yue attacked, everyone understood that no matter how bright this kid's future was, his life would end here.
Leng Lin Yue wasn't just a middle true god. He was also a peak figure.
However, the two had only exchanged two moves, and Leng Lin Yue was injured. Moreover, it was his head injury. Looking at the situation, if Leng Lin Yue had been a bit slower, his head would have been pierced. The Leng family's eldest son would probably have died here today.
Leng Lin Yue stared at Luo Zheng. His originally calm expression had completely disappeared. Two storms were slowly brewing in his eyes. He also put down the hand behind his back.
Being injured by a Proof of Divinity expert was undoubtedly a great humiliation for Leng Lin Yue.
He also didn't expect that the guy who was at his mercy would curl up into a ball like a hedgehog. If he wasn't careful, he would be injured!
"Looks like I should let you die a bit more painfully." Leng Lin Yue's mouth curved slightly. His figure disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
"Rip!"
This time, Luo Zheng didn't have time to react at all. He felt three sharp pain coming from his side. Three distinct claw marks extended from his waist.
Leng Lin Yue planned to first claw Luo Zheng in two. However, when he really grabbed Luo Zheng's body, he discovered that the layer of seemingly ordinary clothes on the surface of his body was actually indestructible …
As for the others, they were baffled by this.
"Brother Yue held back again?"
"He actually didn't even manage to tear that kid's clothes …"
"But he's obviously angry. Is there something wrong with that kid's clothes?"
Although Luo Zheng was protected by the Concentric Clothes, Leng Lin Yue's casual claw marks could still penetrate a portion of the power into Luo Zheng's body. As the power leaked out, it continued to wreak havoc inside Luo Zheng's body. This was after the Concentric Clothes had reduced most of the power. If not, even if his body was strong, he probably wouldn't be able to escape the fate of being torn apart.
"Hmph." Although Leng Lin Yue was surprised by Luo Zheng's protective clothes, he didn't waste any time talking nonsense. He once again pounced on Luo Zheng …
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
As he rushed over, he waved one hand casually in the air. Claw marks strangely stuck in the air for a long time and didn't dissipate. The moment he got close to Luo Zheng, the claw marks that Leng Lin Yue had left in the air before quickly extended. These claw marks extended towards Luo Zheng's body like blades.
"Slash, slash, slash …"
At this moment, there were more than ten claw marks constantly cutting Luo Zheng's body.
Every claw mark that cut through Luo Zheng's Concentric Clothes emitted a horrifying sound. This power continued to wreak havoc inside Luo Zheng's body and continuously destroy Luo Zheng's five viscera and six bowels.
"Puff …"
Luo Zheng kept spitting out blood, but his body still rolled to the other side. At the same time, he left a bright golden trail of blood on the ground. He was now an arrow at the end of its flight. The power contained in Leng Lin Yue's strange claw marks was too special. Luo Zheng's body simply couldn't withstand it.
Leng Lin Yue walked leisurely. A trace of contempt appeared on his face. Just now, he had accidentally injured Luo Zheng. This showed that he was too careless. It didn't mean that Luo Zheng was strong … Just as Leng Lin Yue was about to chase after Luo Zheng to end his life, a graceful figure suddenly appeared.
This time, it was Han Liusu. She stood in front of Luo Zheng and said coldly to Leng Lin Yue, "Let him go."
"He killed Xie Jiao. He's already dead. You know this very well. Even if I let him go, he'll still die." Leng Lin Yue stared at this woman and sneered. He and Han Liusu didn't have any feelings for each other, but she was still his fiancée. Once an agreement was made, it was almost impossible to change it.
"You don't have to deal with him." Han Liusu said seriously.
Leng Lin Yue tilted his head and sneered, "What if I say no?"
"Crash …"

Chapter 1837
The three siblings of the Han Clan were famous in the Sea of Time.
The youngest siblings of the Han Clan's Sage Emperor, Han Cangyan, Han Liusu, and Han Chuyue, were all well-known figures among the younger generation!
Han Chuyue was ranked second among the Proving Gods, Han Liusu was ranked third among the Lower True Gods, and Han Cangyan was ranked first among the Pseudo Middle True Gods.
There were a total of 36 floating islands in the Sea of Time, which were the 36 Great Clans. The geniuses of each Great Clan were not to be underestimated. Not only were there geniuses from the main Great Clan, there were also geniuses from the side branches of the main Great Clan, and there were also countless outstanding geniuses from other clans …
The three siblings of the Han Clan were all at the top of the rankings. They had indeed created quite a miracle. There were even people who exclaimed that the Han Clan was about to rise again. In the future, they might even fight for the position of the top three Great Clans.
Not long after returning from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Han Liusu condensed her own Godhead.
But her Godhead was very special …
Because the world in her body was completely different from other people's.
Before condensing her Godhead, Han Liusu only knew that her body would mysteriously emit that fatal blue Deviant Fire. That Deviant Fire was completely out of her control, and it had even harmed her loved ones. Because of this, Han Liusu was very distressed.
But even the Sage Emperor was unable to recognize this Deviant Fire, and was unable to help his daughter get rid of it.
When Han Liusu condensed her Godhead, her Inner World underwent a drastic change.
The Inner World that she had painstakingly created suddenly spewed out endless blue Deviant Fire. The Deviant Fire completely destroyed her Inner World, and all the living beings in her Inner World perished.
This process was very painful, and as a girl, Han Liusu watched as the countless living beings she had created were burned. She was in so much pain that she wished she was dead.
When the Deviant Fire descended, a huge blue cloud of fire formed in the sky above her Inner World. It was this cloud of fire that condensed her Godhead, allowing her to become a Lower True God …
At the same time, countless living beings were born in her Inner World.
These creatures were not humans, they were not created according to Han Liusu's own will. They were all monsters born from this sea of fire. These monsters lived with ease in the blue flames and began to reproduce, becoming the "natives" of this world.
Generally, the godhead a Proving God cultivator cultivated would be one of the three thousand divine daos. For example, if one cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao, then the godhead condensed from the Dao Accumulation of the Five Elements Divine Dao would definitely be the Five Elements Godhead.
However, Han Liusu's Godhood was very special. It belonged to the 'Mutated Godhood' category.
The Realm of the Gods was vast and boundless, and there were countless Gods. Indeed, there were a small number of Divine Warriors who had managed to condense some strange and unique Godhoods. These Godhoods were outside of the 3,000 Divine Daos, but most of them did not have any special use. They were even called "trash" Godhoods.
However, this was often the case for Heterogeneous Divinities. Most of them were useless Divinities, but a small number of them possessed unparalleled power!
Han Liusu belonged to the latter category.
Relying on her own understanding of this blue Heavenly Flame, she rapidly accumulated points in the Arena of Gods and quickly climbed to the third place on the ranking board.
She was the only woman among the top ten lower True Gods, and she had stunned everyone on the thirty-six floating islands.
It was said that the reason the Leng Clan was willing to marry the Han Clan was because of Han Liusu's uniqueness.
Now, the blue flames burning on Han Liusu's body were much purer and stronger than back in the Forbidden Grounds of God Refinement.
Han Liusu was definitely not Leng Lin Yue's match, but Leng Lin Yue was very wary of her flames.
Though he was wary, with his strength, it would be too easy for him to defeat Han Liusu. However, Han Liusu was still the woman he liked, and the marriage between the Leng Clan and Han Clan had already spread like wildfire on the floating islands.
Now, in order to stop him, this woman was willing to fight him, and she was doing it for another man.
How could the proud Leng Lin Yue endure this?
The corners of his eyes twitched slightly, his eyes releasing a murderous light as he stared fixedly at Han Liusu, asking, "For an outsider, a nameless junior from the grassroots, you are willing to treat me like this! You're my future wife! "
When the hedonistic sons in the hall heard Leng Lin Yue's words, they exchanged strange looks. Why did brother Yue suddenly explode …
The flames on Han Liusu's hair flickered, and a trace of mockery appeared in her eyes. "You're called Leng Lin Yue right?"
"What do you want to say?" Leng Lin Yue asked.
"We've only met a few times. Although father set the marriage, he never asked for my permission. Do I know you that well? And I'm your future wife. Do you have any shame? If there really comes a day when I have no choice but to marry you, I'll kill myself in advance. Do you understand? "Han Liusu said indifferently, as though it was natural for her to do so.
Leng Lin Yue's eye twitched even more.
No man would be able to endure such humiliation, let alone such an outstanding person like him.
A towering rage was ignited by Han Liusu's words, and a violent killing intent emanated from his handsome face. The aura he exuded was like a storm formed by countless evil spirits, wreaking havoc in the hall.
Anyone who sensed that killing intent would be flustered. Even the upper true gods were the same.
Uncle Gong was already slowly walking forward. At the same time, he said indifferently, "Everyone, although we're all guards of a noble family, there are some things that I have to say clearly. If this brat intends to harm the Han family's princess, I will definitely kill him! If you try to stop him, then prepare to face the Han family's fury … "
These juniors were messing around, so Uncle Gong naturally had to abide by the rules and not participate. At the same time, he couldn't stop them.
But if the Han family's core disciples were in danger, he couldn't just sit by and watch.
The upper true gods also realized the severity of the situation. If the young master of the Leng family really killed the eldest princess of the Han family, the blame on them would not be small either. They wanted to control the situation as quickly as possible.
However, Leng Lin Yue didn't move. They also maintained their restraint. According to the logic of a noble family's guard, these noble family's disciples shouldn't get involved if they could!
"Hahahaha …"
After Leng Lin Yue's killing intent wreaked havoc for a long time, he suddenly looked up to the sky and laughed wildly.
After laughing, he looked down at Han Liusu and said, "What kind of person am I, Leng Lin Yue, to be bound by a woman like you? Let me tell you, the marriage between the Leng and Han families is an established fact! You can die, but if you die, there's still your sister! Your sister can also die, but if she dies, there's still her cousin … "
Hearing this, Han Chu Yue and Han Bi Luo glanced at each other, their hearts filled with sorrow.
Han Liusu's eyes slightly jumped. Leng Lin Yue wasn't someone who would admit defeat. If she could do what she said, then so could Leng Lin Yue.
"I won't talk about this with you today." Leng Lin Yue continued, "But the person behind you must die!"
"You can try." Han Liusu had already thrown caution to the wind. Luo Zheng stood up for Han Chuyue. She couldn't let Luo Zheng die because of this.
Luo Zheng who was lying on the ground couldn't move. Blood continuously flowed out. He leaned against the door of the hall and looked at Han Liusu's back. The flickering flames on her hair made Luo Zheng's eyes blur.
She should've discovered his identity by now?
According to normal logic, even if "Luo Tian Xing" caused this disaster because of Han Chuyue, Han Liusu wouldn't be so determined to protect him …
At this time, Luo Zheng felt a figure flash not far away. A gust of wind blew over. His heart sank. If Leng Lin Yue really wanted to kill him, Han Liusu wouldn't be able to stop him!
Seeing Leng Lin Yue rushing towards her, Han Liusu also rushed towards Luo Zheng and hugged Luo Zheng tightly.
"Huala!"
The blazing blue Mystical Fire swept out with a loud rumble, enveloping an area with a radius of thirty feet.

Chapter 1838
In the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Luo Zheng had been refined by Han Liusu's unknown strange flame …
It was also because of this strange flame that he was chosen by the Mountain Shattering Clan. Otherwise, the two of them would not have been able to leave the Mountain Shattering Clan.
Perhaps because of the Mountain Shattering Clan's Avoiding Disaster Incense, it could stimulate any living being's most primal instincts. That hug was deeply engraved in Han Liusu's heart. Every time she thought about it, it was still fresh in her mind. She had always missed Luo Zheng.
But she knew in her heart that Luo Zheng was destined to be a passerby in her life.
He was burdened with a blood feud, possessed heaven-defying talent, and had lofty ambitions. However, what he wanted to accomplish was destined to be an impossible task.
Even though Han Liusu was clearly aware of this, but when she noticed Luo Tianxing and judged that Luo Tianxing and Luo Zheng might be the same person, Han Liusu no longer had any reservations in her heart. She wanted to do everything she could to help him.
But she did not expect things to reach this stage. First, Luo Zheng killed Xie Jiao. At the same time, Leng Lin Yue wanted to kill Luo Zheng.
Just now, she had already gotten rid of Uncle Gong and asked her brother Han Cangyan to hurry over. But for some reason, Han Cangyan still had not arrived.
In a moment of desperation, Han Liusu could only hug Luo Zheng. At the same time, she used her own godhead to draw out the blue strange flame from her inner world. It completely enveloped her and Luo Zheng …
"Ah, if sister does this, won't it burn Luo Tianxing to death …" Han Chuyue stared blankly at the raging flames.
Han Biluo also stared at this scene. She said softly: "I don't know if Luo Tianxing can withstand it."
"How can he withstand sister's flame? Have you forgotten how strong sister's flame is?" Han Chuyue asked.
They were Han Liusu's closest people. They were most aware of the terror of the blue Deviant Flame on Han Liusu's body. Since young, Han Chu Yue had been warned not to get too close to her sister. However, Han Chu Yue rarely listened to such warnings. She still liked to run with her sister.
Strangely enough, when Han Chuyue was by Han Liusu's side, her uncontrollable Deviant Fire never once erupted.
However, Han Chuyue's uncontrollable outbursts had left a terrifying impression on Han Chuyue. More than once, Han Jia's people had died because of her Deviant Fire, so for a period of time, no one dared to serve Han Liusu's daily needs.
The two little girls stared blankly at this scene. In the end, Luo Tianxing had stood up for the two of them. Who would have thought that he would be burned alive by his elder sister in the end? It was too pitiful.
Leng Linyue and the other rich kids also believed that Luo Zheng would be burned to death.
They had also heard of the mystical flames on Han Liusu's body. Some of them had even lost to Han Liusu multiple times in the Arena of the Gods because of this flame …
"Hualala …"
The blue flame circled around Han Liusu. From the outside, one could only feel the temperature of the strange flame. They couldn't see the situation inside at all. Even divine sense couldn't penetrate it. Han Liusu didn't show any signs of stopping!
Leng Linyue stared at the flame for a long time without moving. He hated Luo Zheng to the core. As the saying went, if he was alive, he wanted to see the person. If he was dead, he wanted to see the corpse. Even if Luo Zheng was burned to ashes, he wanted to see it with his own eyes. But Han Liusu didn't seem to have any intention of stopping the flame.
With Han Liusu's Lower True God cultivation, it wouldn't be a problem for her to burn this flame for ten days.
"Enough, Liusu!" Leng Linyue said indifferently. The resentment in his eyes gradually dissipated …
Just now, he had been very agitated. But now, he had calmed down. Thinking of the Leng family's situation, his actions just now were indeed a bit impulsive. He had forgotten his father's teachings. It could be said that a small impatience spoiled the big plan. If the Leng family's plan succeeded, what was a mere Han Jia?
"Hualala …"
The flame was still wildly pulsing, but no sound could be heard from it.
"Liusu, if you're not willing to stop the flame, I'll have to forcibly extinguish it," Leng Linyue said again. There was a faint hint of threat in his voice.
Leng Linyue definitely couldn't completely extinguish the flame, but he naturally had a way to split it open and see what was really going on inside.
But Han Liusu still ignored him.
Within the flame, Han Liusu once again hugged Luo Zheng tightly …
She hadn't experienced much in her life. Even though she was the Han family's Holy Emperor's first daughter from the day she was born, and had always been treated like the apple of his eye. She had been treated hundreds of times better than her brother Han Cangyan. But most of her time had been spent in loneliness and helplessness. For as long as she could remember, she hated the Strange Flame within her body. It was because of this flame that she was treated like an alien.
Now, many of the younger generation in the rich and powerful families envied Han Liusu's innate talent. With that incomparably domineering Strange Flame, she had easily killed her way to third place on the Lower True God rankings. She even had the momentum to climb up. But what they didn't know was just how much Han Liusu hated her own innate talent.
"Hiss, hiss …"
The flame surrounding Han Liusu and Luo Zheng was divided into two layers. Now, Han Liusu's ability to control the flame had also become stronger. The inner layer of flame was spinning rapidly in a spiral shape, almost able to block everything.
But she was still a little worried. If Leng Linyue really wanted to break through her flame, he should be able to do it.
She gently touched Luo Zheng's cheek, looking at Luo Zheng's golden body. Those small golden Sanskrit characters were still spinning continuously, continuously absorbing her flame.
If before she had felt that "Luo Tianxing" was 90% likely to be Luo Zheng, then now she was 100% sure. Only Luo Zheng could safely and unscathed under her Strange Flame …
Seeing this scene, Han Liusu faintly felt a sense of happiness.
She liked Luo Zheng not because of his innate talent or strength, but because he had a sense of destiny.
Luo Zheng seemed to have a natural need to use flames to burn his body, and in the process, he could become stronger. Han Liusu, on the other hand, was born to release this Strange Flame. She felt that she and Luo Zheng were destined to be together. When women experienced love, they were very superstitious about this point. Without a doubt, Han Liusu was at this stage.
"Why did you change your appearance after coming to the Han Clan's floating island? Why didn't you come find me?" Han Liusu asked softly.
If Han Biluo hadn't returned to the Han Clan's ancestral land, and if she hadn't happened to encounter these things here, she probably would never have known that Luo Zheng had come to the floating island.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything. Instead, he put on an innocent expression, as if Han Liusu had mistaken him for someone else …
