In the beginning, Luo Zheng had indeed planned to bite the bullet and not admit it. That way, Han Liusu wouldn't be able to do anything to him. He never thought that in the end, he would still need this woman to protect him. And with this Strange Flame burning his body, the truth would be revealed.
"Afraid that I would betray you?" Han Liusu continued to ask. Perhaps there was a trace of resentment in her heart, and she faintly had a questioning tone.
She did know Luo Zheng's identity, and even more so knew some of his secrets. However, this didn't mean that she would stand on the side of the Sage Hall … On the contrary, she would do her best to help Luo Zheng!
Seeing Han Liusu's serious expression, Luo Zheng said softly, "No, I'm afraid of implicating you …"
In reality, under Luo Zheng's consideration, Han Liusu and Han Chuyue were indeed uncontrollable factors. However, Luo Zheng was also afraid of implicating Han Liusu. Because once the elders of the Han Clan found out the truth, they definitely wouldn't stand on Luo Zheng's side. At that time, Han Liusu would probably be in an even more difficult position.

Chapter 1839
Feeling Han Liusu's tenderness, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat.
To Luo Zheng, this trip to the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was just a training. He didn't think that he would leave such a deep impression in this woman's heart.
But when he thought about what he had to face, he remained silent.
Relying on a woman's help was not in line with Luo Zheng's character!
From the moment he stepped onto the martial path, he had never placed his hopes on others. For those who helped him, Luo Zheng would always repay them more.
Later on, when he found out that his father had been silently plotting behind him and helping him improve, he was very disappointed. This was his character, and it was hard to change it in a short period of time.
Leng Linyue narrowed his eyes, revealing a faint cold light. He walked around the ball of flames …
He had heard of this blue strange flame long ago, so he naturally didn't dare to touch it.
But that didn't mean he couldn't do anything about it!
This woman had used a blue Deviant Flame to envelop that brat. If news of this spread out, it would be a great humiliation for him, Leng Lin Yue. He hadn't thought that Han Liusu would do something so outrageous. She didn't consider her own clan at all.
If that kid had been burned to ashes, Han Liusu should have removed the flame. What was the meaning of continuing to burn?
Everyone present looked at each other in dismay …
After a while, Leng Linyue finally lost his patience. He stretched out his hand and slammed it down. A series of sharp claw marks extended from his hand.
These claw marks floated in the air and revolved around Leng Linyue. Anyone who approached Leng Linyue would be cut into pieces by these claw marks …
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The claw marks spun faster and faster. At the same time, they condensed along his body. All of the claw marks turned into small spirals and gathered in his hand. When these spirals gathered, it was like a small spinning top in his hand!
Then he threw it lightly and spat out the word "go". The spinning top whistled past and rushed into the flame that Han Liusu had set up!
"Hualala …"
The Strange Fire with tassels could devour everything, but Leng Linyue's spirals were formed from the claw marks formed by the "force". They were essentially a mass of high-speed spinning energy. Once the energy entered the flames, it immediately caused a burst of chaotic airflow. The flames were blown askew, and a path was opened from within.
In less than a breath's time, the flames could no longer cover Luo Zheng and Han Liusu. The flames released by Han Liusu were easily destroyed by Leng Lin Yue!
When Leng Linyue saw that Han Liusu was still hugging Luo Zheng, and that her skin was so close to Luo Zheng's, a vicious glint appeared in his eyes.
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo also looked at each other, their eyes revealing endless astonishment …
"This Luo Tianxing is not afraid of the barrier's Heavenly Flame!" Han Biluo cried out.
Han Chuyue's eyes flashed slightly, "Sister … knows! This person is … this person is … "
"Who is this person?" Han Bi Luo asked Han Chu Yue in astonishment.
Han Chuyue shook her head and did not speak. Her small mouth suddenly curled up.
Previously, Han Liusu had suddenly used the Strange Fire to envelop herself and Luo Zheng. This made Han Chuyue very confused. Wasn't this method burning this "Luo Tianxing" to death?
Now that Han Liusu saw that Luo Tianxing was perfectly fine, she immediately linked it to Luo Zheng.
Her sister had told her that Luo Zheng was the only person who was not afraid of her Strange Fire.
At that time, Han Chuyue did not believe it. She only said that Luo Zheng relied on a body protection treasure or a special technique to resist.
But Han Liusu shook her head and denied it. She told Han Chuyue that Luo Zheng's body was very special, and was indeed immune to most flames.
Now that she saw Luo Tianxing's appearance, no matter how slow Han Chuyue was, she could understand. Moreover, her sister should have already realized that Luo Tianxing's true identity was Luo Zheng. Otherwise, she would not have used her own Strange Fire!
"Interesting, what a pair of dogs!" Leng Linyue lightly said.
"Sou!"
As his voice fell, he followed the spiral path and rushed into the sea of fire.
Leng Linyue rushing in like this was very dangerous. However, he was bold and his cultivation was also a level higher than Han Liusu's. Now, even if he had to take the risk, he would still kill Luo Zheng!
And at this moment, he wanted to kill not only Luo Zheng, but also Han Liusu!
Today, he and the Leng family's rich kids had come to the Han family's floating island just to pay a visit to Han Liusu. As for Liu Chengtian and the others teasing Han Chuyue and Han Biluo, it was a very common thing between rich kids. He did not expect that Luo Zheng's interference would cause the situation to develop to a point where it was difficult to stop.
However, Leng Linyue did not reflect on himself. Liu Chengtian and Xie Jiao were both weak and wanted to kill Luo Zheng at the same time. In the end, Xie Jiao was killed by Luo Zheng. He was just seeking his own death!
It was just that Leng Linyue had viewed Luo Zheng as an ant outside of the floating island from the very beginning. He did not treat him as an equal.
Usually, these scions of the rich and powerful clans would feel this way. The outer clan scions of the various floating islands were not as strong as these scions of the rich and powerful. In fact, there was a considerable gap between them, so the outer clan scions wouldn't easily offend the rich and powerful scions. But this time, they were unlucky to encounter Luo Zheng.
Han Liusu saw that a path had been cut open in the sea of fire and Leng Linyue was rushing in. Her gaze immediately turned cold. Her slender fingers lightly brushed, and wisps of blue flames floated out and swept towards Leng Linyue.
This method was indeed difficult for a lower true god to deal with. Countless geniuses in the Arena of Gods had been incinerated by this move of Han Liusu's. However, as a middle true god ranked in the top five, Leng Linyue naturally had a way to deal with it.
He lightly waved his hands, and small balls of wind formed from his hands!
"Wind Shaping Dance …"
Leng Linyue lightly flicked his fingers, and the balls of wind continuously rolled in all directions around him.
Although Han Liusu's flames were extremely vigorous, those flames were unable to escape within three chi of him. His face was full of contempt. He glanced at Han Liusu indifferently, and then slowly walked towards Luo Zheng. "Letting a woman protect you, and it's someone else's woman … Hehe, this is the first time I, Leng Linyue, have seen such a shameless man! However, this world is fair. Placing your hopes on others will only result in disappointment! Go die … "
As soon as he finished speaking, he had already raised his arm. Faint bits of sharp light faintly condensed between his fingers. This time, he was determined to kill Luo Zheng.
Just as Leng Linyue was about to attack, Han Liusu flipped over, and a fire snake formed from her fingertips and coiled towards Leng Linyue!
This kind of move was no threat to Leng Linyue. He coldly snorted, and his body flickered, disappearing from Luo Zheng and Han Liusu's sight. In the next moment, he was already close to Han Liusu.
"I didn't think that before you've even married into my Leng Clan, you'd be lectured by my Leng Clan. I'll let you know what it means to respect your husband!"
"Slap!"
As soon as he finished speaking, Leng Linyue heavily slapped Han Liusu's face.
This slap was quite heavy, and Han Liusu flew to the side …
Lying on the ground, Luo Zheng's eyes were cold. He stared fixedly at Leng Linyue, not saying a word. At this point, there was nothing he could do.
Only the Evil Old Man in his mind sighed faintly, "Young people are still too impulsive …"
The Evil Old Man naturally didn't want to see Luo Zheng provoke these rich kids. With Luo Zheng's current strength and status, it was purely an act of suicide. But in the end, he was just a soul residing in Luo Zheng's mind. How could he influence Luo Zheng's decisions?
"What a hateful gaze," Leng Linyue looked down at Luo Zheng. Claw marks slowly emerged from his fingers, slowly descending towards Luo Zheng's mind!
Han Liusu and Han Biluo stood motionless, their little fists tightly clenched. They had no idea what to do.
Just as Leng Linyue's claw mark was halfway down, the hall's door suddenly opened with a "kacha" sound. The moment the door was pushed open, a powerful aura swept out, and at the same time, a strong figure fiercely slammed into Leng Linyue!
"Bang!"
Leng Linyue groaned, and was pushed back ten steps. At the same time, he heard a voice say, "You just went to the forbidden area, and you bunch of blind people came to my Han Clan's territory to behave so atrociously. Do you think my Han Clan has no one?"

Chapter 1840
Han Cangyan's figure wasn't very tall, and he even looked a little thin. However, since he stepped into the hall, he naturally exuded a domineering aura …
Most of the rich people on the floating island were concentrated in the three stages of proving divinity, lower true god, and middle true god.
After truly cultivating to become an upper true god, they would break away from these circles and begin to focus on breaking through to the Great Perfection Realm and the higher levels of the world. Middle true gods had an extremely strong right to speak among the rich and powerful disciples. After all, their cultivation was the highest in this circle, so naturally, they were also the strongest.
And Han Cangyan was ranked first among middle true god, so he was considered the strongest among the young generation of this group of rich people!
Han Cangyan didn't care to mingle with these rich people.
These rich people were divided into four or five circles according to their family. They all wanted to rope in Han Cangyan, but he didn't care about them at all …
Han Cangyan glanced at Leng Linyue, then looked around and finally landed on Han Liusu.
Leng Linyue's slap wasn't light. Half of Han Liusu's white face was swollen, and there was a clear palm print.
Seeing that, Han Cangyan frowned and asked coldly, "Who hit you?"
Before Han Liusu could speak, Han Chuyue pointed at Leng Linyue and shouted, "Him!"
Han Cangyan stared at Leng Linyue and said, "You've got guts, Leng Linyue."
Hearing that, Leng Linyue's eyes narrowed. "Han Cangyan, do you know what your sister did?"
Leng Linyue felt that he had the right to teach Han Liusu a lesson. After all, she would marry into the Leng family sooner or later. No man would feel bad about what Han Liusu had done now!
Who knew that Han Cangyan had already started walking towards Leng Linyue. "I don't care what my sister did wrong. Whoever dares to hit her should be prepared to be hit! "
Seeing Han Cangyan slowly approaching, Leng Linyue also slowly retreated. He raised his hands and continued to explain, "Han Cangyan, there must be reason and rules. Our Leng family …"
Leng Linyue still wanted to use the Leng family. After all, the Leng family's foundation was much deeper than the Han family's.
But Han Cangyan no longer gave him a chance.
Everyone felt an oppressive aura burst forth. Everyone's shoulders suddenly sank. Han Cang Yan had already charged towards Leng Lin Yue at an incomparably strange speed.
Han Cangyan didn't even use his own Godhead, directly using a shoulder strike!
Han Cangyan was ranked first among the middle True Gods, while Leng Linyue was ranked fifth. Logically speaking, the actual gap between them was not that big. However, the competition between the middle True Gods was quite fierce. This fierce process was reflected in the second and eighth place.
For example, Leng Lin Yue, who was ranked third last month, was now ranked fifth. It could be said that the difference in strength between the second and eighth place wasn't that big.
The person ranked ninth was no match for the seven of them. Similarly, this group of people was no match for Han Cangyan.
After encountering Han Cangyan in the Arena of Gods, these seven people could only be brutally abused by Han Cangyan.
For example, Leng Lin Yue had encountered Han Cangyan four times in the Arena of Gods. Every time, Leng Lin Yue had used all his means, but he still lost completely. There was no suspense at all.
Encountering this guy outside the Arena of Gods again, how could Leng Lin Yue have the desire to fight?
Facing Han Cangyan's "shoulder strike," Leng Lin Yue didn't try to fight back. Instead, he kept retreating, trying to completely avoid Han Cangyan's strike. However, although his speed wasn't slow, Han Cangyan's speed was even faster …
Seeing that he couldn't avoid it, Leng Lin Yue clenched his teeth and suddenly twisted his body. Two golden Sanskrit characters started to appear on the back of his hands!
As the hope of the younger generation of the Leng family, Leng Lin Yue had received all kinds of preferential treatment in the Leng family. These two golden Sanskrit characters had always been his trump card. Previously, he naturally didn't use them to deal with Luo Zheng. Now, facing Han Cangyan, he didn't have any hesitation. Han Cangyan was a monster in the eyes of all the middle True Gods.
"Is it useful?" When Han Cangyan saw the golden Sanskrit characters on the back of Leng Lin Yue's hands, a trace of mockery appeared on his face. He reached out his hand and pulled at Leng Lin Yue.
An invisible force suddenly pulled the retreating Han Cangyan over!
Before Leng Lin Yue could react, Han Cangyan's slap had already landed on his face.
This slap was much more powerful than the one on Han Liusu's face before. However, Han Cangyan still held back. Otherwise, with his understanding of the Strength Divine Path, this slap would have been able to chop Leng Lin Yue's head off.
In the Arena of Gods, many people had experienced Han Cangyan's "slap." Most of the opponents who were hit by his slap had their heads separated from their bodies.
"Slap!"
A crisp sound echoed in the main hall.
Leng Lin Yue's entire body flipped in mid-air. He flipped for a distance of more than ten feet before landing heavily in the corner of the wall and fainting.
"I, Han Cangyan, don't know any rules. Whoever dares to touch my Han Jia's people should ask themselves if they have the strength to do so," Han Cangyan said loudly. His words seemed to be directed at Leng Lin Yue, but also seemed to be directed at the rich playboys present.
"Han Cangyan … Is your Han Jia begging our Leng Family now? Do you really think you'll have a good life after offending our Leng Family? "A voice came from the crowd. It was Leng Lin Yue's younger cousin, Leng Hao.
When Leng Hao saw his cousin get hit, he naturally felt ashamed and brought up the Leng Family.
Now, everyone knew that Han Jia was sucking up to the Leng Family, but Leng Lin Yue was still being bullied by Han Jia. This didn't make sense!
Hearing Leng Hao's words, Han Cangyan sneered. "You're saying you can represent the Leng Family? Or that guy on the ground can represent the Leng Family? "
As the fourth-ranked rich family, the Leng Family was intricate and complicated.
Even if the Leng Family was willing to marry Leng Lin Yue to Han Liusu, it didn't mean that Leng Lin Yue's position in the Leng Family was very high. Just like Han Cangyan, even if he was the number one middle true god, so what? He only represented the future of Han Jia. He didn't have much power in the family.
In the eyes of the saints, the Arena of Gods was just a child's game. If you wanted to get the full power of your family, you had to cultivate to perfection before you had the right to speak. This was the rule of most rich families.
Leng Hao choked at Han Cangyan's question.
"All of you, get out of Han Jia's floating island," Han Cangyan said coldly.
The rich kids all felt their hearts tremble. They lowered their heads and walked out of the hall. Leng Hao also put Leng Lin Yue on his shoulder. Before he left, he said, "We can't represent the Leng Family, but someone has to take responsibility for Xie Jiao's death."
After that, Leng Hao glared at Luo Zheng before leaving.
When Han Cangyan entered the hall, he noticed the pool of blood in the center. He didn't know that Xie Jiao had been killed.
After the rich kids all left, Han Cangyan asked with a cold face, "Who killed Xie Jiao?"
These rich kids didn't have much power in their families, but if they died, it would be a big problem.
Some kids didn't usually keep their word, but if something happened to them, their parents would naturally be furious. The rich families were no exception.
"It's him," said a Han Jia kid, pointing at Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1841
Han Cangyan didn't know the whole story.
But if Xie Jiao was really killed here, it definitely wasn't a small matter.
The rules of the rich families were that outer family disciples could die, even non-core disciples could die. If a core disciple of the family died, they would definitely be investigated.
Xie Jiao's position in the Xie family was revered. If something like this happened, the Xie family definitely wouldn't let it go.
Under Han Cangyan's gaze, Luo Zheng replied neither servile nor overbearing, "I'm called Luo Tianxing."
"You killed Xie Jiao?" Han Cangyan asked again.
Han Cangyan himself wasn't familiar with Xie Jiao. Xie Jiao's performance among the core disciples of the rich families was outstanding, but it wasn't enough for Han Cangyan to notice.
But there were almost no weaklings in the rich families. Xie Jiao was a lower true god. Luo Zheng, a mere proof god, could kill Xie Jiao. This was hard for Han Cangyan to understand …
"I did kill Xie Jiao," Luo Zheng replied seriously.
Faced with irrefutable facts, Luo Zheng would never try to escape.
After confirming this fact from Luo Zheng, Han Cangyan asked again, "Why did you kill Xie Jiao?"
Under Han Cangyan's series of questions, Han Chuyue rushed in front of Han Cangyan. Her delicate eyebrows were tightly knitted. She stared at Han Cangyan and said angrily, "You're asking him why? What reason do you think there is? Isn't it because these bastards are messing around in our Han family? "
As a rich family disciple, how could Han Cangyan not know these details. Even if he hadn't experienced it, he could roughly guess what had happened.
In the younger generation, Han Cangyan's prestige was extremely high. His position in the Han family was very high. Even the elders of the Han family were polite to him. Only his two sisters were not afraid of him at all.
When Han Chuyue was angry, she vented on Han Cangyan. Han Cangyan could only stare blankly and have nothing to say.
After a while, Han Liusu said softly, "Now is not the time to blame …"
When the siblings heard Han Liusu speak, they both looked at Han Liusu.
The handprint on Han Liusu's face gradually faded, but the slap was extremely deep, half of her originally gentle and beautiful face was already swollen.
Han Cangyan's heart ached when he saw this. He casually took out a bunch of emerald green grass from his Sumeru Space. In the middle of the bunch of grass, there was a small flower in full bloom. In the middle of the flower, there was a small human face.
When Han Cangyan grabbed the grass, he used two fingers to gently twist it. The small face suddenly let out a scream, and at the same time, it was crushed by his fingers, and then smeared on Han Liusu's face.
Soon, Han Liusu's swollen left face disappeared and returned to its original appearance.
"I know, but someone has to take responsibility for this," Han Cangyan said indifferently.
Han Cangyan felt that this matter was not difficult to deal with. He and Luo Tianxing were neither relatives nor friends. Since Luo Tianxing was the one who killed Luo Tianxing and caused the trouble, he naturally had to bear the responsibility!
As a core disciple of the Han Family, everything he did was for the benefit of the family.
Han Liusu coldly glanced at her brother. She had already seen through Han Cangyan's thoughts. "Han Chuyue already said that Luo Tianxing acted for us. Do you still want him to bear the responsibility?"
"Even if he acted for you, he shouldn't have killed Xie Jiao. Xie Jiao's crime didn't warrant death," Han Cangyan replied. He was a bit surprised. Han Liusu and Han Chuyue were not unreasonable people. They clearly understood the seriousness of this matter. It was very likely that it would directly alarm the Xie and Han Family Saint Emperors.
"According to brother's meaning, Luo Tianxing should have just stood there and let Xie Jiao kill him?" Han Chuyue suddenly ridiculed.
Han Biluo stood behind Han Chuyue, not saying anything. She naturally felt that Han Cangyan was in the wrong, but her relationship with Han Cangyan had become estranged. She was too embarrassed to teach this' cousin 'a lesson, so she could only let Han Chuyue do it.
"Those elders only look at the result, not the process. The truth is that Xie Jiao was killed by Luo Tianxing, and Luo Tianxing is fine," Han Cangyan retorted.
"But brother, you don't know how hateful those fellows are …"
"…"
Just as Han Chuyue and Han Cangyan were arguing, Han Liusu suddenly interjected, "I will use my life to protect Luo Tianxing. If he is taken away by the Xie Family to be executed, I, Han Liusu, will die with him."
Han Liusu's words were very indifferent, but they were also very resolute, as if there was no room for negotiation.
"Liusu …" Han Cangyan was also startled.
"Sister …" Han Chuyue's heart skipped a beat. Her guess had finally become reality.
After returning from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, Han Chuyue felt that her sister had always been strange. She rarely accompanied her, and almost put all of her heart and soul into cultivation. At the same time, she had achieved astonishing results in the arena!
Other than that, her sister didn't have much interest in other things. But whenever they recalled the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, whenever Luo Zheng was mentioned, her sister's eyes would light up, as if she had become a completely different person.
At that time, Han Chuyue guessed that her sister had probably fallen in love with that fellow.
She had also seen through Luo Zheng's identity and understood the reason.
But Han Cangyan was completely in the dark. When he suddenly heard Han Liusu's words, he was immediately dumbfounded.
Han Cangyan was also a disciple of a rich family. In his eyes, all the other disciples added together weren't as important as a single Han Liusu. He had always only acknowledged his own family members! Was there a need for Han Liusu to die for an outer disciple? And from the looks of it, this outer disciple should have just entered Han Jia's floating island not long ago!
Han Biluo also felt that it was strange. Luo Tianxing had fallen into such big trouble because he had helped her and Han Chuyue. Han Cangyan wanted to sacrifice Luo Tianxing and leave Han Jia aside. Han Biluo was also very angry, but Han Liusu wouldn't have such a big reaction.
Luo Zheng lay on the ground, constantly circulating his blood and Primordial Astral Nova to heal his injuries. Han Liusu's resolute words moved him very much. The current situation had already exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations. He could only take one step at a time.
"Is there a need to do this for a cultivator who has proven himself to be a Divinity cultivator?" Han Cangyan thought for a while, trying to persuade Han Liusu.
"Big brother, you know my personality, so there's no need to say anything more." Han Liusu shook her head, stopping Han Cangyan's words. "If you try to hand Luo Tianxing over to the Xie family, then all words are meaningless!"
Han Cangyan feared no one in his life. In the forbidden lands, he had once fought with an upper True God and crawled back with half his body. He had once even disobeyed his father, the Han family's Saint Emperor, and risked everything to save someone in a forbidden land.
However, he was very protective of his two younger sisters.
Since Han Liusu said so, there was nothing he could do. "I can't make the decision on this matter. I ask the Han family's elders to make the decision." Saying so, Han Cangyan looked at Han Biluo.
Han Biluo's father was the Han family's current patriarch, the patriarch of the entire Han family, Han Tianxiao.
That night …
Luo Zheng's injuries had mostly healed. Uncle Gong, who had treated Luo Zheng, noticed Luo Zheng's recovery ability and clicked his tongue in wonder. After Uncle Gong healed the rest of Luo Zheng's hidden injuries, he tied him up and sent him to the Spirit Heart Palace on Han Jia's floating island.
In the Spirit Heart Palace, dozens of upper True Gods were lined up in a row in front of the palace, while several Perfection Stage experts stood on the floating platforms at the very top of the palace.
Although these experts had restrained their auras, there was still a powerful aura enveloping the Spirit Heart Palace. When Luo Zheng was sent in by Uncle Gong, all the experts of Han Jia turned to look at Luo Zheng. They wanted to see what kind of person this outsider who had killed Xie Jiao and caused such a huge disaster was.
Those gazes gathered on Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng even found it difficult to breathe, but he still sat on the ground and looked up, his expression neither servile nor overbearing.
"As far as I know, Xie Jiao's ranking among the lower True Gods is quite high. This outsider is just a Testimonial expert. Can he really kill Xie Jiao?"
Most people found it hard to believe when they heard about this.

Chapter 1842
News of Xie Jiao's death hadn't spread yet. At the very least, no one from the Xie Family had come to the floating island to denounce them.
But Han Jia didn't treat this as a small matter.
In truth, the Xie and Liu Families, which were ranked last, didn't have much say in the sea of time.
Although these two families had Sages, they did not have a deep foundation. In the fierce competition, these two families were even at risk of falling into the sea of time.
Powerful families had to have Saints, but a Saint didn't necessarily make them a powerful family!
In the divine realm, there were a few "pseudo-powerful" families. These families had Saints, but their combined strength didn't have the right to enter the sea of time, nor did they have their own floating island.
So to become a powerful family, it didn't depend on a single Saint, but on the combined strength of the entire family.
Han Jia didn't fear the Xie Family, and losing a few core disciples wouldn't be a big deal. But the Xie Family had a good relationship with some of the other powerful families on the floating island, and Han Jia was completely unreasonable. If the Xie Family chose to harass them, it would be troublesome.
Han Jia had to prepare in advance, and before that, Han Tianxiao had to clarify a few things.
"Report your name," Smiling Sky, who was in the middle, said faintly.
Although his voice was calm, there was an irresistible majesty in it. His voice echoed like thunder in Luo Zheng's ears, as if he were the only god in the world.
This was the bearing a god should have.
Luo Zheng looked up at the family leader. This was the first time he'd seen such a person. Although he was shocked, the muscles on his face seemed to have rusted. There was no expression to speak of.
"Luo Tianxing," Luo Zheng replied calmly.
"Do you know why you've been tied up here?" Han Tianxiao asked.
Hearing this question, Luo Zheng felt a bit gloomy.
It turned out that the top experts of the divine realm's powerful families had a lot of nonsense to say. Since Han Jia had tied him up, it was naturally because he'd killed Xie Jiao. Why ask such an obvious question again? Could it be that these True Gods wanted to hold a trial?
"I find it strange. You're all top experts of the divine realm. Why have you tied me up like this? Are you afraid that a measly Proving Divinity expert like me will escape?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Under such a rhetorical question, Smiling Heaven's face suddenly froze slightly.
Those who could step into Spirit Heart Palace, aside from the core members of the Han family, were all upper-class true gods. In Spirit Heart Palace, they had an oppressive aura. Even ordinary true gods would be scared. They would not dare speak.
Who would have thought that a mere Proof of Divinity martial artist would be able to restrain himself so freely, completely unaffected by the atmosphere of the scene!
Now, Han Tianxiao was a little convinced that Luo Tianxing really had the strength to kill Xie Jiao.
"How dare you! He actually dares to be so unreasonable in front of the Han family's family head! "an upper True God shouted coldly.
The voice was like a knife, shooting towards Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng felt a sharp pain on his face and chest, and his ears were buzzing.
"Puchi!"
The sound pierced through Luo Zheng's eardrums, and two streams of blood shot out from his left and right ear holes.
Although Luo Zheng was temporarily deaf, there was no trace of fear on his face. He continued, "Since you brought me here, you must have something to ask me. Since you've pierced my ears, I'm afraid I can only be deaf from now on!"
When the upper True God heard Luo Zheng's words, how could he reprimand him? But after thinking about it, this brat's eardrums were shattered. Obviously, he could not hear him. For a moment, he did not know how to deal with Luo Zheng …
On the other hand, Han Tianxiao smiled, not minding Luo Zheng's rudeness at all. He stretched out his hand, and a green light spread out from his palm. The green light turned into a light that illuminated the entire Spirit Heart Palace. The moment it came into contact with Luo Zheng, he felt a warm feeling coming from the surface of his body. At the same time, the pain in his ears disappeared, and his deafened ears returned to normal.
"You are the first junior who dares to speak like this in the Spirit Heart Palace," Han Tianxiao said slowly as he sized up Luo Zheng. "Some people are brave, and are not afraid of evil. Some people pretend to be calm, and deliberately do shocking things in order to gain appreciation. I don't know if you are the first type, or the second type?"
Without waiting for Luo Zheng to answer, Han Tianxiao continued, "But this is not important … Get up!" Finished speaking, he stretched out two fingers and lightly pinched. The ropes binding Luo Zheng's back instantly broke, and Luo Zheng felt his body relax. He had actually untied himself.
After getting up, Han Tianxiao became much more direct. "I'm sure you know why I summoned you to the Spirit Heart Palace today. A junior from the Xie Clan came to our floating island to make trouble. If he died, then so be it. Our Han Clan will not pity his life. However, if the Xie Clan pursues this matter, our Han Clan is not willing to take responsibility for it. Although you are an outer family disciple, our Han Clan has decided to give you a chance. If it were you, what would you do?"
The dignified patriarch of the Han Clan actually gave Luo Zheng a chance to choose. This was already an unprecedented preferential treatment. If it had been anyone else, or any true god, who had killed Xie Jiao, Han Jia would have immediately denied any involvement and handed the true god over to be dealt with.
So when Han Tianxiao said this, the upper echelons of the Han Clan all revealed looks of surprise. This was not Han Tianxiao's style at all!
Han Tianxiao naturally didn't want to go through so much trouble.
However, his precious daughter, Biluo, had told him again and again how Luo Tianxing had helped her and how he had provoked those rich kids because of her and Han Chuyue. In the end, he had no choice but to kill Xie Jiao, hoping Han Clan would protect Luo Tianxing.
At the same time, Han Cangyan had also spoken to Han Tianxiao, tactfully passing on Han Liusu's words. The eldest princess' meaning was that if they handed Luo Tianxing over to the Xie Clan, and Luo Tianxing was executed by the Xie Clan, Han Liusu would choose to die with him!
Several core disciples of the Han Clan had come out to protect this outsider to this extent!
This made Han Tianxiao very interested. He wanted to see just what kind of ability this kid had. Did he have the qualifications?
Luo Zheng took a deep breath and looked around. He then slowly said, "In all fairness, if the Han Clan hands me over to the Xie Clan, I'm afraid it will be the stupidest thing the Han Clan has done in this God Era!"
When these words came out, the faces of the Han Clan's upper echelons were filled with shock.
On the left side of Laughing in Heaven, a middle-aged brawny man wearing scarlet red armor laughed a few times, "Little fellow, are you confused?" Or do you think too highly of yourself? Perhaps you have some talent, but my Han Clan is filled with talents. How could we lose an outsider like you? Having confidence is a good thing, but being too arrogant will make you a laughingstock! "
The others also smiled and chimed in one after another. This little fellow is really arrogant beyond belief.
They had lived for so many God Eras. What kind of genius hadn't they seen?
Han Tianxiao's smile also slowly disappeared. People who were too arrogant were destined to be disliked. He coldly said, "Perhaps I'm wrong. You don't belong to those two types of people. You're just an arrogant person who doesn't know what you're talking about."
Luo Zheng also knew that his words were destined to make people hate him, but he didn't want to die. He wanted to have a chance to prove his worth. His worth had to be great enough for Han Clan to protect him!
Perhaps Han Liusu and Han Chuyue would make a move, but they might not be able to stop Han Clan's decision.
He could guess why Han Tianxiao wanted to see him. He wanted to measure whether or not he had that kind of value!
At this time, a gentle and elegant voice slowly drifted over. "I believe that handing this little guy over to the Xie Clan is the stupidest thing Han Clan has done in this God Era!"
As the voice fell, another person appeared in the Spirit Heart Palace. It was Han Clan's secondary saint, Aunt Han Jiu. She looked at Luo Zheng with a smile and said, "I was wondering why you didn't go to the Arena of Gods today. You actually came here to slack off. Come back with me quickly."
(After catching a bad cold this month, I've been troubled by inflammation for almost a month. My mental state is a little weak. Sorry, I'll try to reduce the number of wrong words in the future. But I still want to defend myself. Everyone's mental state has its ups and downs. This is especially true for someone who wrote one day in two years without a single day of rest. Moreover, towards the end of the writing, there are some things that are very difficult to deal with. Perhaps it's because this is the first time I've written such a long story. Please forgive me! Chapter 1843
Luo Zheng was personally appointed to the Purple Soul Palace by Aunt Hanjiu.
But ever since Han Meng brought him to the Purple Soul Palace, Aunt Hanjiu had never communicated with him.
It was as if she only saw Luo Zheng as an ordinary disciple.
In reality, those who knew Aunt Hanjiu well knew that because she could only take in disciples with the Extreme Purple Yin Body, her disciples' talents were not as good as other saints'. Her disciples were indeed much weaker than the disciples of other saints.
Luo Zheng was a disciple that had been specially recommended by the Heaven-containing Palace. Such a genius, even if he entered the Saint Emperor City, would receive special treatment and attention. But when Luo Zheng entered the Purple Soul Palace, Aunty Jiu didn't ask about him. This was very strange.
What Luo Zheng did not know was that Aunt Hanjiu had always been silently watching him.
From the first time Luo Zheng stepped into the Arena of Gods, to the last time Luo Zheng appeared to encourage everyone, to Luo Zheng's consecutive victories, and even the battles in the Arena of Gods, Aunt Hanjiu had seen everything!
Normal people might not be able to see the situation inside the Jade Wall, but to Aunt Hanjiu, this was not a problem. She had watched almost all of Luo Zheng's battles …
As a secondary saint, Aunt Hanjiu's eyes were naturally not ordinary!
In her eyes, Luo Zheng was definitely not an Extreme Purple Yin Body. He should have relied on some special treasure to use the Purple Cloud Divine Dao. As for what treasure it was, even with Aunt Hanjiu's extensive knowledge, she could not figure it out.
But what truly shocked Aunt Hanjiu was not the Extreme Purple Yin Body, but Luo Zheng's true strength.
Since he had entered the Arena of Gods, he had won more than 30 consecutive matches. He had easily won against those Proof Divinity experts. The difference was so big that Aunt Han Jiu could not understand it. It was like a battle between a peak lower true god and a Proof Divinity expert. The difference in power was too big.
Aunt Hanjiu could not see how much strength Luo Zheng was hiding. Those Proof of Gods could not make Luo Zheng take them seriously.
Luo Zheng's words were indeed arrogant. Even Han Cangyan didn't have the qualifications to say that. But after Aunt Han Jiu's "endorsement", the nature was different. It was equivalent to Han Jia's secondary saint agreeing with Luo Zheng's words!
"Master." Luo Zheng cupped his hands towards Aunt Hanjiu. He didn't have any interactions with the quasi-sage and didn't expect Aunt Hanjiu to appear at this time. Although Aunt Hanjiu wasn't his master, according to the Purple Soul Palace's custom, he still had to call Aunt Hanjiu as master.
Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly. "Come back with me."
Although this sentence was short, Luo Zheng's heart was still warm …
It was no wonder that the female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace were so reluctant. Although they were reluctant to give up the Han Family's floating island, they were even more reluctant to give up their master.
"Wait." Smiling in the sky stopped him.
But just as Smiling in the sky finished speaking, the purple light in Aunt Hanjiu's charming eyes flashed slightly. Strands of purple smoke rose up in the entire palace.
These strands of purple smoke spiraled upwards. There were a total of 49 strands of purple smoke, forming the Four Nine Divine Transformation Array. A faintly discernible killing intent spread out from this array. This killing intent wasn't dense, but it made people feel fear.
"Last time, it was you who convinced the Saint Emperor to make things difficult for my Purple Soul Palace. I, Aunt Hanjiu, endured it. But Smiling in the sky, you probably forgot who I, Aunt Hanjiu, am. Let alone you, even if the Saint Emperor himself was here, I would definitely bring this little fellow back." Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly.
The faintly discernible killing intent quietly spread out in the Spirit Heart Palace …
Luo Zheng could feel the murderous intent, but he couldn't understand how frightening it was. However, looking at the expressions of the upper Real Gods, the few Consummate Real Gods, and Han Tianxiao, he could tell.
Just a moment ago, these people were still as calm as water, their expressions as if everything was under control. But now, their faces were as pale as paper, and their eyes were filled with the word "fear."
They knew that although Aunt Hanjiu was still smiling, she was in fact angry!
When a secondary saint was angry, the entire Han Family floating island would shake!
Even the head of the family, Smiling in the sky, was stiff all over. His voice didn't seem to come from his throat. "Secondary saint, please calm down. It's just that he killed a direct descendant of the Xie family. If the Xie family decides to punish him, I'm afraid it will be difficult to explain."
Aunt Hanjiu naturally understood the situation before she came. Seeing her smile sinisterly, the killing intent hidden in the darkness suddenly surged. The hearts of the Han Family's upper echelons immediately hung in the air!
"So what if he killed a direct descendant of the Xie family? What is the cultivation of that direct descendant? "Aunt Hanjiu asked.
"Lower true god, ranked in the top 40 of the Arena of Gods," Smiling in the sky honestly replied. Even though he was the head of the Han family, he was still very nervous.
Previously, he had already offended Aunt Hanjiu once. But because of the Saint Emperor's decision, with Aunt Hanjiu's domineering attitude, she should have forcefully endured it. Smiling in the sky, he knew that she had already accumulated a considerable amount of resentment in her heart. If she took this chance to let it all out, then it would be terrible. Right now, he felt like he was walking on thin ice!
"Since he was a lower true god, and was killed by my Purple Soul Palace's testimonial martial artist, even if he was a lower true god, he's still a trash true god. What's so hard to explain to such trash? If the Xie family comes looking for trouble, just treat it as if they're looking for me, Aunt Hanjiu!" Aunt Hanjiu said with a cold face.
Smiling in the sky, Smiling in the sky was completely helpless. If the Xie family came looking for Han Jia, they would definitely come looking for him. Aunt Hanjiu's words were completely unreasonable, but right now, there was nothing he could do …
After Aunt Hanjiu finished speaking, she indifferently sized up Luo Zheng. She reached out her soft white hand and lightly waved it. A purple cloud of dust spiraled up and enveloped Luo Zheng. At the same time, she said, "Let's go!"
As her voice fell, Luo Zheng saw threads of spirals appear in front of him. His body involuntarily began to spin. In the next moment, he was back in the Purple Soul Palace.
After reaching the Great Circle of Gods, one could teleport at will. For a quasi-sage like Aunt Hanjiu, it was even easier.
The Purple Soul Palace's main hall was now completely silent.
In the air above the hall, there were six enormous candles placed in six different positions. Under the candlelight, there were six different angles of shadows under Luo Zheng's feet.
Aunt Hanjiu leaned on a wide chair. Her pleated skirt under the candlelight constantly had moonlight flowing through it, outlining her full and moving curves.
She indifferently looked at Luo Zheng and said, "You injured the fifth ranked middle true god Leng Lin Yue and killed the 40th ranked lower true god Xie Jiao … Your strength still far exceeds my expectations." Aunt Hanjiu looked at Luo Zheng and said, "Speak, what secrets do you have on you?"
Being stared at like this by a quasi-sage, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a chill run down his spine. It was as if he wasn't wearing any clothes. She could see through him in one glance.
Luo Zheng's secrets definitely couldn't be revealed to outsiders.
Even Han Liusu and Han Chuyue were a hidden danger. That was why Luo Zheng had to disguise himself and avoid meeting them.
Now, facing quasi-sage Aunt Hanjiu, Luo Zheng couldn't reveal his secrets. But facing such an expert, Luo Zheng felt even more pressure.
"I was taught by an expert when I was young and had a great opportunity. That's why I became a god …"
Before Luo Zheng could finish his fabricated story, Aunt Hanjiu lightly waved her hand. A formless willpower spread out and Luo Zheng couldn't continue.
"Little fellow, your strength isn't bad. Your ability to fabricate lies is also very good. I'm afraid you've deceived many people?" Aunt Hanjiu's eyebrows curved like the moon. She stared at Luo Zheng and sneered.
Although he was exposed, Luo Zheng's expression didn't change. He calmly replied, "Everyone has their own secrets. Some secrets, if spoken out, are like a person who lost their soul. They can't live. I hope master can understand."
"Look at how nervous you are … I didn't say I couldn't understand." Aunt Hanjiu smiled slightly.
If she really wanted to investigate Luo Zheng's secrets, it wouldn't be difficult. But as a grand quasi-sage, Aunt Hanjiu also agreed with Luo Zheng's words. Everyone has their own secrets. If she forcibly peeled open other people's secrets, there would always be some unnecessary consequences.
But what Aunt Hanjiu wanted wasn't these secrets. She continued to say, "But if you want to keep your secrets, you still need to do something to prove your own worth."
Aunt Hanjiu wouldn't offend Han Tianxiao for some Proof of Divinity martial artist for no reason. Since she forcibly took away Luo Zheng under pressure, she must have some ulterior motive.

Chapter 1844
In Luo Zheng's mind, the secondary sage was an omnipotent existence. What did he need to ask of him?
But looking at Aunt Hanjiu's expression, it didn't seem fake. Luo Zheng was naturally very curious.
Aunt Hanjiu said indifferently, "Last time you mobilized my Purple Soul Palace disciples. Their rankings in the Arena of Gods increased significantly, but … it's still far from enough!"
The Purple Soul Palace was Aunt Hanjiu's blood and sweat. Unless she had no other choice, how could she bear to let them go?
Everyone had their own obsessions, and secondary saints were no exception.
"Far from enough? What does that have to do with me …" Luo Zheng asked.
The Purple Soul Palace had more than thirty thousand people. Their total points couldn't increase. It wasn't something that Luo Zheng alone could make up for.
Recently, Luo Zheng had indeed earned a lot of points. But compared to the entire Purple Soul Palace, it was just a drop in the ocean.
"I want you to earn even more points." Aunt Hanjiu stared at Luo Zheng and smiled.
"How much is' even more '?" Luo Zheng was speechless. He didn't think that he had guessed correctly. The secondary sage really wanted him to reverse the situation.
"About 1.2 million points. This way, on average, every Purple Soul Palace disciple will gain forty points. If it's really not enough, then I can only reluctantly dismiss the bottom ten percent of disciples." Aunt Hanjiu roughly calculated. Her gentle face revealed a charming smile.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng stood there choking for a while. Then he said in a daze, "1.2 million points …"
He didn't expect Aunt Hanjiu to say indifferently, "Is it very difficult? Back then, I earned three million points in the Arena of Gods! "
"Back then, what was Master's cultivation level …" Luo Zheng asked.
"Perfection."
"…"
Luo Zheng finally couldn't help but roll his eyes at this secondary sage.
Those Perfection points were truly frightening. The points recorded on the Jade Wall of the Gods were extremely exaggerated. Almost all of them were above a million!
It could be said that the few Perfect True Gods in some families directly determined the ranking of a family!
However, there was a limit to the number of Great Perfection deities in each family. Moreover, the number of battles between Great Perfection deities was different from that of ordinary True Gods who had three battles every month. When these Great Perfection deities challenged each other to a battle, it would usually attract a huge amount of attention. Sometimes, they might not even fight once in a few years.
There were very few variables in the experts at the top level. The various wealthy families could only target the True Gods at the bottom level. It was more practical and stable to increase the overall points. After all, some Great Perfection experts could lose hundreds of thousands or even millions of points in a battle.
"Defeating a Proof of Divinity martial artist, including the rewards, is only a few hundred points. Wouldn't it be a pipe dream for me to earn so many points?" Luo Zheng was now a bit more relaxed when facing Aunt Hanjiu. Luo Zheng found that she was quite easy to get along with.
Luo Zheng could win hundreds of points in a battle, and it was based on the rewards for consecutive victories. If not for the rewards for consecutive victories, he could only accumulate three points by winning a battle.
Once he lost a battle, his points would be greatly reduced. Moreover, even if he won continuously, he would not be able to reach the 1.2 million points that Aunt Hanjiu had mentioned. Aunt Hanjiu was asking ants to move a mountain. It was not practical at all.
However, Aunt Hanjiu continued, "You don't know the rules of the Arena of Gods …"
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders and simply sat on the steps below to listen attentively.
"At the current stage, after your consecutive victories, your points have long surpassed the number one Proof of Divinity martial artist. Actually … you can apply for a Lower True God to fight against those of a higher level," Aunt Hanjiu said.
The rules of the Jade Wall of the Gods were designed to be more complicated. Although Luo Zheng had a preliminary understanding, his understanding came mostly from Grandma Yue Ying. Naturally, his understanding was not thorough.
"Fight against those of a higher level?" Luo Zheng asked, "You want me to fight against those of a higher level?"
Aunt Hanjiu smiled slightly. "Yes, not only do you need to challenge those of a higher level, but you also need to maintain a winning streak!"
It was difficult to earn points in the Arena of Gods, but the rewards for consecutive victories were considerable. Just like a rolling snowball, the more consecutive victories one had, the more points one would have …
Luo Zheng could almost steadily earn a considerable amount of points in the Proof of Divinity martial artist category. However, if he fought against those of a higher level, the additional rewards would not be too excessive.
However, "Fight against those of a higher level" was different!
For a Proof of Divinity martial artist to fight against a Lower True God, the most basic reward for one battle was almost 100 points.
Because it was almost impossible for a Proof of Divinity martial artist to defeat a Lower True God, the rewards for consecutive victories were even more exaggerated. If he won two consecutive battles, the additional reward would be almost 1,000 points. If he won three consecutive battles, the additional reward would be 2,000 points. If he accumulated like this, it would be a rather frightening number …
However, if a Proof of Divinity martial artist wanted to continuously win against a Lower True God, it was also an extremely difficult task.
Even a Secondary Sage like Aunt Hanjiu had challenged a Lower True God in the past as a Proof of Divinity martial artist. However, after winning four consecutive battles, she was defeated by her opponent.
In the records of the Divine Realm, there were a few Sages who had achieved astonishing consecutive victories.
Among them, the Saint Emperor Han Jia had once achieved 16 consecutive victories. As a Proof of Divinity martial artist, he had accumulated more than 100,000 points in one go. At that time, many of the rich and powerful families had been fiercely shocked.
However, the Saint Emperor Han Jia's experience was not particularly exaggerated. There had once been a genius who had achieved 27 consecutive victories, accumulating 390,000 points in one go. It had to be known that even if a peak Upper True God fought bitterly in the Arena of Gods, they would only be able to accumulate 200,000 points. This was the power of consecutive victories.
However, if one wanted to achieve such a high number of consecutive victories, the difficulty could be imagined. As one's points increased, the True Gods one faced would become stronger and stronger. Just relying on the cultivation of a Proof of Divinity martial artist to continuously win consecutive victories was harder than ascending to the heavens.
"You defeated Liu Cheng Tian and killed Xie Jiao. They were both outstanding Lower True Gods. With your strength, I believe it is possible …" Aunt Hanjiu smiled.
So that's how it was …
Luo Zheng's face revealed an expression of sudden realization.
Combined with the additional rewards of consecutive victories, one could reach a high number of points.
Points were also very important to Luo Zheng. He also wanted to break through to the True God realm! He was determined to obtain the reward given by Han Jia. He needed the mud beneath the prize. More importantly, he needed enough points to go to Bu Zhou Spiritual Mountain. Now that Aunt Hanjiu was protecting him, he also needed to prove his worth to Aunt Hanjiu and Han Jia.
"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Give me your jade pendant." Aunt Hanjiu suddenly stretched out her hand towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng took out his jade pendant and handed it to Aunt Hanjiu. Then, Aunt Hanjiu's slender fingers lightly stroked the jade pendant. A faint green light flashed, and she returned it to Luo Zheng. "This way, in the Arena of Gods, your opponents will not be able to find out your name. It is the same as being anonymous."
"You can do that?" Luo Zheng took back his jade pendant. He was also curious.
"It is very normal. Some disciples of the Great Families do not want to reveal themselves. However, anonymity is only for opponents who challenge you. Once you are defeated, your name will once again appear on the leaderboard." Aunt Hanjiu continued, "Also … during this period, you can enter the Arena of Gods from here."
After she finished speaking, Aunt Hanjiu lightly clapped her hands.
The hall started to tremble slightly, and a Pantheon Jade Annulus that was thirty feet in size slowly rose from the ground. This Pantheon Jade Annulus was very small compared to an ordinary Jade Annulus, but it was enough to allow three people to enter the Arena of the Gods.
After she was done, Aunt Hanjiu actually looked a little tired. She yawned and said, "You can enter the Arena of the Gods anytime you want. I've already applied for a cross-level battle for you. I'm going to rest first …"
After she finished speaking, bits of purple mist appeared around Aunt Hanjiu's body and surrounded her. When the purple mist dissipated, Aunt Hanjiu had already disappeared.
"The Secondary Sage is actually sleepy. This acting is too exaggerated." Luo Zheng curled his lips as he thought to himself.
Then, he walked towards the jade pendant and inserted his jade pendant into it …
"Buzz —"

Chapter 1845
As a soft ray of light from the Divine Jade Wall enveloped Luo Zheng, he felt that everything around him started to disappear, and he instantly entered the Divine Jade Wall.
But this time, he did not enter the battlefield directly. Instead, he stepped into an empty room.
"Eh, how could this be?" Luo Zheng's face was full of confusion.
According to his previous experience, Luo Zheng would enter a spacious battlefield directly after entering the Divine Jade Wall, and then his opponent would appear. What was this empty room all about?
He wandered around the room, and on one side of the wall of the room, there were some strange divine patterns. Following the patterns, he found a small door that was about the height of a person.
The moment he pushed open the small door, a ray of light shone in from the outside, and at the same time, a clamor rushed into Luo Zheng's ears!
"So noisy …"
Outside the small door, there was a huge ring-shaped space. In this ring-shaped space, there were many small doors connected together, spiraling upwards, and forming a staircase of "doors".
In this space, there were countless Lower True Gods. These Lower True Gods were in groups of three to five. Some of them were shouting loudly, and some of them were whispering to each other. Because there were too many of them, the space was chaotic.
"Where the hell is this …" Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was dumbfounded.
"Eh, a Proof of Divinity martial artist has come in." On top of a small door on one side of Luo Zheng, a young man wearing luxurious clothes and a jasper crown on his head looked at Luo Zheng in surprise.
The young man looked at Luo Zheng, and his expression became even stranger. He shouted, "Wow, he's anonymous!"
The young man's strange shout immediately attracted the attention of other Lower True Gods. Many Lower True Gods stared at Luo Zheng strangely. With the help of Aunt Han Jiu, Luo Zheng not only hid his name, but also his appearance. When others saw Luo Zheng's face, it was a blur, and they could not see what he looked like.
"He's really a Divine Warrior …"
"A Divine Warrior intends to participate in the cross-level battle?"
"Which family did they nurture for them to be so confident?"
In fact, it was not rare to see cross-level battles in the Arena of Gods. However, it was usually a lower-level true god challenging a middle-level true god, a middle-level true god challenging an upper-level true god, or an upper-level true god challenging a perfection-level true god.
Among them, the lower true gods participated in the leapfrog battles the most. This was because the difference in strength between a lower true god and a middle true god was not particularly large. It was only the difference in cultivation level.
It was much more difficult for a middle True God to challenge an upper True God because a middle True God could not fly, but an upper True God could. In battle, an upper True God had a great advantage.
It was the same for an upper True God challenging a Great Perfection. Although an upper True God could fly, they could not travel through space. A Great Perfection could travel through space as they wished. Hence, it was rather difficult for an upper True God to challenge a Great Perfection.
However, although the above situations were not common, they were not particularly rare.
However, as a Divine Warrior did not have a godhead, it was rather difficult for him to challenge a lower-level true god. Hence, there were very few Divine Warriors who participated in the cross-level battle. Among the many aristocratic families, only those extremely talented geniuses from aristocratic families would choose to do the cross-level battle. Even so, these geniuses from aristocratic families would not perform too well against a lower-level true god.
Hence, Luo Zheng attracted the attention of many lower-level true gods the moment he entered this place.
Luo Zheng was used to such attention. He asked very calmly, "Where is this place?"
The youth smiled slightly. "This is your first time entering the Hall of Gods, right? Divine Warriors are not qualified to enter this place … "
The Divine Jade Wall was considered a very special place on the floating island. It contained independent spaces of various sizes. These spaces were almost endless, and there were even some hidden spaces that were unknown to others.
Divine Warriors were the most basic warriors on the floating island. They were not qualified to enter the Hall of Gods and could only passively look for their opponents.
However, it was different for lower-level true gods. Every lower-level true god had their own independent room. They could randomly look for their opponents, and they could also choose to challenge a certain true god!
Luo Zheng saw that these rooms were spiraling all the way to the top. The height of these rooms represented the ranking of the lower-level true god. As the ranking rose, the position of the room would also change. Hence, the higher the position of the room, the stronger the strength of the true god.
After hearing the youth's words, Luo Zheng came to a realization. These things might be common to the people on the floating island. Aunt Han Jiu naturally would not explain it to Luo Zheng in detail. Hence, when Luo Zheng entered the Hall of Gods, he was a little dazed.
"I also just entered the Hall of Gods not long ago. I just lost a match." The youth laughed mischievously and asked in a negotiating tone, "How about I challenge you?" After he finished speaking, he coughed twice as if to hide his embarrassment.
"Don't listen to him! Come, challenge me! "
"I just became a true god less than three months ago. My godhead has just formed, and my foundation is not stable. I am the most suitable person to be your first opponent!"
"…"
All of a sudden, Luo Zheng became a delicious piece of cake in the eyes of these lower-level true gods …
This was also very easy to understand. Regardless of whether Luo Zheng was a true genius from a rich and powerful family, he was still a Proof of Divinity expert. In history, there were indeed some Proof of Divinity experts who had created miracles. There were even Proof of Divinity experts who had won all the way to the top. However, it was hard to find even one of them in a few divine eras. They were simply as rare as phoenix feathers and qilin horns. The majority of Proof of Divinity experts had outstanding strength, but when faced with a lower-level true god, they still suffered a crushing defeat. Without a godhead, their strength was too limited.
These people treated Luo Zheng as a soft persimmon. Of course, they hoped to earn points from Luo Zheng.
After feeling the "enthusiasm" of these lower-level true gods, Luo Zheng smiled slightly and said to the youth, "In that case, I accept your challenge. However, you have to tell me how to accept your challenge." Luo Zheng was not at all familiar with the rules of the Hall of Gods.
Hearing that Luo Zheng had accepted his challenge, the youth looked excited. "I'll teach you. Go back to your room and activate the divine patterns on the side walls …"
Luo Zheng nodded and returned to his room. The youth also returned to his room happily.
As for the other lower-level true gods present, their faces were full of disappointment.
"Alas, Qin Hua took advantage of him!"
"It doesn't matter. Since this Proof of Divinity expert participated in the cross-level battle, he can't give up after one battle, right? We still have a chance! "
"That's right. After Qin Hua's challenge is over, we'll challenge that guy again."
These lower-level true gods all thought that Luo Zheng was easy to bully. Naturally, they were unwilling to give up this opportunity. However, after a few words of discussion, they suddenly fell silent. They looked at each other with vigilance on their faces. This anonymous Proof of Divinity expert was a very popular prey. Among them, they were competitors …
According to the youth's instructions, Luo Zheng quickly familiarized himself with the magical effects of the divine patterns on the walls. He only needed to follow the serial number of the room to challenge anyone in the Hall of Gods. He could also choose his own opponent at will.
Just as he was about to touch it, one of the Divine Imprints suddenly flickered with urgency. The instant Luo Zheng touched this Divine Imprint, he instantly understood the information contained within. Earlier, that young man had already issued a challenge to him, and at the same time, Luo Zheng also obtained some basic information about him.
Qin Clan, Qin Hua, had 193 points, and was ranked after 1.97 million …
In Luo Zheng's eyes, this little amount of points was very shabby. However, Qin Hua had just stepped into the Hall of Gods and had lost a battle. This little amount of points was considered normal.
After Luo Zheng accepted Qin Hua's challenge, the scenery around him changed again. He finally stepped into the Arena of Gods.
This lower-level true god named Qin Hua stood not far away. He was very polite. "Although it's unfair for me to challenge you with my cultivation, since you chose to participate in the cross-level battle, I presume you've prepared well?"
Luo Zheng stretched his limbs and smiled at Qin Hua. "Of course."
"Then I won't be polite." Qin Hua cupped his hands at Luo Zheng. Every aristocratic family valued points, especially as the day of the floating island ranking was approaching. Even Qin Hua, as a descendant of the Qin family, was under a lot of pressure. He was eager to win a battle to prove his strength.

Chapter 1846
After Qin Hua's power bloomed from his body, it turned into a bright white light that danced around his right hand.
Finally, that bright white light condensed into a milky-white blade …
"Not many people cultivate the Destruction Divine Dao. My personality is not suitable for it, but it seems that I have a little talent for it," Qin Hua explained modestly.
Hearing this explanation, Luo Zheng was speechless.
This Qin Family disciple was interesting. He was so particular about fighting an enemy.
Seeing Qin Hua dilly-dallying, Luo Zheng wanted to attack him without warning, but he restrained himself.
Luo Zheng's goal was not to win one or two matches by opportunistic means. His goal was the terrifying 1.2 million points. It was impossible to complete the mission by relying on opportunistic means!
In fact, Luo Zheng felt that this mission was not realistic at all.
He had indeed killed Xie Jiao, who was ranked 40th, but that was because he had relied on the element of strength to catch his opponent off guard. If Xie Jiao had used his full strength, Luo Zheng's chances of winning were not high …
Facing this Qin Hua, Luo Zheng still had a 70-80% chance of winning. However, after obtaining consecutive victories in the Arena of Gods, Luo Zheng's points and ranking would rapidly rise. The opponents he would face would also become stronger and stronger. If those peak upper True Gods did not underestimate their opponents, it would be difficult for Luo Zheng to have a chance!
More importantly, Luo Zheng could not use the Emotion Severing Divine Dao in the Arena of Gods. He could not use his strongest killing move. How could he fight against those upper true gods?
However, since Aunt Hanjiu had said so, perhaps she had other preparations. At this stage, Luo Zheng had to adapt to fighting against lower true gods and try to win in a row. As for what would happen after that, he would have to take it one step at a time.
Luo Zheng waited patiently for Qin Hua to prepare …
"Wu wu wu …"
Instead of saying that Qin Hua condensed a blade in his hand, it was better to say that it was a pure blade of light. That blade of light was a part of his hand. When the blade of light was completely condensed, small blade of light constantly escaped, making the blade of light look very illusory.
Luo Zheng smelled a strong destructive power from the saber light.
Luo Zheng thought to himself, "The Divine Path driven by the Godhead has indeed reached a whole new level. Qin Hua is merely at the bottom of the lower True Gods, but his strength still cannot be underestimated …"
"I'm ready." Qin Hua smiled at Luo Zheng. "How about you? Why not use weapons? Take out your weapon! "
Qin Hua felt that it was unfair for him, a lower True God, to challenge Luo Zheng. Now that he had condensed the Blade of Destruction from the Divine Way of Destruction, this anonymous person had not used a weapon. This made him feel even more apologetic. That was why he had opened his mouth to urge Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng curled his lips. There was a big difference in the personalities of these rich and powerful people. Those rich playboys were all ridiculously overbearing, but this person was an exception.
The destructive power in the blade of light in Qin Hua's hand was very strong, but in Luo Zheng's eyes, it was not particularly difficult to deal with. He did not need to take out the Boundless Thousand Heavy Sword. However, since Qin Hua was so polite, he could not provoke him by saying, "You are not qualified for me to take out my weapon," right?
"No need. Let's do it." Luo Zheng smiled and did not dawdle. He took a light step forward, and the tip of his right foot suddenly exploded with an unparalleled power …
"Dong!"
With a muffled sound, Luo Zheng stepped on the ground of the Arena of Gods, creating a small hole. Immediately after, Luo Zheng rushed toward Qin Hua at an extremely fast speed.
"Okay …"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Qin Hua actually replied with a single word, "Okay." Then, when he saw the small hole in the ground, he was stunned. Although the ground of the Arena of Gods was illusory, it was still very hard. It was difficult for ordinary strength to destroy it. This guy could shatter the ground with a light step, and even create a sizable hole.
How could this guy … Oh no!
Just as he thought of this, Luo Zheng rushed toward him at an extremely fast speed.
Qin Hua's abilities were not considered weak. After fully experiencing the Arena of Gods, he could also slowly climb up to the middle level of a Lower True God. However, he had not explored the Forbidden Land and did not have the awareness of experiencing life and death situations.
Suddenly seeing Luo Zheng rush toward him at a speed far beyond his expectations, he could not help but feel somewhat flustered!
"Destruction Slash!"
In a moment of desperation, Qin Hua's right hand opened and closed, and the blade of light danced along with it …
When the Destruction Divine Path was cultivated to the extreme, its pure power was quite strong. However, Qin Hua himself had just stepped into the Divine Path not long ago, and Luo Zheng had Tong Xinyi to rely on, so he was not too afraid!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
Luo Zheng's figure fluttered and easily dodged the blade of light in Qin Hua's right hand. As for the fine blade of light that escaped from the blade of light, Luo Zheng did not care about it!
When the fine blade of light came into contact with Luo Zheng, it exploded with a great amount of power. However, it could not break through Luo Zheng's Tong Xinyi and only made creaking sounds when it collided with his Tong Xinyi.
"You …"
Qin Hua's face once again revealed a look of surprise.
In fact, Qin Hua could scatter those fine blade of light. As long as he used the Destruction Divine Path, these fine blade of light would form a natural barrier that would protect him. Anyone who wanted to get close to him would have to break through these fine blade of light. Although these blade of light were small, even an ordinary lower True God would find it difficult to resist. If they were slightly insufficient, they would be able to cut countless winding wounds on the opponent's body.
How could he have imagined that Luo Zheng would be so barbaric as to use his body to forcibly shatter the blade of light?
Seeing this, Qin Hua immediately became anxious. The blade of light in his hand danced even more violently, like a crescent moon that constantly danced around him. The fine blade of light danced in all directions like countless pieces of paper!
Seeing the flustered Qin Hua, Luo Zheng's heart was as calm as still water.
He only needed to close in and easily end this battle. However, the Destruction Divine Path …
Luo Zheng did not know what would happen when the Infinite Ruler accumulated enough Divine Path. However, his father must have placed the Infinite Ruler in his mind for a reason. Now that Luo Zheng was confident that he could collect the Dao Connotation, he naturally would not let go of such an opportunity.
However, there was one thing that made Luo Zheng very vexed when using the Infinite Ruler. Normally, the ruler would not have any reaction. He could not activate the Infinite Ruler to allow him to see through his opponent's Dao Connotation …
Every time he wanted to activate the Infinite Ruler, he had to take the risk of using his head to guide the Dao Connotation into his mind. Only then would the Infinite Ruler work. It would connect to Luo Zheng's eyes and give his eyes a golden ring that could see through all Dao Connotations!
This ruler should not be so troublesome. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng had not yet grasped the essentials.
Fortunately, Qin Hua's ability to adapt to the situation was not strong. He was already extremely surprised by Luo Zheng's speed. Now that he saw Luo Zheng stretching his neck and stretching his head to drill around him, he did not know what he was doing. He did not know what to think …
"Destruction Divine Path!"
With no other choice, Qin Hua resorted to using his trump card.
This "Destruction Divine Path" was a move that he had comprehended from the Divine Path of Destruction. He was not familiar with it and would not use it against his opponent easily. In the last battle, he had not used it when he was defeated by his opponent. However, he could not bear to use it now.
"Hiss, hiss, hiss, hiss, hiss …"
Countless crescent-shaped Blades of Destruction began to condense. At the same time, they flew around aimlessly. Within three hundred feet of Qin Hua, these Blades of Destruction collided with each other and strangled each other. It was enough to kill all living creatures around him.
"Smoke of Eight Twists!"
Seeing this change, Luo Zheng calmly displayed his Body Movement.
Although those Blades of Annihilation were extremely sharp and contained an extremely strong destructive power, they were not fast. They just slowly floated around in all directions.
After Luo Zheng executed the Eight Zigzags Flying Smoke, he turned into a piece of paper, fluttering in the wind along with the Blades of Annihilation. At the same time, Luo Zheng was constantly absorbing Qin Hua's Dao Connotation!
"You, what on earth are you doing?" Seeing Luo Zheng surrounding him without attacking, Qin Hua finally could not help but ask. If it were someone else, they would have launched an even fiercer attack. However, he directly asked Luo Zheng a question.
Luo Zheng did not answer Qin Hua's question …
The next moment, he suddenly opened his eyes. The edges of his pupils were still plated with two golden rings, which were continuously breaking down Qin Hua's Dao Connotation.
Not long after …

Chapter 1847
Because Luo Zheng was anonymous, his face was always blurred. Naturally, Qin Hua couldn't see the strange look in Luo Zheng's eyes.
Now that Luo Zheng had absorbed the Destruction Divine Dao into the Infinite Ruler, he had pulled away from Qin Hua.
After pausing for about two breaths, Luo Zheng waved his hand lightly …
A bright white light slowly flew out from his hand, and as it continued to condense, it transformed into a broad blade of light!
The Divine Dao was most powerful when activated by "force," which was a match for the Godhead.
Although Luo Zheng didn't have a Godhead, the Dao of the Destruction Divine Dao was purer and more dazzling than Qin Hua's own blade of light!
"You, you, you …" Qin Hua was stunned when he saw the broad blade of light in Luo Zheng's hand. "You also cultivate the Destruction Divine Dao?"
The Destruction Divine Dao wasn't the Five Elements Divine Dao.
There was almost no threshold to cultivating the Five Elements Divine Dao. Most people with a physique could cultivate it.
However, there were many extremely powerful side Divine Daos in the Realm of the Gods, and the threshold to cultivate them wasn't small. When Qin Hua said that he had a little talent for the Destruction Divine Dao, he was actually being modest. The Destruction Divine Dao was quite powerful when cultivated to the extreme, but there weren't many people suitable to cultivate it.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "The Destruction Divine Dao is about destroying everything. Among the thousands of divine weapons, only the domineering aura of the blade matches it. Condensing the blade of light is indeed the right choice."
When Qin Hua saw Luo Zheng execute the Annihilation Slash of the Annihilation Divine Dao, he was already shocked to the core. He had a feeling that Luo Zheng had just learned the Annihilation Divine Dao, and it was even possible that Luo Zheng had learned the Annihilation Divine Dao by himself!
But he didn't dare to believe this illusion.
He had seen many geniuses, including some of the elders of the Qin Clan, such as Qin Murong, Qin Lan, and the others. They were all top geniuses of the aristocratic clans. These top geniuses in the Qin Clan were considered very outstanding among all the floating islands, but they weren't this abnormal. Who could comprehend the other party's Divine Dao while fighting?
Even if he told others about this, no one would believe him …
In the end, after hearing Luo Zheng's words, his "misconception" was finally verified. His misconception was not wrong. Luo Zheng had indeed just grasped the Destruction Divine Dao …
Even though Qin Hua had witnessed it with his own eyes, he still found it hard to believe.
"You, you must have cultivated the Divine Dao of Destruction before and encountered a bottleneck that you couldn't surpass. You were so proud of yourself when you saw me display it, right?" Qin Hua stared at Luo Zheng and continued to ask.
Luo Zheng slightly moved his finger, causing the crescent-moon-like saber light to rotate along Luo Zheng's elbow. At the same time, a faint smile was heard from his blurred face, "Sorry, I … can't answer your question. Take this!"
After saying that, another small hole appeared under Luo Zheng's feet …
Boom!
As the ground shook, Luo Zheng's figure sped forward. The saber light in his hand turned into a bright silver trajectory in the air, and the saber edge chopped toward Qin Hua's front!
Looking at Luo Zheng's surging momentum, Qin Hua swallowed his saliva. He had no choice but to urge the power in his body. The saber light in his hand rose sharply again, and he braced himself to receive the attack …
He felt a burst of sorrow in his heart. It was not easy for him to attain the Divine Dao. After stepping into the Arena of Gods, he had lost the first battle. After meeting Luo Zheng, he originally thought that he had picked up a big bargain. He even felt a little embarrassed. After all, it was not "honorable" for a True God to challenge a Divine Dao expert. However, for the points, for the victory, he had no choice but to risk it all.
He had never thought that the Divine Dao expert he had encountered would be a complete monster!
Whoosh!
Two saber lights flashing with white light crisscrossed with each other, producing an ear-piercing sound.
Along with the sound, countless fine saber lights spread out. These fine saber lights were not only Qin Hua's, but also Luo Zheng's!
These fine saber lights were like flying blades in the air, sputtering in all directions. There was almost no space to dodge.
When the saber lights sputtered on Luo Zheng's body, only a "swish" sound could be heard. If they pierced Luo Zheng's skin, they would leave fine scratches on his skin. If they cut into his concentric clothing, they would leave nothing behind …
Luo Zheng could use his body to resist these fine saber lights, but Qin Hua could not. When the saber lights cut into Qin Hua's body, there was almost no resistance. They constantly circled and rolled inside his body. When they touched his muscles and meridians, they directly cut them. Even the solid True God jade bones were also cut apart!
Puff puff puff puff …
With just one slash, countless tiny saber lights drilled into Qin Hua's body, cutting off his life force. His body stood in place, motionless ….
