Luo Zheng did not use his saber again. He slowly withdrew the saber lights and looked at Qin Hua indifferently.
The first battle was simpler than he had imagined, and the harvest was not small. It had already exceeded his expectations.
Plop …
After a while, Qin Hua's body fell forward to the ground, and his body began to slowly dissipate. At the same time, the entire illusory world spread out in all directions like ripples in water, slowly disappearing from Luo Zheng's eyes. The two returned to their rooms.
On the side of the room, Luo Zheng's information appeared. In the information, Luo Zheng's name was still blurry, but his points and victories could still be seen.
"Defeating Qin Hua only gave me 75 points," Luo Zheng murmured as he looked at the changes in his points.
Luo Zheng's consecutive victories in the Proof of Divinity Battle had given him an additional reward of more than 200 points. 75 points in this battle was really too little.
However, according to the rules of points, Luo Zheng could understand that his consecutive victories in the Proof of Divinity Battle had returned to zero, which meant that he had to start counting his victories all over again. He did not have so many additional points. Moreover, in the Proof of Divinity Battle, if one did not count the consecutive victories, the normal points for winning a battle were only 15 points.
Luo Zheng's reward of 75 points in the first battle was already five times that of an ordinary Lower True God. This was because Luo Zheng had participated in the Proof of Divinity Battle. Only when a Proof of Divinity Battle defeated a Lower True God would there be such an additional reward.
The Proof of Divinity Battle plus the reward for consecutive victories in the Proof of Divinity Battle was double the additional points. Therefore, it was possible to reach the terrifyingly high number of points. This was also Aunt Hanjiu's idea.
Outside the Hall of Gods, the Lower True Gods were still waiting in front of Luo Zheng's door.
"What the hell is Qin Hua doing? Why does he need so long to deal with a Proof of Divinity Battle?"
"Maybe that anonymous person's strength is not bad. A Proof of Divinity Battle who dares to participate in the Proof of Divinity Battle should have some level of skill."
"Most of the Proof of Divinity Battle who participate in the Proof of Divinity Battle are self-righteous. In the end, they can often be taught a lesson, hehe …"
In history, there were too few Proof of Divinity Battle who could crush a Lower True God. These Lower True Gods found it difficult to look at Luo Zheng, unless Luo Zheng could crush them with his strength!
Just as they were discussing, the door of Luo Zheng's room suddenly flashed with a blue light, and then the door turned into a blue streamer, quickly moving along the side of the Hall of Gods.
"Hey! The door, his door ran away! "
"The anonymous person's ranking has risen!"
"He actually defeated Qin Hua …"
The lower-level True Gods who had been bitterly waiting for Luo Zheng looked on helplessly as Luo Zheng's door slid upwards. Each and every one of them had a look of surprise on their faces. They really didn't expect Qin Hua to lose this battle!
The arrangement of the doors in the Hall of Gods represented the ranking.
Luo Zheng had not been ranked so far, so his name would not be displayed on the Jade Wall of Gods, but the position of the door would still change …
After defeating Qin Hua, Luo Zheng's ranking suddenly jumped up by 16 places, so the position of the door also moved up by 16 places.
"Buzz …"
The wandering blue light quickly outlined the shape of a door frame. When the blue light disappeared, Luo Zheng's door reappeared.
At the same time, Qin Hua's door moved down by two places. After all, Qin Hua belonged to the bottom level, and even if he lost, he would not fall too far. It was impossible for him to fall to the Proof of Divinity Battle.
"Kacha …"
While those Lower True Gods were discussing with incredulous expressions, Qin Hua came out of his room, his face full of depression.
"Qin Hua, did you really lose to that anonymous person?"
"Did you throw the match? Or was it an accident? "
"Or that guy really has the ability …"
Seeing that these people were so anxious, Qin Hua, who was originally very depressed, felt a trace of anger in his heart. He was a person who paid attention to fairness. The battle with Luo Zheng was very unfair because Luo Zheng's cultivation was lower than his, and he still lost. One could imagine the impact on Qin Hua.

Chapter 1848
In Qin Hua's opinion, the strength of this anonymous person was already quite strong.
Putting aside the fact that he could instantly comprehend his own Divine Path, in terms of pure strength, he was much stronger than this group of Lower True Gods who had just proven their Divine Path.
He was already in a bad mood after losing to the anonymous person, and this group of guys still surrounded him and made a lot of noise. Even if he had a simple personality, he was happy to see them give points to the anonymous person!
It was just as he had expected ….
This group of True Gods, who were originally a little nervous, suddenly relaxed, and at the same time, they pretended to comfort Qin Hua.
"How could you be so careless?"
"Even if the opponent is weak, you should always be vigilant!"
"You have failed miserably. Learn from this lesson. Hey, hey, wait for me …"
These True Gods said a few perfunctory words to Qin Hua and rushed toward Luo Zheng's door. This was an opportunity that could not be missed. It would not be good if other Lower True Gods beat them to it.
So these True Gods rushed up like a swarm of bees and soon blocked Luo Zheng's door.
The other Lower True Gods saw this group of guys swarming in, and their faces were full of inexplicable expressions. But when Luo Zheng opened the door, they suddenly realized that there was a Divine Warrior who had started a leapfrog battle, and this group of guys came to pick up the scraps. So more Lower True Gods gathered around.
Luo Zheng opened the door and was shocked to see so many Lower True Gods. He glared at them and then asked, "What are you doing?"
"Quick, accept my challenge!"
"My strength is weaker than Qin Hua's. Maybe you can defeat me!"
"Choose me …"
These Lower True Gods were eagerly waiting for Luo Zheng to open his mouth.
Luo Zheng rolled his eyes and suddenly realized that he was in a state of anonymity, and these people could not see his expression.
According to Aunt Han Jiu, as long as he kept winning, it would be easy to get a huge number of points. Then he could just keep picking these people, right?
These guys were at the bottom of the Lower True Gods, or they had just proven their Divine Path. Their divinity was not stable, so naturally, they could not exert their powerful strength. With Luo Zheng's current ability, defeating them was not particularly difficult.
However, on second thought, something didn't seem right.
These guys didn't realize their own strength, so naturally, they kept challenging him. Once they realized that he wasn't a "pig" but a "tiger", how could they still be willing to challenge him or accept his challenge?
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that this kind of face-to-face challenge actually had many restrictions. It was to prevent the same family from 'accumulating points'. This was because the winning streak reward existed for everyone. If everyone could find their own opponent, they could definitely accumulate points like crazy. For example, if there were both True Gods of Han Jia, and they sacrificed one True God of Han Jia to intentionally lose to another True God of Han Jia, then wouldn't the True God of Han Jia's points increase infinitely?
The Arena of Deities had been perfected for so many years. It was impossible for there to be such a huge loophole in the rules.
According to the rules, one could not challenge the same opponent consecutively. At the same time, one could not challenge an opponent whose ranking was more than a hundred places higher or lower than oneself!
As long as one participated in the battle, the ranking would constantly change. Sometimes, after defeating an opponent, one's own door could instantly rise by a few hundred places, or even a few thousand places. Under the constant changes in ranking, it was impossible to cheat like this.
However, no matter how perfect the rules were, it couldn't prevent cheating. Some of the top ranking families and some of the bottom ranking families would secretly come to an agreement and choose to give up points. When the disciples of these two families encountered each other, they would choose to surrender according to the agreement. Outside the Arena of Deities, of course, there would be a lot of compensation. It could be considered spending money to buy points.
At this stage, it was easy to earn points, so naturally, Luo Zheng wouldn't reject it. He calmly pointed at one of them and said, "That's you!"
Luo Zheng pointed at a lower True God with green skin. He didn't know what kind of bloodline this True God had. He looked like a zombie and had a very scary appearance.
This True God was lucky enough to be chosen by Luo Zheng. He was immediately overjoyed and immediately rushed down to his own room. Soon, he issued a challenge to Luo Zheng …
The remaining lower True Gods were still waiting in front of Luo Zheng's door. Although Luo Zheng's room would change position regardless of whether he won or lost, at least they could track Luo Zheng's position immediately!
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense …
A blue light flashed on Luo Zheng's door frame again.
"It's over, it's over. This time, it's my turn, right?"
"Humph, I've also waited for so long. Why should it be your turn?"
"Don't fight, one by one …"
Just as everyone was fighting over it, the blue light once again flew upwards!
The expressions on the faces of the True Gods who were fighting over the blue light suddenly froze.
"He, he won again?"
"A Proof of Divinity martial artist fought against someone of a higher level and won twice in a row …"
"I'm afraid this isn't a fluke!"
"This guy isn't simple!"
These lower True Gods weren't fools either.
Luo Zheng had defeated Qin Hua for the first time. As Qin Hua had said, perhaps he had been too careless and thus lost to Luo Zheng.
However, if the first time was a fluke, would the second time be a fluke as well?
Qin Hua stood not far away. When he saw the expressions on those guys' faces, he secretly felt a little better.
"This anonymous person isn't simple. He might be able to break a record! I have to pass this news to my family … "Although losing two rounds in a row made Qin Hua depressed, he had collected a valuable piece of information.
Thus, Qin Hua returned to his room and exited the Deities Jade Wall. Then, Qin Hua reported this news to his family.
Some of the higher-ups of the Qin family were also interested in this news. There were people in the wealthy families who specialized in analyzing the rankings on the Deities Jade Wall and speculating on future changes. There were even people who could memorize all the names and rankings on the Deities Jade Wall.
Soon, the Qin family sent people to investigate the identity of the "anonymous person." However, they couldn't find any clues!
"That anonymous person was able to fight someone of a higher level. He must be very outstanding among the Proof of Divinity martial artists. Only the top ten Proof of Divinity martial artists are qualified to fight someone of a higher level. However, the top ten Proof of Divinity martial artists haven't fought for a long time!" A higher-up of the Qin family looked at the Deities Jade Wall and said faintly.
"Is it possible that this guy hasn't lost a single round?" Another higher-up of the Qin family analyzed.
"It's possible. If the anonymous person hasn't lost a single round among the Proof of Divinity martial artists, his ranking can't be calculated. Therefore, he also doesn't have a ranking among the lower True Gods …"
"Hehe, I don't know which family this little guy is from. He's actually so radical. He just won two rounds in a row. Just continue to pay attention."
In addition to the Qin family, there were two or three other wealthy families that had their eyes on Luo Zheng. They probably came to the same conclusion as the Qin family …
After Luo Zheng defeated the second lower True God, he received 75 points again. At the same time, he also received the first additional reward, which was also 75 points. The reward for winning consecutive rounds in a higher level was indeed very fast. The reward for winning consecutive rounds in a row almost doubled for the second round!
However, this time, Luo Zheng left the room with a slightly depressed expression. He couldn't help but shake his head. "I took such a big risk, but I didn't succeed in activating the Infinite Ruler. It's a pity that I couldn't absorb this guy's Taoist Connotation. This Infinite Ruler is really not easy to use …"
When Luo Zheng pushed the door open again, the door was still surrounded by the lower True Gods. However, this time, many of the True Gods had a cautious look in their eyes.
It was luck that he could defeat Qin Hua. If he defeated Liu Shu, it wouldn't be as simple as luck. This anonymous person's strength was definitely not ordinary.
"No one … wants to challenge me?" Luo Zheng's voice came from the blurry face again.
The True Gods, who had been constantly scrambling to compete with Luo Zheng, were now silent. They were all thinking about the real strength of Luo Zheng.
After about ten breaths of silence, someone finally couldn't take it anymore. Probably still wanting to take the risk, he said, "I, I challenge you!"
"I'll wait for you!"
Luo Zheng smiled and turned back to his room.
After he closed the door of his room, the True Gods present were at a loss again. If this guy's strength was really ordinary, wouldn't they have missed the opportunity?
However, this time, they didn't hesitate for long. After 30 breaths of time, Luo Zheng's door turned into a blue streamer again and continued to move upward …

Chapter 1849
After entering the Hall of Gods, Luo Zheng won three matches in a row, and his ranking steadily increased by more than a hundred places.
However, his ranking was only reflected in the ranking of the door in the room. As he did not lose any matches, it was still impossible to find him just by looking at the Pantheon Jade Wall.
After he was a hundred places ahead of those lower True Gods, it could be said that they had completely given up on him. There was a limit of a hundred places for initiating a challenge. These lower True Gods were no longer qualified to challenge Luo Zheng.
Moreover, Luo Zheng had already defeated three lower True Gods in a row, demonstrating his strength.
Defeating the first person could be attributed to luck. Defeating the second person could be attributed to the opponent underestimating him. Defeating the third person … the same thing could not happen more than three times. It would be too stupid to treat Luo Zheng as a Divine Warrior.
No one took the initiative to challenge Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng also tried to randomly choose his opponent. The opponent he chose was also calculated according to the ranking. At Luo Zheng's current stage, the opponent's strength would not be too strong.
The fourth opponent was Li Fengxiu, a disciple of the Li family who was ranked seventeenth on the floating island. The Divine Path he had comprehended was the "Great Art of Manipulation." This Great Art of Manipulation could control all things in the world. It was similar to the Puppet Technique that Luo Zheng had come into contact with. It could also be seen as a top technique of Puppet Technique. However, the Great Art of Manipulation was even more domineering. It could use thousands of invisible threads to invade the opponent's mind and control their soul!
This time, Luo Zheng did not stiffen his neck and let those threads enter his mind. Li Fengxiu was doing everything possible to turn him into a puppet. If his soul was really controlled, it would be troublesome. In that case, not only would he have to face defeat, his identity and secrets would be exposed after his soul was invaded. That would be the biggest problem.
The Great Art of Manipulation's ability was indeed very special, but its combat power was not strong. Perhaps it was because Li Fengxiu did not have any powerful puppets. The puppets he summoned could not stop Luo Zheng at all.
After a careful fierce battle, Li Fengxiu was defeated in the end. He unwillingly withdrew from the Arena of Gods. Due to the special nature of the Great Art of Manipulation, Luo Zheng could not absorb this Divine Path.
If he could activate the Infinity Ruler at will, it wouldn't be so troublesome. At that time, he wouldn't have to take the risk of absorbing the opponent's Taoist Connotation. He could see through the opponent's Taoist Connotation completely with the pair of golden eyes. Luo Zheng was very depressed as he thought about this problem!
There was no distinction between day and night in the Hall of the Gods. All the disciples of the floating island were choosing their opponents to battle. The spiral-shaped "doors" would occasionally transform into a blue stream of light to change their position. If they went up, they would naturally win. If they slid down, they would lose.
By the end of the fourth battle, Luo Zheng had already earned over 500 points. Auntie Han Jiu was right, the additional rewards for fighting above one's level were indeed terrifying. If he calculated it like this, then his tenth battle would probably earn him thousands of points in additional rewards!
Of course, as his ranking in the room increased, the opponents he encountered would also become stronger, and the difficulty of obtaining points would also increase.
Luo Zheng didn't continue to challenge new opponents. Instead, he returned to his room and exited the Pantheon Jade Wall.
To Luo Zheng, incorporating the Infinity Ruler into the Divine Path was a very important matter. While earning points in the Arena of Gods, Luo Zheng also hoped that he could fill up the scales on the Infinity Ruler as much as possible. He decided to properly refine the Infinity Ruler.
Unexpectedly, as soon as he exited the Pantheon Jade Wall, he saw Auntie Han Jiu standing behind him. Her arms were folded, and her soft and charming eyes seemed to have a deep meaning as she stared at Luo Zheng.
When he met Auntie Han Jiu's gaze, Luo Zheng was slightly startled. He felt that the atmosphere was a bit strange, so he turned his head and saw Han Liusu in plain clothes. Her slender legs were tightly pressed together, and she stood gracefully at the side of the hall. Her face seemed to have a trace of shyness. He guessed that Auntie Han Jiu had chatted with her about something.
"Our Han family's eldest princess has personally come to see you. Why don't you come and pay your respects?" Auntie Han Jiu said to Luo Zheng in a mocking tone.
Hearing this, Han Liusu's face carried a trace of a smile as she complained, "Auntie Jiu! Don't talk about paying respects? Isn't that a bit too much? "
Auntie Han Jiu was her name. Naturally, it wasn't a matter of seniority. But on this floating island, if seniority was important, Auntie Han Jiu would most likely have to call Han Liusu Grand-aunt … After all, Han Liusu was the Holy Emperor's eldest daughter. Although she was born later than Auntie Han Jiu by who knew how many years, she was much older than Auntie Han Jiu by countless years. As for how much seniority this was, it was impossible to tell. In fact, no one really cared about that.
When the Han family's direct descendants met Auntie Han Jiu, they would also habitually call her "Auntie Jiu."
Luo Zheng followed Auntie Han Jiu's words and really did cup his hands in greeting to Han Liusu. "Purple Soul Palace's Luo Tianxing greets the eldest princess!"
Han Liusu fiercely glared at Luo Zheng. From the bottom of her heart, she hated Luo Zheng calling her so formally. Her extremely beautiful face suddenly revealed a trace of an evil smile. "Liusu doesn't know any Luo Tianxing. Liusu only knows …"
If she continued, she would probably call out Luo Zheng's identity.
Luo Zheng immediately became nervous. He kept winking while changing the topic. "Eldest princess, this Luo has already participated in the Realm Skipping Battle in the Divine Jade Wall. If I meet the eldest princess in the future, I hope that you will show mercy."
Han Liusu resentfully glanced at Luo Zheng. She wouldn't really expose Luo Zheng's identity in front of Auntie Han Jiu. She was afraid that Luo Zheng would really be angry. Instead, she was very cooperative and chatted with him.
Auntie Han Jiu didn't know that Luo Zheng and Han Liusu were old friends in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
When Han Liusu came to visit Luo Zheng, Auntie Han Jiu teased Luo Zheng. She only said that if it wasn't for Han Leng deciding on her marriage, she would also consider Luo Tianxing. She didn't avoid Han Liusu and talked endlessly about how outstanding Luo Zheng was.
Auntie Han Jiu didn't know that if she really exposed Han Liusu's thoughts, Han Liusu's expression would naturally become embarrassed and unnatural.
She originally didn't have any feelings for Leng Lin Yue. After this incident, she was even more disgusted with that kind of self-centered man. How could she be willing to become his God Companion? She had already made a decision in her heart. Her heart had an indomitable courage.
A woman like Han Liusu looked gentle and lovely, but once she made a decision, there was almost no possibility of her repentance!
Today, when Auntie Han Jiu saw Han Liusu personally come to visit, she naturally thought that she had come to thank Luo Zheng. But when she heard Luo Zheng and Han Liusu talking back and forth, some inexplicable words appeared in Auntie Han Jiu's heart.
"A few days ago, thank you for helping my Chuyue out. This is a thank you gift." As Han Liusu spoke, her fingertips flashed and a small brocade box appeared. This brocade box was completely transparent and very exquisite. Inside it was a light green Gold Core.
"Liusu is really generous. A Seven Transformation God Gold Core is a rare item." Auntie Han Jiu smiled when she saw the light green Gold Core.
This Seven Transformation God Gold Core could be swallowed into the dantian to stimulate the body's True Yuan Sea. It could make the sea water more easily vaporize and naturally increase the speed of godhood condensation. However, this kind of pill was usually only for Second or Third Transformation God Gold Core. Very few people would refine a Seven Transformation God Gold Core. A Seven Transformation God Gold Core was just for the sake of condensing godhood. Only a rich and powerful family like the Han Family would refine a Seven Transformation God Gold Core for their direct descendants. Of course, it was also extremely rare.
Even the Extreme Evil Old Man couldn't help but praise the extravagance of these rich and powerful families when he saw the Seven Transformation God Gold Core in his mind.
"Thank you." Luo Zheng respectfully received the brocade box from Han Liusu.
The moment Luo Zheng received the brocade box, Han Liusu's fingers suddenly pinched. She had forgotten that Luo Zheng's physical body was comparable to a grandmist treasure. Luo Zheng didn't have any reaction, but her fingertips were aching!
Although Han Liusu's actions were subtle, how could it escape Auntie Han Jiu's eyes? However, it wasn't appropriate for her to say it in front of the Eldest Princess.
"I wish you the best of luck in attaining the Divine Dao as soon as possible so that you can contribute to my Han Family …"
"Since I've entered the Han Family's floating island, I should do so …"
The two exchanged a few pleasantries in front of Auntie Han Jiu. Han Liusu said her goodbyes and left. Just as she turned around, the fake smile on her face disappeared without a trace. In an instant, it was replaced with an extremely disappointed expression, and even a little angry.
She had come to find Luo Zheng in hopes of having a chance to be alone with Luo Zheng. She hadn't expected Auntie Han Jiu to directly bring Luo Zheng to her palace!
Earlier, she had even hinted that she wanted to talk to Luo Tianxing alone, but Auntie Han Jiu had pretended not to understand. She had no intention of leaving. Other than giving her a 'gift of thanks', she had come for nothing.
After Han Liusu left Auntie Han Jiu's palace, Auntie Han Jiu's face lit up with interest. She stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Luo Tianxing, did you see the Eldest Princess outside the floating island?"

Chapter 1850
Aunt Hanjiu's question seemed nonchalant, but Luo Zheng found it hard to answer.
Luo Zheng wasn't sure if Han Liusu had ever been outside of the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. As the princess of the Han Clan, her every word and action would be watched. If he casually made up a story now, Aunt Hanjiu would see through it!
Noticing Luo Zheng's troubled expression, Aunt Hanjiu said, "Han Liusu should know something, such as your true identity. All these years, Han Liusu hasn't taken a single step out of the floating island. She hasn't even been to the Han Clan's ancestral land. The only time she left was when she entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. If you know her, then you must be from the Forbidden Land of God Refinement!"
Her eyes flickered with a faint purple light. In her eyes, there seemed to be two worlds slowly rotating, deep and full of attraction. Experts with high cultivation always had their own unique and unparalleled charm. "People outside of the floating island don't have the qualifications to enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. They can only be disciples of rich and powerful families or disciples who joined rich and powerful families. You just joined the Han Clan, so you can't be a disciple of a rich and powerful family."
Hearing Aunt Hanjiu's analysis, Luo Zheng's heart was in his throat. It had to be said that the brains of experts were very useful. With such simple information, she was able to analyze so many things!
"It's very likely that you came from a world created by a saint." Aunt Hanjiu stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Most creatures that come from this world must be received by the Jiang Clan. All the names are recorded in the Saint Hall. I remember that not long ago, the 833rd rule of the Governing Slate was activated. The Governing Slate punished illegal immigrants, but the result was nothing. The Saint Hall sent people to investigate, but there was no result …"
Aunt Han Jiu narrowed her charming eyes and stared at Luo Zheng with interest like a cunning fox. The smile on her face became even more meaningful. "Yu Taibai has been very restless recently. I don't like that kid very much. Ever since Luo Xiao's death, he has led the old subordinates of the Luo family to cause trouble everywhere, relying on Buzhou Spirit Mountain. Last time, he almost fought with me. This time, he actually attacked the Jiang family in order to force the Jiang family to withdraw all the envoys. Their goal … should be related to his master, Luo Xiao?"
Luo Zheng stared at Aunt Hanjiu. His face had completely darkened. Although he tried to hide his emotions, his heart was still beating wildly. He didn't know what he was about to face. It was as if he was a skinned lamb, bloody and exposed in front of Aunt Hanjiu. Whether Aunt Hanjiu steamed him or roasted him, it was all up to her.
"You came from the Great Yan World, and it was Luo Xiao's arrangement." Aunt Hanjiu walked slowly towards Luo Zheng. A strong fragrance assailed his nose. Although the body fragrance was strong, it was not tiresome. On the contrary, it was very fascinating. He couldn't help but inhale it again and again.
She raised her head and gently touched Luo Zheng's chin. "From the beginning, I thought it was very strange. It is impossible for such a genius to be born among the divine people. Even among the rich and powerful, it is difficult to find one. You may be the crystallization of that woman, and not an ordinary divine fetus. More importantly, you did not erase your surname, it is still Luo …"
"That woman … what does she mean?" Luo Zheng asked in a dry voice. She said he was the crystallization of that woman, and that she was a divine fetus. She should be referring to his mother! Previously, Luo Zheng had a clue that his mother was still alive. He and Luo Yan were not purely children created by Luo Xiao. Now that he received confirmation from Aunt Hanjiu, he naturally had to ask!
"No need to ask so many questions." Aunt Hanjiu smiled slightly.
Luo Zheng's face was still very gloomy. He never thought that a simple conversation between Han Liusu and Aunt Hanjiu would be able to deduce everything. Moreover, Yu Taibai was planning to fight with her? Then Han Jia, or Aunt Hanjiu, was standing on the opposite side of the Luo family? This was only one possibility, but it was definitely not good news!
"What are you going to do?" Luo Zheng asked again, even dropping the fake "master" title.
Aunt Hanjiu's originally tightly narrowed eyes suddenly widened, and the fox-like expression disappeared in an instant. Her eyes widened, and she became an innocent and flawless young girl again. There was a burst of bell-like laughter. "Don't be nervous, this world is boring enough. At that time, Luo Xiao wanted to make this world more interesting. I was actually very much looking forward to it, but I didn't expect that he would fail …"
"But I know his character. He is the kind of person who will not give up even if he dies … Sure enough, he is probably almost dead now, but he still hasn't given up. At least I see new hope." Aunt Hanjiu said again.
Despite Aunt Hanjiu's words, the sense of crisis in Luo Zheng's heart did not disappear. These people were too smart. Who knew what she was thinking?
So Luo Zheng asked tentatively, "When you say hope … you mean me?"
Aunt Hanjiu shrugged, neither admitting nor denying. She only stared fixedly at Luo Zheng, looking him up and down. There was a strange feeling in her eyes, as if she had some kind of longing for Luo Zheng, but it was as if she wanted to say something, but didn't.
This feeling was rather chaotic. Luo Zheng also couldn't explain clearly what this quasi-sage meant.
The two of them stared blankly at each other for a long time. Then a voice came from outside the palace. "Master, the little princess has come to visit."
The Han family's Sage Emperor only had two daughters, one big and one small, and they were both born in the last few decades. The eldest princess was Han Liusu, and the little princess was naturally Han Chuyue.
Aunt Hanjiu glared at Luo Zheng. "I don't know how much of your father's talent you inherited, but in this aspect you are quite similar to your father …"
Han Chuyue and Han Liusu's relationship was so good. If they came to visit Luo Zheng, the two sisters would definitely come together. Now that they came separately, either there was some kind of gap in the relationship between the two sisters, or there was some kind of taboo between them!
Aunt Hanjiu remembered that Han Chuyue and Han Liusu entered the Forbidden Land of God Refinement at around the same time. Han Chuyue should also know Luo Zheng. For her to secretly avoid her sister like this, other than having feelings for Luo Zheng, what other explanation could there be? She didn't know what the Sage Emperor would think when he found out about this in the future!
Luo Zheng curled his lips. From the entrance of the palace, a woman wearing a blue dress ran in like a wisp of smoke. Her quick-witted eyes spun. After seeing Aunt Hanjiu, Han Chuyue casually called out "Aunt Hanjiu" before jumping in front of Luo Zheng. "Luo … Luo Tianxing!"
She stuck out her tongue. In a moment of carelessness, she almost called out the wrong name.
Aunt Hanjiu said with a strange smile, "I'm really curious, what is your real name …"
Han Chuyue's little face reddened. She touched the top of her head, revealing a trace of apology. She pretended not to know and said, "Real name? What real name? He's called Luo Tianxing! "She naturally didn't know that under her cover, it would only make people more suspicious …
Aunt Hanjiu naturally wouldn't expose Han Chuyue in front of her, so she very cooperatively nodded.
"Luo Tianxing, thank you for helping me and Bi Luo a few days ago! I'll invite you to my house some other day … "Han Chuyue was much more daring than Han Liusu. As she spoke, she secretly winked at Luo Zheng, indicating that she had many things to secretly say to him.
However, Luo Zheng's face was wooden. After hearing Aunt Hanjiu's words, Luo Zheng's heart was in a mess. How could he still be in the mood to play with this little girl?
Unfortunately, Han Chuyue didn't know how to read people's expressions. She took the initiative to shake Aunt Hanjiu's hand and said coquettishly, "I have something to say to Luo Tianxing, Aunt Hanjiu, can you leave for a moment?"
In the Han family, there were no more than four people who dared to speak to Aunt Hanjiu like this.
Aunt Hanjiu was very fond of the Saint Emperor's two daughters, but this time Aunt Hanjiu refused, "Luo Tianxing still has important matters to attend to, I'm afraid he can't come today!"
Hearing this, Han Chuyue showed a disappointed expression, but she quickly adjusted. In any case, Luo Zheng had already arrived at the Luo family's floating island. There would be opportunities in the future. She then said, "That's right, I have a thank you gift here!"
Saying this, she only saw her lightly clapping her hands …
Like magic, she pulled out a huge table from her Sumeru Space. With a wave of her hand, all kinds of cups and bowls appeared. They were filled with all kinds of expensive dishes, some of which even Aunt Han Jiu had never seen before.
After Han Chuyue left, Aunt Hanjiu stared at the table and smiled, "I've troubled this little girl. Some of the dishes are very expensive, to be able to collect them all is not easy …"
Before she finished speaking, a voice came from the door, "Master, the Patriarch's daughter Han Bi Luo requests an audience …"

Chapter 1851
Han Liusu, Han Chuyue, and Han Biluo were the three golden flowers of this generation of the Han Family.
Among them, Han Liusu and Han Chuyue were the daughters of the Holy Emperor, while Han Biluo was the daughter of the Patriarch. On the Han Family floating island, the three of them received the most attention of the younger generation.
If the three of them had come together to visit Luo Zheng, then it would have been fine. After all, Luo Zheng had helped them that day. But the three of them had come alone, which made people feel very strange.
Han Biluo was younger than Han Chuyue by a year, but she was more scheming than Han Chuyue. As soon as she came in, she bowed to Aunt Jiu, "Han Biluo greets Aunt Jiu. I haven't seen Aunt Jiu in a long time, Biluo missed you so much, so I came today. Aunt Jiu has become more beautiful again!"
Aunt Jiu faintly smiled and stared at Han Biluo, "Little girl's mouth has always been so sweet, but I see that the one you miss is not Aunt Jiu, and the one you want to visit is also not Aunt Jiu."
Han Biluo's heart skipped a beat, and she calmly said, "Aunt Jiu, what are you talking about …"
"Haha, Aunty Jiu, do you want to guess that because Luo Tianxing helped you out that day, you've naturally prepared a gift to thank him?" Aunty Hanjiu puckered her lips as she spoke with a smile, and she revealed a scheming and astute appearance.
A trace of uneasiness appeared in Han Bi Luo's eyes, but it quickly disappeared. She unhurriedly said, "Han Bi Luo is grateful for Luo Tianxing's assistance that day. It is normal for me to come and thank him, right?"
"En, en, very normal," Aunt Jiu's face was like a blooming rose, smiling, "But you little girls came one by one today, that's a bit strange!"
Han Biluo was slightly surprised, she did not expect that someone had come before, "Han Chuyue also came?"
"Of course, not only Chuyue, Liusu also came," Aunt Jiu continued to smile.
Hearing Aunt Jiu's words, Han Biluo actually had some doubts in her heart …
Han Liusu was the first to guess Luo Zheng's identity. Later, Han Chuyue also saw through it, but Han Biluo was kept in the dark.
She didn't know that Luo Zheng had already met the two of them in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, and even when Luo Zheng taught Liu Chengtian a lesson last time, Han Bi Luo thought that he was doing it for her!
Han Chuyue and Han Liusu also came to visit Luo Tianxing alone, which really made her feel strange.
Although she was confused, Han Biluo remained calm and collected. She had originally planned to bring Luo Tianxing to Saint Royal City to be nurtured. That was the territory of her father, Han Tianxiao.
But recently, Aunt Hanjiu and her father had been at odds. Moreover, Luo Tian Xing had entered the Purple Soul Palace and had been personally summoned by Aunt Hanjiu. The importance she attached to him could be seen. Of course, she wouldn't be so foolish as to provoke Aunt Hanjiu. Moreover, this wasn't something she could meddle in. At most, she could only suggest it to her father …
Therefore, Han Biluo took out a gift she had prepared long ago and gave it to Luo Zheng. It was an azure necklace, a graded grandmist artifact that could protect one's soul from harm.
Although it wasn't a top-grade grandmist artifact, it was still valuable in the eyes of ordinary disciples. After giving this gift, Han Biluo bid farewell and left.
After Han Biluo left, Aunt Hanjiu sighed softly, "I'm afraid she doesn't know your true identity?"
Luo Zheng nodded. Since Aunt Hanjiu had already guessed most of the truth, there was no need for him to hide it.
"The three girls have something they want to say to you secretly. Today, I've become a candle." She seemed to recall some unhappy memories and continued, "I won't interfere in the matters of you youngsters, but they are still my Han Family's women. If you dare to disappoint them like your father, I'll be the first to not forgive you."
These words sounded a little strange. Looking at Aunt Hanjiu's expression, Luo Zheng faintly suspected that his father had done something wrong to Aunt Hanjiu in the past. After all, Aunt Hanjiu had been a secondary saint for many years. At that time, the Luo Family's floating island hadn't declined and his father should still be the Luo Family's Holy Emperor …
Luo Zheng only dared to make wild guesses in his heart. Naturally, he wouldn't say it out loud.
"Also … 'Luo Tian Xing' should be an alias. What is your real name?" Aunt Hanjiu stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Everyone knew that Mu Haiji had launched a Universal War against Luo Zheng's Universe of Expansion. She already understood that Luo Zheng had changed his name to hide his identity.
With Luo Tian Xing's current cultivation, once his identity was exposed, there was no chance of survival.
"My surname is Luo …" Luo Zheng replied.
"Nonsense?" Aunt Hanjiu glared fiercely at Luo Zheng.
"My name is' Zheng '," Luo Zheng continued.
"Luo Zheng?" Aunt Hanjiu pondered the meaning of this name for a while before saying, "Luo Xiao actually gave you a very ambitious name. In the past, he wrote a famous Dao Struggle piece called 'Conquering the Heavens'. Presumably, your name came from that famous Dao Struggle piece." Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly.
Luo Zheng didn't comment. He didn't ask for too much. He only hoped that Aunt Hanjiu would keep his identity a secret. Now, it seemed that this quasi-sage didn't have any enmity towards him.
Aunt Hanjiu didn't discuss this issue too much. Instead, she changed the topic and shifted the topic to the Arena of Gods. "I observed your battle with the four true gods. Your body is indeed abnormally strong. The realm of your soul is even stronger than most of the lower true gods. However, you relied entirely on brute force to kill your opponents. At the present stage, your opponents are only lower true gods who have just entered the Divine Path. If your ranking continues to rise, sooner or later you will encounter the top geniuses of the rich and powerful families. I'm afraid it will be difficult for you to win so easily. "
In reality, the problem that Aunt Hanjiu spoke of was precisely what Luo Zheng was worried about.
Warriors who had just proven their divinity could indeed gain a lot of extra points if they participated in the cross-level battles. However, if they lost a battle, the loss of points would also be considerable.
Right now, Luo Zheng could handle these lower true gods with ease. However, as his ranking rose, the true gods he would face would become stronger and stronger. It would become more and more difficult to deal with them.
However, Luo Zheng couldn't condense a godhood now. It would be very difficult for his strength to increase. Furthermore, he didn't dare to use the Severing Emotions Divine Path. Even if Luo Zheng participated in the cross-level battles anonymously, his points would ultimately be counted with Han Jia. When that time came, Han Jia would have a disciple who cultivated the Restriction Path. He probably wouldn't be tolerated by the Sage Halls.
Furthermore, according to Aunt Hanjiu's strategy, if Luo Zheng really had a chance to condense a godhood, she wouldn't allow it.
There wasn't much time left. If he wanted to earn enough points in less than three months, he could only do so through the cross-level battles in the Arena of Gods. If his cultivation could really break through to the true god realm, cross-level battles naturally wouldn't exist. The extra points reward wouldn't be that high either. Unless he used the identity of a lower true god to challenge those middle true gods …
"In a short period of time, I'm afraid it will be very difficult for my strength to increase greatly," Luo Zheng said after thinking for a while.
Who knew that Aunt Hanjiu would smile slightly. "It's not impossible. Close your eyes!"
After saying this, Aunt Hanjiu stretched out a finger. Her fingertip lightly tapped Luo Zheng's forehead. Wisps of extremely pure purple mist surrounded Luo Zheng.
In the blink of an eye, everything around Luo Zheng disappeared. He was pulled into a strange world by Aunt Hanjiu …
When Luo Zheng opened his eyes again, what entered his eyes was a world filled with purple mist. It was like the end of the world. In the thick purple mist, unimaginably large serpentine beasts slowly wandered. When those beasts sensed Aunt Hanjiu below them, they all let out resounding cries, as if welcoming her arrival.
In addition to these serpentine beasts, this world also had huge plants that were tens of thousands of feet tall. These plants' leaves covered the sky, and above the leaves hung a dense mass of purple bats.
The resounding cries of the serpentine beasts startled the purple bats below the leaves. These bats all spread their wings, and immediately covered the sky.
Everything seemed bizarre and variegated.
This place was very similar to the Extreme Purple Realm …
Luo Zheng had just entered this place when he thought this in his heart. However, he still opened his mouth to ask, "Where is this?"
Aunt Hanjiu calmly replied, "Purple Sky Absolute Land. This is the universe I created."
The Purple Sky Absolute Land was a special universe. Aunt Hanjiu had relied on the Purple Sky Absolute Land to become a quasi-saint. Moreover, as the Purple Sky Absolute Land was perfected, she also had the possibility of becoming a saint!
"Why did you bring me here?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Although I don't know where your power comes from, your current methods are too simple. I'll teach you a technique in the Power Divine Dao that can unleash your power to the extreme … This technique was created for True Gods who have condensed nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier. Although you haven't opened a star, you can still benefit greatly," Aunt Hanjiu said.
Targeted at nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier?
Luo Zheng was slightly startled. His nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier were not only completely condensed, but had already sunk into the world within his body …
Aunt Hanjiu naturally didn't know that there was such an incomprehensible thing as the Primal Chaos Secret Technique in this world, and that someone had even successfully cultivated it.
When Jiu Sheng gifted Luo Zheng a huge amount of power source to help Luo Zheng condense the nine stars, it naturally sunk into the world within his body. Before Luo Zheng, only Saints could merge the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier with the universe!

Chapter 1852
The Realm of the Gods had a very thorough understanding of the Divine Dao.
No matter which Divine Dao it was, it was a mature and complete system.
But after countless years of research, a few geniuses were able to cut apart the Dao of the Divine Dao and combine it with their own comprehension to create their own techniques. For example, Saint Jiu's' Ice Soul Devil Flame 'and Han Yaozhi's' Five Elements Illusory Destruction '.
In the Divine Realm, there was a unified term for this kind of cultivation method. It was called "Outer Dao Divine Ability", which meant that it was a divine ability outside of the Divine Dao …
There were some extra-dao divine abilities that were extremely powerful, such as the Ice Soul Devil Flame. To this day, there were still countless True Gods who wanted to become Saint Jiu's disciples in order to cultivate the Ice Soul Devil Flame.
As for the Five Elements Vanish, it was relatively ordinary. It was not much more powerful than the Five Elements Divine Dao itself. Rather than spending time cultivating the Five Elements Vanish, it was better to invest time in the Divine Dao itself. Therefore, the value of the Five Elements Vanish in the eyes of True Gods was only ordinary. True Gods did not have much interest in it.
As for the debate between the supernatural powers of the Outer Dao and the Divine Dao itself, it was also a Dao struggle that lasted for a long time. Some people said that the Divine Dao was the foundation of the Divine Realm, and that focusing on cultivating the Divine Dao was the right path.
There were also people who said that external divine abilities were the future of the divine realm. The three thousand Divine Daos were deliberately created by powerful beings. Outside the divine realm, there was an even vaster primal chaos. To be limited to the three thousand Divine Daos was to shut one's eyes …
This Dao battle was still going on, and there was still no conclusion to it.
"The most precious thing about the Strength Supreme Dao is that it is able to use one's own power. In fact, it is very powerful, because the source of power can be molded into all kinds of shapes, and the effects are very, very direct. Compared to other Supreme Daos, this is the natural advantage of the Strength Supreme Dao." Saying this, Aunt Hanjiu waved her hand towards the dark purple sky.
"Hualala …"
The snake-like beasts in the purple fog suddenly surged in one direction and disappeared in the blink of an eye. At the same time, 21 purple stars appeared in the deep sky.
Luo Zheng counted and then asked, "Isn't it nine stars? Why are there twenty-one stars? "
Aunty Hanjiu smiled. Nine of the twenty-one purple stars began to shine. These nine stars shone with a multicolored light. They were as beautiful as gems under the sun.
Luo Zheng also knew that this was the color of the source of power. In Luo Zheng's Inner World, there were also nine similar stars twinkling with rainbow-like colors.
Luo Zheng's nine stars were slightly smaller than Aunt Jiu's stars, but they were only about a quarter smaller. Moreover, Luo Zheng had only absorbed a third of Jiu Sheng's power source. This meant that although Aunt Jiu Sheng already possessed nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and had also incorporated them into the "Purple Sky Absolute Ground", her power source was still less than that of an ordinary Saint.
As Aunt Hanjiu waved her hand again, strands of invisible power descended from the sky.
Under normal circumstances, these energy fluctuations were colorless and formless, and could not be seen.
The reason why Luo Zheng was able to injure Leng Lin Yue was because of the long sword condensed from power. Leng Lin Yue did not notice that invisible long sword at the crucial moment and almost had his head pierced through.
In reality, Luo Zheng's two battles with Leng Lin Yue and Xie Jiao had caused many disciples of wealthy families to be very confused. They did not know how Luo Zheng used that invisible power, nor did they know what kind of Divine Dao Luo Zheng cultivated.
After all, they could not have imagined that a martial artist who had proven himself to be able to use the "Transcendence Mystical Power" to draw out pure power from his body …
The Purple Sky Absolute Ground was constantly shrouded in purple fog. The invisible power was very conspicuous, and the fog highlighted the outline of this power.
Under Aunt Hanjiu's control, the invisible power quickly descended. After the power continuously pushed away the purple fog, it transformed into all kinds of shapes. There were human figures, dragons, phoenixes …
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's eyes widened.
In reality, Luo Zheng could also do this. Using the Transcendence Mystical Power, he could easily knead the power into all kinds of shapes. However, he could only soften one shape at a time. He could not do it as easily as Aunt Hanjiu, and create so many at once.
"This Mystical Power is an Outer Dao Mystical Power that is comprehended from the Divine Dao of Strength. It is commonly known as' Power Tracing '." Aunt Hanjiu continued, "Actually, the lowest level of Power Tracing has always been used by martial artists after opening the eight dao pillars!"
The most primitive power was purely within a martial artist's body. When fighting an enemy, one had to squeeze out the power from their muscles and meridians.
After opening the eight dao pillars, the extra power would condense in the dao pillars. When a body cultivator needed power, they would draw it out from the dao pillars and transmit it through their meridians to their hands.
This was the most basic form of Power Tracing. It was to shape the power into a fluid and quickly transmit it through the meridians to where the martial artist needed it.
"This also counts as Power Tracing …" Luo Zheng curled his lips. This Power Tracing sounded very mysterious. He didn't think anyone could do it.
Aunt Hanjiu glanced at Luo Zheng indifferently, "That is only the lowest level of usage. It is unable to force the power out of the body. Once you fully understand Power Tracing, you will be able to reach this step and greatly increase your ability to defend against enemies."
After speaking, Aunt Hanjiu stretched out her slender fingers and lightly flicked them. From her fingertips, five invisible powers extended an inch away, like the outlines of sharp and invisible nails.
"… …"
This scene made Luo Zheng choke for a long time. He was so stunned that he couldn't say a word.
He already had the Transcendence Mystical Power. Doing this was simply a piece of cake!
Aunt Hanjiu didn't see Luo Zheng's battle with Leng Lin Yue. Otherwise, she wouldn't have done something so superfluous. She very seriously explained, "The most powerful use of Power Tracing is that once you have the Transcendence Mystical Power, you can transform into countless shapes. It is impossible to guard against. Power Tracing is divided into three stages. I will first teach you the first stage. I will teach you how to force the source of power out of the body … …"
The first stage of power tracing was to simply shape the power, and at the same time, use the power as an extension of oneself. Just like the invisible fingertips on Aunt Jiu's fingertips, if used properly, it would often be able to catch the opponent off guard.
The second stage was to extend the power even further. At the same time, the power could be shaped into simple shapes.
The third stage was the most complex use. It was to turn the power into an independent entity and control it. The third stage was the most powerful, but the requirements were the most stringent. Only those who had the Transcendence Mystical Power had the qualifications to use it as they wished. However, the Transcendence Mystical Power was the exclusive ability of Saints!
In fact, Luo Zheng was now in between the second and third stage. With the Transcendence Mystical Power, he could already easily shape some simple shapes. However, he was far from being as flexible as Aunt Hanjiu.
Just as Aunt Hanjiu was about to teach the most basic principle of Power Tracing, Luo Zheng finally couldn't help but ask, "Master … … Is this the first stage?"
After saying this, Luo Zheng lightly punched out. From his fist, a pure power surged out, parting the purple mist in front of it to form the outline of a fist.
Seeing this, Aunt Hanjiu's face was calm. "Since you have already comprehended the Dao of Power Tracing, why haven't I seen you use it in the Arena of Gods?"
With Luo Zheng's talent, it wasn't too strange for him to comprehend it on his own. Aunt Hanjiu was very calm.
Luo Zheng didn't stop. Instead, he continued to say, "Is this the second stage?" After saying this, he lightly shook his fist. His hand lightly waved a few times in the purple mist. Gradually, the outline of a long sword condensed in Luo Zheng's hand. However, this sword was still held in Luo Zheng's hand, never leaving Luo Zheng's hand.
"You!"
At this moment, Aunt Hanjiu's face revealed a look of surprise. She really hadn't thought that Luo Zheng, a Proof of Divinity expert, would actually cultivate Power Tracing to such a level. If this was the case, then her actions were indeed superfluous.
In reality, without using the Transcendence Mystical Power, Luo Zheng simply couldn't understand the so-called Power Tracing. This Power Tracing was originally a supplementary ability of the Transcendence Mystical Power.
Luo Zheng glanced at Aunt Hanjiu and smiled slightly. The hand holding the formless long sword lightly waved. The formless long sword pierced through the purple mist. After rushing out a distance of several tens of feet, it circled around and returned to Luo Zheng's hand, leaving a clear trail in the purple mist.

Chapter 1853
Luo Zheng seemed to have displayed the first stage of power retracing, but Aunt Han Jiu didn't feel anything.
Since he was proficient in so many Divine Daos, and could even cultivate Purple Cloud Divine Dao, it wasn't difficult for him to figure out the most basic use of power retracing through Strength Divine Dao.
Comprehending the second stage on his own was a bit difficult. That required a very deep understanding of power, but relying on the power of the eight-layered dao platform wasn't enough to support comprehension, so Aunt Han Jiu was naturally shocked.
Shock was shock, but it was still within Aunt Han Jiu's acceptable range. However, Luo Zheng was actually able to make the long sword formed by power fly out of his body — this was too unreasonable!
Not to mention Luo Zheng, even a consummate True God couldn't completely gather the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier without constructing a universe.
Power that left the body was like a rootless source, and would be exhausted in an extremely short period of time. Moreover, it couldn't be controlled at all … this was also why power wasn't valued so much in the divine realm.
When one reached Aunt Jiu's level, one could display their power to the extreme after using a transcendent divine ability. However, Saints often didn't need pure power. Thus, after Saint Jiu constructed the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, he went against the norm and destroyed the nine stars. All of the power source was stored in a large pool, and from time to time, he would give away some …
When one's cultivation was low, they couldn't use it, and when one's cultivation was high, they couldn't use it. This was the awkward aspect of body refining.
However, there were exceptions to everything, and Aunt Han Jiu was one of them.
Aunt Han Jiu merged the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier with the world within her body, but her internal world was a "Purple Sky Absolute Land" constructed with Purple Cloud Divine Dao, not a complete universe, so she was only a Secondary Saint.
Although Aunt Han Jiu didn't have a complete universe, after the nine stars merged with Purple Sky Absolute Land, she also had the Transcendence Mystical Power, and with her own comprehension of Purple Cloud Divine Dao, she could transform all power into a huge formless sword array, and also display the Transcendence Mystical Power and power retracing to the extreme.
This was also Aunt Han Jiu's strongest point, and where her greatest confidence lay.
However, Aunt Hanjiu never thought that one day, she would see a martial artist who had proven himself as a divinity so easily do this. Even if she was a quasi-sage, an existence who could see all the mountains and small mountains among the gods, her vision far surpassed that of ordinary gods, she still couldn't understand the scene before her eyes.
She was silent for a while before she said, "No wonder … I said that with your performance in the Arena of the Gods, it would be very difficult for you to kill Xie Jiao, and it would be even more impossible for you to injure Leng Linyue. Now I have found the reason."
Hearing Aunt Hanjiu's words, Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. She was indeed worthy of being called a Second Sage. Her perception was terrifyingly strong. Although she wasn't present at the battle between him and Leng Linyue, she was still able to deduce the reason why he had injured Leng Linyue in an instant!
"Since Master wants to teach me the 'Power Tracing' technique, I wonder if I can reach Master's level?" Luo Zheng took advantage of the situation to ask.
Luo Zheng's control over the origin of power was still very limited. At the very least, he hadn't used the Transcendence Divine Ability to the extreme. In front of him, Aunt Hanjiu was likely to be a peak expert in this field. Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let go of such an opportunity. After all, this would allow Luo Zheng to have more confidence when facing the top ranked lower true gods in the Hall of the Gods!
Aunt Hanjiu glanced at Luo Zheng …
Although Power Tracing was an external divine ability of the Divine Path of Strength, there were many people who cultivated it. Aunt Hanjiu relied on this ability to become a quasi-sage, so naturally it had its own unique advantages.
Before, Aunt Hanjiu had only planned to teach Luo Zheng a simple Power Tracing technique. She didn't plan to involve the second stage, and it was even more impossible for her to involve the third stage.
Although Luo Zheng called her Master, it was only an honorific. He wasn't her disciple and didn't take her as his Master.
Thinking of this, Aunt Hanjiu suddenly had an idea. Taking Luo Xiao's son as her disciple didn't seem like a bad decision?
"This divine ability of mine has never been passed on to outsiders … However, if you can really take me as your Master and become my disciple, it isn't impossible," Aunt Hanjiu said.
If Aunt Hanjiu's disciples could really construct the "Purple Sky Absolute Ground," their strength would increase explosively. The possibility of becoming a quasi-sage would be much higher than ordinary upper true gods. Unfortunately, until now, no disciple had constructed the Purple Sky Absolute Ground. Naturally, no one had been able to obtain Aunt Hanjiu's true inheritance.
Now, an excellent candidate had appeared. He was merely a Proof of Divinity warrior, but he had all the conditions to cultivate her true inheritance. Moreover, these conditions shouldn't appear on a Proof of Divinity warrior. It could even be said that True Gods didn't have the qualifications.
Aunt Hanjiu was naturally moved.
"I already have a Master. My Master is Gu Bei," Luo Zheng replied straightforwardly.
Now that Aunt Hanjiu knew his identity, he wasn't afraid that she would know more.
"… …"
Aunt Hanjiu's eyes revealed a trace of depression. She should have thought of this long ago. Gu Bei and Luo Xiao's relationship was so good. Now that Luo Xiao had suffered so much, it was impossible for Gu Bei to not help.
"However, if Master is willing to teach me the Absolute Ground, I will definitely cultivate diligently," Luo Zheng said with a smile. At this stage, he didn't dare to use the Emotion Severing Divine Path. He also didn't dare to casually reveal the aura of primal chaos. Moreover, the Ten Thousand Laws Enemy Book that could sever karma had no effect in the divine domain. Although the divine domain also had the rules of karma, this wasn't the universe. The reason why the Ten Thousand Laws Enemy Book was powerful was because Luo Xiao had given it the ability to sever karma.
Aunt Hanjiu fiercely glared at Luo Zheng. Since Luo Zheng was her son, she could make an exception. However, she was still somewhat unwilling in her heart. Then, Aunt Hanjiu reluctantly said, "In that case, I can teach you the God Slaughtering Sword Formation."
"What? God Slaughtering Sword Formation? "Luo Zheng was immediately stunned.
"You've heard of it?" Aunt Hanjiu stared at Luo Zheng.
Although Aunt Hanjiu was a Secondary Sage, she wasn't like the Sage Emperors of the various floating islands who could enter and exit the Sage Halls at will. She was worried about Luo Xiao's safety, but at that time, the Sage Halls were closed. She couldn't even contact the Sage Emperors of the Han family. The highest floating island was also closed.
After the Universal War ended, Aunt Hanjiu only knew that the Great Yan Universe was safe and sound. As for Mu Haiji, he was punished because he had violated the rules of the Governing Slate.
Hearing this news, Aunt Hanjiu was naturally very happy. However, she didn't know how the war between the Great Yan Universe and the Great Yan Universe had been carried out.
Thus, she naturally didn't know that Xi Youqin had used the God Slaughtering Sword Formation in the Great Yan Universe.
"Right …"
Luo Zheng's mind spun quickly. He suddenly discovered something strange. There was originally a connection between the two.
Although the Great Yan Universe's Extreme Purple Realm was very damaged, it was very similar to the Purple Sky Decimating Ground. Xi Youqin had also only started to learn sword formations after passing through the Extreme Purple Realm. Xi Youqin had truly grasped the God Slaughtering Sword Formation in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement.
What was the connection between them?
While Luo Zheng was thinking, Aunt Hanjiu's face once again revealed an excited expression. "If you've really seen the God Slaughtering Sword Formation, it definitely couldn't have been in the Divine Realm. It must have been in the Great Yan Universe! According to the agreement from back then, a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body appeared in the Extreme Purple Realm! "
Xi Youqin indeed had a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body. However, the Great Yan Universe's Extreme Purple Yin Body's classification should be different from the Divine Realm's, right? At least, he hadn't heard of the Extreme Purple Yin Body's classification in the Purple Soul Palace.
However, since Aunt Hanjiu had said this, Luo Zheng had basically thought of everything that he should think of …
The Extreme Purple Realm was a special and damaged world. The Great Yan Universe's laws couldn't completely cover it. Moreover, the Extreme Purple Realm didn't seem to experience a great extinction.
After pondering for a while, Luo Zheng met Aunt Hanjiu's eyes. Aunt Hanjiu actually guessed what Luo Zheng wanted to say. She immediately said, "You've guessed correctly. The Extreme Purple Realm is where I once stayed. Your father specially opened it for me. Your father and I created the God Slaughtering Sword Formation together. I once promised him that if a First Grade Extreme Purple Yin Body really appeared in the Great Yan Universe, he would naturally be able to obtain the inheritance of the God Slaughtering Sword Formation. As for Luo Xiao's specific arrangements, I don't know."
She paused for a moment and revealed a curious expression. "Since you've seen the God Slaughtering Sword Formation, do you know who cast it? Did he come to the Divine Realm?"
Luo Zheng blankly thought for a while.
After he'd smuggled himself out, he was arranged to stay in the Vast Sky Territory. Why was he in Han Jia's territory? Luo Zheng had been puzzled for a while. Now, he finally understood. This was the intention of his father's arrangement. His father hoped that he could find Aunt Hanjiu to rely on.

Chapter 1854
When Aunt Hanjiu first met Luo Xiao, she was only a middle true god, but her performance in Han Jia was stunning. She was the focus of cultivation.
At that time, the study of Purple Cloud Divine Dao wasn't as mature as it was now. Even whether Purple Sky Absolute Land could form was questioned. Opening a universe in divine territory wasn't easy.
Purple Cloud Divine Dao's only advantage was in "transformation" and "control." It was much weaker than other Divine Daos.
Later, Luo Xiao made an exception and let Aunt Hanjiu into his universe. He opened a world and tried to create Purple Sky Absolute Land. This was the origin of the Extreme Purple Realm.
Compared to the difficulty of opening a space in divine territory, it was much easier in the Great Yan Universe. Plus, Aunt Hanjiu had Luo Xiao's help.
Not long after, the Extreme Purple Realm was opened. Aunt Hanjiu displayed shocking talent and created many great sword formations at the same time. The most powerful of them all was the God Slaying Sword Formation.
Just as Aunt Hanjiu was very satisfied with her creation, Luo Xiao told Aunt Hanjiu that if his guess was correct, the strongest God Slaughtering Sword Array was not the "real sword," but the "virtual sword." The so-called "virtual sword" here was actually using power to force the power out of the body into an invisible sword.
However, at that time, Aunt Hanjiu had yet to become a quasi-saint and was unable to use the Transcendence Divine Power. Her Power Retracing could only reach the second stage of Power Retracing. Therefore, she was still unable to leave the Solid Sword, which was the Long Vine Sword, Buddha Emperor Sword, Double Sun Sword, and Heaven Fang Sword that Luo Xiao had given her.
Those four swords were no longer of use to Aunt Hanjiu, but she still kept them in her hands. Occasionally, in the dead of night, she would take them out and caress them. Her heart was filled with nostalgia. The days she spent in the Grand Evolution Universe were her happiest times.
Back when the Luo Family's floating island was in danger, Aunt Han Jiu had stood by Luo Xiao's side without hesitation. However, at the time, she was only an upper true god. She hadn't been able to complete the God-Slaying Array's Void Sword Array. She wanted to help Han Jia, but Han Jia ordered her to stay on the floating island. Han Jia even sent a Paragon to imprison her. In the end, she could only watch as Han Jia's floating island suffered a calamity and Luo Xiao perished.
Even now, that scene still lingered in her mind. She swore in her heart that if she had the chance, she would do everything in her power to help Yu Taibai and the others. Perhaps there would be a sliver of a chance for Luo Xiao to turn things around. After all, as long as his universe was indestructible, so was he.
However …
Yu Taibai didn't seem to buy her words and was rather hostile towards her. A long time ago, when she was exploring Buzhou Spirit Mountain, she encountered Yu Taibai and he actually wanted to attack her.
Today, after confirming Luo Zheng's identity, she naturally understood that a sliver of a chance had quietly arrived. This had to be Luo Xiao's arrangement. That man wouldn't give up even if he died!
And the man in front of her was exactly the same as him. Even in terms of women, they were very similar …
When she left the Grand Yan Universe, the Extreme Purple Realm hadn't been erased. She'd left her inheritance there, especially the God-Slaying Array.
She and Luo Xiao had agreed that if one day a first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body appeared in the Grand Yan Universe, that person would naturally be qualified to cultivate it. After all, Aunt Han Jiu had the best Extreme Purple Yin Body.
Luo Xiao had even joked that Aunt Han Jiu's Extreme Purple Yin Body couldn't be born naturally. She was a complete exception. Unless Luo Xiao personally created an Extreme Purple Yin Body, only then would he have the qualifications to meet Aunt Han Jiu's requirements and receive her inheritance.
Now that a first-grade Extreme Purple Yin Body had finally appeared, this Extreme Purple Yin Body must have been deliberately created by Luo Xiao. However, she hadn't expected that the woman who received her inheritance would be Luo Zheng's wife …
Aunt Han Jiu's beautiful eyes blinked, then she asked again, "Has she entered the divine territory?"
"I'm not sure." Luo Zheng shook his head.
Luo Zheng brought Ning Yudie and her family into the Immortal Abode. Xi Youqin was naturally willing to enter the Immortal Abode, but Luo Zheng didn't allow it.
As one of the ten Dao Disciples, Xi Youqin was destined to have her own path. According to Xingwei's arrangements, Xi Youqin should have snuck in after attaining godhood.
She had already undergone the blood exchange, and now Yu Taibai was still dragging the Jiang Family's footsteps. Witnessing this was to create opportunities for these Dao Disciples.
When Luo Zheng left the Great Yan Abode, Hua Tianming had already left one step ahead of him. Now, Luo Zheng didn't know how the other Dao Disciples were faring or whether they'd reached the divine territory.
"According to Luo Xiao's arrangements, she should have snuck into the Han Family's ancestral land. Someone like that needs to be personally nurtured by me." Aunt Han Jiu narrowed her eyes. "During this time, I'll arrange for some people to search for the Extreme Purple Yin Body in the territory controlled by the Han Family … What's her name?"
"Xi Youqin," Luo Zheng replied. "However, if she enters the divine territory, it's very likely that she'll choose an alias. I'm afraid it won't be that easy to find her …"
The divine territory was so vast that it was difficult to cross just one territory.
The reason why Luo Zheng was able to meet Aunt Hanjiu in such a short time was because he had formulated a strict plan. At the same time, he had relied on his own powerful talent to successfully step onto the floating island. It was also because of a freak combination of factors that he had entered the Purple Soul Palace.
"You don't have to worry about that." Aunt Han Jiu shook her head, showing great determination. Many years ago, she'd missed an opportunity. This time, no matter what, she wouldn't miss it. She then continued, "Let's talk about the God-Slaughtering Sword Array …"
Aunt Han Jiu stared at Luo Zheng and continued, "Only those who possess the Transmigration Theurgy have the qualifications to wield such power. Just what kind of world have you constructed? You haven't even condensed a Godhood, yet you can use the Transmigration Theurgy."
Luo Zheng smiled bitterly. "It's also because of this that I find it difficult to condense a Godhood …"
"Even you find it difficult to condense a Godhood?" Aunt Han Jiu's gaze hardened slightly. Of course, she knew how important Luo Zheng was to Luo Xiao. If he couldn't even condense a Godhood, it definitely wasn't because Luo Zheng's talent wasn't enough. Most likely, it was because Luo Zheng's Godhood was special, or the world he'd constructed in his body was special.
Luo Xiao and Gu Bei were considered the most creative Saints. Their vision wasn't limited to the divine territory at all. It was normal that they'd cultivated such a shocking little monster. However, if he couldn't condense a Godhood, it was ultimately a great hidden danger. Aunt Han Jiu asked again, "Do you need me to help you with anything?"
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I've already found a solution." The problem he'd encountered, let alone Aunt Han Jiu, even if Gu Bei came personally, he probably wouldn't have a good solution. Moreover, he'd already found more "mud." As long as he obtained enough points in the Arena of the Gods this time, he'd be able to collect the mud. Perhaps the Godhood would be condensed.
Seeing Luo Zheng so confident, Aunt Han Jiu didn't ask any more questions. That might be Luo Zheng's greatest secret. She immediately said, "Since Xi Youqin has already cultivated the God Slaughtering Sword Formation, and she's your wife, I'm sure you've seen the God Slaughtering Sword Formation before …"
"I've seen it before." Luo Zheng nodded. "Other than the God Slaughtering Sword Formation, there's also the Little Purple River Formation, the Yin-Yang Descending Sword Formation …"
"They're all sword formations that I've cultivated in the past. However, back then, I could only use the 'Solid Sword.' Although the God Slaughtering Sword Formation is powerful, it's destined to have great limitations," Aunt Han Jiu said calmly.
Later, after Aunt Han Jiu had cultivated to the level of a Quasi-Saint, she'd used the "Void Sword." In other words, she'd traced the shape of the God Slaughtering Sword Formation with power.
After all, after using the Transcendental Theurgy to drive the "Sword of Power", not only would it be more powerful, the changes within would also be faster and more casual. In terms of power alone, it would be more than a hundred times stronger.
As she spoke …
Aunt Han Jiu stretched out her hand and lightly waved.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Not far away, the silhouettes formed by power suddenly spread out, transforming into strands of formless swords that were about ten feet long, shooting out in all directions.
In this space, there were thousands of formless and colorless longswords of power. These longswords shuttled back and forth in the purple mist, leaving behind long and narrow sword silhouettes.
Very quickly, these sword silhouettes surrounded Luo Zheng …

Chapter 1855
Luo Zheng had seen Xi Youqin use the God Slaying Sword Array before, and its power was indeed terrifying.
Even if Xi Youqin was only a Realm Lord at that time, she could still kill a Heavenly Venerable with the God Slaying Sword Array, and there were probably more than five Heavenly Venerables that died in the God Slaying Sword Array.
Moreover, the four swords Xi Youqin used were the four swords formed from the divine patterns of the Heaven Tier race, so their power would be greatly reduced.
If Luo Zheng really grasped the four sword transformations of the God Slaying Sword Array and could use them as he wished, there would be a qualitative change to his current strength …
As Aunt Han Jiu's voice fell, the countless formless swords began to merge, and as the purple mist continued to roll, condensing into four swords.
Through the outlines outlined by the purple mist, Luo Zheng could distinguish the shapes of these four swords.
One of the swords was surrounded by vines, so he could see the irregular outline of the vines protruding around the sword. This sword was called the Long Vine Sword.
The end of one sword was like a Maitreya Buddha sitting upright, the middle as wide as a wheel. This was the Buddha Emperor Sword.
Another sword was layered like a pagoda. This was the Heavy Sun Sword.
The last sword was covered with sharp thorns and fangs. This was the Heavenly Fang Sword.
"You can use your power to retrace the shape of these four swords," Aunt Han Jiu said in a commanding tone.
"This … the shape of the swords isn't important, right?" Luo Zheng asked. Did he really need those four swords to set up the God Slaying Sword Array? He was using power to shape the swords now, so there shouldn't be such a big limitation.
"Less nonsense," Aunt Han Jiu shouted coldly. She, a dignified Secondary Saint, imparted her own ultimate arts to this brat just like that, so how could she have so many other thoughts …
"Oh." Luo Zheng nodded, silently extracting the power source of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier.
In reality, Luo Zheng hadn't truly comprehended the power of retracing the shape of power, and Luo Zheng couldn't do it at all. His greatest advantage was that he could directly use the Transcendence Mystical Power to shape the swords as he wished, which was equivalent to directly jumping from the first stage to the third stage of retracing the shape of power!
"Hu … hu …"
After Luo Zheng extracted the power, he directly used the Transcendence Mystical Power to form the shape.
The first sword to take form was … the Long Vine Sword!
Luo Zheng firmly engraved the shape of Auntie Jiu's sword in his mind. Soon, an identical long vine sword appeared in front of Luo Zheng. However, it was slightly smaller than Auntie Jiu's sword. For Luo Zheng, it was just a piece of cake.
"The second one!"
When Luo Zheng began to draw the second sword, the first Long Vine Sword suddenly trembled and collapsed.
"This …" Luo Zheng scratched his head.
Aunty Hanjiu laughed coldly, "Didn't you understand the dao of the Great Violet Qi Art? You don't know how to use the Purple Clouds Divine Dao's Mind Control Technique? "
What Aunt Han Jiu didn't know was that Luo Zheng himself hadn't comprehended the Great Purple Qi Art. What truly comprehended the Great Purple Qi Art was the Infinite Ruler in his mind.
The two most prominent abilities of the Purple Qi Art were transformation and control. For example, Xi Youqin had a Grade One Extreme Violet Yin Body. She was born with a powerful control ability, so she could control thousands of huge swords.
Of course, controlling those huge swords was much easier than what Luo Zheng was facing now. This was because Luo Zheng had to maintain the Long Vine Sword while controlling the other sword. It was very difficult for Luo Zheng to maintain the Long Vine Sword without activating the Purple Clouds Divine Dao.
Under Aunt Han Jiu's guidance, a small scale in Luo Zheng's mind flashed with a purple light. At the same time, a purple light flashed in Luo Zheng's eyes.
The dao of the Purple Clouds Divine Dao …
Luo Zheng condensed the first Long Vine Sword while comprehending. Then, he waved his arm gently and opened his palm to carefully extract the power source.
"The second one!"
"Whoosh …"
"Buddha Emperor Sword!"
The second Buddha Emperor Sword was condensed by Luo Zheng …
Luo Zheng was not happy at all. His eyes flashed again, and he took advantage of the situation to attack.
"The third one …"
"Whoosh …"
The third Double Sun Sword was also condensed by Luo Zheng.
However, the three swords appeared at the same time for only half a breath's time. The three swords collapsed at the same time.
"Lousy, again!" Aunt Han Jiu sneered.
Her face was full of disdain, but in fact, she was in great shock.
Luo Xiao's son's talent was a bit too strong!
When she had just become a quasi-saint, she had obtained the ability to transcend worlds. She had thought about how to modify the Slaughter Sword Formation. According to Luo Xiao's prediction, she had tried to retrace her power and restore the four swords to their original state.
However, she had spent a full month to retrace her power to two swords at the same time. The third one had taken almost half a year.
A year and a half was not short for mortals, but for Aunt Han Jiu and the true gods, it was just a blink of an eye. She was quite satisfied with her speed.
But now …
