Luo Zheng, this guy, had only tried twice and was able to condense three swords at the same time.
Aunt Han Jiu, who was full of confidence, suddenly felt hurt. Was it because her talent was not enough, or was it because Luo Zheng was too monstrous?
"Let's try again. I also just started cultivating. Why do you have to be like this …" Luo Zheng mumbled. He also felt that Aunt Han Jiu's request was too much.
"What are you talking about?" Aunt Han Jiu asked with an unhappy face.
Luo Zheng curled his lips. He did not dare to speak and continued to extract the power source to retrace his power.
On his third attempt, Luo Zheng was able to maintain the three swords for three breaths of time. After three breaths of time, the three swords collapsed at the same time.
On his fourth attempt, twenty breaths of time …
On his fifth attempt, fifteen minutes of time …
On his sixth attempt, fifteen minutes of time …
On his seventh attempt, Luo Zheng was able to stabilize the three swords. Luo Zheng was able to stabilize the three swords.
"How is it?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
"Average …" Aunt Han Jiu's heart was filled with complex feelings. She once again doubted her own talent.
This father and son pair really had the same moral conduct.
Back then, when Aunt Hanjiu fell in love with Luo Xiao and cultivated in the Grand Evolution Universe, she was also repeatedly struck by Luo Xiao's powerful innate talent and comprehension ability. She also rapidly matured under Luo Xiao's strict requirements and strikes.
At that time, Aunt Han Jiu also understood that her talent would not be able to catch up with this man. However, Luo Xiao was also a Sage at that time. She was inferior to him. She adored and worshipped Luo Xiao. Naturally, there was no difference in her heart. It seemed that it was normal for her to be inferior to Luo Xiao.
Now, her son had run over. Moreover, her son was a Divine Martial Artist. At this moment, Aunt Han Jiu's heart was filled with depression and jealousy. Not to mention that a quasi-sage was broad-minded, even a woman who cultivated to the Sage level was still a narrow-minded Sage.
"Since you've already grasped three swords, now is the time to start on the fourth." Aunt Han Jiu continued, "I've already slowed down the flow of time in the Purple Sky Absolute Land. You can slowly cultivate here."
Time was still very precious to Luo Zheng. There were only two months left until the floating island recalculated the rankings. If he wasted a few months in the Purple Sky Absolute Land, the rankings would have already been created. The Purple Soul Palace would probably have no choice but to expel eighty percent of their disciples.
The difficulty of tracing out four swords at the same time was an order of magnitude more difficult than three. Luo Zheng's first attempt was completely destroyed before the swords' shapes were completed.
However, Luo Zheng's expression did not change. He did not even blink. Once again, he urged his power source …
Aunt Han Jiu silently looked at Luo Zheng's back. Her eyes were hazy. The seriously cultivating Luo Zheng's back was almost exactly the same as Luo Xiao's. This caused Aunt Han Jiu's thoughts to constantly drift back to the days when she cultivated in the Purple Extreme Realm. In order to allow her to study sword formations, Luo Xiao had asked her to gently close her eyes. When she opened them again, she saw that the Purple Extreme Realm was filled with giant swords.
There were 99.9 billion giant swords, densely dotted in the sky of the Purple Extreme Realm. They were at her disposal. At that time, her heart had been filled with joy …
After a full ten days, Luo Zheng was finally able to make the four swords exist for a breath of time at the same time.
Back then, Aunt Han Jiu had done this for six months.
After two months in the Purple Sky Absolute Land, Luo Zheng's four swords could exist for five breaths of time at the same time …
Half a year later, the four swords finally stabilized.
In this half a year, Luo Zheng did not seem to have any other distracting thoughts. He was still focused on condensing swords, dispersing them, condensing swords, dispersing them. He kept repeating this boring process.
Fortunately, Aunt Han Jiu had changed the flow of time. Half a year in the Purple Sky Absolute Land was only three days in the outside world.

Chapter 1856
The God Slaying Sword Formation was created by Aunt Hanjiu herself, but the idea was provided by Luo Xiao.
However, at that time, the God Slaying Sword Formation was the most primitive variation of the four swords, and there were still many flaws.
After Aunt Hanjiu was conferred the title of Secondary Saint, even the four swords formation had a huge change. Purely in terms of power, it had increased a lot.
"The reason why you must trace the power back to the four swords of Long Vine, Buddha Emperor, Double Sun, and Sky Tooth was because each sword had different abilities. Among them, Long Vine mainly used the 'entanglement' formation, Buddha Emperor used the 'killing' formation, Double Sun used the 'protection' formation, and Sky Tooth used the 'biting' formation. In fact, the thirty-two and ninety-six sword variations of the God Slaying Sword Formation no longer have these restrictions, but now you can't control the thirty-two sword variations, and the four sword variations are still mainly based on these four swords …"
The four swords each had their own single sword variations, which were entanglement, killing, protection, and biting.
And when the two swords cooperated with each other, they could display entanglement, killing, biting, and so on, including three sword combinations, four sword combinations, and many other variations.
On one hand, he had to maintain the stability of these swords, and on the other hand, he had to familiarize himself with the coordination of these four swords. Even for Luo Zheng, it was a considerable challenge. Fortunately, the time in the Purple Sky Absolute Land could be controlled. With Aunt Hanjiu's help, Luo Zheng's understanding of the God Slaying Sword Formation increased little by little.
In a month's time, Luo Zheng's mastery of the God Slaying Sword Formation had reached a certain level. He had mastered the single sword variations, which meant that with any sword, Luo Zheng could unleash its greatest advantage.
In three months, Luo Zheng had mastered the coordination of the two swords. The two swords' variations were even more mysterious, and whether it was power or agility, they were a level higher.
In half a year, Luo Zheng had mastered the three sword combinations. When the three swords were used together, it was the essence of the God Slaying Sword Formation, and it could be called the ultimate move.
When the four swords were used together, Luo Zheng spent a whole year to completely master it. This was the ultimate move of the God Slaying Sword Formation. What he had to understand was not only the formation, but also the characteristics of the four swords …
Seeing Luo Zheng's speed of improvement, Aunt Hanjiu's heart calmed down.
His father was already so monstrous, and this son was already extremely special. If he inherited his father's monstrous talent, he would only be more terrifying than his father …
Thinking about it carefully, Aunt Hanjiu also knew that she had indeed taught a very terrifying person.
The true God Slaying Sword Formation could only be mastered by a Quasi-saint or even a Saint. Luo Zheng could already control it as he wished at the Proof of Divinity stage. She was also looking forward to Luo Zheng's performance in the Arena of Gods.
One year in the Purple Sky Extinction Ground was equivalent to six days in the God's Domain. In addition to the three days that Luo Zheng used to retrace his figure, only nine days had passed.
When Aunt Hanjiu and Luo Zheng left the Purple Sky Extreme Land and returned to the palace …
As soon as he returned, a middle-aged man had been waiting for him in the palace for a long time. It was the Han family's head, Han Tianxiao, whom Luo Zheng had met once before.
Aunt Hanjiu was extremely angry with Han Tianxiao. Seeing him, she naturally didn't have a good expression. Instead, she said coldly, "I wonder why the family head has come again?"
A trace of helplessness appeared on Han Tianxiao's face. He then said, "The Xie family has already come knocking. Our Han family has gone against reason. I wonder how Quasi-saint plans to deal with this?"
In the end, Xie Jiao had died on the Han family's floating island. Moreover, he had been killed by an outsider disciple of the Han family. Among the rich and powerful families, this kind of thing definitely needed an explanation. The Xie family had sent people to bring it up a few times before, but because of Aunt Hanjiu, the Han family had always avoided answering.
This time, in their anger, the Xie family had actually joined forces with the Leng and Liu families to pressure the Han family.
In reality, Han Tianxiao didn't have any misgivings about the Xie and Liu families. The two families ranked at the bottom had only become rich and powerful because they were riding on someone else's coattails. If they weren't careful, they might accidentally be eliminated, and these two families would be removed from the list of rich and powerful families. Although the Xie and Liu families also had two Saints, rich and powerful families did need Saints. However, having a Saint didn't mean that they would become rich and powerful!
Han Tianxiao hadn't expected the Leng family to suddenly appear.
Thinking back to the conflict between Han Liusu and Leng Linyue, the biggest problem might lie here. Although Han Tianxiao was well aware of this, he still wanted to throw this problem to Aunt Hanjiu …
"The Xie family wants an explanation. What does that have to do with the Leng family? What right does the Leng family have to say 'yes'? "Han Liusu replied coldly.
This matter wasn't that big of a deal. The Xie family had many direct descendants, and Xie Jiao wasn't particularly outstanding. Since Xie Jiao wanted to kill Luo Zheng, being killed by Luo Zheng was just asking for humiliation.
The only problem was Luo Zheng's identity. He was just an outsider.
"You know that the ranking this time is very important to our Han family. The Leng family will help us. For this reason, the Holy Emperor even agreed to the marriage between Han Liusu and Leng Linyue … I don't want this to affect our relationship with the Leng family!"
The marriage between Han Liusu and Leng Linyue was just a catalyst for the two families to form an alliance. It wasn't important, but Han Tianxiao didn't want to see a conflict arise because of this catalyst.
"Right now, we just have to push that kid out. Then we can easily resolve this matter." Han Tianxiao glanced at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's arrogant words made Han Tianxiao very unhappy. Although Luo Zheng had shown extraordinary strength by killing Xie Jiao and injuring Leng Linyue, so what? Han family didn't lack experts or geniuses. It wasn't worth offending the Leng family for a mere outsider. Using Luo Zheng to quell their anger was the easiest way.
To Han Tianxiao, Luo Zheng was just a grasshopper. No matter how strong he was, he was just a slightly stronger grasshopper. It wouldn't be a pity to sacrifice him. This was the way people in power thought.
Even if Aunt Han Jiu didn't know Luo Zheng's identity, she would still protect Luo Zheng. Now that she knew he was Luo Xiao's son, how could she agree to Han Tianxiao's request?
"I don't know what the Holy Emperor is doing. Since you want to hide the matter of the ranking from me, then continue to hide it. However, Luo Tianxing cannot be handed over. If the Leng family wants to question him, then come find me!" Aunt Han Jiu said coldly. "Didn't I make myself clear that day in the Spirit Heart Palace?"
Han Tianxiao didn't back down this time. Using a stiff tone, he said, "Don't force me to go find the Holy Emperor."
"Go find him and ask me. I'll still say the same. Besides …" Aunt Han Jiu continued, "Even if we don't rely on the Leng family, our Han family may still be able to maintain our current ranking!"
"Rely on your Purple Soul Palace?" Han Tianxiao sneered.
Han Tianxiao knew that Aunt Han Jiu had mobilized the Purple Soul Palace's female disciples to strive for the Arena of Gods rankings and points. However, in this period of time, the various large families had also worked very hard. The Purple Soul Palace's disciples' points had increased a bit, but their rankings had fallen instead of rising!
Talent and strength were on the same level. This wasn't something that could be improved with willpower …
"In two months, the results will naturally be clear. Asking me to hand over Luo Tianxing is wishful thinking. I hope you won't come find me for this matter again," Aunt Han Jiu said coldly.
Hearing Aunt Han Jiu's resolute attitude, Han Tianxiao looked suspiciously at Luo Zheng.
Before, Aunt Han Jiu had protected Luo Zheng. Han Tianxiao could understand. There were too few geniuses in the Purple Soul Palace. It was normal that Aunt Han Jiu didn't want to give up on this little fellow. But now, Aunt Han Jiu's behavior was too much.
Could it be that this Luo Tianxing's background was special?
For someone as smart as Han Tianxiao, he could always guess all sorts of possibilities. He knew how Aunt Han Jiu had risen. She hadn't relied on the Han family's Holy Emperor, because at that time, the Extreme Purple Yin Body wasn't valued in the rich families.
She had relied on Luo Xiao's help to become a Secondary Saint.
This brat was also surnamed Luo. Could he be Luo Xiao's descendant?
Aunt Han Jiu's guess had been confirmed by Han Liusu and Han Chuyue, but Han Tianxiao's guess had no basis. He could only let his imagination run wild.

Chapter 1857
Han Tianxiao was once again rebuffed, and could only return resentfully.
Aunt Hanjiu turned to look at Luo Zheng and said calmly, "Don't blame him. Everyone has their own responsibilities."
Admittedly, Han Tianxiao wanted to throw Luo Zheng out of the Han Clan's floating island, but as the head of the Han Clan, there was nothing wrong with doing so for the sake of the clan's greatest benefit.
"I understand. My responsibility is to earn points." Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Everyone had their own difficulties. Those in high positions also had to face the trouble of making choices. When one's own strength was insufficient, one could not blame others for ignoring them!
Aunt Hanjiu was afraid that Luo Zheng would be angry at Han Tianxiao. Seeing Luo Zheng's carefree expression, she immediately revealed a gratified expression. She sighed inwardly. Luo Xiao had probably spent a lot of effort to raise such an outstanding little fellow …
What she knew was that Jiu Sheng was skilled at research and solving problems, Northern Sage was skilled at exploration and creation, and Luo Xiao was best at strategy and planning.
Back then, when Luo Xiao had been forced by the Sage Halls to fall into his current predicament, he had probably already prepared Luo Zheng as an escape route. Aunt Hanjiu naturally did not expect that the entire Great Evolution Universe had been set up for Luo Zheng.
Luo Xiao naturally could not truly control everything, but his strategy had achieved the best results. Even luck was on his side.
At least, Luo Zheng had walked step by step, slowly reaching Luo Xiao's expectations. It was just that he had walked more than half of this path, and Luo Zheng had gradually drifted further and further away. The remaining part would be the most difficult …
Inside the Hall of the Gods …
Luo Zheng returned once more. When he opened the door, it was still noisy.
There were only two months until the rankings on the floating island were settled. In order to motivate their disciples, the various great clans had announced all sorts of tempting rewards one after the other.
As a result, the atmosphere in the Hall of the Gods grew more and more intense.
Some of the great clans' disciples, who had originally only completed a minimum of three battles per month, now spent all their time in the Hall of the Gods.
Every time this happened, the Pantheon Jade Annulus would become very popular. In order to enter the Arena of the Gods, there would often be fights over positions …
"Two months … I don't have much time left." Luo Zheng glanced at the lower-level Real Gods in the Hall of Gods indifferently. Then, he returned to his room and randomly chose his opponent.
He had just closed the door of the room when a few gazes swept over.
"Anonymous seems to have returned."
"He didn't choose his opponent, nor did he give others a chance to choose him. He must be planning to choose his opponent at random …"
"Very confident!"
According to the rules of the Hall of the Gods, there was no need for them to gather in the Hall of the Gods. If they were confident in their own strength, they could just pick a random opponent and crush them.
In truth, for the vast majority of lower True Divinities, carefully choosing an opponent that they were confident in defeating was nearly a compulsory course. For this reason, some people could even restrain their aura and make themselves look like 'food' for others. All of this was to increase their chances of winning.
In addition to choosing an opponent, one could also bet on a battle in the Hall of the Gods. The winner or loser would bet a sum of Shenwu Coins, which could also make the battle more exciting.
Luo Zheng was now only concerned about crazily earning points. Coupled with his absolute confidence in his own strength, he was not very interested in the Hall of the Gods.
He was not interested in other people, but naturally, there were people who were interested in him.
When Anonymous won two battles in a row, he had already attracted the attention of two or three great clans.
Anonymous, participating in a cross-level battle, and winning two battles in a row — this was quite unusual to begin with.
Later, Luo Zheng's winning streak reached four battles, which naturally attracted even more attention!
Some of the great clans were secretly investigating Luo Zheng's identity, but from beginning to end, Luo Zheng did not show anything special. Questioning the four people who had fought Luo Zheng also yielded no results. The only thing they found was the two golden spots on Luo Zheng's face. They looked a little like the Vulcan's Eyes, but Luo Zheng did not use the Vulcan's Eyes ability …
Thus, all the great clans sent their own disciples to keep an eye on Anonymous's movements. If there was even the slightest movement, these people would naturally report to their clans.
All these years, the floating island's ranking had been very stable. Which clans could fall and which could rise, the great clans were very clear in their hearts. Usually, there were very few accidents.
For example, Han Jia felt that there was a danger of falling out of the top ten this time, so they joined forces with the Leng clan, hoping that the Leng clan could help Han Jia.
However, a person like Anonymous was an unexpected factor. If that person kept winning, it would directly affect a clan's ranking. Thus, there was a need to monitor him.
Not long after Luo Zheng returned to his room …
The door to his room turned into a streak of azure light and floated upwards. His ranking rose by another thirty places.
"So fast …"
"Including the time he took to return to his room to find his opponent, it took him less than twenty breaths to challenge a lower True God!"
"Five wins in a row. This is troublesome. I wonder which clan it is!"
The doors in the Hall of the Gods kept turning into streaks of light and moving up and down. It was already a common occurrence, but none of them had attracted as much attention as this one.
After Luo Zheng obtained his fifth win in a row, about an incense stick's worth of time later, the door moved upwards once more!
Six wins in a row!
Luo Zheng had won two fights in a row very easily. What made Luo Zheng the happiest was that he had smoothly absorbed two new types of Divine Daos in these two fights, and the additional points awarded had increased by several folds. In these two fights, he had won more than twelve hundred points. The extent of the increase was astonishing.
However, according to Aunt Han Jiu's instructions, Luo Zheng didn't use the God Slaughtering Sword Array.
In the eyes of the rich and powerful Saints, the God Slaughtering Sword Array was like Aunt Han Jiu. As a Secondary Saint, Aunt Han Jiu was even qualified to compete with Saints. What she relied on was her God Slaughtering Sword Array. Many Saints didn't pay enough attention to the Transmigration Mystique, but Aunt Han Jiu was a woman who used the Transmigration Mystique to the extreme.
The only difference between her and a Saint was that Purple Sky Absolute Ground couldn't guarantee Aunt Han Jiu's immortality. A true Saint, as long as the universe wasn't destroyed, they wouldn't die.
She wanted Luo Zheng to become a variable in Han Jia!
At the crucial moment, large amounts of points would flow into the Purple Soul Palace. Only then could Han Jia's floating island rank have a chance of rising. If Luo Zheng revealed the God Slaughtering Sword Array too early, the other rich and powerful families, such as the Qin and Tang families, might target Han Jia first and suppress its points!
So when Luo Zheng faced these opponents, he still used his own strength and soul to crush them …
Two hours later, Luo Zheng had won seven consecutive matches, and his bonus points had increased to 1,000 points in a single match.
Four hours later, Luo Zheng had already won nine consecutive matches, and his points had increased to 2,600 points per match!
Six hours … Ten consecutive victories, and his points increased to 4,700!
His door's rank circled around the Hall of the Gods, rising higher and higher.
The clamor in the Hall of the Gods weakened a lot. Seeing this scene, many people left the Hall of the Gods to report the situation to the upper echelons of their families …
"The new anonymous person has already accumulated ten consecutive victories!"
"If he wins one more, he'll get 8,300 points!"
"To be able to achieve such a winning streak even when fighting someone of a higher level than him, he could almost easily become a Complete Real God. Which guy released such a monster?"
"This is a crisis. If that guy is from the Zhou family, our Bai family might really be pushed down by the Zhou family!"
"This is also an opportunity. If we defeat the anonymous person, we can get a large amount of points from him! Which guy did this guy come from? "
"There's no way to find out. He's an anonymous person, and he hasn't lost a single battle since becoming a True God. His ranking hasn't even appeared. It's impossible to guess!"
"Snipe the anonymous person!"
Every time someone who participated in a battle with a higher rank appeared, the true gods in the Arena of the Gods would become unprecedentedly excited.
Although many rich and powerful families hated this kind of person, who directly used a large amount of points to break the balance between the families, more importantly, it was an anonymous person. This caused the rich and powerful families to be filled with suspicion and jealousy!
But at the same time, it was also a huge opportunity. Everyone was willing to get a large amount of points from the other party. Some lower true gods who were confident in their own strength were already rubbing their hands and waiting for Luo Zheng.

Chapter 1858
Luo Zheng's ranking in the Hall of the Gods rose rapidly, attracting unprecedented attention.
However, he himself was not sure.
After his tenth consecutive victory, Luo Zheng finally decided to take a breather in the Hall of the Gods.
The moment he opened the door to the room, a dense crowd of Lower True Gods surrounded the door so tightly that not even a drop of water could trickle through.
These lesser true gods had not expected Luo Zheng to appear at this time. They were exchanging information with each other, trying to guess who this anonymous person was. When the door opened, everyone fell silent at the same time. This scene was very strange.
Luo Zheng scanned the crowd, then asked indifferently, "Why are you looking for me …?"
These lesser true gods sized up Luo Zheng, trying to find any clues on him. However, Luo Zheng did not have any specific characteristics. In order to maintain his anonymity, he had even turned his long robe light blue.
"I want to challenge you!" someone shouted loudly.
After the first person issued the challenge, the others immediately followed suit, practically swarming over.
"Can I challenge you? My ranking is seventy places away from yours …"
"Pick me!"
There were very few people in the Arena of the Gods who were so popular.
In their eyes, this anonymous person who was participating in the leapfrog battle could have his winning streak ended at any time. Once they defeated this anonymous person, they would also be rewarded handsomely!
Losing to an anonymous person would only result in a small loss of points. Everyone was willing to give it a try!
"If you defeat me, I can give you an extra twenty Shenwu coins!" someone suddenly had an idea and said.
To the lesser true gods of the floating island, twenty Shenwu coins was not a large sum.
Thus, someone quickly followed suit!
"Eighty Shenwu coins. As long as you accept my challenge and can defeat me …"
"Two hundred Shenwu coins …"
"Three hundred …"
Like an auction, the group of true gods in front of Luo Zheng began to raise the price.
"They can do this..." Luo Zheng was dumbfounded as he looked at this group of crazy guys. This scene was also unexpected for Luo Zheng, but he was quite happy to see it.
However, according to the rules of the Hall of Gods, those who were qualified to challenge Luo Zheng were only lower-level true gods who were one hundred ranks higher than him and one hundred ranks higher than him. There were only two hundred people in total. Moreover, the lower-level true gods who had obtained their current rankings were not considered to be the top existences on the floating island. Naturally, they were not particularly wealthy.
After a few rounds of bidding, the final bidder was a fat man who offered 550 Shenwu coins.
Previously, these people were so enthusiastic because losing to Luo Zheng wasn't a big deal. However, losing five hundred Shenwu Coins was not something an ordinary person could bear.
"Five hundred and fifty Shenwu coins, how about that?" Fatty stared at Luo Zheng and said loudly.
"Deal." Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He could earn points and Shenwu coins at the same time, so why not?
"I'm number seven hundred and sixty thousand eight!"
After the fatty left his room, he was overjoyed. He ran to his room with a jolt of his buttocks.
Five hundred Shenwu coins was indeed a heartache for Fatty, but in his eyes, it was worth it.
In his opinion, even if he really lost to Luo Zheng, he could still force Luo Zheng to use his trump card and determine Luo Zheng's background …
As soon as Luo Zheng returned to his room, he saw rays of light pop up on the wall of his room. There were actually dozens of challenge requests.
Faced with these challenges, Luo Zheng did not care at all. Then, he accepted Fatty's challenge.
"Whoosh …"
As the surrounding scenery changed, a brand new environment appeared around Luo Zheng.
"Eh?"
Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a look of surprise.
Previously, the environment in the Arena of Gods was the same. It was a boundless plain with a clear starry sky. Luo Zheng had been fighting in the same environment since he became a Godly martial artist. There were no changes at all.
However, it was different this time. He was in a world of ice and snow. Beneath his feet was a layer of slippery ice. Under the layer of ice, there were huge fishes swimming back and forth. In the sky, there were also fist-sized snowflakes that smashed down, making "puff puff puff" sounds.
"Why is the environment in this Arena of Gods different?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Fatty heard Luo Zheng's surprised voice and laughed. "You don't know?"
Luo Zheng shook his head. "I really don't know."
"You can change the environment at will in the Arena of Gods. However, you need to pay a certain amount of points," Fatty said proudly.
"Change the environment …"
Luo Zheng scanned the white snowy plain around him. Then, he said indifferently, "This is not an illusion, but a real world. This world should be beneficial for True Gods who cultivate the Law of Ice or the Snow Imprint Divine Path."
"Correct." Fatty chuckled. He shook his fat belly, and ice marks slowly condensed from his belly. Soon, the outline of an armor appeared. It was actually an ice armor.
"Snow Imprint Divine Path." Luo Zheng was not very interested in Fatty's ability. His Immeasurable Ruler had already absorbed the Snow Imprint Divine Path. "However, this is a little unfair. Since the Arena of Gods is a place where True Gods compete, it should be as fair as an arena. If even the environment can be changed at will, everyone would be willing to choose an environment that is beneficial to them, right?"
When the fatty heard Luo Zheng's words, he chuckled again. He stretched out his round leg and lightly stomped on the ground. A frost vortex appeared on the ground, and a six-foot long staff appeared from the vortex. The surface of the staff was covered with snowflake patterns, as if it was formed by snowflakes. He grabbed the staff and continued, "You think the Arena of the Gods is just an arena? You've underestimated it. The history of the Arena of the Gods is complicated, and the things it contains are far beyond what you can imagine. You can pursue anything here, but there's no such thing as fairness! Haha! "
"What do you mean …?" Luo Zheng did not understand Fatty's words.
Fatty ignored Luo Zheng and waved the staff in his hand. Then, he jumped up and pounced toward Luo Zheng!
At the same time, a white fog appeared under Luo Zheng's feet. The white fog turned into snowflakes and quickly spread out. The temperature around Luo Zheng began to drop rapidly. His entire body became extremely slow.
In comparison, this fatty was actually extremely agile. He appeared in front of Luo Zheng in almost an instant, and a long staff smashed down on his head.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all. He did not even try to block the attack. Instead, he reached out and grabbed the staff.
At this stage, Luo Zheng's opponents were still able to surprise him and crush him with great strength. Luo Zheng did not need to think too much.
Unexpectedly, Fatty revealed a strange smile at this moment. At the same time, the moment Luo Zheng grabbed the staff, he felt a slight chill in his hand. That staff was actually a handful of snowflakes … Then, Luo Zheng turned his hand and chopped at Fatty's face. Unexpectedly, this "Fatty" also turned into a pile of snowflakes and scattered in front of Luo Zheng.
"I knew you had extraordinary strength. However, you are too naive if you think you can defeat me with just strength …"
News about the strength and characteristics of the anonymous person had already spread among the Lower True Gods. Many True Gods had studied Luo Zheng's characteristics. Fatty had obviously come prepared.
Fatty's voice rang out behind Luo Zheng. At the same time, there was a whistling sound. Fatty's concealment technique was unexpectedly unpredictable. In an instant, he had stuck close to Luo Zheng's back and launched an attack at the same time.
Luo Zheng did not say anything. He suddenly twisted his body and turned around to kick Fatty.
"Swish …"
After the kick, Fatty turned into a pile of snowflakes again. After being kicked by Luo Zheng, the scattered snowflakes suddenly fluttered like thousands of tiny silver insects dancing in the air. It was extremely beautiful …
"Wrong again!"
Fatty laughed mischievously. He finally made his move. He appeared behind Luo Zheng again. Little ice marks appeared on the white staff in his hand. Suddenly, he smashed Luo Zheng's back.
However, this staff only lightly touched Luo Zheng's back, leaving a faint ice mark. It did not cause any substantial damage to Luo Zheng.
After he smashed the staff, he quickly rolled away like a ball, creating a distance between him and Luo Zheng. One of his hands was emitting surging cold air. He constantly stroked the top of the staff, as if he was constantly pouring something into it.
His face was still wearing that complacent smile, as if victory was already in his hands.

Chapter 1859
The fat man's hand kept swinging on one end of the long staff. Not long after, the tip of the long staff started to flicker with an icy blue light. Impressively, another Frost Mark appeared on it, exactly the same as the one on Luo Zheng's back.
"I'm coming again. Are you ready?" The fat man laughed, the fat on his face shaking.
Although Luo Zheng's ranking in the Hall of Gods was rapidly rising, according to Luo Zheng's estimation, he shouldn't encounter too much trouble at this stage. After encountering this fat man, Luo Zheng also stopped underestimating his opponent.
As soon as the fat man's voice fell, he rushed toward Luo Zheng like a raging bull, waving the long staff in his hand wildly …
But when he was one foot away from Luo Zheng, he strangely stopped and then turned into a ball of snowflakes, continuously dispersing.
"Still behind?"
Luo Zheng almost didn't hesitate at all, turning around and punching!
What Luo Zheng didn't expect was that he guessed wrong. That ball of dispersing snowflakes once again condensed into that fat man, and the fat man once again pointed the long staff in his hand at Luo Zheng's back!
"Boom!"
Two Frost Marks were left on Luo Zheng's back. These Frost Marks overlapped on his back like tattoos, and Luo Zheng had no way to get rid of them.
The two Frost Marks overlapped, and the color became a layer darker.
"I heard that your skin is rough and your flesh is thick, and you also have a very special protective magic treasure on you. With my strength, I'm afraid it will be difficult to kill you …" After leaving the second Frost Mark, the fat man once again turned into a pile of snow and dispersed. Soon after, a little bit of snow floated out about ten feet away from Luo Zheng, and the fat man once again appeared. At the same time, he continued to say, "But there are many ways to kill people. You don't necessarily need to see blood with every strike, and I hate blood the most …"
After saying this, he waved the long staff in his hand again, and heavily stomped it on the ice surface.
"Crash …"
At the same time, six piles of snow instantly appeared around Luo Zheng.
"All of you, break for me!"
As soon as the six piles of snow appeared, Luo Zheng's hands suddenly waved in all directions like roulette wheels, and an invisible force spread out along his body!
The accumulated snow was blown away by Luo Zheng!
However, as the snow danced in the air, it condensed again and turned into a fat man that pounced on Luo Zheng.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a headache.
This fatty's movement technique was too strange.
It was impossible for a lower True God to teleport, but Luo Zheng could not distinguish the real one from the fake one. What was worse was that Fatty's clones were all made of real snowflakes, not phantoms or illusions.
Seeing the six fatties pouncing towards him, Luo Zheng also threw out six punches in an instant!
"Pa pa pa pa pa pa!"
All six punches landed …
But as soon as he destroyed the six "snowmen", Luo Zheng felt a weak force on his back.
Boom!
The long staff touched Luo Zheng's back again, leaving another Frost Mark.
"Three Frost Marks, it should not be enough. Although you are only a Proof of Divinity martial artist, your comprehensive ability is much stronger than an ordinary lower True God," Fatty said lightly with a strategist's expression. "To defeat you, I need four Frost Marks …"
Luo Zheng felt a sense of crisis in his heart. He did not want his hard-earned 10-win streak to end just like that.
If the Frost Marks accumulated and exploded, Luo Zheng did not know whether he could resist. Of course, he was not willing to take such a risk. Just as Fatty was speaking, he had already silently rushed over.
Facing Luo Zheng, Fatty did not make any dodging movements. Instead, he opened his arms and wanted to hug Luo Zheng.
The moment Luo Zheng punched Fatty, Luo Zheng's face suddenly revealed a strange smile, "Not you, it's this side!" Luo Zheng had already guessed that the fat man in front of him was a fake and was just a pile of snow. He rolled his body and hit the other side, but there was only emptiness. There was nothing there.
Poof!
After Luo Zheng's punch hit the empty space, snowflakes burst out again. Fatty's voice sounded again, "Good eyesight, but unfortunately, you guessed wrong!"
The fat man below waved the long stick in his hand again and lightly tapped Luo Zheng's back again …
The fourth Frost Mark was left on Luo Zheng's back!
Huff huff huff …
Snowflakes continued to fall.
Fatty's voice sounded again in the distance. At the same time, he stared at Luo Zheng with a sarcastic look, "Sure enough, seeing you in person is not as good as hearing about you. During this period of time, people have been crazily spreading rumors about how powerful the anonymous person is. Some people even predicted that you would achieve 20 wins in a row. I did not expect you to be so weak! This battle … I've won! "
After saying that, he stretched out his chubby hand and held Luo Zheng in it. His eyes flashed with excitement, "Actually, I haven't seen a fourth layer Snow Print Ice Spear for a long time. Because usually, the opponent is defeated before it is used!"
At the same time, the Frost Mark on Luo Zheng's back flickered with a faint luster.
Not far behind Luo Zheng, a huge snowflake pattern appeared. It seemed to be nurturing some kind of powerful attack …
Whoosh!
A light blue Ice Spear shot out from it and hit Luo Zheng's body at an extremely fast speed.
It was so fast that Luo Zheng had no way to dodge!
Boom!
However, the power of this Snow Print Ice Spear was not particularly terrifying. Even if Luo Zheng could not dodge, he could still withstand it with his tyrannical body!
"The rumors are true. Your body is much stronger than those ferocious creatures. Even an ordinary Lower True God would be injured when facing the first layer of the Snow Seal Ice Spear." The fatty stuck out his tongue. Seeing Luo Zheng's casual manner, he was speechless. "The second ice spear …"
Whoosh!
From the huge snowflake pattern, the second Ice Spear shot out.
This Ice Spear looked even purer. The body of the Ice Spear had changed from light blue to azure blue. It was as beautiful as the color of the sky, but it contained a strong killing intent.
At this time, Luo Zheng understood that each Snow Print Ice Spear was stronger than the previous one!
After realizing this, Luo Zheng instantly condensed an invisible long sword and shot it at the second Ice Spear.
Strangely, the Snow Print Ice Spear seemed to not exist. It passed by Luo Zheng's invisible long sword, and Luo Zheng was unable to block it …
Boom!
The second Ice Spear also hit Luo Zheng's body accurately.
Although he had the Concentric Garment to protect his body, the impact of the second Ice Spear spread across his body and caused his blood to boil. However, he still managed to withstand it!
"So powerful. I wonder what kind of treasure your garment is. It should have blocked most of the power, right?" Fatty laughed mischievously.
Whoosh …
Just as the fatty was speaking, the third Snow Imprinted Ice Spear shot out from the snowflake patterns. This Snow Imprinted Ice Spear was bluish-black in color, and the tip of the ice spear had streams of light swirling around it!
"Boom …"
Although Luo Zheng tried his best to dodge, it seemed impossible to dodge these Ice Spears. The third Ice Spear still hit Luo Zheng's body without any suspense.
Blue ice shards scattered in all directions. Under the bombardment of the huge force, Luo Zheng's entire body kept sliding on the ice. At the same time, he spat out a stream of blood. The huge force spread through Luo Zheng's body, causing damage to his internal organs!
"What a tenacious life," Fatty clicked his tongue twice and couldn't help but shake his head. "The fourth Ice Spear is enough to kill you …"
In fact, Fatty had only cultivated the "Snow Print Divine Spear" of the Snow Print Divine Dao to the fourth level.
After he defended against the enemy, he only needed to use the "Mirage Snow Steps" to leave three frost marks on the opponent, and the battle would almost be over. Every spear of the Snow Print Divine Spear was twice as powerful as the previous spear. Generally, few opponents could withstand three spears.
However, there were always some monsters in this world. Fatty had once fought with Han Cangyan, and Han Cangyan also took the first two spears. However, he dodged the third spear at an incredible speed, which was also the most fatal spear. Therefore, Fatty had lost the battle with Han Cangyan quite miserably!
This time, Fatty had prepared "four spears" for Luo Zheng. He really valued Luo Zheng very much!
Whoosh whoosh whoosh …
A Snow Print Spear emerged from the snowflake pattern. Although this Snow Print Spear was also condensed from ice and snow, its entire body had turned pure black.

Chapter 1860
After seeing the black Snow Imprinted Divine Spear, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
According to the increasing power of the previous three Snow Imprinted Divine Spears, the fourth Snow Imprinted Divine Spear could seriously injure him, or even kill him!
But this Snow Imprinted Divine Spear could not be stopped, he could only rely on his physical body to resist …
"Xiu!"
As the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear shot out, a faint trace of regret flashed across the fatty's face. "This battle was won too easily. Die, anonymous …"
In the fatty's opinion, Luo Zheng was dead for sure after this black spear shot out.
At this moment, Luo Zheng also mobilized all his attention. He quickly retreated backwards and used both hands to block in front of his chest. His whole body slid forward as if he was flying on a layer of ice!
But the black Snow Imprinted Divine Spear's speed was even faster. In almost an instant, it arrived in front of Luo Zheng.
"Eight Turns Flying Smoke!"
Luo Zheng's body flickered with a faint white mist.
Although the Eight Turns Flying Smoke was ingenious, it had a great limitation. When the opponent's speed was too fast, it could easily break through this kind of movement technique. After all, when Second Senior Brother chose this technique, he only considered the opponents Luo Zheng would face in the universe. He did not consider the present …
Now, Luo Zheng's purpose of using the Eight Turns Flying Smoke was naturally not to dodge the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear, but because the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear contained such a great power. The moment it got close to Luo Zheng, it would still produce a huge force to push him away, thus reducing the power of the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear!
"Huu …"
From the moment the black Snow Imprinted Divine Spear appeared to the moment it got close, Luo Zheng did not have much time to react. In the eyes of ordinary people, it was merely a thousandth of a blink of an eye. In this little bit of time, Luo Zheng had already arranged everything!
"Rumble!"
The moment the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear hit Luo Zheng's arms, the power in his arms instantly erupted.
Although his bones were as strong as an ordinary grandmist treasure artifact, his arms not only had to bear the power of the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear, but they also had to bear the power of Luo Zheng's own body. Under the fusion of the two enormous forces, Luo Zheng's arms were immediately covered with cracks, and they quickly burst open!
Boom!
Although Luo Zheng had paid the price of his arms, the Snow Imprinted Divine Spear's momentum was only slightly reduced. Once again, it bombarded Luo Zheng's chest, where there was the Heart Jacket as a barrier.
The soft Heart Vest was indestructible, and was able to offset the power of the Snow Mark Divine Spear to the greatest extent. However, the powerful penetrating power still penetrated through the Heart Vest, and seeped into Luo Zheng's internal organs...
"Ka ka ka ka ka …"
The ribs in his chest were also broken into pieces, and his chest was directly torn open. If Luo Zheng lowered his head, he could even see his heart beating non-stop.
"Tsk, tsk, tsk, tsk … As expected of the anonymous player with ten consecutive wins, he's still alive!" The fatty shook his head, and his face revealed a rueful expression, "In order to win against you, I lowered my ranking by several hundred thousand. As expected, this trip was not in vain. You actually survived my fourth spear strike. Tell me your name. Our Profound Moon Clan likes little monsters like you. No matter which clan you belong to, we're willing to pay any price to poach you over.
What do you think? "Luo Zheng lay on the ground, panting. Every time he breathed, it squeezed his lungs, and blood spurted out from his chest. He panted as he asked," You lowered your ranking? "
The fatty shrugged his shoulders, "That's right, I've been hovering around the top ten, hehe …"
"Profound Moon Clan … what's your ranking on the floating island?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
From the beginning to the end, he had never heard of this clan.
The fatty's eyes widened, and he stared at Luo Zheng as if he was looking at a monster, "You've really never heard of our Profound Moon Clan! Our clan is ranked third on the floating island! "
It was no wonder that the fatty was speechless.
Luo Zheng had only stepped onto the floating island for a short period of time. After stepping into the Purple Soul Palace, Luo Zheng did not have the time to properly understand the outside world before he began to fight in the Arena of Gods. As such, Luo Zheng did not have a systematic understanding of the top ten clans.
Every time before the floating island's ranking, all the wealthy clans would encourage their disciples to do their best to earn points. However, there were only three clans that were not interested. These three clans were the Profound Moon Clan, the Sword Clan, and the Dongfang Clan.
Starting from the fourth place, the Leng Clan, all the way to the last place on the floating island. Although they were all wealthy clans with Sages, they were still considered secular wealthy clans. This meant that the structure of the wealthy clans was no different from ordinary clans.
However, from the third place onwards, there was a huge change.
Profound Moon was not a surname, but a title. However, as time passed, they were collectively known as the "Profound Moon Clan." In fact, the Profound Moon Clan consisted of four wealthy clans. However, as time passed, these wealthy clans merged into one, and the previous surname was no longer important. In the end, the name "Profound Moon" was created.
The disciples of the Profound Moon Clan did not place much importance on the Arena of Gods' points. The number of Perfect True Gods in the top three clans far exceeded that of ordinary wealthy clans. Usually, they only needed to make a few moves at crucial moments to leave the fourth place, the Leng Clan, in the dust!
As time passed, the wealthy clans in the ordinary sense could not be bothered to compete with the top three clans on the floating island. After all, they could not win.
This Profound Moon Mu noticed that an anonymous participant had appeared in the Hall of Gods and was very curious. However, his ranking was much higher than Luo Zheng's. In order to challenge Luo Zheng, he could only lower his own ranking.
It was very difficult to raise one's ranking in the Hall of Gods, but it was very easy to lower one's ranking. If one met an opponent, one just had to admit defeat.
As a result, his ranking fell from 17th place to a few hundred thousand, and he finally caught up to Luo Zheng. There was also a small interlude. Because Luo Zheng's ranking rose too quickly, he was 10,000 or so lower than Luo Zheng. Helpless, he had to crush two more opponents to rise up …
It was not easy for him to close in on Luo Zheng's ranking to within the top 100. However, he did not expect Luo Zheng to not come out at all and not accept the challenge. Profound Moon Mu was very depressed. This time, he finally saw Luo Zheng open the door. How could he let go of this opportunity? He immediately raised his price and challenged Luo Zheng!
"No wonder." Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Indeed, I did not expect someone to lower their ranking to challenge me …"
Profound Moon Mu's strength was considered to be at the peak of the Lower True Gods. In addition, he had done a thorough investigation of Luo Zheng's fighting style and strength. In addition, Luo Zheng had underestimated him at the beginning. In the end, Luo Zheng was injured to such an extent!
"Accept your fate and join our Profound Moon Clan. No matter which clan you come from, we will give you the best treatment!" Profound Moon Mu smiled very proudly. He was not interested in points. What he liked was the process.
"Why should I accept my fate? Do you think I will lose? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Profound Moon Mu puffed up his cheeks and said, "Do you think you still have a chance now?"
"A chance … Isn't it always there?" Luo Zheng said calmly.
"What do you mean?" Profound Moon Mu asked.
"Look behind you." Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Profound Moon Mu turned his head and suddenly saw a circle …
Just now, as Luo Zheng kept retreating, he drew an oval mark in the ice. Profound Moon Mu still did not understand what Luo Zheng meant.
But as soon as Luo Zheng's voice fell, a wisp of icy blue flame suddenly appeared and rapidly spread around the circle!
"Huala …"
At this moment, the Ice Soul Devil Flame burned and enveloped Luo Zheng and Profound Moon Mu.
"Your methods are indeed very strange. Even now … I can't find where your main body is, but it should be within this circle." Luo Zheng continued, "Then we just need to freeze everything in this circle!"
"This is …" When Profound Moon Mu saw the icy blue flame, shock appeared in his eyes. "This is the Ice Soul Devil Flame! And it is very complete! How can you completely comprehend Jiu Sheng's Ice Soul Devil Flame! "
"Hu hu hu …"
The speed at which the Ice Soul Devil Flame spread was extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, it covered the entire circle.
Luo Zheng didn't answer Xuan Yuemu's question. The Ice Soul Devil Flame was able to display a stronger effect in an extremely cold place. At the very least, the speed at which the Ice Soul Devil Flame was spreading had exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations.

Chapter 1861
Once the Ice Soul Devil Flame entered one's body, it was extremely difficult to expel.
If a normal Lower True God had been frozen like this, Luo Zheng probably would have won long ago.
Perhaps it was because he cultivated the Snow Imprint Divine Way, or perhaps this fatty had some special protective magic treasure, but Mystic Moon Harmony, who had curled up into a ball, lay motionless on the ground. However, the outcome of the Arena of Gods had yet to be decided.

"Crack!"
The frozen Dark Moon Harmony, along with his own body, was crushed by Luo Zheng's foot, turning into a pile of broken pieces …
Then, the world of ice and snow began to dissipate, and Luo Zheng returned to his room. Naturally, all the injuries on his body had also disappeared.
"Eleventh consecutive victory …"
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. He didn't think it would be so difficult.

In terms of gloominess, Dark Moon Harmony was probably even more gloomy!
After retreating from the Arena of Gods, the fat on his round face was constantly trembling. "Ice Soul Devil Flame … How could it be Ice Soul Devil Flame!"
Losing a few hundred Shenwu Coins was nothing much, but losing to a Proof of Divinity martial artist who was participating in the Rank Skipping Battle was something that Xuanyue Miu could not accept!
Many disciples of rich and powerful families had gone to the Forbidden Grounds of God Refinement, and Xuanyue Mu was no exception. In the end, he still failed. Comprehending the Ice Soul Devil Flame in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement was an impossible mission. Could that brat be Saint Jiu's disciple?
After leaving her room, Xuan Yuemu left the Pantheon Jade Wall.
"Hehe, Harmony! I heard that you also lost to that anonymous person? "A long-haired young master in white mocked.

Those who were familiar with Xuan Yuemu knew how tyrannical his Snow Imprint Divine Spear was …
After being stacked three frost imprints, it was almost certain that one would lose.
Four times …
Most likely, many middle True Gods would die.
The anonymous person was actually able to survive and even kill Xuan Yuemu?

The disciples of the floating island could enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement easily. Ordinary citizens of the God Domain didn't have the qualifications to enter the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Those who could enter were extremely lucky.

There weren't many people on the Gu Clan's floating island but almost all of them were monstrous experts.

Because Luo Zheng accepted Xuan Yuemu's ranking, many people saw hope. Thus, the lower True Gods waited in front of Luo Zheng's room with even greater enthusiasm. In order to challenge Luo Zheng, the competition for the top 100 rankings became even more intense.
Some experts even gave up and admitted defeat in order to lower their rankings in order to obtain the qualifications to challenge Luo Zheng.
To everyone's disappointment, Luo Zheng didn't accept any more challenges. He only chose his own opponent.
This way, Luo Zheng's opponents once again returned to their normal level of strength. Naturally, it was much easier for him to win. Thus, his winning streak began again …
On that day, Luo Zheng obtained four consecutive victories. In total, he obtained fourteen consecutive victories. Luo Zheng's total points also reached a terrifying number. He had 112,000 points. Every time he won a match, he would obtain an additional 17,000 points.
As for the lower True Gods who were about the same ranking as Luo Zheng, they usually only had around a thousand points. To others, Luo Zheng's points were simply an astronomical number.
However, 110,000 points wasn't Luo Zheng's limit. According to Aunt Han Jiu's calculations, if he wanted to protect the Purple Soul Palace and change the ranking of the Han Clan's floating island, he would need at least 1.2 million points. His path in the Arena of Gods wasn't short.
In the following days, Luo Zheng only fought two matches.
Four days later …
Luo Zheng had already accumulated twenty-two consecutive victories.
Because Luo Zheng maintained a perfect winning streak, his ranking also rapidly rose. From around 500,000, he quickly rose to the top 10,000, top 1,000, top 100, and finally stopped at 96th!
Moreover, in the last twenty-second match, he directly jumped from 920th to 96th. In other words, no matter how he randomly chose his opponent, his opponent would always be in the top 200.
These people were all at the peak of the lower True Gods. They were also the geniuses of the various large families.
The geniuses naturally rubbed their palms in anticipation of Luo Zheng's challenge. No matter who defeated the anonymous opponent, they would obtain a considerable amount of points!

Chapter 1862
The difference between the top 200 in the Arena of Gods wasn't that great.

Luo Zheng needed to use the God-Slaying Sword Formation to fight against the top 200 lesser true gods.
At present, there was still one and a half months before the floating island would be re-ranked. Luo Zheng, who had already achieved 22 consecutive victories and had 370,000 points, wasn't in such a rush. He just needed to wait for the final period of time and win a few more crucial matches.
That way, even if others found out that Luo Zheng came from Han Jia, it would be too late to target Luo Zheng. This was Aunt Han Jiu's strategy.

Now, Han Liusu was ranked third among the lesser true gods. It was extremely difficult for her to continue winning. Even if she won three or four consecutive victories, she wouldn't get more than a thousand points. It wouldn't change the big picture.
During this time, Han Cangyan had also been persevering in the Arena of Gods. Moreover, her brother had participated in the leapfrog battle. As an intermediate true god, he was challenging an upper true god.
After her brother won three consecutive victories, he suffered three consecutive defeats. Those upper true gods could fly as they wished. Moreover, they were able to cultivate to the level of an upper true god. Their innate talent wouldn't be too bad. Han Cangyan was having a hard time in the leapfrog battle.

Her brother also didn't want to see the Leng family's face. He wanted to use his own strength to help her, but this was beyond Han Cangyan's ability. It was too much!
However, Han Liusu still had a chance.
Recently, an anonymous player had suddenly appeared in the Hall of Pantheon. He had won 22 consecutive victories and accumulated more than 300,000 points in a short period of time. That was an outrageous number.
If this guy won another seven or eight rounds, his points would probably reach a million …
When he defeated the Anonymous One, he would be able to take away 90% of his points. By then, Han Liusu alone would be equivalent to the points of a Great Perfection Paragon. Based on the number of people on the floating island, Han Jia's floating island would definitely be able to maintain its ranking. It might even be able to raise Han Jia's ranking by one or two places!
Everyone was looking forward to Anonymous' winning streak, and Han Liusu was no exception. She was even more eager than the others.
But after five days, Anonymous's ranking was fixed at 96th place. He didn't participate in any more fights, which made Han Liusu very discouraged.
Almost every day, she would enter the Pantheon Jade Wall and leave her room, pacing back and forth in front of Anonymous's room.
It wasn't just Han Liusu. The first ranked Mu Ning, the second ranked Tang Fangyuan, all the way up to the one hundred ninety-sixth ranked Zhao Chen, all of the lower True Gods who had the qualifications or the opportunity to challenge the anonymous person hoped to create a miracle. "Is this Anonymous One scared? He hasn't appeared in the Hall of Pantheon for five days … "

"He probably knows how much he's worth, so he stopped while he's ahead? I don't think there's any hope. Everyone, you don't have to come here every day. Let's disperse … "
Although the person who spoke advised everyone to disperse, that person didn't leave. Clearly, he still had a sliver of hope that Anonymous would suddenly open the door to his room.

Han Xiu Ran was ranked one hundred and thirtieth in the Han Family. In the Han Family, his talent was outstanding. However, his status and strength could not compare to Han Liusu's. Hearing Han Liusu's question, he respectfully nodded towards Han Liusu. "Replying to Eldest Princess, there is no new news. However, Xuan Yuemu seems to know something, but he is not willing to reveal it!"

Those weaker lower true gods might not be able to force out Anonymous's true strength, but Xuan Yuemu should be able to do it!
"Those bastards from the Xuan Yuemu family are very dishonest. In any case, the floating island ranking doesn't matter to them. Xuan Yuemu is very tight-lipped!" Another genius said angrily.
Han Liusu sighed softly. Actually, there were times when Han Liusu suspected that Anonymous might be Luo Zheng …

She had sent people to the Purple Soul Palace several times to inquire about Luo Zheng's recent situation. They all said that Luo Zheng was cultivating under Aunt Han Jiu's guidance …
She wanted to go to the Purple Soul Palace to verify this, but Luo Zheng had been staying in Aunt Han Jiu's palace. Thinking of Aunt Han Jiu's teasing tone, Han Liusu felt uncomfortable and didn't dare go to the Purple Soul Palace.

Han Liusu looked up and saw that it was Qin Tianze standing not far away.

The Qin Family's Holy Emperor helped Qin Tianze suppress the backlash of the Great Wish Art, allowing Qin Tianze to become a lower True God. However, he still had a huge influence. At least, it limited his talent. Qin Tianze was only ranked 21st, but his strength still couldn't be underestimated.

"Not interested," Han Liusu replied coldly.

That guy seems to be called Luo Tianxing, right? "

Chapter 1863
Hearing that voice, the eyes of Han Liusu and the others flashed slightly.
Qin Tianze chuckled. "That Yu Taibai guy is here again! Every time the floating island is ranked, he'll appear once … "
"In order to protect the Luo family's floating island, this guy is really giving it his all!" another genius from a wealthy family said.
"It's said that he's already taken full control of Buzhou Spirit Mountain. His strength has probably increased greatly. Mu Xuerong can be considered unlucky. Just treat it as paying off the Mu family's debt …"
Over the years, the floating island's ranking had undergone many changes.
Most of the Luo family's people had fallen, while a small portion had already left and hid in the forbidden grounds.
However, the Luo family's floating island had never fallen into the Sea of Time. It was even able to maintain its third from the bottom because of Yu Taibai.
When he had left the Sea of Time, he had taken a piece of the Pantheon Jade Wall. Every time the floating island was ranked again, he would continuously challenge a few Perfect True Gods to obtain enough points to protect the Luo family's floating island.
Moreover, his first target was always Mu Xuerong.
Back then, it wasn't just the Mu family that had destroyed the Luo family. It could be said that all the Sage Halls had joined forces. However, Mu Haiji was indeed the one who had rushed to the forefront. This time, it was Mu Haiji who had started the Universal War. Yu Taibai naturally wanted to vent his bad luck on the Mu family.
In the Arena of Gods, True Gods would fight thousands of battles every day. However, the battles that attracted the most attention were probably the battles between Perfect True Gods.
These Perfect True Gods would easily have tens, or even millions of points. The outcome of their battles would often determine the floating island's ranking. This was also why the Sages placed so much importance on nurturing their disciples. The possibility of a disciple becoming a Sage was very small, very small. However, they still played a crucial role.
Normally, a battle between Perfection True Gods would be held in public, and everyone on the floating island would be able to watch it in person. Depending on their cultivation level, the points they would have to pay would also be different. For example, a Proof of Divinity expert would only have to pay five points, but a lower True God would have to pay forty points, a middle True God would have to pay two hundred points, and an upper True God would have to pay six hundred points.
These Consummate Real Gods had cultivated some kind of Divine Path to perfection. Moreover, they usually possessed extraordinary comprehension and extremely powerful Divine Powers outside of Dao. By observing them, one would be able to gain quite a bit of comprehension. Hence, Real Gods usually flocked to them.
Thus, after today's public battle was announced, countless True Gods deducted their points and chose to observe this public battle …
Luo Zheng was very relaxed these days. He took advantage of this time to ponder over the Immeasurable Ruler in his mind!
The last time he had fought with Xuan Yuemu, Luo Zheng had come to understand something. Strength wasn't omnipotent in God's Domain. Strictly speaking, Xuan Yuemu's strength wasn't outstanding. At most, he was just a little bit stronger than Xie Jiao. But Xuan Yuemu's strange techniques had rendered Luo Zheng helpless.
This was especially true for the Snow Imprint Divine Spear that was summoned by the Frost Imprint. Its explosive power had already far exceeded the upper limit of a Lower True God.
The Snow Seal Divine Way wasn't good at this kind of attack. According to Luo Zheng's judgment, the Snow Seal Divine Way must have been a combination of several special Divine Daos. If Luo Zheng had been able to activate the Infinite Ruler at that time, he would have been able to see through the origin of this Divine Way, and from there, he would have been able to counter it. He could have even taken this powerful technique for himself.
Unfortunately, it was already difficult to endure that kind of situation, let alone incorporate this Divine Way.
The Infinite Ruler still lay quietly in Luo Zheng's mind. During this time, the Evil Old Man was afraid that Aunt Hanjiu would find out about his existence, so he sealed himself away …
When the ruler was first stored in Luo Zheng's mind, he used many methods. For example, he used his own soul to wrap it up, or he injected his own consciousness into the Infinite Ruler. He even introduced a trace of chaos into his mind, but this jade ruler still didn't have any reaction.
"The key to activating the Infinite Ruler comes from Dao Connotation. No matter what Divine Dao Connotation it is, even if it's a Divine Way Divine Way's Dao Connotation, it will be activated …" Luo Zheng sank into deep thought. Every time the Infinite Ruler was activated, it was because he had absorbed enough Dao Connotation.
"Up until now, the Infinite Ruler hasn't incorporated the Dao Connotation of the Emotion Severing Divine Way."
His goal wasn't to incorporate the Emotion Severing Divine Way into the Infinite Ruler. After all, he mainly cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Way and didn't need to add another Divine Way to the Infinite Ruler. In the future, he might be able to incorporate all the Divine Daos into the Infinite Ruler, but he wasn't in a hurry for now.
"Then can the Dao Connotation of the Emotion Severing Divine Way activate the Infinite Ruler?"
"If it does, I'll quickly disperse the Dao Connotation …"
After thinking for a few days, Luo Zheng had a flash of inspiration. He felt like he had grasped the crux of the problem.
This method obviously wasn't a proper one. Unfortunately, his father hadn't taught him how to use the Infinite Ruler. He didn't even know what the true ability of this thing was. However, he felt like he could give it a try!
When he thought of this, he closed his eyes. A faint golden light emerged from his soul.
"Selfless!"
When he opened his eyes again, they were as calm as water. There wasn't a trace of emotion in them.
However, he was in Aunt Hanjiu's palace. Although Aunt Hanjiu was someone he could trust, he still didn't want to reveal that he cultivated the Dao Restriction. Therefore, he restrained his aura to the extreme.
Traces of Dao Connotation escaped from Luo Zheng's soul and slowly spread in his mind.
"Is it useful?"
Luo Zheng was constantly paying attention to the changes in the Infinite Ruler. If he hadn't fallen into the Emotion Severing state, he would have been looking forward to it. Now, his face was numb. No emotions could be seen on his face.
"Buzz …"
A trace of light suddenly flickered along the side of the Infinite Ruler. That light constantly wandered around the scales of the Infinite Ruler. Finally, it stopped at the scales at the very end of the Infinite Ruler.
"The Emotion Severing Divine Way is ranked at the end of the 3,000 Divine Daos?"
Luo Zheng also knew that every Divine Way corresponded to a scale on the Infinite Ruler. Moreover, the positions of the scales seemed to be predetermined. They weren't arranged according to the order in which Luo Zheng absorbed the Divine Daos. Now that the golden light stopped at the very end of the Infinite Ruler, that was where the Emotion Severing Divine Way should be.
At this moment, that scale began to absorb the Dao Connotation released by Luo Zheng. At the same time, Luo Zheng felt his eyes heat up. Two golden rings surrounded his pupils and lit up.
"It's done!"
Luo Zheng immediately absorbed all the Dao Connotation in his mind.
His goal was to activate the Infinite Ruler through the Emotion Severing Divine Way. He didn't really want to absorb the Emotion Severing Divine Way into the Infinite Ruler!
After he retrieved all of the Dao Connotation of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao, the golden rings in his eyes continued to flicker. Through this pair of golden rings, there was nothing he didn't understand about the surrounding environment. However, once he saw the Dao Connotation of the Divine Dao displayed by others, he could instantly see through its mystery and absorb it into the Immeasurable Ruler.
Luo Zheng's unintentional attempt had actually succeeded!
When Luo Zheng broke free from the "emotion severing" state, his face was filled with ecstasy. Through this method, many of the problems he encountered would be easily solved. He would also be able to unscrupulously comprehend the Dao Connotations of other True Gods.
The golden rings in his eyes lasted for about an incense stick's worth of time before slowly dimming.
At this moment, Aunt Hanjiu's sound transmission slowly floated over …
Faced with this sudden sound transmission, Luo Zheng thought that his actions had been discovered by Aunt Hanjiu. Just as he was feeling nervous, he heard Aunt Hanjiu say, "Yu Taibai wants to challenge Mu Xuerong. It's a public battle. You can enter the Pantheon Jade Wall and observe."
"Yu Taibai!" Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly jumped.
Back in the Central Domain, it was this youth who had taken away his little sister. That youth had only used the strength of a finger to send him flying several hundred miles …
At that time, Luo Zheng secretly swore in his heart that he would definitely defeat this guy one day.
He never would have thought that this youth was his father's chief disciple, Yu Taibai.
"Wasn't the Luo Family's floating island destroyed? How can Yu Taibai still enter the Arena of Gods to challenge Mu Xuerong? " Luo Zheng was very puzzled, but after Aunt Hanjiu sent him a sound transmission, she fell silent and didn't answer his question.
Luo Zheng didn't delay any longer. He hurriedly entered the Pantheon Jade Wall.
Not long after, Luo Zheng paid 40 points and stepped into a huge world.
A blood-red sun hung at the edge of this world. The entire world seemed to have been washed by red blood, and the pungent smell of blood assailed his nostrils.
Although this was a fictional space, it seemed that everything in it was absolutely real.
"So … many people." Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless expression. Around him, there was a crowd of spectators. There were martial artists who had proven themselves to be gods, lower true gods, and middle true gods. At a glance, there was an uncountable number.

Chapter 1864

Han Liusu also coldly glanced at the woman in black. This woman had always been so rude and unreasonable.

Mu Ning, a woman from the Mu Family …

Among the younger generation of the lower True God Realm, Mu Ning and Han Liusu were the two most famous geniuses on the floating islands.

Mu Ning was extremely arrogant, and she didn't take Han Liusu seriously. If others compared her to Han Liusu, she would be very unhappy.

Last time, Luo Zheng and Xuan Yuemu had agreed to a bet of 350 Shenwu coins!
In the end, after Luo Zheng won against Xuan Yuemu, that damn fatty disappeared without a trace. With Luo Zheng's current identity as an anonymous person, it was impossible for him to find the Xuan Yuemu Clan's floating island.
If he really did win against Mu Ning and still wasn't willing to show himself, it would be better for him to give the bet to Han Liusu.
As for whether or not he was a member of the Han Clan, they could guess as much as they wanted.
Mu Ning coldly glanced at Han Liusu. Luo Zheng's words were undoubtedly a great humiliation to her, but for the sake of challenging Luo Zheng, she gritted her teeth and agreed.
"Then that's fine." Luo Zheng shrugged, then walked over to Han Liusu and smiled. "You also said that you wanted to challenge me?"
Han Liusu had no idea what was going on. She was still a little stunned, but since the anonymous person had taken the initiative to ask her, she nodded. "En."

Chapter 1865

In her left hand was a slender short sword. This short sword looked very ordinary, as if there was nothing special about it.

Like a block of wood, Yu Taibai strangely moved a few inches to the side, easily dodging the bloody arc. That arc continued to expand in the air. It expanded again and again, from a few feet to a few dozen feet, and then to a few hundred feet. After the energy contained within was completely released, it turned into a bloody tsunami that could topple mountains and overturn seas. It extended all the way to the ends of the world before slowly calming down.

Chapter 1866
Mu Xuerong was like a nimble deer. With a short saber in one hand and a short sword in the other, she slashed out like a shuttle.
As for Yu Taibai, he seemed to be able to calculate everything. With the simplest movements, he dodged Mu Xuerong's attacks.
The two of them had fought so many times, and each time was a life or death struggle. Thus, they were very familiar with each other.
But in comparison, Yu Taibai was much calmer. He allowed Mu Xuerong to attack as he dodged …
Huala …
Every time Mu Xuerong's saber slashed through the air, it would set off a monstrous wave of blood that constantly spread out in the boundless space.
To the juniors of the Realm of the Gods, this sort of scene was extremely rare. Only those great perfection masters would be able to see it in public battles.
But to Luo Zheng, he had seen this scene many times. After all, in the Great Evolution Universe, the destruction that those Empyreans could cause with a wave of their hands was far more terrifying than what he was seeing now.
However, the Great Evolution Universe was still an inner world; it couldn't compare with the Realm of the Gods.
Those Empyreans might be able to destroy a great world in the universe, but in the Realm of the Gods, they might not even be able to flip over a mountain.
The speed at which Mu Xuerong and Yu Taibai exchanged blows became increasingly fast. The two of them quickly circled in the air. Then, without any warning, Mu Xuerong's figure suddenly vanished from behind Yu Taibai and reappeared above his head. At the same time, a bright red saber light slashed down.
"Space jump …" Luo Zheng curled his lips.
Perhaps Mu Xuerong's move was outside of Yu Taibai's expectations, or perhaps Yu Taibai had miscalculated something, but when Mu Xuerong slashed down from above, Yu Taibai wasn't able to dodge in time!
At this time, Yu Taibai's eyes flashed with a trace of surprise. He grasped out with one hand, catching Mu Xuerong's saber light.
Ka ka ka ka!
Just how terrifying was the power of that saber light?

However, the look on his face became much more cautious. At the same time, he retreated, shuttling through space to create some distance between him and Mu Xuerong.

Luo Zheng didn't think much of it. He couldn't understand what the two were talking about. But when it reached the audience, it immediately caused an uproar.
"Yu Taibai actually miscalculated!"

Hearing these words, Luo Zheng finally understood.
Why was Yu Taibai able to easily kill Mu Xuerong fifteen times?
He relied on the Great Yan Divine Dao!
Luo Xiao was the first person to completely comprehend the Great Yan Divine Dao, becoming the first person to leave his name on the Dao tablet of the Great Yan Divine Dao.
This was because after Luo Xiao became a Saint, many people flocked to the Great Yan Divine Dao. But, those that could truly comprehend the Great Yan Divine Dao were few and far between. This was because the Great Yan Divine Dao was too ethereal and difficult to comprehend. And, even if one did comprehend it, it was difficult to use.
Luo Xiao had established the glory of the Great Yan Divine Dao and used it to become a Saint. But, to what extent he had used the Great Yan Divine Dao, not many people knew. In the Realm of the Gods, Saints basically didn't do anything …
In comparison, Yu Taibai's Great Yan Divine Dao was much more well-known to the gods!
With a single hand, he could calculate his opponent's height, cultivation, strength, and even the lighting, environment, and all other possibilities. With this, he could calculate his opponent's movements using the Great Yan Divine Dao.
Mu Xuerong had cultivated the Killing God Dao to perfection, and her strength was extremely fierce. In the entire Realm of the Gods, besides Saints and Quasi-Saints, there were no other Perfect Saints that could defeat her.
But, Yu Taibai had defeated her fifteen times.
Every time she attacked, every blink of an eye, every heartbeat, every small movement, all of it had been perfectly calculated by Yu Taibai, without a single mistake …
This ability of Yu Taibai's was hailed as a miracle by countless True Divinities of the Realm of the Gods.
There were even some who said that Yu Taibai could use the Great Yan Divine Dao to calculate the future. The reason he protected Buzhou Spirit Mountain and protected the Luo Family was because he had calculated a clear future. Thus, he persisted in helping the Luo Family. There would be a day when Luo Xiao would 'revive'.
To say that Yu Taibai could calculate all of his opponent's methods was not an exaggeration. But, to say that he could calculate the future of the Realm of the Gods was a bit too exaggerated.
If a leaf floated down from a tree and one knew the height of the branch and the strength of the wind, one could calculate where the leaf would land. Even if one didn't use the Great Yan Divine Dao, some talented True Divinities could calculate this.
But, the Realm of the Gods had countless True Divinities and countless divine citizens. There were also countless variables. No matter how formidable the Great Yan Divine Dao was, how could it possibly be calculated?
No matter what, this was the first time Yu Taibai had miscalculated against Mu Xuerong.
"It's good that you miscalculated." Mu Xuerong revealed a proud expression. "This time, I'll let that tattered Luo Family's floating island sink into the sea of time. It's just too much of an eyesore …"

"Since you already know, don't waste any more energy." Mu Xuerong's voice resounded in the air.

"Pu!"

"I already said that Mu Xuerong lost because of the Great Yan Divine Dao's calculations. In a direct confrontation, Yu Taibai might not be stronger than her!"
"What happened to Yu Taibai's crushing? I bet my entire fortune on Yu Taibai. "
Seeing this, Luo Zheng revealed a trace of worry. Yu Taibai wouldn't really die, but would the Luo Family's floating island fall into the sea of time because of his failure?
Yu Taibai's expression was calm. He reached out his hand and gently touched his chest. The two halves of his body quickly merged back into one, and the shocking wound disappeared.
Yu Taibai said seriously, "The floating island of the Luo Clan won't fall into the Sea of Time … You lost to me fifteen times before you managed to break my 'How's Divination?'
