"This is the source of power flowing through his veins, as if it's replacing his blood … How did Yu Taibai do it?" Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was incomparably shocked.

Chapter 1867
Body refinement arts weren't valued too much in the divine realm, and even the divine path of strength wasn't very popular.
Traditional true gods still obediently condensed godhood, cultivating all the way to perfection, and then working hard to become saints.
This was a path that had been proven by countless gods. Although from ancient times until now, only a few people could stand at the peak, it was still a path of success.
However, there had never been a lack of people in this world who had taken a different path. Back then, Luo Xiao had walked the Grand Divination Divine Dao alone, and he had also calculated a Grand Divination Universe. However, people like Luo Xiao were extremely rare in the end. Those paths that had never been stepped on by others were filled with an unknown fog, and it might even be a huge trap. The difficulty was obvious.
Just like the chaos secret arts Luo Zheng cultivated, it was still difficult to condense godhood. If he hadn't encountered that strange mud, Luo Zheng probably still wouldn't have any clues.
Whispers also sounded around Luo Zheng …
"Yu Taibai actually used such a strange body refinement art!"
"This completely exceeds the eighth layer of Dao Pedestal and nine stars of Spiritual Pedestal Tier. What's up with that power source, how can it replace blood!"
"That's not Yu Taibai's path. Isn't he good at calculating the opponent's flaws, and using the simplest method to kill in one strike?"
Yu Taibai always gave people the impression of a man of few words, a gentle and modest person.
When the Luo Family's floating island was destroyed, Yu Taibai also left without a word, but when he turned back, his gentle eyes were filled with reluctance. Then, he disappeared from the sight of the rich and powerful families, and was even forgotten for a time.
It was also during that time that the Luo Family's floating island's ranking fell again and again.
Just when the rich and powerful families thought that the Luo Family's floating island would fall into the sea of time next time, Yu Taibai strangely appeared in the Arena of Gods, and with his own strength, almost carried the burden of the entire Luo Family's floating island.
Afterwards, the Luo Family's floating island was fixed at the third from the bottom, never falling even one place, miraculously maintaining it for so many years.
This was a man's loyalty and tenacity.
But today, for some reason, he showed his muscles for the first time in front of the gods.
The muscles all over his body swelled crazily, giving off a sense of power and beauty. However, his head didn't change at all. His huge and broad body seemed a little out of place with a small head.
When Mu Xuerong saw this, she almost reflexively jumped backwards. Her figure seemed to be in a trance as she shuttled through space three times, creating a distance of several thousand feet as she stared at Yu Taibai in front of her.
For Mu Xuerong to mislead Yu Taibai's "How's the calculation", she was indeed very proud of herself.
Mu Xuerong had always felt that her strength was above Yu Taibai's. It wasn't that she didn't have a chance of defeating him. It was just that her "divination" had already calculated everything.
Her Blood Moon and Mortal Sword were both top-notch grade-one grandmist treasures …
Her comprehension of the God-Slaying Dao continued to make breakthroughs, and she had almost comprehended the Dao of Ultimate Killing between heaven and earth.
But when faced with "How's the derivation", she was almost unable to break through.
Today, she had broken through "How's the derivation", and finally thought that she had won. This Yu Taibai no longer had any means to fight against her. She would give Yu Taibai the humiliation of the past and kick away the biggest stumbling block in the depths of her heart.
She never thought that she would release an incomprehensible monster. Yu Taibai gave her a sense of danger that even surpassed some Saints.
Yu Taibai indifferently looked at Mu Xuerong. His eyes were still gentle, like the sun in the human world, giving people a faint feeling of comfort and contentment. But it made Mu Xuerong feel a chill in her heart. "Today, I'll have to trouble you to cooperate …"
As soon as his voice fell, Yu Taibai disappeared.
The perfection stage of Spatial Transference was different from the greater teleportation and space shuttling that Luo Zheng had comprehended in the universe. There were almost no signs, and there were no so-called spatial fluctuations, so once one moved, their speed was much faster.
Just as Yu Taibai disappeared, Mu Xuerong opened her arms. Although she didn't understand the Great Expansion Divine Dao and didn't know how to use "How's the derivation", she knew that Yu Taibai's next step would be to pounce on her.
As soon as she opened her arms, her entire body began to spin, like a blood-red whirlwind as she attacked behind her.
"Kacha, kacha …"
At the same time, Yu Taibai's figure appeared behind Mu Xuerong, his hand reaching out to grab her. Mu Xuerong had correctly calculated Yu Taibai's movements, and she was delighted.
By relying on the sharpness of the Condensed Blood Moon and the Mortal Sword, by relying on her own Killing God Dao, by relying on her own killing edge …
She wanted to cut Yu Taibai to pieces!
But she had forgotten that Yu Taibai was still able to calculate her reaction.
At this moment, she didn't try to avoid Yu Taibai's "How's the derivation", so Yu Taibai was able to calculate every word that she excitedly shouted in her heart, and he was even able to calculate her counterattack.
Yu Taibai only moved for an instant. His thick right hand passed through Mu Xuerong's sharp aura and grabbed her by the waist.
Mu Xuerong's waist was very thin, and he could completely grasp it with a single hand.
However, her reaction was also extremely fast. In the face of an extremely unfavorable situation, she actually calmed down. Only a ten-thousandth of a blink of an eye had passed since Yu Taibai had grabbed her, and blood colored scales had already begun to appear on her body.
In order to defeat Yu Taibai, she had prepared far too many things. Since Yu Taibai was willing to face her head on, there was no need for her to hold back!
The Killing God Dao's most powerful aspect was its frontal attack. Almost no True Divinity was willing to face a True Divinity who cultivated the Killing God Dao head on.
The consciousness in her eyes had completely fallen. At this moment, countless tentacles extended from her dantian and continued to spread throughout her body. Wherever they passed, the structure of her body would change accordingly. What others would see was that her body was shrouded in dense blood-red scales …
"What kind of divine ability is this? The Killing God Dao doesn't have such a divine ability … "
"Look at the scales on her body. It seems to be a God-Devil Sacred Body, but I've never seen such a strange God-Devil Sacred Body …"
"She has at least fused three different Divine Daos. I didn't think that Mu Xuerong would be so strong. The Mu Family truly has high hopes for her."
The people watching today's public battle all thought that it would be a repeat of the previous battle, but what they saw was completely different from what they had imagined. Both sides were using unique skills that had never appeared before.
Mu Ning tightly clenched her fists, staring at her big sister in the distance. She hadn't thought that her big sister would use her own unique skill so quickly.
Mu Ning remembered that her father had said that with this move, she would be immortal and nearly invincible for a short period of time …
But just as this thought flashed through Mu Ning's mind, the scene in front of her changed her mind.
Yu Taibai used his left hand to grab Mu Xuerong, and his right arm suddenly flashed with a multi-colored light. His fist danced as it smashed towards Mu Xuerong.
There was a certain drawback to using the source of strength, and that was that it couldn't be completely fused with one's body …
But Yu Taibai's understanding of the Divine Dao of Strength and the Body Refining Technique seemed to have risen to another level!
It even surpassed the horizons of those Saints …
Even if Mu Xuerong's unique skill was known as immortal and indestructible, she still couldn't withstand it.
"Boom …"
Yu Taibai's fist smashed into Mu Xuerong's body. Black spikes began to spread out from Mu Xuerong's body. These were ripples created by the violent shaking of space, and these ripples actually spread out for dozens of miles!
Kacha, kacha …
Mu Xuerong could be considered to have strengthened her body to a terrifying degree. Even after taking this punch, her body didn't disintegrate. Instead, countless cracks appeared on the blood-red scales on her body. At the same time, she began to plummet downwards at an extremely fast speed.
But just as she fell 20 or 30 feet, Yu Taibai appeared beneath Mu Xuerong once more.
"Boom …"
As the massive spatial ripples spread out, a second punch smashed into Mu Xuerong's body. By this time, more than half of the blood-red scales on her body had disappeared.
"Boom …"
When Yu Taibai smashed out a third punch, the scales on Mu Xuerong's body had completely disappeared. Her body collapsed in front of everyone, leaving behind only the massive black ripples that continued to reverberate and spread out. It was as if these spatial ripples were too intense, as if they would continue to spread out through this imaginary space forever.

Chapter 1868

Although there were True Gods who had fallen in the Forbidden Grounds, any family or True God would still scramble like a flock of ducks.

In this fictional world, there were still some hidden existences that were undetectable by others.
The Saints Saints from the wealthy families to Yu Taibai ….

Every time they explored the Buzhou Spirit Mountain, the disciples of the Tang family would always gain the most.

There were many large and small forbidden lands in the Divine Realm, and there were even some small forbidden lands that had yet to be fully explored. There might be a big chance among them.

Chapter 1869
In the Mu family, the doors of the Killing God Temple were pushed open.

This stone slab was connected to the entire mountain, and it was wrapped in strange lightning. This lightning looked very ordinary, but it contained a powerful force. Even a Saint couldn't resist it.

Mu Xuerong didn't go up the mountain. She looked up at the stone slab, then circled behind the mountain and entered a huge cave.

The mud at the bottom of the Thousand Beast Golden Luan Statue was rather big, but he didn't know if it was enough for him to successfully condense his Godhood …

He was still participating in the cross-realm battles. If his cultivation really made a breakthrough, he would lose the qualifications to do so. Devouring this mud should be after the rankings of the floating islands were decided.
…
There were only thirteen days left until the rankings of the floating islands were decided.
During this period of time, the number of public battles in the Arena of Gods gradually increased.
Those who fought in the public battles were all Perfection True Gods from the Domain of the Gods. They were also the strongest experts on the various floating islands apart from the Sages and Quasi-sages.
The disciples of the various wealthy families were also flocking to the public battles. Almost every arena was filled to the brim …
For example, Tang Wan from the Tang family defeated Zhou Muqun from the Zhou family …
For example, Fang Henwan from the Fang family completely defeated Li Lianyi.
For example …
Luo Zheng didn't miss any of these Perfection True Gods battles. He also had a general understanding of the grudges between these wealthy families.
The wealthy families on the floating islands were roughly divided into three factions.
Among them, the wealthy families that destroyed the Luo family formed one faction. Furthermore, it was led by the Dongfang family.
The Han family and the Li family were considered independent wealthy families. Normally, they wouldn't ally with other wealthy families.
However, recently, the Han family seemed to have plans to ally with the Leng family. Han Liusu and Leng Linyue's engagement was proof. However, they didn't expect that there would be such a huge rift between the two.
As for the Xuanyue family and the Sword family, these two wealthy families ranked second and third respectively, their origins were more special. The experts on these two floating islands came from the four sacred trees.
The Domain of the Gods had four poles. They were the four supporting trees.
Under the nourishment of the Ruo Wood, Fusang Wood, Jianmu Wood, and Xun Wood, many powerhouses were born. It was said that before the Sea of Time appeared, the Four Great Divine Trees were the gathering place of powerhouses. With the appearance of the Governing Slate, the powerhouses also discovered the importance of rules. Finally, they established floating islands in the Sea of Time, attracting powerhouses from all over the world. The Sea of Time became the center of the divine territory …
What surprised everyone was that the Gu family's two Perfection True Gods didn't participate in the public battle this time.
One had to know that every time the floating islands were ranked, these two Perfection True Gods would receive the most attention. The Gu family only had a few people on their floating island. Compared to the other wealthy families, their points were definitely far behind. However, Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng were able to push the Gu family to the eighth position.
Someone once said that if Yu Shenfeng and Jing Wuhuan were placed in other wealthy families, they would be able to bring their families into the top three!
This year, Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng were nowhere to be seen. If they didn't participate in the battle, the Gu family's floating island ranking would definitely fall out of the top ten.
There were only three days left until the floating island ranking …
Luo Zheng finally entered the Pantheon Jade Wall again. When he entered his room, he saw 196 people challenging him.

Chapter 1871
Seeing this scene on the wall, Luo Zheng was speechless.
In other words, he didn't need to find an opponent on the spot. He only needed to accept the challenge. There were one hundred and ninety-six people he could choose.
"Do I really look that weak …" Luo Zheng rubbed his nose.
The one hundred and ninety-six people who challenged Luo Zheng naturally didn't think that Luo Zheng was weak.
The Hall of the Gods was a very reasonable system. Through constant adjustments to the rankings, it could accurately reflect a True God's true strength.
Of course, an anomaly like Luo Zheng was naturally not included. This was because from the first day he entered the Jade Wall of the Gods, he had never lost a single battle. His true ranking didn't exist at all. There was no record of this anonymous person in the Jade Wall of the Gods. It was just that the changes in the room placed him at ninety-sixth place.
"Since I can freely find my own opponent …"
Luo Zheng reached out and slowly moved his hand along the wall. These names were neatly arranged in front of him. Finally, his finger stopped at a name at random. Fang Xiaoyu, ranked one hundred and ninety-sixth.
The difference in strength between the top two hundred rich and powerful disciples wasn't too great.
However, in order to obtain more consecutive victories, Luo Zheng naturally chose the weakest opponent, even if the opponent wasn't really weak.
Inside the Hall of the Gods …
This contest for the floating island's ranking had reached its most intense moment.
Some rich and powerful families estimated that the points were in their favor and told their disciples to try their best not to accept challenges, so as to avoid losing points if they were defeated.
Other rich and powerful families felt that their ranking would drop, so naturally, they forced their disciples to fight in the Hall of the Gods!
The Fang family was ranked sixth on the floating island, but they were very ambitious. The Dongfang family, the Sword family, and the Xuanyue family, these three giants, were unshakable. However, if the Fang family worked hard, they had the qualifications to push the Leng family down. Therefore, the Fang family's True Gods were very active this time.
Today, Leng Yu was also looking for an opponent in the Hall of the Gods. She had already obtained four consecutive victories, and her ranking had risen from two hundred and forty-seventh to one hundred and ninety-sixth. Then, she immediately issued a challenge to the anonymous person.
The anonymous person carried a huge amount of points. If she defeated the anonymous person, her contribution to the Fang family would be about the same as the family's Great Perfection. Everyone wanted to obtain this glory and the highest reward from their family.
If he lost, he would only lose a few dozen points …
However, she also knew that the chances of the anonymous person choosing her were slim.
What she didn't expect was that Anonymous actually accepted her challenge two hours after she issued it to him this morning.
Seeing the light flickering on the wall, she was slightly stunned. Then, her face revealed a surprised and happy expression.
After a short while …
She entered the Arena of the Gods.
As the surrounding environment changed, Luo Zheng also quietly entered the Arena of the Gods. Then he saw a world filled with drizzling rain. There were countless pavilions and towers in this world, and they were completely immersed in the rain. As he breathed in gently, the unique fragrance of the rain immediately wafted into his nose.
"This world is not bad," Luo Zheng said after looking around.
He knew that one could choose the venue in the Arena of Gods. Some of the rich and powerful would choose a venue that was beneficial to them. However, in this misty world, other than having a good environment and atmosphere, there was probably no other use for it …
After a short while, Fang Xiaoyu appeared opposite Luo Zheng. Seeing the anonymous person, she smiled. "How lucky. Why did you accept my challenge?"
This woman looked gentle and had a pitiful look. Hearing her words, Luo Zheng calmly replied, "Because you are ranked the lowest."
Fang Xiaoyu smiled faintly, then shook her head. "Then I'm afraid you've chosen the wrong person."
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
Fang Xiaoyu suddenly spread out her hand, and a drop of clear rainwater fell into her palm. This raindrop swayed gently, as if it had the power of life. It slowly jumped and attracted the raindrops around it, turning into a transparent aqua-colored dagger.
She gently waved her five fingers, and this aqua-colored dagger floated up and aimed at Luo Zheng. It began to spin continuously. At the same time, she said, "The anonymous person is most proficient in pure strength. His soul is also very strong. In addition, he has comprehended the complete Ice Soul Devil Flame. That is Sage Jiu's legacy. With these abilities … you are no match for me."
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly, his face full of surprise. The only person who knew that he could use the Ice Soul Devil Flame was that fatty, Xuan Yuemu. "Xuan Yuemu told you?"
Fang Xiaoyu's beautiful eyes revealed a trace of pity. "Don't you know? That damned fatty kept this information a secret at first. Later on, he sold it for money … "
Xuan Yuemu was, after all, an expert ranked 20th. The anonymous person had accumulated a large number of points, so he naturally attracted attention. Even the elders of the rich and powerful families were willing to obtain first-hand information about the anonymous person. At first, Xuan Yuemu was too lazy to reveal it, but under the temptation of Shenwu Coins, he opened his mouth.
Roughly all the lower-level True Gods ranked in the top 200 had a copy of Luo Zheng's information. They even had an analysis of Luo Zheng's strength and weaknesses.
"You do know how to make money." Luo Zheng shook his head.
"If you are as Xuan Yuemu said," Fang Xiaoyu waved her hand lightly, and thousands of raindrops began to gather. With Luo Zheng as the center, countless water-colored short swords appeared in a radius of a thousand feet. She continued, "Then I will be very honored to end your winning streak!"
"Swish swish swish swish swish …"
The water-colored short swords were like a torrential downpour, flying toward Luo Zheng from all directions …
"Even if the water in the divine territory is much more dense than the water in the universe, can it really hurt people?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a strange expression.
The densely packed water-colored short swords shot toward him. It was almost impossible to dodge. Luo Zheng really did not dodge and allowed these "water swords" to hit him.
"Pu pu pu pu …"
These water swords had almost no power to speak of and could not cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
However, Fang Xiaoyu still had a faint smile on her face, as if victory was in her hands.
"Hmm?"
Soon, Luo Zheng also discovered the problem.
After these water swords hit him, they turned into a puddle of rainwater. However, these rainwater did not flow along his body to the ground. Instead, they stuck to his body.
After realizing this, Luo Zheng shook his body slightly, trying to shake off the rainwater. However, he did not expect that the rainwater would stick to his body like glue and could not be shaken off …
"This water is very strange." Luo Zheng frowned slightly. After having a bad premonition, he did not delay any longer.
Previously, when he faced Xuan Yuemu, Luo Zheng had suffered a great loss. These top geniuses from wealthy families all had very special unique skills. With this thought, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed and he rushed toward Fang Xiaoyu.
"You only realized the problem now? It's a little late … "
Fang Xiaoyu stood on her tiptoes and quickly slid backward. She constantly retreated on the rain-filled street, causing waves of water to splash.
The splashes of water also turned into short swords made of rainwater and continued to shoot toward Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not chase her very far. The rainwater that stuck to his body was increasing. A thick layer of water covered his body and he could not shake it off. It also greatly hindered Luo Zheng's speed. How could he catch up to Fang Xiaoyu?
"Burn it for me." Luo Zheng frowned and a young flame brewed in his body. The blazing flame had just spouted out when it was extinguished in an instant!
"Li Water is the bane of all divine fires in the world, not to mention your young flame that has not yet grown." Fang Xiaoyu shook her head. "It is impossible to break free from Li Water with just strength. I want to know what ability you have to fight against me?"
As she spoke, the rainwater that hit Luo Zheng's body had already formed a water ball about ten feet in size, tightly wrapping Luo Zheng in it.

Chapter 1872
Fang Xiaoyu stood not too far away, looking at Luo Zheng with a mischievous look on her face.
Countless raindrops continued to converge, transforming into rainwater swords that shot towards Luo Zheng. The ball of water enveloping Luo Zheng also grew larger and larger...
After sizing up Luo Zheng for a while, she suddenly lowered her head and said, "Actually, if I think about it carefully, defeating you is meaningless."
Separated by a thick layer of water, Fang Xiaoyu's voice sounded somewhat hazy in Luo Zheng's ears. However, Luo Zheng also revealed a strange expression, not understanding what this woman meant.
Fang Xiaoyu slowly walked towards the large ball of water. She extended a finger and lightly tapped the edge of the ball of water. A small whirlpool appeared at the edge of the ball of water. This whirlpool constantly rotated, forming a fist-sized hollow passage that led directly to Luo Zheng's head. Her control over water had already reached the realm of perfection.
"The divine realm is so boring. It wasn't easy for a twenty-two-match winning streak to appear. It would be a pity to lose to me." Fang Xiaoyu smiled at the passage.
"Are you sure you can defeat me?" Luo Zheng asked in return.
He couldn't speak in the water, but this passage enveloped his head, so he could naturally speak.
Even if his movements were limited, this water alone couldn't kill him. It couldn't possibly drown him, right?
Fang Xiaoyu smiled. "If I have the ability to trap you, then I naturally have the ability to kill you. Let me ask you, do you want to defeat me?"

Hearing this, Luo Zheng's expression became strange again.
What were these disciples of the Realm of the Gods thinking all day long? This Fang Xiaoyu looked very normal, so why would she make such a request?
"You don't care about points?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Points? Of course I care. "Fang Xiaoyu smiled." If I defeat you, I can obtain a large amount of points. Even my big brother will have a whole new level of respect for me. But what's the use? The floating island's ranking actually doesn't have much meaning … "
Fang Xiaoyu's brother was Fang Henwan, who had obtained two consecutive victories in the open battle. This time, the Fang family's momentum was very fierce. The Fang family, which was originally ranked sixth, might be able to reach fifth or even fourth place.
"Now that I think about it, it's more interesting for an anonymous person to appear out of nowhere and remain undefeated in a battle against someone of a higher level." Fang Xiaoyu's face revealed a desolate expression. "This divine territory … is too boring."
The highest pursuit of many living beings was eternity.
From the mortals of the universe to the Divine Sea Realm, and then to the Celestial Venerables, all of them pursued a higher level of longevity and broke free from the shackles of time.
But whether it was an Empyrean with a lifespan of over a hundred million years or a mortal with a lifespan of 60 or 70 years, no one could resist time. Sooner or later, they would be killed by time. Time was the greatest enemy of all life.
However, since the beginning of the God Domain, everything had changed.
The lifespan of God's people was not short, and once they proved their Divine Dao, the meaning of their lives would be sublimated. This was a leap from "limited" to "infinite", thus completing the ultimate pursuit of all living beings!
But once this pursuit was satisfied, many living beings would lose their goal.
Luo Zheng had stepped onto the floating island from the Vast Sky Territory in a very short time, so he did not see the negative side of the God Domain …
In fact, a portion of the True Gods in the God Domain quickly fell into depression. They could build their own world, and they could experience the different flavors of life in their own world. They could easily possess everything.
One divine era was like this, two divine eras were like this, a hundred, a thousand, ten thousand … Time had lost its meaning to True Gods. Some True Gods finally understood that perhaps this was not a wonderful thing.
Many True Gods quietly passed through two or three divine eras, then chose to end their own lives by self-destruction.
Of course, even more True Gods continued to climb towards the peak of the Divine Dao, hoping to cultivate to perfection and become a Saint one day. They would then establish a floating island and become the pillar of a wealthy family.
As for Fang Xiaoyu, who was born into a wealthy family, she had an elder brother who doted on her very much. In her eyes, the God Domain, which had not changed for countless divine eras, was so boring. Thus, she was interested in anything that could exceed her expectations.
For example, this anonymous person who had won twenty-two matches in a row before her. When the anonymous person chose her, she was naturally extremely excited. At first, she wanted to snatch the points from Luo Zheng. In fact, she had made ample preparations, but now she changed her mind.
"Sorry, I can't fulfill this request of yours." Luo Zheng shook his head. It was normal for other people to have strange thoughts in their minds, but Luo Zheng had no reason or time to play with them.
Fang Xiaoyu's face revealed disappointment. "I promise I won't tell anyone …"
"Impossible." How could Luo Zheng believe a stranger's promise when it was a matter of life and death? Although, in Luo Zheng's opinion, the possibility of Fang Xiaoyu keeping a secret was very high.
"Then I can only kill you." Fang Xiaoyu raised her eyebrows slightly and threatened.
Looking at her serious expression, Luo Zheng suddenly smiled. Then, he slowly raised his hand and pointed his palm at Fang Xiaoyu. "You seem to have fallen into a misunderstanding. Do you think that by trapping me with this water, you can definitely win?"
"According to the information given by Xuan Yueji, that is indeed the case," Fang Xiaoyu said seriously.
"What a naive child …" Luo Zheng sighed.
These children of wealthy families grew up in the best environment and rarely fell into mutual deception. When they truly possessed strength, they could rely on their powerful strength to crush their opponents. Those cunning tricks were indeed useless against them.
As soon as his voice fell, an invisible force appeared in front of Luo Zheng's palm. That invisible force quickly pushed away the surrounding water, and the shape of a sword slowly condensed.
Fang Xiaoyu did not care too much. She only thought that Luo Zheng had taken out an ordinary magic weapon. She sighed softly. "Then I can only make a move …"
After saying that, Fang Xiaoyu suddenly stretched out her hand and the huge water ball wrapped around Luo Zheng suddenly twisted. Black water droplets constantly formed on the surface of the huge water ball, turning into four dark turbid currents that slowly surged in the water ball, like four rapidly wriggling poisonous insects crawling toward Luo Zheng.
The Five Elements Divine Dao was a fusion of the five most common laws.
Metal, wood, water, fire, and earth were also a separate Divine Dao, such as the "Fledgling Fire Divine Dao", or the "Li Water Divine Dao" that Fang Xiaoyu cultivated.
In the eyes of many True Gods outside the Time Sea, the Li Water Divine Dao was not considered powerful. At least this Divine Dao was good at assisting others, and its power was considered low.
But in fact, after cultivating the Li Water Divine Dao to a certain level, one could cultivate several powerful divine abilities. For example, the "Spirit Devouring Turbid Current" that Fang Xiaoyu used often had a miraculous effect when facing extremely hard objects. Therefore, the stronger Luo Zheng's physical body was, the more powerful the "Spirit Devouring Turbid Current" would be.
Noticing the four black turbid currents slowly drifting toward him, Luo Zheng also felt a sense of crisis in his heart. In an instant, traces of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao's Dao Connotation escaped from his mind.
After repeated attempts, Luo Zheng was already quite proficient. The moment he activated the Infinity Ruler with the Emotion Severing Divine Dao's Dao Connotation, he removed the Dao Connotation completely!
"Buzz …"
Two golden rings were plated in his eyes.
"The Li Water Divine Dao … if cultivated to the extreme, it is indeed quite powerful …"
"This Spirit Devouring Turbid Current is actually a kind of corrosive water. It can even melt primordial treasures …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng's eyes saw through the Dao Connotation in an instant, and he constantly interpreted it …
Realizing the power of the Spirit Devouring Turbid Current, Luo Zheng did not hesitate in the slightest!
The Nine Stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier in his inner world rapidly revolved, and a formless power quickly gushed out. The sword hilt at the end seemed to have a Maitreya Buddha, and the middle section was as wide as a wheel.
The Buddha Emperor's Sword!
The killing sword of the God Slaying Sword Formation.
Killing Fang Xiao Yu did not require the use of four swords, and now Luo Zheng had unleashed this sword.
"Whoosh!"
Because Luo Zheng only controlled a single sword, he did not need to activate the Purple Cloud Divine Dao. With a thought, this Buddha Emperor's Sword broke through the water at an extremely fast speed and shot toward Fang Xiao Yu. This Buddha Emperor's Sword was formed from power, and the Li Water did not constitute the slightest hindrance to it.
"This sword …"
At this moment, Fang Xiao Yu's eyes revealed surprise and bewilderment.
"Li Water Divine Mirror!"
Realizing that something was wrong, Fang Xiao Yu suddenly clapped her hands, and a water mirror rapidly condensed. This Li Water Divine Mirror was also an extremely powerful Divine Dao. Li Water turned into all things, and in an extremely short period of time, it could duplicate the things reflected in the mirror and then reflect them back!
"Whoosh!"
An identical Buddha Emperor's Sword also shot out from the Li Water Divine Mirror.
"Kacha …"
The two Buddha Emperor's Swords collided in the air, and both Buddha Emperor's Swords instantly dissipated.
However, Fang Xiao Yu's ability was ultimately limited, and this Li Water Divine Mirror could not perfectly duplicate the opponent's Mystical Power. The Buddha Emperor's Sword that flew out from the mirror naturally disappeared after it dissipated, and the Buddha Emperor's Sword that was controlled by Luo Zheng condensed once again.

Chapter 1873
The real Buddha Emperor Sword had blood grooves on its body, and the word "kill" was engraved on the roulette.
After killing the opponent, the blood would flow through the grooves and merge into the word "kill", combining with the surrounding divine patterns to condense even more baleful aura.
According to Aunt Han Jiu's strict requirements, Luo Zheng naturally restored all the details of the Buddha Emperor Sword, and naturally formed the word "kill" floating in the air.
Fang Xiaoyu stared at the sword in front of her in astonishment, her eyes filled with a bizarre color.
Before this, Luo Zheng had also used the Transcendence Divine Ability, but no one recognized it. After all, no one would think that a Divine Martial Artist could use the ability of a Sage.
Furthermore, Luo Zheng had used the most superficial method, and did not have a deep understanding of the Origin of Power.
"This is the Origin of Power of the Divine Dao …" Fang Xiaoyu stared blankly at the Buddha Emperor Sword in front of her. Because the power was formless, she could only faintly see an outline. At the same time, she could see her own blood flowing slowly, condensing into the word "kill".
"This is not right!" Fang Xiaoyu said softly, "The Origin of Power cannot leave the body, and even if it did, it would be destroyed by my Divine Water-Leaving Mirror, making it even more impossible to use it again. How did you do it …"
The passage that Fang Xiaoyu opened just now had disappeared, and Luo Zheng was soaked in water, unable to speak. Hearing her surprised voice, he shrugged his shoulders, and with a light flick of his index finger, the Buddha Emperor Sword had already pierced through Fang Xiaoyu's head.
"Splash …"
Fang Xiaoyu was "killed", and the Li Water surrounding Luo Zheng suddenly dispersed, rushing towards the surrounding streets like a flood. The scene was very spectacular …
However, the Li Water did not disperse, and a scale on the Immeasurable Ruler in Luo Zheng's mind emitted a faint light. He raised his hand slightly, and some of the Li Water turned into sparkling and translucent water swords that floated up.
If Fang Xiaoyu saw this scene, she would probably be so shocked that her mouth would not close.
"The Li Water Divine Dao … can only be used in an environment with water, which is why Fang Xiaoyu chose this misty environment."
"If there is no water, I would have to think of another way, and the Li Water Divine Dao's divine ability would be greatly reduced, right?"
"Huala …"
Luo Zheng's finger shook slightly, and one of the translucent water swords turned black. It was the Divine Power of the Li Water Divine Way, the "Devouring Turbid Current". Luo Zheng's finger moved slightly, and the black water sword rolled towards him. Soon after, it corroded a hole in his arm!
As expected, the Soul-Devouring Turbid Current is indeed capable of restraining various magic treasures. It's even effective against my fleshly body. No wonder she was so confident. However, this ability doesn't mean much to me. Instead, the Divine Water Mirror she used at the end was actually capable of replicating my attack, and its might was actually on par with mine.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and patted lightly, and the Li Water turned into a mirror. The Divine Li Water Mirror that he used was naturally not as good as Fang Xiaoyu's, but this divine ability could actually replicate his Buddha Emperor Sword, which indeed surprised Luo Zheng. If he cultivated the Divine Li Water Mirror to the extreme, wouldn't he be able to replicate any opponent's attack?
This was indeed an extraordinary method …
As he was thinking, the space around him slowly faded away. Luo Zheng had returned to his room, and the Divine Water Mirror naturally disappeared as well.
He did not know that the Divine Water Mirror was originally a secret of the Fang Clan. Until now, it had only been passed down to Fang Xiaoyu and Fang Henwan. Luo Zheng had been able to easily destroy the Divine Water Mirror by coincidence. Luo Zheng's Buddha Emperor Sword was very special because the Buddha Emperor Sword was formed purely from energy. After it left the body, it could only be maintained in the form of the Buddha Emperor Sword by using the Transcendence Divine Ability.
If Luo Zheng had used other divine powers, Fang Xiaoyu would have been able to control it herself. All she needed to do was to inject her own force into it. She did not have a divine power that transcended worlds. Naturally, she could not restore the Buddha Emperor Sword that had been destroyed.
Fang Xiaoyu was only ranked 196 in the Coliseum of Deities, but she had already researched him thoroughly. This really surprised Luo Zheng.
The next battle would probably not be easy to deal with. After all, he had attracted too much attention. Just as Fang Xiaoyu had said, almost everyone had bought Xuan Yueji's information. They would all use methods that targeted his strengths.
After defeating Fang Xiaoyu, Luo Zheng's additional reward went up another level. He had already accumulated 460,000 points.
"Anonymous won again!"
"He defeated Fang Xiaoyu …"
"His ranking went up by 20 places. I'm ranked 182. I have no hope of challenging him. Sigh …"
Those who challenged Luo Zheng were at least 100 places below him. Luo Zheng's ranking had jumped up by 20 places. Naturally, 20 people had lost the right to challenge him.
Mu Ning stood at a higher place and looked at Anonymous's room. Her round eyes revealed a faint murderous intent.
This time, her sister had lost to Yu Taibai without any suspense. The Mu family had to bear a certain amount of pressure. A wealthy family had more than one Great Perfection True God. Perhaps they had been affected by Mu Xuerong's crushing defeat, but the other Great Perfection True Gods had also been defeated. This was definitely not good news for the Mu family.
Now, the Mu family was ranked seventh in the Floating Island. According to the current trend, they would probably fall by a few places. If they fell out of the top ten, it would be troublesome.
"Damned Anonymous, you must remember what you said!" She was really afraid that Anonymous would not dare to challenge her. For the Mu family, she had to plunder this guy's points. However, if Anonymous did not go out, she could not do anything to him.

She believed that Anonymous would do as he said.
In the Fang family's Floating Island …
After Fang Xiaoyu lost to Luo Zheng, she quickly exited the Pantheon Jade Wall.
She was an open-minded person, so she wasn't angry at all, but her eyes revealed a deep sense of doubt.
"You're out so soon today?" A gentle voice sounded.
Standing behind her was her brother, the strongest Great Perfection True God of the Fang family, Fang Henwan.
"Brother, I encountered something strange!" Fang Xiaoyu said.
Fang Henwan revealed a hint of curiosity. "Is there anything else that makes you feel strange?"

"Is there anyone who can retrace their power and release it outside their body?" Fang Xiaoyu asked.
Fang Henwan smiled faintly. He stretched out his hand and lightly clapped. An invisible palm print shot out in the air. Fang Henwan did not cultivate the Strength Divine Dao, but he had already condensed nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier. It was just that he could not place it into the world inside his body, so it was not difficult for him to do that.
Unexpectedly, Fang Xiaoyu shook her head. "It's not like that, Brother. The other party used his power to retrace and form a flying sword …"
Before she could finish her sentence, Fang Henwan struck out with his palm again. An invisible sword formed from the source of power shot out and hit the wall again. Fang Henwan shrugged his shoulders, "Little sister, this isn't a difficult task."
"Can't you let me finish first?!" Fang Xiaoyu glared fiercely at Fang Henwan and gestured. "After the flying sword leaves the body, it can still be freely controlled. Moreover, after it was smashed by my Divine Li Water Mirror, it can still be retraced."
Hearing this, Fang Henwan's face revealed a strange expression. He then shook his head. "This is impossible. After the power leaves the body, it can't be casually controlled. It will only weaken rapidly. Did you see wrongly? Or is it another kind of Mystical Power? But it can't be retracing power. What you said can only be done by Saints using Mystical Power. I can't do it now."
"So it's really like that?" Fang Xiaoyu smiled. "I thought I remembered wrongly. That guy really did it …"
"Who?" Seeing that his sister's expression did not seem to be joking, Fang Henwan asked suspiciously. In fact, he still doubted Fang Xiaoyu's words. Fang Xiaoyu's opponents were only lower True Gods, so it would be strange if they could really do it.

Chapter 1874
Fang Henshao had also heard about the anonymous person.
Putting aside the matter of anonymity, it was indeed a miracle to be able to achieve twenty-three consecutive victories in a battle of a higher level. There were not many people who could achieve this in the Arena of Gods.
At least, Fang Henshao could not have done it back then.
"Even if he's anonymous, it's impossible for him to do what you said." Fang Henshao reached out a hand and an invisible force condensed once again. Then, he tossed it lightly and the force began to weaken rapidly.
Once the Eight Dao Pillars were activated, it transformed into pure energy that could be stored away. If one couldn't maintain the supply of energy, it was inevitable that it would wither away. Only a world-spanning divine ability could extract energy from the body, and maintain a certain distance away from the body so that it wouldn't weaken.
"A Divine Warrior and Transcendence Mystique? I find it hard to believe … "Fang Henshao shook his head.
"Have I ever lied to you?" Fang Xiaoyu was a little angry.
"You have never lied to me. You are talking nonsense." Fang Henshao laughed.
Fang Henshao's words caused Fang Xiaoyu to punch him. Fang Xiaoyu vented her anger and said, "The shape of the sword is a little strange. The hilt is like a Maitreya Buddha and the blade is like a wheel. After seeing blood, the blood formed the word 'kill' …"
"Kill?"
Fang Henshao raised his eyebrows and said, "That is the Buddha Emperor's Sword, the Han Family's Second Sage's ultimate skill."
Aunt Han Jiu's God Slaying Sword Formation was quite famous in the divine territory. The Buddha Emperor's Sword's shape was very special. Fang Xiaoyu described it and Fang Henshao immediately got the answer.
The brother and sister looked at each other and got the answer at the same time. "Han Family!"
"The anonymous person came from the Purple Soul Palace …" Fang Henshao said calmly.
"Interesting. Finally exposed!" Fang Henshao was very calm, but Fang Xiaoyu was very excited …
…
…
After defeating Fang Xiaoyu, Luo Zheng did not rest and immediately chose his next opponent.
God's Domain's top forces were all gathered on the floating island. News spread quickly among them. Whatever methods he had used, it would spread very quickly.
His next opponent was Zhao Xun, who was ranked 176th.
Like Fang Xiaoyu, he was also very excited to be chosen by Luo Zheng, but his excitement did not last long.
This Zhao Xun cultivated the Five Elements Divine Dao, and Luo Zheng had already absorbed the Five Elements Divine Dao. He tried to force Zhao Xun to use her Outer Dao Divine Art, but Zhao Xun seemed to be cultivating step by step and didn't inherit the Outer Dao Divine Art from her family. Since that was the case, Luo Zheng didn't hesitate. Just when Zhao Xun thought she had the upper hand, he didn't hesitate to take out the Buddha Emperor Sword and kill her.
Twenty-four consecutive victories! 610,000 points!
To obtain nearly 150,000 points from defeating a lower True Divinity, this was already comparable to the points obtained from defeating a Great Perfection True Divinity. It was truly astonishing.
After returning to his room, Luo Zheng's eyes were calm as he stretched out his finger and selected another name.
"Continue!"
As Luo Zheng continued to challenge opponents, the lower True Divinities in the Hall of Gods gradually understood the pattern of Luo Zheng's opponent selection.
When he was ranked 96th, he challenged Fang Xiaoyu, who was ranked 196th. When he was ranked 76th, he challenged Zhao Xun, who was ranked 176th. When he was ranked 57th, he challenged 157th. In other words, he would choose someone lower than himself.
This made the lower True Divinities, who were ranked higher than Luo Zheng, rather depressed. According to this pattern, the probability of Luo Zheng choosing them was too low …
After that, Luo Zheng won another three matches in a row, obtaining a 27th consecutive victory.
From the history of the Coliseum of Gods, the highest record for a person of a higher level was 27 consecutive victories. That was a record from more than 40 Divine Epochs ago. It had been quite a long time since then, but now, Luo Zheng had tied this record.
At the same time, the people in the Hall of Gods slowly understood the new ability used by the anonymous person.
"I'm one hundred percent sure that it's the Buddha Emperor Sword!"
"The ultimate skill of the Han Family's Secondary Sage, the killing sword of the God Slaying Sword Formation …"
"The God Slaying Sword Formation is very ordinary if used by others. 'Solid Sword' isn't considered a top divine ability. Using strength to create 'Virtual Sword' is the most powerful. It's also Aunt Hanjiu's greatest reliance. But if it's used with the Transmigration Divine Ability, what's there to say about a Proof of Divinity martial artist?"
"He used the Transmigration Divine Ability and used strength to create the Virtual Sword …"
"What nonsense. Can a Proof of Divinity martial artist use the divine ability of a Saint?"
After Luo Zheng took out the Buddha Emperor Sword, it was like a stone that caused a thousand ripples.
The lower True Gods who fought with Luo Zheng were not weak. The True Gods who could make it into the top two hundred were the targets that the big families focused on nurturing. Some were like Fang Xiaoyu, who was a direct descendant and had a respected status.
They had already confirmed that Luo Zheng came from Han Family.
Han Family's Purple Soul Palace was not the only martial artist who cultivated the Purple Clouds Divine Path. However, Aunt Hanjiu was the only one who knew the God Slaying Sword Formation, so it was not difficult to guess.
The problem was the Transmigration Divine Ability … In the eyes of all True Divinities, this kind of inconceivable divine ability was a Saint's unique talent. It could not appear on a Proof of Divinity martial artist. It was beyond common sense.
Therefore, there would often be arguments. The people who spoke vowed solemnly, but the people who listened did not believe them at all!
In the midst of the heated debate, Luo Zheng once again leaped forward …
Twenty-eight consecutive wins …
Twenty-nine consecutive wins …
Thirty consecutive wins …
His accumulated points had reached 1.4 million, which had already surpassed the top Upper True Divinities and even some Perfect True Divinities.
Now, the situation had become very delicate.
The big families did not like anonymous people. Like Luo Zheng, who had obtained such a huge amount of points, no matter which family he belonged to, it would affect several rankings. Facing such a disruptor, they were helpless.
After thirty consecutive wins, Luo Zheng was ranked seventeenth. This time, he chose to randomly choose his opponent.
Very unexpectedly, he was matched with Qin Tianze.
When Qin Tianze entered the Arena of Gods, he was also a little dazed. Qin Tianze was originally ranked twenty-first. After winning a match two days ago, he was now ranked nineteen. He was also eager to challenge the anonymous person. However, according to the anonymous person's choice, even if he was ranked first, he would still choose the hundredth place as his opponent. Qin Tianze was afraid that he had no hope.
He never thought that Qin Tianze would meet the anonymous person when he was randomly matched …
Qin Tianze had been defeated by Luo Zheng in the past. In the Sky Vault Clan, Qin Tianze had been thoroughly defeated.
However, in the Divine Region, it was a watershed for the disciples of big families to become Lower True Divinities. For disciples of big families like Qin Tianze, they were usually able to cultivate the top Outer Dao Remarkable Powers. Naturally, their battle strength was much stronger than ordinary True Divinities. In addition to his own talent, he easily entered the top twenty.
Luo Zheng was not interested in tangling with Qin Tianze. In a few consecutive matches, Luo Zheng took the initiative to attack. If his opponent used a Divine Path or Outer Dao Remarkable Powers that he lacked, he would activate the Infinite Ruler to absorb them. If he did not need them, he would kill them directly. He had inherited the God Slayer Sword Formation from Aunt Hanjiu, but he had not even used the Purple Cloud Divine Path. This was because controlling the Buddha Emperor Sword did not require the Purple Cloud Divine Path's Mind Control Technique.
Qin Tianze still wanted to say something, but Luo Zheng had already arrived with the Buddha Emperor Sword.
Qin Tianze's strength had indeed improved by leaps and bounds. At least, he would not be completely defeated like before. After all, his cultivation was one realm higher than Luo Zheng's. After activating the Godhood, the Remarkable Powers he displayed were indeed quite powerful.
He used the Green Wood Divine Path to produce a very unique willow branch that could actually confine the formless Buddha Emperor Sword within it.
When the Buddha Emperor Sword was obstructed, Luo Zheng did not hesitate and took out his second sword, the Long Fang Sword.
If the Buddha Emperor Sword was said to be focused on "killing," the Long Fang Sword was focused on "biting." It could often cause pure destruction to the opponent. Under the Long Fang Sword's hoarseness, Qin Tianze was quickly defeated …
However, at this time, Qin Tianze suddenly shouted, "Wait, I still have something to say …"
The two swords immediately stopped in front of Qin Tianze. Luo Zheng was a little curious. In this Arena of Gods, one would not really die. He could not possibly be begging for mercy, right?
Who knew that Qin Tianze's face was full of depression. "I wanted to say it from the beginning, but you did not give me a chance at all! Someone asked me to pass a message to you. She wants you to walk out of your room and meet her in the Hall of Gods! "
It turned out that Luo Zheng had been unwilling to leave his room. Even if the people in the Hall of Gods wanted to talk to Luo Zheng, they did not have a chance. They could only use this method to pass a message to Luo Zheng. However, Luo Zheng chased after Qin Tianze the moment they met, so Qin Tianze could not find a chance to speak at all.
From Qin Tianze's point of view, losing to Luo Zheng was not a big deal. However, if he delayed that person's matter, he would be in trouble!
"Did the person who asked you to pass the message predict that I would challenge you?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Of course not. However, she informed the top 130 True Gods. You will eventually challenge these people," Qin Tianze said helplessly.
"Who is it?" Luo Zheng asked.
The True Gods at the top of the rankings all had extraordinary backgrounds. Most of them were direct descendants of wealthy families. Who had such great appeal?

Chapter 1875
"Her name is Dongfang Yunzhu. She's ranked second in the Hall of Gods," Qin Tianze answered.
Dongfang Yunzhu?
Luo Zheng had seen this name before, but he didn't pay much attention to it.
Currently, the top three in the Hall of Gods were Mu Ning, Dongfang Yunzhu, and Han Liusu.
All three of them were women, but they dominated the top three. This was something that had never happened before.
The Dongfang family was also targeted by Yu Taibai, and they were ranked first in the floating island. However, Luo Zheng hadn't met a True God with the surname "Dongfang" among the lower True Gods.
Logically speaking, if the Dongfang family could be ranked first, they must have a lot of points. However, Luo Zheng had fought his way up from the bottom of the Hall of Gods and hadn't met a single True God.
"Why is she looking for me?" Luo Zheng asked curiously. He didn't know this woman at all.
Qin Tianze shook his head helplessly. "Anyway, I've already passed on the message. If there's anything, go find her yourself. I admit defeat …"
After the battle, Luo Zheng finally pushed open the door to his room.
From here, he could look down on the huge Hall of Gods. He watched the spiral-like rooms spiral down. Now, he was almost at the top.
Every time Luo Zheng opened the door, there would always be a group of people gathered outside. This time, he was very surprised.
Some True Gods just stared at him silently. They didn't come close, including Han Liusu and Mu Ning. They just watched from afar. However, there was a woman with a calm expression standing on one side of his room.
The woman's face was covered by a light blue veil, and only half of her face could be seen. The pair of eyes that were exposed reflected a faint sharp light. Just one look from her eyes seemed to be pregnant with countless words. It was a pair of eyes that could speak.
"You've finally come out," she said.
"You're Dongfang Yunzhu?" Luo Zheng asked.
She smiled faintly and nodded.
Dongfang Yunzhu rarely came to the Hall of Gods. This time, she heard about the anonymous person as soon as she entered. However, the anonymous person didn't go out at all, so she had no chance to meet him.
Her position on the floating island was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
Just like what Qin Tianze said, she only needed one sentence, and all the children of the rich and powerful families dared not disobey. Therefore, the first thing Qin Tianze did was not to seriously fight with Luo Zheng, but to pass on Dongfang Yunzhu's words.
Otherwise, Qin Tianze wouldn't have been defeated so quickly …
"Do you also want to challenge me?" Luo Zheng asked indifferently.
Although the other party came from the number one floating island, Luo Zheng didn't have a good impression of the Dongfang Clan either.
This was because the Dongfang family was also on Yu Taibai's list. According to Luo Zheng's judgment, these eight or nine families belonged to the same faction, and the Dongfang family was the most prosperous among them. This family was his enemy, not his friend.
Dongfang Yunzhu shook her head. "I'm not interested in your points."
"What's the matter?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"I heard that you seem to have mastered the Buddha Emperor Sword of the God Slaying Sword Formation. You should have inherited it from Purple Soul Palace's Aunt Han Jiu, right? Although I don't know how you used the third stage of power tracing, you are indeed qualified, "Dongfang Yunzhu said.
"What qualifications?"
"You are qualified to join my Dongfang family." Dongfang Yunzhu stared at Luo Zheng. Her tone was surprisingly firm, as if letting Luo Zheng join was a simple matter.
Not far away, Mu Ning and Han Liusu frowned slightly. Some of the other children of the wealthy families also showed a trace of displeasure.
The Dongfang family was still as domineering as ever …
When Luo Zheng took out the Buddha Emperor Sword, plus the strange request Luo Zheng made when Yu Taibai appeared, Han Liusu was about ninety percent sure of Luo Zheng's identity.
Luo Zheng was currently in Aunt Jiu's palace, and he had just used the Buddha Emperor Sword recently. Plus, there were some other characteristics that were circulating. How could Han Liusu not be sure?
But since Luo Zheng didn't admit it, Han Liusu couldn't take the initiative to say anything. Besides, there was always a slight possibility that it was a coincidence.
"No." Luo Zheng was a bit helpless to find him out of his room because of this.
Although Luo Zheng rejected Dongfang Yunzhu, she wasn't angry at all. She continued, "For many people, this is a great opportunity. You don't have to give it up."
"I've already joined another floating island. I won't change places." Luo Zheng still shook his head.
"The Dongfang family will naturally take care of the Han family. You don't have to worry about anything else." Dongfang Yunzhu smiled.
Han Liusu's face also showed anger. She knew that the anonymous person belonged to the Han family, but Dongfang Yunzhu still wanted him with such fanfare. That was too much. All of the children of the floating islands were quite afraid of the Dongfang family. This was because they were taught to provoke the Sword family and the Xuanyue family, but they must never offend the Dongfang family!
Over time, these children of the rich and powerful families would take a detour around the Dongfang family. If it wasn't necessary, they wouldn't directly clash with them.
But now, Han Liusu couldn't take it anymore. Dongfang Yunzhu didn't put the Yun family in her eyes. She took a few steps forward. Behind her, Mu Ning sneered and said, "You want to meddle in other people's business?"
"So what if I do?" Han Liusu said coldly.
"You know that the Dongfang family has always been like this. There's no reason in their eyes." Mu Ning sneered.
The Mu family and the Dongfang family had a good relationship, but rather than an alliance, it was more like a relationship between superiors and subordinates. In the Holy Halls, the Saints of the rich and powerful families also had disputes. In these years, the Dongfang family had the absolute advantage. Other than the Xuanyue family and the Sword family, the other rich and powerful families didn't have the right to clash with the Dongfang family.
Han Liusu snorted. She could tolerate other things, but this was the only thing she couldn't tolerate.
But before she could say anything, Luo Zheng said stiffly, "I said this has nothing to do with the Han family!"
Who knew that Dongfang Yunzhu still had that indifferent expression. "Whether or not you join the Dongfang family has nothing to do with you."
Although Dongfang Yunzhu had always been polite and didn't have an overbearing attitude, her words didn't seem to take Luo Zheng's original intentions seriously. The Dongfang family could indeed force the Han family to join them. Even if the Han family's Saint Emperor was unwilling, if the Dongfang family was determined to do it, the Han family couldn't refuse.
The Saints were also divided into ranks. The Han family's Saint Emperor was an extremely powerful existence among the Saints, but no matter how powerful he was, he couldn't compare to Luo Xiao back then … Luo Xiao was a living example of disobeying them. No matter which rich and powerful family it was, they would have to consider it carefully.
Luo Zheng stared at Dongfang Yunzhu's beautiful eyes. In her indifferent expression, Luo Zheng saw naked contempt.
Not to mention that Luo Zheng had already joined the Purple Soul Palace, even if he hadn't stepped onto the floating island, he still wouldn't agree to such an invitation. After about three breaths of time, Luo Zheng chuckled. "I don't know if I wasn't straightforward enough, or if your brain isn't good enough to understand, but if that's the case, I can only tell you one word."
"What?" Dongfang Yunzhu asked.
"Scram!" Luo Zheng said, then went into his room without looking back and closed the door with a bang.
The top floor of the Hall of the Gods was silent …
They had already forgotten how many years it had been. The Dongfang family had gradually risen and occupied the number one position on the floating island, almost becoming the overlord of the rich and powerful families. All the Dongfang family's disciples were respected more than ordinary True Gods. All these years, everyone had been careful, and no one dared to insult the Dongfang family to their face.
But the anonymous person actually scolded Dongfang Yunzhu without any hesitation.
Offending the Dongfang family meant that he didn't have the right to stay on the floating island …
They naturally didn't know that Luo Zheng had already planned to leave the floating island after receiving Yu Taibai's hint.
Mu Ning's eyes revealed surprise. She didn't think that the anonymous person would be so bold.

Chapter 1876
Dongfang Yunzhu stood there without moving. There seemed to be a suppressed flame in her eyes.
She was just a lobbyist for the family …
The wealthy families of the floating island were arranged like a pyramid. The top three wealthy families were far stronger than the average wealthy family. They were in a dominant position.
Only the top three wealthy families had the right to ask for people from other wealthy families. The average wealthy family wouldn't refuse.
If they wanted to target certain wealthy families, they could suppress them.
The "Qian family" was suppressed like this. Back then, the Qian family offended the Dongfang family. They didn't have a direct conflict, but in the Arena of Gods, the Dongfang family worked with the other wealthy families to suppress the Qian family. The Qian family, originally ranked 13th, quickly fell out of the rankings. According to the rules, the Qian family could only move out of the sea of time and watch their floating island fall into the sea …
Because of this, the top three wealthy families actually had a dominant position in the sea of time.
Dongfang Yunzhu stood in front of Luo Zheng's room for a while before turning to leave. Just as she was about to return to her room and leave the Hall of Gods, Mu Ning smiled and said, "He shouldn't be a member of the Han family. People from the grassroots are always disobedient."
Dongfang Yunzhu glanced at Mu Ning indifferently. Mu Ning took a step back. She was afraid that Dongfang Yunzhu would vent her anger on her. Dongfang Yunzhu then said, "You're right. He belongs to the Han family. He shouldn't belong to the Han family. It's useless to threaten such an arrogant person …"
She then turned to look at Han Liusu. "Tell your family's elders to hand over the anonymous person."
Han Liusu was already very angry with Dongfang Yunzhu. She didn't care about the Han family at all. Hearing this, she replied coldly, "Why?"
"Because of the Dongfang family. That's enough," Dongfang Yunzhu said.
"But the anonymous person doesn't necessarily belong to the Han family!" Han Liusu argued.
Thinking of the Dongfang family's powerful influence in the sea of time, Han Liusu had to say this. She had to consider the entire Han family.
"Aunt Han Jiu should know whether he belongs to the Han family or not." Dongfang Yunzhu shook her head. "Forget it. It's useless to tell you. Just find a time to come and ask for him personally."
Then, Dongfang Yunzhu gently pushed open the door to her room and disappeared.
The disciples at the top of the Hall of Gods all lowered their heads and whispered to each other. No one thought that Han Jia would offend the Dongfang family because of this anonymous person.
Everyone believed that the anonymous person came from Han Jia because of the God-Slaying Sword Formation Luo Zheng had used. However, this was not certain. Moreover, Han Jia might not even know about it.
However, after these people discussed for a while, they all returned to their rooms.
Since Luo Zheng had randomly challenged Qin Tianze, he might choose to randomly challenge Qin Tianze again. No one wanted to miss such an opportunity …
…
…
Spirit Heart Palace …
Han Liusu also came out of the Pantheon Jade Wall. After tidying herself up, she quietly headed deeper into the Spirit Heart Palace.
They followed a quiet path to the end.
At the end of the path was a teleportation platform. Below the platform stood six armored guards. When they saw a figure approaching, one of the guards took a few steps forward and shouted, "This is the Han Family's forbidden area. Ordinary people are not allowed to enter!"
When Han Liusu was close enough, the guard finally recognized her. "It's the eldest princess!"
"Greetings, Eldest Princess!"
The guards bowed one after another.
"Eldest Princess, the Holy Emperor is not seeing anyone during this time. If there's nothing important …" the guard said softly.
They were guarding the Han Clan's Saint Emperor's palace. This palace was located in the center of the floating island and could only be accessed through the teleportation array. Last time, Aunt Han Jiu had tried to force her way in. These guards were loyal to their duty and had even suffered at the hands of Aunt Han Jiu. It could be considered an injustice.
"I have an important matter to discuss with father," Han Liusu said calmly.
"But …" The guard still wanted to argue.
Han Liusu was too lazy to argue with them. She walked towards the teleportation platform. The guards did not dare stop her.
She circled around the teleportation platform, and a faint blue light flashed from the teleportation platform. Her figure sank into it and disappeared in front of the guards.
Not long after, Han Liusu entered the floating island of Han Jia.
A huge palace appeared in front of her.
Ever since Han Liusu had returned from the Forbidden Land of God, she had not had many opportunities to see her father. Even when she did, his attitude was much calmer than before. Han Liusu did not know why.
Inside the palace, a middle-aged man in luxurious robes sat in an empty hall. He seemed to have entered a meditative state. He was Han Jia's Holy Emperor, Emperor Hanqing.
"Father."
After entering the palace, Han Liusu bowed politely.
Emperor Hanqing opened his eyes and glanced at her. "What is it, Liusu?"
"There are some things I wish to report to you about in the Arena of Gods," she said.
Emperor Hanqing smiled faintly. "Liusu should not interfere in these matters."
"Liusu knows, but what Liusu says might affect Han Jia's floating island ranking," Han Liusu said seriously.
"Speak …" Emperor Hanqing nodded.
"Does Imperial Father know that recently in the Arena of Gods, a martial artist who has proven himself as a Divinity has appeared?" Han Liusu asked.
Emperor Hanqing nodded.
The Holy Emperors did not pay much attention to the Arena of Gods, but whenever the floating islands were ranked, they would still ask. After all, the floating island ranking was very important to every powerful family.
That anonymous cultivator's points were enough to change the ranking. Naturally, someone had reported to him. Emperor Hanqing had indeed heard of this anonymous cultivator.
"If I'm not wrong, the anonymous cultivator should be someone from Han Jia, and someone from the Purple Soul Palace," Han Liusu said. Luo Zheng's figure involuntarily appeared in her mind.
"What? Something like that happened? "Emperor Hanqing's face revealed a rare look of surprise.
The person who had reported to him had not mentioned this. After all, Luo Zheng had not revealed the Buddha Emperor's Sword. Han Liusu had been in the Hall of Gods, so she had received the news firsthand.
"Yes, he seems to have cultivated Aunt Jiu's God Slaughtering Sword Formation. What's strange is that he uses the Void Sword," Han Liusu said.
"Void Sword? That's impossible. "Qing Emperor shook his head. He was a Saint himself, and he knew that Proving Gods couldn't use Crossing Worlds. Ordinary Gods could use their powers to create Virtual Swords, but they couldn't use them to form Sword Formations. Without the support of Crossing Worlds, the Virtual Swords couldn't leave their hands.
Han Liusu was not in the mood to convince her father. That was not important. Han Liusu continued to say, "He has won thirty-one times in a row. His points should be around one million six hundred thousand."
"Oh! That's higher than Han Tianxiao's points? "
Han Tianxiao was a Paragon, and he was the leader of the Han family. His Arena of Gods points was only one million two hundred and sixty thousand. Not long ago, he had lost a public battle.
Emperor Hanqing looked happy. If such a powerful young man came from the Han family, it would be their good fortune.
"But today, Dongfang Yunzhu suddenly appeared in the Hall of Gods. She asked for an anonymous person to join the Dongfang family," Han Liusu said.
When she said that, Emperor Hanqing's smile disappeared. He was not afraid of a little girl like Dongfang Yunzhu, but if the Dongfang family had sent Dongfang Yunzhu, it meant the Dongfang family had taken notice of the anonymous person.
"What did the anonymous person say?" Emperor Hanqing asked.
"He told her to piss off," Han Liusu said flatly. She was smiling.
Emperor Hanqing frowned. "Then why have you come today?"
"I beg you, father. If the Dongfang family comes to ask for someone, can you refuse?" Han Liusu said seriously. Ever since she was young, her father had always listened to her. She hoped this time, he would agree.
Emperor Hanqing was silent for a long time, then sighed heavily. "Actually, there has been a huge hidden worry bothering father and the Han family these past few years. Liusu, you are still young, so father doesn't want you to worry. If the anonymous person really is from the Han family, then father probably won't be able to refuse if the Dongfang family comes to ask for you."
"Why? Why would someone like that contribute so much to the Han family? "Han Liusu's voice rose a few octaves.

Chapter 1877
In the past year, the Han Clan's Saint Emperor had done a few things that confused many in the clan.
Becoming enemies with the Qin Clan …
In order to curry favor with the Leng Clan, he even sent the eldest princess out for a marriage alliance.
This time, he actually agreed to Han Tianxiao's request to disband the Purple Soul Palace!
What they didn't know was that the person who forced Aunt Han Jiu to disband the Purple Soul Palace was Emperor Han Qing himself. Han Tianxiao was only acting on his orders.
So that day, after Aunt Han Jiu and Han Tianxiao had an intense argument, she went to find Emperor Han Qing and silently returned to the Purple Soul Palace …
The rules of the Sea of Time weren't unchanging. Over countless epochs, the rules of the Governing Slate had been constantly increasing and changing.
Not every rich and powerful family had the ability to modify the Governing Slate.
The Shenwu Coins that Han Jia had accumulated over countless years were only enough to slightly change a few words on the Governing Stone Tablet and make a small change to the rules.
But when the Dongfang Clan made a move, they could often overturn a certain rule.
Of course, because of the restrictions of the Sacred Halls, the rules couldn't be changed carelessly. Every time a rule was changed, it was usually the result of many parties competing.
Moreover, if they wanted to change a rule that greatly benefited the Dongfang Clan — for example, only the Dongfang Clan's floating island could exist in the Sea of Time — this kind of overbearing rule would require an astronomical amount of shenwu coins. Even the Dongfang Clan couldn't afford it.
But this time, the Dongfang Clan was about to make a move, and they'd already obtained the tacit approval of the Xuanyue Clan and the Sword Clan.
Dongfang Clan, Mu Clan, Leng Clan … Zhao Clan, Liu Clan, Qin Clan. The Nine Great Clans' alliance was led by the Dongfang Clan. The Sacred Halls weren't ruled by the Dongfang Clan alone, but they had nine seats. Apart from that, the Mu Clan and the Zhou Clan were close, while the Zhao Clan and the Fang Clan and the Tang Clan had good relations …
Because of this, the Dongfang Clan could control the Sacred Halls to a certain extent.
If the other independent clans, like the Han Clan, went against the Dongfang Clan, they'd be going against the entire Sacred Halls!
As long as the Dongfang Family's rules were approved by the Xuanyue Family and the Sword Race, it was equivalent to the Saint Halls approving it. This time, the Dongfang Family came aggressively, changing the rules would have a far-reaching impact on all the powerful families.
In order to protect the Han Clan's position, Emperor Han Qing had no choice but to lower his head.
Emperor Hanqing didn't want to explain these things to his daughter.
"You're still young, there are some things you'll understand in the future. To be able to win thirty-one times in a row, you've already surpassed my father in the past. You'll definitely have a bright future, but if the Dongfang Clan wants you, let them take you away …" Emperor Hanqing said calmly.
Emperor Hanqing didn't take this matter too seriously.
First of all, there was a high chance that the anonymous person was from the Han Family. Now, Emperor Han Qing was trying to please the Leng Family in order to join the Wealthy Families Alliance. If the Dongfang Family had such a request, he could go with the flow.
What's more, there were only a few days until the new ranking of the floating islands. Even if the Dongfang Clan came to ask for him, they wouldn't be in such a hurry. They didn't care about points, so who could shake the Dongfang Clan's position?
Anonymous had helped Han Jia earn a lot of points, and then he would return the favor to the Dong Fang family. To Emperor Han Qing, this was the perfect ending. He was happy to see this happen.
Of course, he didn't know what Han Liusu was thinking. He also didn't know that Han Liusu had already met Anonymous in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement and even had feelings for him. He also didn't know that Anonymous was Luo Xiao's son.
Father's words were completely out of Han Liusu's expectations. She stared at Emperor Han Qing and said, "Father, how can you use people like this?"
Han Liusu was still rather naive. In her opinion, Anonymous had contributed a lot to Han Jia, so Han Jia had to return the favor. Since Luo Zheng didn't want to go to the Dongfang Clan, Han Jia would protect Anonymous.
Whether or not Anonymous was Luo Zheng, they had to do this.
In Han Liusu's eyes, her father's actions were very utilitarian.
"But …" Han Liusu wanted to argue, but Emperor Han Qing waved his hand. "You don't have to worry about this. Han Tianxiao will take care of it. Father, I heard you had a conflict with the Leng Clan's child?"
"Yes!" Other than Anonymous, Han Liusu also wanted to talk about the marriage with Leng Lin Yue. She didn't expect him to bring it up first.
"Leng Lin Yue is also very outstanding. This marriage is very important to our Han Jia. Let Han Tianxiao take you to the Leng Clan and you can apologize to Leng Lin Yue …" Emperor Han Qing said.
Han Liusu had never thought about marrying Leng Lin Yue.
She was filled with helplessness before, but she had always been obedient and didn't go too far. Now that she met Luo Zheng again, how could she be willing?
She thought she was the apple of her father's eye, but in the end, she was just a bargaining chip. She felt sad and her expression grew colder and colder. She stared at Emperor Han Qing and said, "Father, I don't want to marry that person."
Emperor Han Qing didn't seem to notice Han Liusu's mood. He continued, "Leng Lin Yue is also very outstanding. Although he can't compare to your brother Cang Yan, with the Leng Clan's nurturing, he will definitely reach perfection …"
Emperor Hanqing was still mumbling to himself when Han Liusu interrupted him. She asked in a voice full of grief and indignation, "In Imperial Father's eyes, Liusu is only something used to curry favor with the Leng Clan. So Liusu is only worth this much?"
Emperor Han Qing was startled by his daughter's question.
Even if Han Liusu was his daughter, in a place as high as the hierarchy of the floating island, she could not question him like this. Moreover, he was the Saint Emperor of the Han Family. This was an extremely overbearing action.
Emperor Han Qing didn't have many children, especially his daughters, Han Liusu and Han Chuyue. If it was some other Saint Emperor with many children, they would be severely punished.
A trace of anger appeared on his face as he coldly shouted, "Impudent!"
"Yes, I am impudent!" Han Liusu was very stubborn. "If father really wants to force me to marry that guy, why don't you just kill me with your own hands?"
"You think father wouldn't dare?" Emperor Han Qing's eyes shone with a sharp light. An invisible aura spread out.
Most of the time, a Saint would conceal his presence, so he looked like an ordinary True God. How powerful was a Saint's presence? When he released his presence, Han Jia's floating island began to tremble slightly.
Just one look was enough to kill Han Liusu a thousand times, no, ten thousand times …
But as soon as that aura spread out, Emperor Hanqing retracted it.
Saints had gone through countless cycles of life and death. Their control over their emotions had reached the pinnacle.
In the end, he controlled his anger and sighed. "You can leave now. We'll talk about this another day …"
"I'll make it clear today!" Han Liusu looked like she was going to throw caution to the wind.
But Emperor Han Qing only waved his hand, and an invisible force spread out. Han Liusu couldn't help but retreat. She was pushed out of the palace by the force, and the palace's doors slammed shut.
Han Liusu's body was still trembling. It took her a while to calm down. Then she thought of Aunt Han Jiu and slowly left.

Within the Arena of Gods …
Luo Zheng stood on a blood-red plain.
When Yu Taibai had challenged Mu Xuerong, they'd used this exact setting.

As promised, he accepted Mu Ning's challenge.
