Chapter 1878
What was the Dongfang family's attitude towards Anonymous? How would Han Jia deal with him?
This had nothing to do with Mu Ning. Her goal was Anonymous's points.
Normally, no one would covet Anonymous's points too much, because the floating island rankings changed once every few decades. Even if they stole Anonymous's points, they would become the target of others.
Although Mu Ning was ranked first among the lower True Gods, who could guarantee that she would not be defeated?
In the end, the high number of points would be slowly diluted by the battles …
But it was different now. The floating island rankings were about to be decided, and she had already completed three compulsory battles this month. If she won, Anonymous would guarantee that all the points would be with her, which would be equivalent to the Mu family having another Perfect Saint. At that time, her points would be even higher than her sister Mu Xuerong's.
The key was to defeat the anonymous person in front of her. For this battle, Mu Ning had made ample preparations.
The power that spread out from her body contained two different kinds of power …
One was the Killing God Dao.
Most of the Divine Daos in the rich and powerful families were basically the Five Elements Divine Daos.
But the Mu family was different. Mu Haiji's Killing God Dao was the Killing God Dao, and the Mu family cultivated it the most.
Mu Xuerong was the same, and so was Mu Ning …
But Mu Ning was slightly different from her sister. When she attained the Divine Dao, she comprehended another Divine Dao, the Life Divine Dao.
At that time, Mu Ning was somewhat confused. There were many children of wealthy families who comprehended several Divine Daos at the same time, but in the end, they would focus on one Divine Dao, and the others would only be supplementary. But when Mu Ning condensed her godhead, she unexpectedly condensed an extremely rare dual godhead!
That godhead was split into two from the middle. The left one was blood red, and the right one was green. One godhead emitted two completely different auras …
When her father, Mu Haiji, found out about this, he went to the Life Divine Dao's dao tablet to study it. Then, he combined his own Killing God Dao with the Life Divine Dao to create an outer dao ability suitable for Mu Ning to cultivate.
The Divine Dao of Death and the Divine Dao of Life were originally like water and fire, two absolute extremes. However, Mu Haiji had immersed himself in the Divine Dao of Death for countless Divine Epochs and had managed to perfectly blend the two together. Moreover, he was certain that in the future, when Mu Ning's cultivation reached a certain level, she would definitely surpass her older sister, Mu Xuerong.
Now, Mu Ning had indeed obtained first place amongst the lower True Divinities. After defeating Han Liusu, she hadn't tasted defeat for several months.

Vigorous vitality rushed towards Luo Zheng, and black and red plants broke out of the ground. These plants, which were originally born from the divine dao of life, emitted a strong aura of slaughter, as if they were plants that grew in the Abyss of Demons …

Mu Ning's eyes slightly blinked, and the ponytail behind her swayed gently. Her body brimming with vitality was like a little deer in the forest. Under the constant bouncing, she pounced towards Luo Zheng with a black dagger in her hand!
"Whoosh!"

"Do you only know how to run?" Mu Ning's eyes revealed a trace of impatience.
Those who cultivated the Killing God Dao would often be affected by it, and they would enjoy the feeling of crazily killing. Mu Xuerong was like this, and Mu Ning was no exception. Instead, she stood in place and looked at Luo Zheng with a face full of pity. As Luo Zheng retreated, blood continued to gush out from his arm!
Luo Zheng retreated more than a hundred feet, and the blood on his arm crazily splashed out, forming a golden ribbon of blood on the ground.

This small hole should have been bitten by the blood snake, but when did this circular mark appear?
With Luo Zheng's powerful Qi and blood, this small wound healed almost in the blink of an eye. However, when he retreated, this small wound not only didn't heal, but also formed a strange force that constantly drew out the blood in his body.
What was strange was that when he stopped, his blood was no longer being drawn out …

"Don't you understand?" Mu Ning faintly smiled, "I overestimated your strength. You were bitten by the Bloody Desolation Bite so quickly. As long as you move, the blood in your body will gush out from the wound until the last drop of blood in your body is sucked out!"
The Killing God Dao had the "Bloody Desolation" magical ability. No matter what kind of defense the opponent took, the "Bloody Desolation" would always cause the most terrible wound to the opponent, and the wound was very difficult to heal.
However, after Mu Haiji helped Mu Ning merge the Killing God Dao and the Life Divine Dao, he seemed to have a new understanding of the Life Divine Dao and helped Mu Ning create this "Bloody Desolation Bite". This kind of wound simply couldn't be healed, and as long as the wound moved, it would trigger the power of the mark and frantically draw out the opponent's blood.
"Interesting Outer Dao Magical Ability …" Luo Zheng faintly smiled, "If I don't move, wouldn't this move be useless?"
After saying that, he actually sat down cross-legged …
"You really think you're smart!" Mu Ning coldly smiled, "I just want to know if you don't move, how many times can you last in my hands …"
Before she finished speaking, she saw that Luo Zheng's front, back, left, and right were constantly producing power fluctuations. The power source was quite heavy, constantly distorting the space around him. Not long after, four invisible long swords were rapidly formed.
Among them, the Buddha Emperor Sword, the Heavenly Fang Sword, and the Long Vine Sword were placed horizontally in front of his chest, while the Double Sun Sword was constantly circling around Luo Zheng's head, completely protecting him.
At this moment, Luo Zheng said, "I just want to know if I don't move, can I kill you?"
"The God Slaying Sword Array, you've really mastered it …" Mu Ning's eyebrows slightly raised, and her expression revealed a look of vigilance.
Without waiting for Mu Ning to finish speaking, Luo Zheng gently waved his hands and used the Mind Control Technique of the Purple Cloud Divine Dao. The Buddha Emperor Sword and the Heavenly Fang Sword were like twin stars shuttling back and forth in the starry sky, intertwining and spiraling up and down, shooting toward Mu Ning.
The Long Vine Sword quietly drilled into the ground. When it drilled out in the next moment, the Long Vine Sword had already transformed into invisible vines of power from its blade, entangling toward Mu Ning!
The God-slaying Sword Formation had four swords as one. The Long Vine Sword was mainly for "entanglement." Its purpose was to entangle the opponent and then kill him with the Buddha Emperor Sword or the Heavenly Teeth Sword. As for the Chongyang Sword that was circling around Luo Zheng, it was used to protect the original body.
This was the first time Luo Zheng used the complete God Slaying Sword Array to fight against an enemy. As he controlled the four swords with his mind, he once again used his Taoist Connotation to activate the Infinite Ruler in his mind. The golden rings in his eyes suddenly appeared, and he looked at the wound on his arm.

Chapter 1879

Chapter 1880

"Can you still recover this time?"

Fortunately, Luo Zheng's worries didn't last for too long. After about the time it took for a stick of incense to burn, the Scarlet Plain slowly collapsed in front of his eyes.
In the blink of an eye, he had returned to his room. On the wall facing Luo Zheng, there was an additional Arena of Victory.
His winning streak had reached 33 matches, and his points had broken through the unprecedented 2 million mark.

Chapter 1881
Defeating Mu Ning would be his final victory in the Arena of Gods.

Chapter 1882

With a thought from Luo Zheng, this "monster" slowly straightened its legs and stood up like a human. The Old Man Extreme Evil was very excited as he transformed again and again.

Why was the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue placed on such a tray? It was a pity that the origins of the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue were difficult to verify. Otherwise, Luo Zheng might have to trace it back to its roots …
"Crack!"

"Plop …"
After this piece of mud was thrown into Luo Zheng's sea of chaos, it didn't move at all.
Luo Zheng also observed this scene with a nervous expression. The mud couldn't have lost its effect because it had been dug out for too long, right?
Now that he thought about it carefully, the first piece of mud he swallowed had a slower reaction, because that piece of mud had been dug out by the Tie family for too long. On the other hand, the "fresh" mud he got from the cave had a faster reaction.
The tray under the Ten Thousand Beasts Golden Imperial Statue had existed for a longer time. Perhaps the reaction time would be slower.
However, the waiting process was somewhat torturous. If the mud really lost its effect after a long time, Luo Zheng's time to condense the Godhood might have to be pushed back again!
Half an hour later … …
A bubble emerged from the bottom of the sea of chaos, and more bubbles began to gush out …
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng heaved a sigh of relief. It seemed that his worries were unnecessary.

Chapter 1884
Luo Zheng stared at the crystal on the godhead quietly. He couldn't contain his excitement.
He didn't have a godhead, so he couldn't use the power of faith. He could only use the "force" to perform the Divine Dao's remarkable abilities.

"If a true god can be born in my inner world, then my" force "is equivalent to a true god. Then, what's the difference between this force and the force of an ordinary true god?"
He didn't know what the difference was, but he was faintly looking forward to it.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
The cloud of swords kept drifting and shrinking. From afar, it looked like a monster was swallowing the cloud of swords.

"It's not small anymore …"
This crystal had completely merged with the two crystals of mud that Luo Zheng had absorbed. However, compared to the entire godhead plate, it was still an independent small piece.

This cloud of swords also left a transparent crystal in the godhead plate. The two transparent crystals overlapped and occupied about three-seventh of the godhead plate's surface area. According to the current progress, the remaining transparent crystal formed by the mud would occupy the entire godhead plate. There would be more than enough space!
Luo Zheng then threw the third and fourth piece of mud into the godhead plate. The transparent crystal in the godhead plate grew bigger and bigger. The strange thing was that this crystal didn't condense around the godhead plate. Instead, it was in the center of the godhead plate. It turned into a round crystal and lay there quietly, glowing faintly.
It was also his first time condensing a godhead. Naturally, he didn't know what was strange about it.

As this piece of mud turned into a cloud of mist, a complete crystal slowly formed in the godhead plate.
At the same time...
The bottom of the divine lattice disk began to rotate slowly, and the circle of divine runes on its surface began to flicker with a faint light.
This god lattice disk was originally used to support the godhead and stabilize it in the inner world, but when Luo Zheng's godhead condensed, the god lattice disk suddenly shook.
"Ka ka …"
Countless cracks appeared on the surface of the divine lattice plate, and not long after, the divine lattice plate disintegrated right in front of Luo Zheng's eyes, turning into countless pieces that fell into the Chaos Ocean!
"What's this?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
At the same time, he felt his mind tremble …
Once again, the Infinite Ruler shone with a dazzling light, piercing through Luo Zheng's fleshly body, his internal organs, and into his internal world.
The scattered light gathered once again and focused on the Godhead!
"Rumble …"
Luo Zheng's Inner World began to tremble …
The light emitted by the Infinity Ruler hit the transparent Godhead, producing rays of light. After these rays of light were refracted, they scattered in all directions. Some of the rays of light hit the Chaos Sea, and some of the rays of light hit the continent.
"What kind of phenomenon is this?"
On a continent in Luo Zheng's Inner World, the Evil Deities stood on the peak of a snowy mountain, looking up at the Godhead.
At this moment, he could feel an extraordinary power being nurtured within the Godhead!
"The feeling contained within is very similar to the Dao Connotation in the sword cloud condensed by the Creator, very similar … but more complex!" The eyes of the Evil Deities flickered with boundless light.
Almost everything that could be explored in this world had been comprehended by the Evil Deities.
Apart from the huge Sanskrit characters at the edge of this world, nothing else was a secret to him. Even the Icy Soul Devil Flame in the Ice Field was controlled by the Evil Deities. Unfortunately, the Creator didn't allow him to leave this world, so he could only be confined to this world forever.
However, the complex Dao Connotation emitted from the Godhead brought new hope to the Evil Deities. He felt that he could obtain new Divine Abilities from it and become even more powerful!
Luo Zheng looked at his Godhead with an incredulous expression.
That round godhood was constantly refracting all kinds of light, and a circle of densely packed scales appeared around the godhood. These scales came from the Immeasurable Ruler!
Not only that, but the Divine Dao imprints absorbed by the Infinite Ruler also appeared on these scales!
"The Dao Connotation of these Divine Daos has been engraved on my Godhead!"
In the Purple Soul Palace, Luo Zheng already had a certain degree of understanding of the Divine Domain. Many Divine Proof martial artists might be able to comprehend several Divine Daos, but when condensing their Godhead, they would choose the Divine Dao they mainly cultivated. Only a very small number of geniuses could condense two Godheads, or even three Godheads.
When Luo Zheng first came into contact with Divine Daos, he didn't understand the benefits of having multiple Divine Daos, because many Divine Proof experts cultivated more than one Divine Dao. For example, Luo Zheng himself cultivated the Emotion Severing Divine Dao and the Great Vow Technique, and even comprehended the Icy Soul Devil Flame of the Profound Ice Divine Dao.
During this period of time in the Purple Soul Palace, he understood that there was a limit to cultivating several Divine Daos at the same time.
All the Divine Abilities, excluding the "Outer Dao Divine Abilities" derived from them, were uniformly ranked by the True Gods of the Divine Realm. They were divided into the first three stages, the last three stages, and the Great Perfection stage, a total of seven stages.
For example, the first three stages of the Five Elements Divine Dao were "Five Elements Unite", "Metal and Wood Annihilation", and "Fire and Wood Spirit Movement". The last three stages were "Five Elements Spirit Convergence", "Water and Wood Separation", and "Five Elements Circulation".
Apart from these six stages, there was the Great Perfection stage of the seventh stage, "Divine Fate Five Spirit Body". After cultivating the Divine Fate Five Spirit Body, one would become a Great Perfection True God.
As for the Outer Dao Divine Abilities comprehended from the Divine Dao, they were comprehended from different stages of the Divine Dao. For example, in the Heaven Containing Palace, the "Five Elements Disillusion" that Han Yaozhi used was actually an Outer Dao Divine Ability comprehended from the "Five Elements Spirit Convergence". The "Five Elements Spirit Convergence" was the first stage of the last three stages of the Divine Dao Divine Ability. This "Five Elements Disillusion" was naturally not considered a powerful Outer Dao Divine Ability. No one was willing to cultivate Han Yaozhi's "Five Elements Disillusion". After all, some Outer Dao Divine Abilities were not even as good as the original Divine Ability.
As for the "Ice Soul Devil Flame" that Luo Zheng cultivated in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement, it was an Outer Dao Divine Ability that Saint Jiu comprehended from the sixth stage of the Profound Ice Divine Dao, "Profound Ice Sacred Fire". Any Outer Dao Divine Ability that was born from the Great Perfection stage was relatively powerful. Therefore, countless people hoped to obtain Saint Jiu's inheritance. However, up until now, there were very few who could obtain it …
It wasn't too difficult to cultivate the first three stages of a Divine Dao. After spending a certain amount of time and a certain amount of comprehension, one would be able to break through these limitations. This was especially true for the geniuses in the rich and powerful families. With their talent, it was very common for them to cultivate several Divine Daos.
However, if one wanted to cultivate a Divine Dao to the last three stages, one would need to condense a Godhead. Otherwise, it would be difficult to unleash the Dao of the last three stages.
However, Outer Dao Divine Abilities were an exception. Even if a True God had never cultivated a Divine Dao, if they could grasp an extremely powerful Outer Dao Divine Ability, they would be able to unleash an extremely terrifying power. Just like how Luo Zheng was able to unleash the Ice Soul Devil Flame even though he didn't cultivate the Profound Ice Divine Dao.
The most important thing was the Great Perfection stage Divine Ability. After condensing the Five Elements Godheads, one could only reach the Great Perfection stage through the Five Elements Divine Dao's Great Perfection stage "Divine Affinity Five Spirit Body". At the same time, one couldn't cultivate the Great Perfection stage Divine Abilities of other Divine Daos.
However, true gods with multiple Godhoods could choose which Divine Dao they wanted to cultivate with ease.
There were even some who could cultivate two kinds of Great Perfection stage Divine Abilities and become a Double Great Perfection True God. Their strength would be far stronger than ordinary Great Perfection True Gods!
Of course, True Gods with Double Godheads were already extremely rare. True Gods who could cultivate two kinds of Godheads to the Great Perfection stage were even rarer in the Divine Domain!
However, Luo Zheng saw that around his Godhead, there were three thousand little scales. This scene gave rise to an extremely strange feeling in Luo Zheng's heart.

Chapter 1885
In order to understand why he couldn't condense a godhood, Luo Zheng went to the Purple Soul Palace and flipped through many ancient books.
In one of the ancient books, there was a detailed description of godhoods.
All godhoods were condensed after a True God had gained a profound understanding of a certain Divine Dao and condensed into a cloud of Dao Essence!
These godhoods all had different shapes. Some were square, some were round, some were rectangular, some were even polygonal, and so on. If there were two godhoods, each would take half, and three godhoods would take one-third.
The edge of Luo Zheng's godhood was densely packed with scales, exactly the same as the Immeasurable Ruler. It was as if the ruler was wrapped around the edge of the godhood. Moreover, Luo Zheng could also comprehend the Dao Connotation of other Divine Daos from these scales.
After the Immeasurable Ruler's light beam shot into the godhood, it scattered three thousand rays of light. Most of the rays were pale golden, but some of them were different in color.
For example, the light beam that contained the Snow Imprint Divine Dao emitted a pale, cold light beam.
Whoosh!
His figure suddenly floated and shot down along with this cold light beam.
This cold light beam landed in the Primal Chaos Sea …
Around this light beam, faint snowflakes were slowly floating down. These snowflakes hid the Dao Connotation of the Snow Imprint Divine Dao, and the surface of the Primal Chaos Sea was slowly freezing.
Soon after, Luo Zheng's figure suddenly flashed and appeared under another purple light beam.
This purple light beam landed on the coast of a continent.
On the coast, there were several hundred people pointing at this light beam. These people, born in Luo Zheng's Inner World, seemed to be puzzled as to why these light beams appeared.
"Is this purple light an auspicious sign?"
"I think there's something we can't understand hidden in this light beam …"
"I wonder what kind of genius can comprehend it?"
Just as these people were discussing, Luo Zheng's sudden appearance gave them a big fright.
In this world, there were many people who could teleport, but there was almost no one like Luo Zheng who could arrive without the slightest spatial fluctuation …
These people came from a Rank Six Holy Land on the continent. Among them, there were many who had reached the Divine Sea Realm. Their foresight was not bad!
"This is …" One of the Divine Sea Realm elders stared at Luo Zheng.
"It's World Bearing Knight!"
As the other middle-aged man at the Spirit Sea Realm said, he suddenly knelt down and bowed deeply towards Luo Zheng, "Greetings, Sir World Revered One!"
Luo Zheng had many nicknames in this world, which were passed down through the development of different cultures. In the culture of this continent, not only did Luo Zheng create this world, he also carried the entire world on his shoulders.
Under the worship of the two mighty warriors of the Spirit Sea Realm, almost everyone knelt down on the ground. There was a young warrior behind them who was not convinced. This person was a recognized genius in a sixth-rank sect and also the son of the Sect Master. At such a young age, he had achieved something that was beyond the reach of ordinary people. Naturally, he was arrogant to the extreme.
Although he had heard from his elders that he knew Luo Zheng's true identity, he still refused to kneel …
"Kneel down, Jianhua!"
The mighty warrior of the Spirit Sea Realm said hurriedly. He waved his hand and a gust of wind was about to force the young warrior to kneel down.
Noticing this, Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He waved his hand and an invisible force spread out. "You may all rise."
These cultivators suddenly felt their knees lighten, and they couldn't help but stand up …
Luo Zheng didn't pay attention to them anymore. He carefully observed this purple light.
After this light gathered here, it turned into strands of purple Qi, which kept flowing and jumping. This was the Taoist Connotation of the Purple Qi Divine Way. And this Taoist Connotation was inherited from the Taoist Stele set up by Aunt Han Jiu in the Heaven-Containing Palace.
In fact, the Taoist Connotation that Luo Zheng was absorbing was not the complete Taoist Connotation.
The Taoist Connotation of the Five Elements Divine Ways he had absorbed was indeed at the sixth level, first perfection. It could be considered complete …
As for the other Divine Ways, they were more or less incomplete. For example, the Purple Qi Divine Way only had the Purple Qi Transformation, the Spirit Splitting Thought, and the Purple Qi Carrying Luck. Because when Aunt Han Jiu set up the Taoist Stele, she was only an upper True God. She hadn't comprehended the complete Purple Qi Divine Way, let alone cultivated the Purple Sky Extinction Ground. Naturally, she couldn't gather the complete sixth level, first perfection, and seventh level Divine Ways.
"There are three thousand Taoist Steles in the Realm of the Gods. Could it be that these Taoist Steles were also derived from Taoist Connotation …?"
Looking at these strands of light, Luo Zheng's mind was also constantly beating. These strands of light were like the skeleton of a world, connected to the Godhood above. He gradually understood why his father had placed the Infinite Ruler in his mind.
His father knew that he had cultivated the Primal Chaos Secret Technique.
His father and master might not have been able to foresee what stage he could cultivate the Primal Chaos Secret Technique to, but they must have guessed what the final result would be.
True essence could evolve into a martial artist's inner world, which was a secondary world. As the martial artist's cultivation continued to increase, the inner world would also grow larger and larger. However, this world was ultimately a secondary world. Any creature in the secondary world could not leave the martial artist's dantian, otherwise they would be directly reduced to Heaven and Earth Yuan Qi.
And the final form of a secondary world could only be the universe.
Although the universe was vast and granted the Saints the ability to be immortal and indestructible, in the end, it was only a vassal of the Divine Realm!
But the inner world derived from the Primal Chaos was different. The final form of the inner world derived from the Primal Chaos was the Divine Realm. It was just that the process of constructing the Divine Realm was fundamentally different from the inner world. The Infinite Ruler was the key.
Therefore, his father might have guessed that he would reach this step sooner or later, so he gave him the Infinite Ruler.
The Infinite Ruler, the Primal Chaos Secret Technique …
He pondered silently. Perhaps the reason why the Luo Family was targeted by the Mu Family and the Dongfang Family and even destroyed was related to these things …
Just as Luo Zheng was pondering, the old martial artist at the Spirit Sea Realm on the coast finally plucked up his courage and slowly floated towards Luo Zheng. With a respectful expression, he called in a low voice, "Venerable Tuo Shi."
Luo Zheng was originally lost in thought. Then he turned his head and nodded slightly, "What's the matter?"
"This old man can see that this purple Qi seems to contain infinite truths. Can I comprehend a thing or two from it? I hope Venerable Tuo Shi can give me some advice … "the Spirit Sea Realm martial artist asked.
Luo Zheng shook his head, "Only women can cultivate the Taoist Connotation in it, and they must have the Extreme Purple Yin Body. If there is a woman in this world who has the Extreme Purple Yin Body, you can try."
"So it's the Taoist Connotation. I see." The old martial artist at the Spirit Sea Realm nodded hurriedly. Naturally, he was a little disappointed. Taoist Connotation was a very new word for him, let alone the Extreme Purple Yin Body. It seemed that he could only ask all the female disciples in the Sect to try.
"You can't cultivate the Purple Qi Divine Tao, but …" Luo Zheng pointed to the sky, "Do you see those rays of light?"
The old martial artist at the Spirit Sea Realm nodded hurriedly. Today's vision of heaven and earth was indeed extraordinary. He had followed one of the rays of light and led the elites of the Sect to this place.
"Except for the golden rays, you can learn the Taoist Connotation condensed by the other rays of light," Luo Zheng said.
Those golden rays of light were the Divine Tao that Luo Zheng had not included. Almost all the scales that had been included in the Divine Tao refracted rays of light that contained various auras and colors.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the old martial artist at the Spirit Sea Realm was both surprised and delighted. At this moment, he wished he could go there immediately. However, with Venerable Tuo Shi here, he dared not make any noise …
"Clatter …"
At this moment, a huge Realm Cloud suddenly appeared in the extreme distance of his Inner World …
Someone had stepped into the Realm Lord realm!
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with surprise. The shadow of that Evil Deities flashed in his mind. Then, he smiled in relief. That Evil Deities' talent was indeed terrifying. He had just attained the Divine Tao, but he could also step into the Realm Lord realm right after him.
He said to the old martial artist indifferently, "Goodbye." His body flashed and disappeared in front of everyone without causing any spatial fluctuations.
Seeing Luo Zheng leave, the old martial artist at the Spirit Sea Realm suddenly turned his head and pointed to a grey ray of light not far away, "Quick, let's go there quickly. Don't let the Yin Sifting Sect get there first!" Halfway through, he shouted again, "Send more people here to guard this purple ray of light. Then bring all the female disciples of the Sect, no, all the female warriors of the Sect!"
The Yin Sifting Sect was also a sixth-rank Holy Land …

Chapter 1886
All the Holy Lands and big families in the Inner World took action one after another. They fought over the light rays that contained the Taoist Connotation, and even started a war between sects …
Back then, Luo Zheng didn't like the Evil Deities, so he built his own Divine Kingdom to collect the power of faith. After he suppressed the Evil Deities, the Evil Deities disbanded the Divine Kingdom and set a rule in his Inner World that all Warriors were not allowed to build their own Divine Kingdom, nor were they allowed to offer incense or absorb the power of faith.
Every time an ambitious person appeared and wanted to develop his Sect into a Divine Kingdom, the Evil Deities would intervene.
As the strongest in this world, the strength of the Evil Deities far exceeded that of ordinary Warriors. Although those ambitious people didn't know why the Evil Deities didn't allow it, they had no choice but to develop their Holy Lands. Therefore, this world only had Holy Lands and no Divine Kingdoms.
Luo Zheng didn't interfere with these Holy Lands fighting over the Dao Connotation in these rays of light. The evolution of a world was like the slow growth of a baby, and there was a natural law. As long as it didn't affect the balance of the world as seriously as the Evil Deities did, Luo Zheng generally wouldn't interfere.
On the peak of the Great Snow Mountain.
The Evil Deities sat cross-legged alone at the peak …
Since the Evil Deities retired, he had become a legend second only to the Creator, and played the role of a manager in this world.
Only a few top experts knew of the existence of the Evil Deities …
As the strange phenomena in the sky continued to appear, the Evil Deities felt the bottleneck on his body being lifted.
His bottleneck wasn't caused by himself, but by this world. This world couldn't tolerate the existence of a Realm Lord before, so he was destined to be unable to break through his cultivation.
However, when Luo Zheng completed his Godhood, he was like a wild horse that broke free of the restraints of this world and stepped into the Realm Lord realm.
After becoming a Realm Lord, he would disturb the world's essence and form a layer of Realm Cloud. It was this expanding Realm Cloud that attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
"Ten miles … Twenty miles … Thirty miles …"
Luo Zheng raised his head and looked at the rapidly expanding Boundary Clouds, his eyes revealing a trace of surprise.
In the Great Yan Universe, even the most outstanding Sector Lord could only spread out 20 li of World Cloud. The stability of the Evil Deities' inner world far exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations!
"Huff huff …"
In the end, the Realm Cloud of Evil Deities stopped at a range of sixty kilometers.
Evil Deities naturally didn't understand what these realm clouds meant. After all, he was the first person to break through to the sector lord realm in this world. These 60 miles of realm clouds were enough to make anyone else feel inferior.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
A few figures were flying up the snowy mountain. They were Evil Deities' wives, daughters, and family. Evil Deities' family was currently living in seclusion on this snowy mountain. When they saw Evil Deities break through, they quickly rushed up.
"Father, you've made another breakthrough in your cultivation!"
"Congratulations, husband!"
"Father has finally broken through the bottleneck that has troubled him for so many years …"
Evil Deities also had a faint look of joy on his face. However, stepping into this new realm was only an insignificant achievement for him. When his Godhood condensed, Evil Deities understood that he had reached the end of the martial path. The more powerful Divine Dao was his starting point.
At this moment, he turned his gaze and saw a figure floating in the sky. Evil Deities stroked his daughter's hair and said to his wife, "I'll be back soon …"
With a wave of spatial laws, Evil Deities appeared in front of Luo Zheng. He didn't kneel as he knew that Luo Zheng didn't care about such formalities.
"Congratulations," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Evil Deities nodded, "Thank you …" He thought for a moment before asking, "There are new changes in this world. Has Lord also broken through?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
"This world is too small, and it's limiting me. My Lord, can you increase your cultivation a little faster? Or can I go to a world outside of this world? "Evil Deities asked cautiously.
Evil Deities didn't like these invisible restrictions. He also believed that his cultivation speed was faster than Luo Zheng's. If he could break these shackles, his strength would surpass Lord's very quickly …
Luo Zheng stared at Evil Deities with a faint smile on his face. Then he said calmly, "Perhaps you still don't understand the nature of this world … It's your fortune to be born in this world. Or rather, it's your fortune."
Evil Deities' heart skipped a beat when he heard this. "What does Lord mean?"
Luo Zheng said calmly, "You must have tried to bring the creatures in your inner world into this world, right?"
Evil Deities nodded. After reaching the Ultimate Divine Realm, he had indeed tried to take out the creatures in his inner world. However, the moment he took them out, they instantly dissipated and turned into a wisp of vital essence …
Evil Deities couldn't understand this.
"The people outside of this world also have an inner world like yours. The creatures that leave their inner worlds end up exactly the same as what you saw," Luo Zheng continued.
In the Divine Territory, no matter if it was a Lower True God or a Perfect True God, they couldn't release the creatures in their inner worlds. The only place that could do so was the universe created by the Saints. Even the universe had strict restrictions. Only by entering the Divine Path and being recognized by the Divine Path could one become a "creature of the same level" and be qualified to enter the Divine Territory.
In the Great Yan Universe, Luo Zheng obtained the recognition of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao before he obtained the qualifications to enter the Divine Domain. Later on, in the Heaven-containing Mansion, he obtained the recognition of several hundred Divine Daos through the Immeasurable Ruler.
If it weren't for the fact that the chaotic world created by Luo Zheng was very special, Evil Deities wouldn't have been able to leave for the rest of his life. At least, he would have to wait until Luo Zheng became a Saint …
Evil Deities' expression changed slightly. "If I leave this world, will I also turn into a ball of Vital Essence like the creatures in my inner world?"
"That's compared to the inner worlds created by other True Gods. The world I created is a little special. The creatures that leave this world won't turn into Vital Essence. When the time is right, I'll let you leave naturally …" Luo Zheng said lightly.
Hearing this, Evil Deities' eyes revealed a strange expression. He didn't understand some of the words of the Lord. There seemed to be other Gods in the Lord's world? The inner worlds created by those Gods were the same as his.
Why was the Lord's inner world different?
How many Gods were there in the Lord's world?
Could they also cultivate the Taoist Connotation …
Evil Deities' mind was filled with countless questions.
It wasn't that Evil Deities wasn't smart. In fact, he was extremely talented.
It was just that he was limited by the natural order of life!
Evil Deities, who grew up in this relatively small world, couldn't understand the vastness of the Divine Realm. According to his current thinking, he thought that Luo Zheng's world was about the same size as his.
"Now that my Godhood has been formed, there are some things I need to trouble you with," Luo Zheng said lightly.
Although some people in this world knew of Luo Zheng's existence, they didn't form a unified belief. After condensing the Godhood, Luo Zheng could absorb the Power of Faith through the Godhood.
The Power of Faith was the "momentum" of a world. After becoming a True God, the Divine Warriors would abandon Vital Essence and use the Power of Faith instead. This way, they could avoid consuming the Qi of Chaos.
However, Luo Zheng was still a little hesitant about whether to choose the Power of Faith or to continue using the Qi of Chaos.
For ordinary True Gods, using Vital Essence to cast Magical Powers consumed too much energy. If the consumption was excessive, it could even cause the entire inner world to collapse and need to be rebuilt. Naturally, the loss outweighed the gain. However, Luo Zheng's Qi of Chaos was more durable than Vital Essence. Moreover, Luo Zheng's World Tree could still continuously absorb the Qi of Chaos from outside the world. Therefore, Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to use the Power of Faith.
However, Luo Zheng needed to figure out what was the difference between the Power of Faith produced by the Qi of Chaos and the Power of Faith of other True Gods.

Chapter 1887
Soon after, Luo Zheng looked around and pointed at a mountain peak on one side of the Snowy Mountain.
All the snow on that mountain peak disappeared, revealing a dark blue rock wall.
With a wave of his hand, Luo Zheng's mountain peak floated up. Under Luo Zheng's control, this huge mountain peak continuously split apart. For a moment, smoke and dust billowed and stone fragments danced in the air.
Seeing this scene, the Evil Deities were secretly shocked.
Relying on the power of the Evil Deities, it was very easy to destroy a mountain peak, but to lift it as easily as if it was as light as a feather, and even casually carve it, it was impossible.
Not long after, that huge mountain peak turned into huge rocks. Then, Luo Zheng slightly closed his eyes and an invisible power spread out from his body. In this world, he was an omniscient and omnipotent existence, so he had no resistance to any kind of power.
After that invisible power brushed past, the surface of all the huge rocks turned into fine powder and drifted away with the wind.
When all the powder was peeled off, these huge rocks turned into huge statues of Luo Zheng, floating in the air.
"I'll place these 100 statues in every corner of this world. I'll leave the rest to you." After saying that, Luo Zheng's figure strangely disappeared in the air again. Together with the 100 statues that covered the sky and the sun, he disappeared as well.
Not long after, Luo Zheng appeared in Cui Song City, thousands of miles away from the Snowy Mountain.
When he strangely floated in the air without triggering any restrictions in the city, he attracted the attention of many people. Many cultivators pointed at Luo Zheng, speculating about his identity.
Then, Luo Zheng gently waved his hand.
"Boom!"
A huge statue fell on the open space of Cui Song City, causing the entire city to tremble. Luo Zheng then disappeared in front of everyone without looking back.
The moment Luo Zheng's statue fell on the ground, it seemed as if it was connected to the entire city. At the same time, it emitted an irresistible majesty, as if it was above all living things.
"This, this is a statue of God!"
"This is a statue of my Lord …"
"This statue will be a divine object of Jadepine City. No one is allowed to violate it!"
The warriors around the open space knelt down one after another, worshipping the statue of Luo Zheng. The statue itself emitted a sacred and inviolable aura. Under the shroud of this aura, it was difficult for anyone to get close to the statue. Just by looking at it, one would feel an irresistible sense of awe, which came from the instinctive awe of living creatures.
"Tian Yun City …"
"Boom!"
Luo Zheng's statue fell into the central square of Sky Fortune City …
Qingxuan Holy Ground …
"Boom …"
Luo Zheng placed statues of himself in all the main cities and holy lands …
After finishing all of this, he finally came to an end. Then, his incarnation turned into a stream of Chaos Qi and quietly disappeared from this world.
Next, Evil Deities gathered all the strong people in this world and announced the establishment of a secret cult.
Most of the leaders of the holy lands didn't like secret cults. After all, these leaders were the highest rulers of the holy lands. Accepting this secret cult was equivalent to giving up a portion of their power.
But although Evil Deities had been in seclusion for countless years, they still had absolute power in this world. No one dared to disobey them.
Moreover, Evil Deities was working for the 'Lord' of this world. Who would dare to slight them?
Not long after …
Secret cult temples were set up in the various cities.
Whether it was mortals or warriors, they all had to worship Luo Zheng's statue and contribute their faith.
The power of faith also flowed endlessly into Luo Zheng's godhood …
…
…
When Han Liusu defeated Anonymous and obtained extra points, it confirmed that Anonymous came from Han Jia.
The Qin Family ranked below Han Jia, as well as the Tang Family and other families ranked above them, were helpless. The floating island ranking was about to begin, and in this month, Han Jia's disciples had all gone through three battles. They simply didn't have a chance to attack Han Jia.
As for Han Liusu, after obtaining Anonymous's points, she naturally wouldn't return to the Arena of Gods …
Two days later …
The wind in the Sea of Time grew stronger and stronger. The beautiful Sea of Time turned into waves that kept rolling on the surface of the sea.
Occasionally, a few strange fish would come out of the sea. These creatures that wandered the fragments of time had very mysterious powers. They could prevent themselves from being torn apart by the Sea of Time …
The growing wind was like a pair of chopsticks stuck into muddy water, constantly stirring up the thick clouds. The thick clouds that couldn't be dispersed were like a giant spiral, spiraling above the Sea of Time.
At the edge of the Purple Soul Palace, there was a floating observation platform. Many female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace stood there. These female disciples all had haggard looks on their faces.
In the past few months, for the sake of points, they had fought hard in the Arena of Gods. But due to their limited strength, the points they obtained were still limited.
A few days ago, the Purple Soul Palace's points were calculated. They were far from meeting their master's requirements. Almost everyone fell into despair. According to Aunt Han Jiu, most of them would be leaving Han Jia's floating island.
To be able to enter the center of the divine realm and enter a rich family, every female in the Purple Soul Palace was the pride of their clan.
Once they were expelled from the floating island, it would be an unimaginable nightmare for them.
But they never would've thought that a day ago, a piece of news suddenly spread. Han Jia's eldest princess Han Liusu had suddenly defeated an anonymous person who had participated in the leapfrog battle. In one battle, she had obtained nearly two million points!
This meant that Han Jia had gained a Paragon out of nowhere.
It was this piece of news that saved the Purple Soul Palace disciples from despair …
"Say, what kind of person do you think that anonymous person is? A Proving Divinity expert can accumulate several million points. I worked myself to death just to accumulate two hundred points. "A female sighed.
"Hahaha, the most important thing is that the anonymous person lost to the eldest princess. He gave away all the points he had painstakingly accumulated. I think that anonymous genius must be heartbroken!" Another female laughed.
"You all have pig brains. Think carefully … Why did the anonymous person lose to the eldest princess? It's said that the anonymous person is from our Han Jia. He lost to the eldest princess on purpose! "
"There's such a thing?"
"I don't really believe it. The eldest princess's strength is already extraordinary. She's ranked third among the lower true gods. It's normal for the anonymous person to lose to the eldest princess!"
Quite a few female faces were filled with shock …
These low-level true gods and Proving Divinities of the rich families had very limited sources of information. Now, all the rich families had confirmed that the anonymous person came from Han Jia, or even the Purple Soul Palace. However, most of the female members of the Purple Soul Palace didn't know.
Luo Zheng sat at the edge of the observation deck, overlooking the boundless sea of time. Hearing all sorts of discussions and guesses, he smiled faintly. At least the Purple Soul Palace had been saved for Aunt Han Jiu. He had also successfully stepped into the true god realm.
Ever since his godhood had formed, he could feel the power of faith in his body surging. However, he hadn't had a chance to use it. He also didn't dare enter the Arena of Gods at such a sensitive time …
Aunt Han Jiu secretly told him not to act rashly. After the ranking ended, the Dongfang family could come to the Han Jia at any time. At that time, she would also face great pressure.
"I wonder how Han Liusu is doing these days." Thinking of Han Liusu's gaze that day, it was like a needle piercing the soft part of his heart. However, his eyes quickly flashed with a cold light. "Forget it. As long as I enter Buzhou Spirit Mountain, everything will be put down …"
Once the ranking ended, the forbidden area would be opened. As long as he entered Buzhou Spirit Mountain, everything would end. The pressure from the Dongfang family would naturally disappear. So what if they knew his true identity?
Although that was what she thought, she still felt a little uncomfortable in her heart …
"Hey! Luo Tianxing, you've only been here for a few months and you've already proven your godhood! How can you advance so quickly? "A pretty girl suddenly asked.
Luo Zheng joining the Purple Soul Palace as a man was already eye-catching. Not long after joining, he had already broken through to the True God Realm. Naturally, this attracted the curiosity of some girls …
"I've been stuck at the Proving God Realm for thousands of years. When I entered the floating island, I was able to comprehend the Purple Soul Palace's subtleties. I broke through the bottleneck and became a True God," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"I see. Then you're considered lucky. I've been in the Purple Soul Palace for more than thirty years, but my cultivation hasn't improved." The girl shook her head.
"There will always be opportunities. The only thing lacking in the path of godhood is opportunity," Luo Zheng comforted her.
"Mm." The girl nodded. Suddenly, she pointed below and said, "Look, the floating island is starting to move. The ranking has begun …"
"Rumble …"
Following a dull rumble, Luo Zheng saw the Xie Clan and Liu Clan's floating islands slowly rise up. At the same time, the Luo Clan's floating island began to slowly descend. It was actually falling into the Sea of Time!

Chapter 1888
The ranking of the 36 floating islands in the Sea of Time started from the bottom.
The Luo Clan's floating island was originally ranked third from the bottom. Now that the Xie and Zhou Clans' floating islands had moved up, the Luo Clan's floating island had started to move down slowly …
According to the ranking rules of the Sea of Time, the last floating island would be removed from the Sea of Time and replaced by a new wealthy clan.
Of course, the new wealthy clan had to have a Saint overseeing it.
The most powerful Saints in the Divine Dominion were almost all gathered in the Sea of Time, but not all of them were here. Although some large clans had Saints, their overall strength was too weak and could not compete with the wealthy clans on the floating islands. Therefore, they were not qualified to become wealthy clans.
"The Luo Clan can't hold on this time?"
"There's no other way. The Saint Halls have long been unhappy with the Luo Clan's empty floating island. There's no one on it, and it even took up a spot!"
"The Xie and Liu Clans originally came from the east of the Divine Dominion. They were wealthy clans supported by the Dongfang Clan. This time, these two clans performed extremely well in the Arena of Gods. I'm afraid they won't be able to maintain the Luo Clan's ranking with the few points Yu Taibai earned …"
These women were also talking about the Luo Clan's floating island. When they mentioned the Luo Clan's floating island, many people thought it was a miracle. They had relied on one person's points to stand in the Sea of Time for so many years and had not fallen into the Sea of Time.
Hearing these words, Luo Zheng's frown deepened.
"Huff … Huff …"
His clothes fluttered in the wind. He was like a statue as he stared at the Luo Clan's floating island. In his subconscious, this floating island seemed to have something important. If it fell into the Sea of Time, he would miss it for the rest of his life.
When the Luo Clan's floating island descended to the second last level, the Zhou Clan's floating island, which was ranked fourth from the bottom, suddenly shook and began to move downward rapidly!
"Look, the Zhou Clan's floating island is also moving …"
"This time, the Zhou Clan's performance is not very good. It's said that they are being suppressed by the other wealthy clans!"
"I wonder who will be last between the Zhou Clan and the Luo Clan?"
Noticing the Zhou Clan's floating island that was rapidly descending, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly. A glimmer of hope rose in his heart.
Every floating island was a huge monster the size of a city.........................
Soon after …
The Luo Clan's floating island swayed in a circle below the second-to-last level, and after a slight tremble, it actually came to a steady stop. Meanwhile, the Zhou Clan's floating island descended from the side of the Luo Clan's floating island, taking the lead and stopping at the 36th position.
"The Zhou Clan … has been eliminated by the Templars!"
"Looks like the Luo Clan's floating island has escaped yet again …"
"I'm afraid I won't have a chance in the next floating island ranking!"
Seeing this, Luo Zheng heaved a deep sigh of relief.
The Luo Family's floating island was already in such a dilapidated state. It was nothing more than a barren floating island in the Sea of Time. There was no point in leaving it there, but Yu Taibai had desperately tried to keep it there. There had to be some other reason.
As for Luo Zheng, although he had never set foot on the Luo Family's floating island in his life, there was an indistinct bond between him and this floating island. He did not wish for this floating island to be buried.
'If there's a chance, I'll definitely make this floating island surpass all the other major clans,' Luo Zheng thought to himself.
The upper echelons of the major clans already knew the ranking. On the last day of the ranking, every major clan had someone in charge of calculating the points.
After the Zhou Clan's floating island fell to the last place, they finally notified everyone to evacuate the floating island …
Whether it was the Zhou Clan's external martial artists or the Zhou Clan's main clan members, they all had sorrowful expressions on their faces.
The Zhou Clan could be considered an old major clan in the Sea of Time. They had already entered the Sea of Time 340 divine epochs ago. At the same time, they had brought all the core members of the Zhou Clan into the floating island.
The Zhou Clan had once had a glorious period. About 180 divine epochs ago, the Zhou Clan had even entered the top five rankings.
At that time, the Zhou Clan had never imagined that they would be forced to leave the Sea of Time one day …
From all around the Sea of Time, there was an endless stream of Blind Tribesmen driving small boats over and picking up the Zhou Clan's members one by one. Luo Zheng also noticed that apart from the small boats, the Blind Tribesmen also had large boats that were hundreds of feet long …
The Blind Tribesmen were rather efficient. There were actually tens of thousands of small boats and large boats that had gathered from all directions. They were like brown leaves that constantly floated over and carried people back and forth.
However, there were also many people in the Zhou Clan. There were probably more than 700,000 to 800,000 main clan members and external martial artists. It would not be easy to transport so many people out in a short period of time …
Looking down at the scene of the Zhou Clan moving out of the Sea of Time, Luo Zheng's eyes flickered slightly. 'The Zhou Clan's floating island fell into the Sea of Time because they were suppressed by the Grand Clans Alliance. The Dongfang Clan and the Mu Clan supported the Xie Clan and the Liu Clan to suppress the Zhou Clan. Instead of eliminating the Luo Clan, they directly drove the Zhou Clan out of the Sea of Time …
'The Zhou Clan's Saint must also have a grudge against the Dongfang Clan …
'I wonder if there's any possibility of roping in these Saints who were eliminated from the Sea of Time …'
He pondered hard in his mind. Suddenly, he grinned and shook his head with a smile. His father's plan could be said to have scruples in all aspects. What he could think of, his father must have made arrangements. Suddenly, Luo Zheng felt that the situation was not as pessimistic as he had imagined.
When the Zhou Clan's people left their floating islands, the Xie Clan and the Liu Clan's floating islands could be said to be soaring. These two Grand Clans at the bottom kept surpassing the other floating islands.
The Xie Clan finally stopped at 17th place, while the Liu Clan stopped at 13th place!
"Sigh, the Xie Clan and the Liu Clan are sensible people. After becoming Grand Clans, they immediately sought refuge with the Dongfang Clan. The Dongfang Clan will naturally join forces with the other Grand Clans to raise their rankings!"
"In the future, the All Saints Hall will probably belong to the Dongfang Clan …"
The female disciples of the Purple Soul Palace sighed softly.
The positions of the 36 floating islands were constantly changing. This time, the ranking of the floating islands changed greatly, but most of it was within expectations.
"Rumble …"
Luo Zheng suddenly felt a tremor beneath his feet. The entire floating island began to slowly float upwards.
The Han Clan was previously ranked 10th out of the 36 floating islands. Now, under the constant tremors, it actually surpassed the Tang Clan, the Gu Clan, and the Fang Clan, finally stopping at 7th place!
And the Gu Clan's floating island, which was originally ranked 8th, actually fell straight down, from 8th place to 17th place!
Because the Gu Clan had two extremely powerful Paragon existences, Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng, in addition to the Gu Clan having very few people, they had a huge advantage in the calculation of points. The Gu Clan's floating island had always been relatively stable. It was almost unprecedented for it to suddenly fall so much, causing many disciples of the Grand Clans to sigh.
Many people only just remembered that the Gu Clan's two people didn't seem to have participated in the public battle of the floating islands this time.
After all the floating islands were once again ranked, the disciples of the Zhou Clan's floating island also completely evacuated. Under everyone's gaze, the majestic floating island tilted slightly to the side, and swept towards the Sea of Time.
The moment the huge floating island came into contact with the Sea of Time, the originally magnificent and colorful Sea of Time seemed to have turned into a ruthless giant beast. Under the constant chewing, it tore the floating island into pieces and then devoured it completely in the blink of an eye. Not even a bit of residue was left, nor did it cause any waves.

Chapter 1889
The Spirit Heart Palace was immersed in a peaceful atmosphere …
The palace maids used the God Willow Dew to sprinkle around the palace, coating the entire palace with a layer of flawless radiance.

Aunt Hanjiu wore a purple robe. That kind of beautiful aura isolated her from the rest of the world. She reclined on a long bench, her gaze drifting. No one knew what she was thinking.
"Actually, the biggest contributor to our Han Clan this time is none other than Second Sage. I didn't expect Second Sage to send out such a handsome disciple at such a crucial time!" Tianxiao laughed.
Everyone in the Han Clan looked at Aunt Hanjiu, their eyes full of admiration.

Therefore, after Luo Zheng went into seclusion, Aunt Hanjiu deliberately kept a distance from him to avoid being noticed.
As for the question of who it was, she could just play dumb. At the very least, there was no direct evidence to prove that the anonymous person was in the Purple Soul Palace...

According to what Aunt Hanjiu said, the anonymous person really wasn't from the Han family? Just a coincidence that he lost to the eldest princess? That really was Han family's great fortune!
At this time, a faint laughter sounded, echoing throughout the Spirit Heart Palace.

As the Holy Emperor of Han family, Emperor Hanqing rarely showed himself.
Although the Han family's floating island's rise in ranking was something worth celebrating, the Holy Emperor's appearance in the Spirit Heart Palace was even more unexpected.
Aunt Hanjiu's face showed a meaningful smile, she slowly stood up and slightly bowed to Emperor Hanqing.
Emperor Hanqing smiled and said, "If this is all a coincidence, then the anonymous person wouldn't have lost to Liusu. If he belongs to the Han family, then he purposely lost to Liusu because he didn't want to be exposed. I heard from Liusu that the anonymous person didn't want to join the Dongfang family."
"Perhaps so. I just don't know." Aunt Hanjiu smiled faintly. She had been dissatisfied with Emperor Hanqing for a long time. This Holy Emperor had long since lost his ambition. In recent years, he had been leaning towards the Dongfang family.
At this time, Emperor Hanqing suddenly put away his smile. Those deep eyes seemed to contain the time of several worlds. He stared at Aunt Hanjiu and said, "No matter who that person is, no matter what your intentions are, you must find him."
The smile on Aunt Hanjiu's face suddenly froze, and the peaceful atmosphere in the Spirit Heart Palace disappeared without a trace.
Han Tian Xiao and the others all sat up straight. They didn't know why the Holy Emperor had suddenly changed his expression. There seemed to be something they didn't know …
"What is the meaning of this, Holy Emperor? I don't understand. "Aunt Hanjiu's face was covered in a thin layer of frost.
"The Dongfang family must find him. The Han family can't do that." Emperor Hanqing continued.
Aunt Hanjiu snorted, "And if I say no?"
Since the words had already been spoken, Aunt Hanjiu also knew that there was no point in playing dumb and did not continue to conceal it.
Emperor Hanqing smiled coldly, "Tomorrow is the Ling Sun. After that, the Dongfang family will personally send people. If you can deal with them, then you have the ability."
With that, Emperor Hanqing turned to leave …
"Emperor Hanqing!"
Aunty Hanjiu suddenly shouted. Her body tensed up, outlining her perfect curves. Her face was filled with anger.
Even a quasi-sage like Aunt Hanjiu couldn't call him by his name …
Han Tian Xiao and the others gasped. Their Sages and quasi-sages wouldn't make a move, would they?
Emperor Hanqing stopped in his tracks.
"Does the Han family need to become someone else's vassal?" Aunt Hanjiu asked.
As the master of the universe, the Saints had already surpassed the category of Gods. After constructing a huge and complex world, their understanding of the world and life had increased to another level. It could be said that every Saint had absolute wisdom. They had seen the collapse of the world, seen the birth and death of billions of lives, and experienced the endless reincarnation of life and death.
How could a Sage like that become someone else's vassal?
In Aunt Hanjiu's eyes, Emperor Hanqing was a domineering existence. The Han family had never relied on anyone before. Why had it changed so much in recent years?

Chapter 1890
Emperor Han Qing's last words were profound. Aunt Han Jiu, Han Tianxiao, and the rest of the Han Family's upper echelons were stunned.
Over the years, the Han Family had been trying to get close to the Dongfang Family. This was the Holy King's decision, and the Han Family's people had no reason to object. Before Luo Zheng had come to the floating island, she'd been thinking about building another one. She'd been thinking about the next opportunity to become a True Saint …
So what should she do?
At this time, the image of Luo Zheng flashed through Aunt Hanjiu's mind.
Perhaps her choice should be simpler?
…
…
After the floating island's ranking was decided, Luo Zheng returned to the Purple Soul Palace's training room.
After forming his godhood, he began to gather faith.
The Evil Deities acted very quickly. The martial artists in the Inner World, and even the intelligent creatures of other races, began to worship Luo Zheng's statue.
Many years ago, Luo Zheng had witnessed the power of faith in the universe. For example, Soot's greatest reliance was the power of faith accumulated by the Demon Night Clan. This kind of power could be considered a kind of spiritual energy. After accumulating it, it would undergo a quantitative change. The Demon Night Clan even used a special Faith Pool to store the power of faith.
As for the Ancestral Dragon, it fed on the power of faith to grow rapidly.
But in Luo Zheng's Inner World, a very strange scene occurred.
The statue erected in Jadepine City quickly became a holy object in everyone's hearts. Every day, there would be people worshipping it, offering their faith power to their Lord.
Luo Zheng's statue also didn't need to be cleaned. All the dust and filth couldn't contaminate the statue of the Lord.
But on a certain day, after a young warrior finished worshipping, he looked up and said, "Master! The statue of the Lord has turned black! "
In the Esoteric Religion established by the Evil Deities, staring at Luo Zheng's statue was a blasphemous act. Most people lowered their heads and worshipped it. By the time the young warrior said it, Luo Zheng's statue was already quite black.
In fact, it wasn't just the statue in Jadepine City. The hundreds of statues scattered around the world were also dyed black after a period of time.
And the more people worshipped, the darker the statue …
"Who defiled the Lord's statue!"
"Who has such guts?"
"Find him and burn him to death …"
The devout people in Jadepine City clamored. They had forgotten that these statues emitted a sacred and inviolable aura. Almost no one could approach these statues!
Luo Zheng's Inner Avatar and the Evil Deities appeared in the sky above Jadepine City once again.
The Evil Deities looked at Luo Zheng's black statue with a puzzled expression. "This shouldn't be! How could the power of faith make the statue become like this? "
According to the time of this world, the Evil Deities also collected the power of faith tens of thousands of years ago. At that time, Luo Zheng hadn't condensed his Godhood and couldn't use the power of faith. The power of faith collected by the Evil Deities at that time was normal. Luo Zheng had also seen it with his own eyes.
But after condensing his Godhood and formally absorbing the power of faith, this kind of inexplicable thing happened. Naturally, he was very puzzled.
Luo Zheng stepped forward and stretched out his hand to touch his statue. A strange expression appeared on his face. He glanced at the people kneeling on the ground, then said to the Evil Deities: "Take this statue away first."
After saying that, Luo Zheng and the statue disappeared from Jadepine City's square at the same time. The Evil Deities naturally followed Luo Zheng and left.
Unexpectedly, as soon as Luo Zheng took the statue away, the people worshipping the statue actually started to wail.
"Lord, don't abandon us!"
"We are your most loyal servants …"
"…"
It turned out that these people thought that Luo Zheng was taking his anger out on them, which was why he took the statue away. After continuously worshipping it, Luo Zheng had already become their spiritual sustenance.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng once again returned to his original place and curled his lips. Originally, he was rather disgusted with secret religions. But after attaining the Divine Path, collecting the power of faith and using the "power of faith" in his inner world was practically a compulsory course.
Only Saints didn't care about Faith Energy. Because they could utilize the 'force' of the entire universe at will. For example, during the War of the Universe, regardless of whether it was the largest ferocious beast or the smallest blade of grass, all of them emanated that 'force'. Saints didn't have to absorb Faith Energy intentionally. Even if someone established a secret sect in the universe or seized Faith Energy like the Yao Ye Clan, Saints couldn't be bothered to interfere.
Helpless, Luo Zheng gently waved his hand, and a brand-new statue rose from the ground amidst the slight earthquake. Luo Zheng turned around and left amidst the intense cheers.
On top of the snowy mountain …
The pitch-black statue was particularly eye-catching against the backdrop of the white snow.
Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed one of the statue's hands. With a light twist, the stone hand immediately shattered. At the same time, a faint power was emitted from the broken stone. He looked at the broken stone in his hand and said, "It's also black inside …"
Evil Deities also picked up a broken stone and pondered, "The aura emitted by this stone is very similar to the power of faith, but why is it black?"
"Black power of faith … what secret religion did you establish?" Luo Zheng asked.
The believers had different beliefs, so the color and aura of the power of faith they produced were also different.
For example, Xun was the King of Massacre of the Yao Ye clan. When countless people of the Yao Ye clan worshipped Xun, they would all emit a killing intent. Therefore, Xun's statue was blood red. The power of faith gathered by her faith pool was like a pool of blood. This power of faith also contained a ruthless and murderous intent.

Chapter 1891
Another example was Ying, the King of Life. The Power of Faith she collected contained the vitality of life. Therefore, her statue was green, and so was the Power of Faith, which contained endless Power of Life.
If the Evil Deities established a cult, it was possible to produce this kind of black Power of Faith.
When Luo Zheng asked this question, the Evil Deities instantly understood what he meant. They shook their heads and said, "This is absolutely impossible. The Esoteric Cult was established according to the previous method. There can't be any mistakes. What's more, as you can see, those believers are not evil people. They can't bring this black Power of Faith, and all the statues in the world are the same."
Luo Zheng nodded and looked at his statue. "Well, I don't feel any evil in this power, but why is it like this …"
The Power of Faith could not be separated from the stone. How could it be used?
According to the process of condensing the Power of Faith, the accumulated Power of Faith would automatically flow into the Godhead and be stored. The Godhead was equivalent to the Pool of Faith.
But how could these black stones be stored …
"It should be a problem caused by practicing the Secret Arts of Chaos," Luo Zheng thought and frowned.
Luo Zheng might have gained a lot from practicing the Secret Arts of Chaos, but he also encountered a lot of trouble along the way. It was not easy to condense the Godhead by accident, but now he encountered a new problem.
While Luo Zheng was thinking, the Evil Deities seemed to think of something and said, "Lord, some strange things have happened in this world, and some things have appeared … In fact, they are somewhat similar to these black stones."

Luo Zheng's figure disappeared and followed the Evil Deities to another continent.
The continent in his Inner World was divided into seven pieces. Tens of thousands of years ago, the continent in front of him was called the Demon Slayer Continent. Because it was connected to the World Tree a long time ago, the warriors on this continent called the green creatures on the World Tree monsters. For this reason, countless years of struggle were waged. This was also the origin of the name "Demon Slayer Continent".

He had the ability to be omniscient and omnipotent in this world, but he could not find any origin. In other words, the area covered by the golden light was out of his control.
Luo Zheng could not understand why he could not fully control the world he created …
"I'm not sure. It seems to have appeared not long after Lord condensed his Godhood. This golden light has a strong repulsive force. If you want to enter, you can only enter through the entrance of the gourd," the Evil Deities said and flew down, heading for the gourd-shaped entrance.
"A gourd-shaped area, and there's only one entrance …" Luo Zheng thought and then entered.

How could there be an existence in this world that the Evil Deities couldn't fight against? If there was, it could only be Luo Zheng himself.
"Take me there," Luo Zheng ordered.
The two of them, one in front and one behind, constantly shuttled through this area.
Just as the Evil Deities had said, in this special area shrouded in golden light, there were indeed all kinds of giants. These giants generally had multiple eyes, and some giants even had eyes all over their bodies. They looked very strange and terrifying, as if these creatures shouldn't exist at all …
The more eyes the giants had, the bigger they were, and the stronger they were.
At first, the Evil Deities led the way. The three-eyed giants, ten-eyed giants, and Evil Deities were easy to deal with, but the hundred-eyed giants that appeared later were more difficult to deal with. Although there weren't many of them, the Evil Deities often needed hundreds of rounds to kill them.
"Die!"
Luo Zheng stretched out his finger and pointed lightly at the three hundred-eyed giants beside the Evil Deities. The nine stars in the sky flashed slightly, and an invisible force descended from the nine stars, instantly tearing these hundred-eyed giants to pieces …
"Let's go," Luo Zheng said faintly. It was now his turn to lead the way.
The Evil Deities smiled slightly. The last time he entered this place, he also took a big risk. When he faced the Thousand-Eyed Giant alone, he almost didn't manage to escape.
Not long after, Luo Zheng finally found the Thousand-Eyed Giant that the Evil Deities had mentioned. This giant was nearly ten thousand feet tall, and the surrounding hills were just small slopes compared to it!
Just now, Luo Zheng didn't find such a huge monster outside the area covered by the golden light. This meant that the golden light had indeed blinded him. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng felt an indescribable strangeness in his heart.
"Ao!"
When the Thousand-Eyed Giant saw Luo Zheng and the Evil Deities, it didn't say anything and swung its mountain-like fist at them.
In the face of this sky-covering fist, Luo Zheng stretched out his finger and pointed lightly. The fist suddenly stopped in mid-air, unable to advance any further. Then, Luo Zheng flicked it lightly, and an even more fierce and domineering force directly overturned this huge monster.
"Rumble …"
When the Thousand-Eyed Giant fell to the ground, the entire area shook constantly, and the giants in the depths of the forest began to howl one after another.
The Thousand-Eyed Giant still wanted to get up, but Luo Zheng had already jumped up and stood on the mountain-like head of the Thousand-Eyed Giant. He gently stepped down, and the Thousand-Eyed Giant's head was stomped into the ground. For a while, the Thousand-Eyed Giant couldn't pull its head out, and could only wave its hands in vain. Although this Thousand-Eyed Giant had a thousand eyes, these eyes didn't seem to be able to see anything.
"This kind of creature shouldn't be born in my Inner World, and such a powerful creature can't be formed overnight," Luo Zheng said to the Evil Deities. The Human Race and other intelligent creatures in his Inner World had also experienced constant evolution over time before gradually reaching this point.
"I think so too." The Evil Deities went over the Thousand-Eyed Giant's body and searched for something in the depths of the forest. After a while, some black things appeared in his hands. The Evil Deities presented these things in front of Luo Zheng and said, "Lord, look, these are the things. These small black discs also seem to contain the power of faith …"
Those black things were thin black discs. Seeing these small discs, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly revealed an expression of disbelief. "These … are shenwu coins!"
This time, it was the Evil Deities' turn to be puzzled. "Shenwu coins? What are those? "

Chapter 1892
The scene before him solved many of Luo Zheng's doubts.
The Evil Old Man told Luo Zheng that the Shenwu coins were all found in the Forbidden Lands. However, there was a big question. How did the Shenwu coins come about?
Just like the Zhenyuan Stones in the universe, the Zhen Yuanyu relied on the condensation of Heaven and Earth's vitality. After countless years, they slowly sank and turned into mineral veins. Even if a mineral vein was dug up, a new one would be formed. Therefore, the Zhenyuan Stones in Zhen Yuanyu were almost inexhaustible.
The Realm of the Gods had existed for countless Divine Epochs.
In these countless Divine Epochs, countless True Gods had stepped into the Forbidden Lands.
There were Shenwu coins in all the Forbidden Lands big and small. No matter how many Shenwu coins there were, they would all be dug up one day. How could those who came after find them?
According to the Evil Old Man, every time a True God stepped into the Forbidden Lands, they would always find Shenwu coins. This meant that Shenwu coins would appear continuously …
Now, Luo Zheng finally understood. Shenwu coins were formed from the True Gods' power of faith!
"Where is this place?" Evil Deities asked curiously.
Luo Zheng stood in place and scanned his surroundings. Finally, his gaze fell on the Thousand-Eyed Giant again. He smiled and said, "This is the world of your imagination!"
"The world of our imagination?" Evil Deities looked surprised.
Luo Zheng nodded. With a thought, the history of the Demon Slaying Continent all these years appeared in his mind. With the help of his omniscient ability, he quickly found the clues he needed. He said calmly, "Countless years ago, the Demon Slaying Continent belonged to the Mighty Clan …"
"I've heard of the Mighty Clan. It was an ancient race." Evil Deities nodded.
The conflict between the Mighty Clan and the Teng Clan was quite far away from the birth of Evil Deities. However, the history of these two ancient races was still passed down.
"According to historical records, the Mighty Clan's Queen had unparalleled beauty, so she attracted the help of the Gods. Is the God the Lord?" Evil Deities suddenly thought of this and asked with a smile, "Is the Queen of the Aristocrats really as beautiful as the legends say?"
According to historical records, the Queen of the Noble Family was known as the number one beauty since ancient times. She was a woman who could move the heart of a "god". As a man, he was naturally interested in Evil Deities.
Luo Zheng nodded. "That's me. She is indeed a perfect woman."
At that time, Luo Zheng organized the war between the Hao Clan and the Teng Clan. At the same time, he supported the queen of the Hao Clan, but he didn't give her too much power.
As the creator of this world, Luo Zheng would not interfere too much. In fact, even now, he could mold the Queen out of mud with just a thought, but it would be meaningless …
"At that time, the Tyrant Clan and the Teng Clan were very afraid of the Heavenly Spirit Clan in the World Tree, so the local mortals hoped that there would be a kind of living being that could protect them!" Luo Zheng continued. "That kind of living being must be strong and huge. It must also have a terrifying appearance, enough to scare off the Heavenly Spirit Clan!"
Evil Deities quickly guessed. "The living being in their imagination must be these monsters with eyes all over their bodies?"
"Smart." Luo Zheng nodded with a smile.
In the beginning, the Tyrant Clan could only imagine the Three-Eyed Giant, but once their imagination was opened, they would continue down this path. More and more Tyrant Clan members began to fabricate things that didn't exist. Thus, the Ten-Eyed Giant, the Hundred-Eyed Giant, and even the Thousand-Eyed Giant appeared …
This was the most primitive belief.
If the Tyrant Clan had imagined a dragon at the beginning, then what Luo Zheng and Evil Deities encountered here would not be these giants, but a huge dragon with a strange shape.
For countless years, the legend of the Thousand-Eyed Giant did not disappear. Instead, it became a part of the culture of the Human Race and was passed down from generation to generation. Their imagination also turned into the unconscious power of faith and gathered together. When Luo Zheng's divinity was formed, it materialized into this gourd-shaped forbidden land.
Why was it in the shape of a gourd … Because in the legends, these giants were born from gourds!
Similarly, Buzhou Spirit Mountain, Soul Wilderness, these large and small forbidden lands were also formed from the thoughts of the Tyrant Clan members.
As the saying goes, "I think, therefore I am." These thoughts were actually the power of faith. Countless legends passed down through the ages of the Gods formed one forbidden land after another. When a forbidden land appeared, the later generations would be more convinced of the existence of the forbidden land. This would form a cycle, causing the forbidden land to absorb more and more power of faith …
According to Luo Zheng's speculation, it was not a coincidence that the four great forbidden lands, Buzhou Spirit Mountain, Soul Wilderness, Takeshi Xuan Forest, and the Abyss Magical Realm, became the most powerful forbidden lands in the Divine Domain. Instead, the legends of these forbidden lands were the earliest to spread in the Divine Domain and had the advantage of time.
For example, in the legends of the Tyrant Clan, the Abyss Magical Realm should be an existence equivalent to hell. It was a place where the Tyrant Clan members would go after their deaths. The number of Tyrant Clan members who held such thoughts was the highest, and the power of faith absorbed into the Abyss Magical Realm was the most powerful forbidden land. Therefore, the Abyss Magical Realm was also the most difficult forbidden land to enter. Even a Saint would find it difficult to protect himself in the depths of the Abyss Magical Realm!
"It's incredible," Evil Deities exclaimed as he looked at the Thousand-Eyed Giant. He did not expect this world to be so wonderful.
