Luo Zheng suddenly reached out and aimed at the Thousand-Eyed Giant that was still struggling. Then, he gently flipped his palm and pressed it down. An invisible force instantly crushed the huge Thousand-Eyed Giant into powder!
"Logically speaking, even if I kill all the giants here, these giants will reappear after a certain period of time, including the Shenwu coins in your hands. You can come in and verify it next time," Luo Zheng said faintly. "Let's go to the next place and have a look!"
Evil Deities nodded and then asked, "Evil Deities still has one question that I don't understand. The Lord said that everything in this place is imagination. Then what about these 'Shenwu coins'? Did these black discs also appear in the legends of the Demon Slaying Continent? "
Luo Zheng stared at the Shenwu coins and shook his head. "These Shenwu coins are the crystallization of the power of faith itself. This forbidden land needs an endless supply of power of faith. When this power of faith is absorbed into this space and materializes enough giants, the power of faith will reach saturation. After saturation, the power of faith will continue to condense and form Shenwu coins …"
This was also Luo Zheng's own deduction. According to the clues he now understood, it should not be wrong. As for why these crystallized power of faith were black, he could not answer now …
After collecting all the Shenwu coins in the forbidden land, Luo Zheng and Evil Deities left the forbidden land.
Afterward, Luo Zheng and Evil Deities found another forbidden land on the Ice Plains Continent. This forbidden land was covered by a light purple light. This light purple light was triangular. Just like the situation on the Demon Slaying Continent, Luo Zheng could not investigate any information inside this forbidden land.
After entering this forbidden land, the two encountered a kind of humanoid snow monster covered in long hair. These snow monsters were very powerful. Of course, that was compared to Evil Deities. No matter how powerful the monsters in this world were, they could not cause any harm to Luo Zheng.
In this forbidden land, Luo Zheng also found hundreds of Shenwu coins. At the same time, Luo Zheng also found the legend of the 'polar snowman', which confirmed his guess once again.
Right now, the forbidden land in Luo Zheng's inner world was only in its initial stages. The amount of Shenwu coins it had condensed was not much.
However, the time in his inner world was much faster than in the Divine Domain. In the future, as the forbidden land grew larger, the amount of Shenwu coins would also increase …
Shenwu coins could be used to change the rules of the Governing Slate, but it was not something he could consider now. How was he going to use the power of faith within the Shenwu coins?

Chapter 1893
The divine domain had existed for so many years. The geniuses of the divine domain must have thought of all sorts of ways to study the use of Shenwu Coins.
But in the end, they could only change the rules of the Governing Slate. This meant that the true gods were unable to use the power of Shenwu Coins …
Luo Zheng used a powerful force to crush the Shenwu Coins into powder, but all he got was a handful of black powder. He was unable to squeeze out the power of faith from the Shenwu Coins.
He even sprinkled the powder on the Godhead or into the Sea of Chaos. No matter what method he used, his Godhead did not react at all.
After some research in his internal world, he used all sorts of bizarre methods. The Evil Deities also gave him suggestions, but in the end, he could only choose to give up for now.
He might have overlooked some things. Or could it be that the power of faith in the 'divine domain' built from chaos itself could not be used directly? These were all possibilities …
But Luo Zheng was not dispirited. After all, with the formation of the Godhead, he had also stepped into the true god realm. The increase in his cultivation would also increase his strength by quite a bit!
After dispersing his internal incarnation, Luo Zheng instructed the Evil Deities to temporarily stop the secret teachings.
Currently, he was unable to use the power of faith. It was useless to collect the power of faith in the idol. Besides, the extra power of faith would automatically become Shenwu Coins.
After that, his consciousness returned to the secret cultivation room.
Although he had spent quite a bit of time in his internal world, he had only spent a few hours in the divine domain.
When he opened the door to the secret cultivation room, the sky was already bright …
Luo Zheng did not see Aunt Hanjiu for the past few days.
After all, the Dongfang family had already spoken. They were probably looking for an anonymous person like him. Furthermore, his performance in the Arena of Gods made them almost certain that he was in the Purple Soul Palace. Aunt Han Jiu also had to avoid arousing suspicion, otherwise it would easily arouse suspicion.
"It's time to leave the floating island …" Luo Zheng gazed at the churning clouds in the sky and said.
The rankings of the floating islands had been rearranged yesterday. The passageways to the forbidden lands should be opened today.
After that, Luo Zheng left the Purple Soul Palace and once again stepped into Saint Emperor City.
The entire Sea of Time forbade flying and spatial transference. These were the rules of the Governing Slate.
However, in order to monopolize the various forbidden grounds, the Saint Hall had once again revised this rule. Teleportation channels could be constructed on the floating islands, and the teleportation channels could be used to perform spatial transference. The various teleportation channels were directly connected to the forbidden grounds.
The teleportation channel on the floating island became the only entrance to the forbidden area.
It was precisely for this reason that the countless grassroots True Gods of the Divine Realm gnashed their teeth in hatred!
A True God like the Extreme Evil Old Man lost the chance to enter the forbidden grounds. Training in the forbidden grounds, mining Shenwu Coins, and searching for opportunities had become a privilege for the descendants of the wealthy families.
It was also because of this that the opportunity for countless gods to move upwards was blocked.
In the past, some grassroots True Gods still had a chance to become a Paragon or even a Saint. But now, this chance had basically disappeared. One of the reasons was the monopolization of forbidden lands.
When they reached Saint Royal City, they walked north for a while. In front of a wide plaza stood a row of Han Jia's guards. These guards were blocking the path of a large group of true gods.
"Didn't the rankings of the floating islands already come out?"
"Why are you not allowed to enter the forbidden area today? It wasn't easy for me to accumulate enough points! I want to enter Buzhou Spiritual Mountain! "
"What the hell is going on? This is so strange! "
These True Gods were complaining. Regardless of whether they were lower True Gods or upper True Gods, they were all blocked outside.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly.
His gaze passed behind those guards and could see that the plaza was densely packed with teleportation channels. Han Jia's teleportation channels seemed to be stone walls. He wondered if the other wealthy families' teleportation channels were the same.
"Friend, can't we use the teleportation channel to enter the forbidden grounds today?" Luo Zheng asked one of the True Gods.
That True God glanced at Luo Zheng and said gloomily, "I was thinking that since no one has entered the forbidden grounds these few days, there should be more Shenwu Coins inside. But they just can't let us in. God knows why!"
Shenwu Coins were the main goal of these True Gods. However, there had been people exploring the forbidden grounds for many years. The moment Shenwu Coins appeared, they would be immediately picked up. Since no one could enter the forbidden grounds these few days, it was natural that there would be more Shenwu Coins inside.
Luo Zheng nodded. The power of faith in divine territory was not unlimited. Shenwu Coins would only appear when the power of faith was saturated.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had a bad feeling. Han Jia's ban on entering the forbidden grounds couldn't be directed at him, could it?
That was indeed a possibility …
If the Mu family guessed something, and the Dongfang family was still alive, they would probably pressure Han Jia to temporarily close the tunnel until the anonymous person was found.
Just as he was worrying, a melodious bell rang in his ears.
Clang!
That bell didn't come from the floating islands. Instead, it came from the depths of the clouds.
When everyone heard the sound of the bell, they immediately raised their heads. Then, a golden light directly penetrated the clouds, swept past the floating islands, and shone on the surface of the Sea of Time. This golden light hit the surface of the sea and bounced back, refracting an extremely magnificent colorful light.
When they saw this golden light, many True Gods revealed expressions of realization, including the True God beside Luo Zheng.
"Oh! I understand now. No wonder we can't enter the forbidden grounds today. Today is the transit of the sun! "said the True God.
"Today is the transit of the sun?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
He didn't expect that after the floating islands were ranked, they would encounter the transit of the Blind Tribe. During the transit of the sun, the teleportation channel couldn't be used.
The transit of the sun was the Blind Tribe's biggest festival …
On this day, even the ferries on the Sea of Time would stop.
The mysterious tribe in the Sea of Time would also invite some important figures from the wealthy families to participate. Luo Zheng was among the invited guests.
Luo Zheng recalled the words of the old man from the Blind Tribe. He seemed to have an aura that terrified the Blind Tribe. It was very similar to the sea monster "Emperor's Hatred" in the Sea of Time.
"Sigh, it's the transit of the sun again. Those who are qualified to participate are almost all the core members of the wealthy families. Of course, we can't count on it!"
"Let's disperse. We'll come back another day!"
"It's said that participating in the transit of the sun will bring unimaginable benefits. You can even explore the secrets of time …"
The Blind Tribe had always been shrouded in a mysterious veil in the eyes of the world. These True Gods would never be able to participate in the transit of the sun in their lifetime. Naturally, there were all kinds of speculations, but they were just speculations.
They didn't know what the transit of the sun was exactly.
These True Gods sighed for a while before slowly dispersing.
"Should I participate in the transit of the sun?" Luo Zheng thought to himself. His face revealed a hesitant expression.
The biggest problem now wasn't the Mu Family. The Mu Family might have noticed the "Anonymous Person," but they might not be able to link him to the Anonymous Person. Moreover, Mu Haiji, who had seen him, was still being punished by the Governing Slate.
As for the Dongfang Family wanting to poach him, it wasn't out of malice. They just wanted to recruit him and let him join their floating island.
The worst-case scenario was this …
At worst, he would activate the "Gen" token and escape back to the immortal mansion in the Takanxuan Forest.

Chapter 1894
Thousands of transparent discs slowly rose from the bottom of the floating island and stopped at the entrance of the floating island.
There were True Gods from each of the floating islands who stepped on the discs and slowly descended to the lowest level of the floating island. These True Gods had different cultivation levels. Some were lower True Gods, while others were peak True Gods. No matter what cultivation level they were at, they were all elites of large families.
Dozens of transparent discs also descended from Han Jia's floating island.
Han Liusu was expressionless as she stood alone on one of the discs.
On the other disc, Han Chuyue and Han Biluo stood together.
Han Chuyue and Han Biluo were whispering to each other. From time to time, they would steal glances at Han Liusu. Han Chuyue's cute little face was filled with worry. During this period of time, Han Liusu had been locked up in her room, diligently cultivating. It was as if she had suddenly become a different person.
In fact, the intelligent Han Chuyue could easily guess the crux of the problem. Han Liusu's change was 100% due to Luo Zheng, but she could not guess what had happened between her sister and Luo Zheng.
"I think sister Liusu is in a bad mood because of that Luo Tianxing," Han Biluo whispered.
"I think so too …" Han Biluo did not know Luo Tianxing's true identity, but Han Chuyue did. She had a bad premonition that if her sister continued to be involved with Luo Zheng, they would meet with even greater trouble.
Many younger sisters had an innate admiration for their older sisters, and Han Chuyue was no exception.
In fact, most of the good feelings she had for Luo Zheng were also because of Han Liusu. It seemed that whatever her sister liked was the best in the world, and she even faintly felt jealous.
But if Luo Zheng harmed her sister, this was not the result she wanted to see.
"After the transit day, I'll look for Luo Tianxing again," Han Chuyue whispered.
At the same time, Han Cangyan also descended from the other side. Han Cangyan had always been busy, so he rarely paid attention to Han Liusu. Now that he saw Han Liusu alone, his expression was very ugly. He also asked, "Liusu, why are you unhappy?"
Hearing this voice, Han Liusu suddenly raised her head and forced out a smile, "Brother, Liusu is not unhappy."
"That's good!" Han Cangyan smiled, "Today is the transit day, perform well!"
"Liusu knows," Han Liusu nodded in response.
Not long after, this group of people had already descended to the bottom of the giant disc, and beneath their feet was the turbulent Sea of Time.
The round plate was already filled with the elites of the wealthy families. They were all waiting for the Blind Tribe's invitation.
Other than saints and quasi-saints, the Blind Tribe wouldn't specially invite anyone else. The true gods waiting here all had to weigh their own worth and see if they were qualified.
The Blind Tribe originally had a special method. They could sense every movement in the Sea of Time. If it wasn't a transit, as long as you stood in the Sea of Time, they would appear and ferry you to your destination.
It was the same for the Transit of the Sun. If they felt that you were qualified to participate in the Transit of the Sun Festival, they would naturally wait for a boat that belonged to them.
One after another, small boats came from the boundless Sea of Time. Those blind people were whistling and inviting the true gods to their boats. Then, they left at an unbelievable speed.
Not long after, Han Liusu, Han Cangyan, Han Chuyue, and Han Biluo were also taken away by different Blind Tribesmen.
The remaining people craned their necks and looked at each other, continuing to wait.
Seeing that it was about time, Luo Zheng also left Saint Emperor City. He came to the edge of the floating island and stepped onto a transparent round plate.
Other than Luo Zheng, there were also some Han Family disciples and even some outsiders.
Every time the Transit of the Sun Festival was held, some people would choose to try their luck. Although they weren't disciples of the main family, there would always be a chance to try. What if the Blind Tribe really invited them? Although the probability of this happening wasn't high, there would always be people who succeeded.
The true gods who descended with Luo Zheng had such thoughts.
Not every elite of the floating island would be invited. No one knew what the criteria and basis of the Blind Tribe's judgment were. Therefore, there would always be a group of people whose hopes were dashed and who were even furious.
"These Blind Tribe members are all blind! I, Tang Xiuyuan, am ranked 320th among the intermediate true gods. Why aren't they inviting me? "The intermediate true god of the Tang Family cursed as he looked at the boats that were constantly disappearing into the distance.
Seeing that there were no more boats coming, the expressions of these elite disciples were rather ugly. They were rather angry.
At this moment, Luo Zheng and the others stepped onto the transparent round plate and descended. This group of people naturally wanted to "try their luck."
Tang Xiuyuan was already very disappointed. When he saw this group of people, he became even angrier. Although these fellows who wanted to try their luck rarely had the qualifications to be invited, every year, there would always be a few fellows who were lucky enough to be invited by the Blind Tribe.
"You bunch of trash still want to participate in the Transit of the Sun Festival? Haha, what a joke. Why don't you take a look at your own weight? "Tang Xiuyuan mocked loudly.
"There are so many people in this world who don't know their own weight. Why bother arguing with them?" another elite disciple echoed.
Hearing these words, the true gods who wanted to try their luck naturally felt uncomfortable. However, they couldn't afford to offend these elites from rich families. They could only swallow their anger and silently stand to the side, hoping that a boat would come and take them away.
Luo Zheng stood to the side with a stupefied expression and didn't say a word.
Tang Xiuyuan complained for a while, but also secretly wanted to provoke them. If any of these fellows dared to act rashly, he would naturally take action and teach them a lesson. However, this group of people all kept their mouths shut like idiots.
Therefore, after looking around, his gaze finally fell on Luo Zheng. "Eh? There's even a lower true god here to join in the fun. There are more and more weirdos now. It would be fun if a large group of Proving Gods were to join in the fun one day! "
Most of the true gods who were willing to try their luck had something to rely on. They either felt that their talents were not bad or that their cultivation was not bad. Almost all of them were middle true gods or upper true gods. Only Luo Zheng was a lower true god.
Luo Zheng was originally expressionlessly looking at the distant Time Sea. When he saw Tang Xiuyuan staring at him, he simply closed his eyes and rested.
Unexpectedly, Tang Xiuyuan was even more persistent. He walked up to Luo Zheng and said, "Tell me, which floating island did you come from?"
Luo Zheng was motionless like a statue.
"I'm talking to you. Didn't you hear me?" Tang Xiuyuan saw that Luo Zheng wasn't responding to him at all and felt even more sullen.
The other true gods noticed the commotion over here and revealed expressions of pity. They probably felt that Luo Zheng was going to be unlucky. They felt that Luo Zheng should speak up and admit defeat. At the very least, Tang Xiuyuan would let him off.
Seeing that Luo Zheng still had his eyes closed, Tang Xiuyuan's expression turned cold. He reached out his hand and slapped Luo Zheng's face with lightning speed. At the same time, he scolded, "I'm asking you a question …"
The moment Tang Xiuyuan made his move, Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly opened and his head tilted back slightly.
Tang Xiuyuan's fingertip brushed past the tip of his nose by an inch. His slap missed.
When he saw that Luo Zheng had dodged his slap so easily, he was slightly stunned.
Then, he saw Luo Zheng's figure flash and his lapel was grabbed by the other party. An irresistible force came, and his whole body fell back uncontrollably!
He was originally standing at the edge of this huge circular plate, and behind him was the seething Sea of Time. If he were to fall into the Sea of Time, let alone a mere middle-level true god like him, even a great perfection or secondary saint would be torn into pieces by the seawater.
Just as his face was filled with horror, his body suddenly stopped in mid-air. At the same time, a faint voice sounded. "A dignified intermediate true god, but your temperament is like a lowly mortal gangster. Which rich and powerful family has a scum like you? How unfortunate!"
"You, you, I, ah …"
This giant disk floated on the Sea of Time and was very close to it. As the colorful Sea of Time rolled, waves would spread toward Tang Xiuyuan's head from time to time. They could swallow him up at any time. How could he still dare to refute Luo Zheng's words? She just kept screaming, and her previous overbearing attitude had completely disappeared!

Chapter 1895
Tang Xiuyuan's face was deathly pale, and he felt incomparably sullen.
As a descendant of the Tang Family, even an ordinary middle true god wouldn't dare speak carelessly in front of him, let alone a lower true god.
A lower true god like Luo Zheng definitely wasn't an elite of a rich family. He didn't expect that this guy would dare to fight back, and even try to kill him!
But now that his life was hanging by a thread, how could he dare say anything? He could only desperately beg for mercy.
Luo Zheng really didn't want to provoke these rich and powerful descendants.
There were many outstanding people on these floating islands, and their innate talents were also ridiculously strong, but in the end, some scum would emerge.
He would try his best to ignore this kind of person, but this Tang Xiuyuan was too much for not letting him off.
But he still had some misgivings, and didn't plan to kill Tang Xiuyuan. After all, his identity was sensitive, and this place wasn't safe for Luo Zheng!
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng suddenly exerted force, directly swinging Tang Xiuyuan up and smashing him heavily on the round plate.
He didn't know what this round plate was made of, but its surface was abnormally hard. Even though Tang Xiuyuan was a middle true god, being smashed on it was quite uncomfortable.
Just a moment ago, Tang Xiuyuan had been constantly begging for mercy in order to survive, but as soon as he landed, his entire aura changed. He believed that Luo Zheng had attacked him only because he had been ambushed. If not for that, that kid definitely wouldn't have been able to resist him!
He flipped over and stood up, releasing the power in his body. But before he could steady himself, he saw a figure flash in front of him. He saw Luo Zheng raise his leg and kick him in the face.
"Boom!"
This enormous power struck his head from top to bottom. His entire body couldn't withstand this enormous pressure, and his legs went soft, causing him to kneel in front of Luo Zheng.
"If you attack again, I'll kill you directly. Don't think I don't dare," Luo Zheng said coldly, looking down at Tang Xiuyuan.
Although Luo Zheng couldn't use the "power of faith" in his inner world, nor could he emit "potential", after condensing the Godhead, his strength had increased significantly. His innate cultivation also made him look completely different, whether it was the body or the soul.
Tang Xiuyuan felt that the cold aura was like an invisible guillotine that was placed on his neck. It seemed that if he moved, the guillotine would fall without hesitation. He really knelt in the same place, motionless, like an obedient rabbit.
Everyone on this huge round plate was silent. They were secretly sizing up Luo Zheng and guessing Luo Zheng's origin.
However, among the thirty-six clans of the floating island, the number of True Gods was in the millions. The only time Luo Zheng fought was in the Han Clan's Saint Emperor City. Naturally, no one here could recognize him.
Then Luo Zheng ignored Tang Xiuyuan and stood aside. His eyes fell on the Time Sea again.
Seeing that Luo Zheng had put some distance between them, Tang Xiuyuan slowly got up from the ground. Although he had been humiliated, he still didn't dare to fight back.
He couldn't see through Luo Zheng's strength at all. Lower True Gods with such strength were usually descendants of the Saint Emperor. But this guy in front of him … Which clan did he belong to? Why hadn't he seen him before?
There were also Han Clan members present. These Han Clan members had left the floating island with Luo Zheng. Naturally, they knew that Luo Zheng was a member of the Han Clan. However, they naturally wouldn't say Luo Zheng's origin. After all, they belonged to the same floating island, a clan.
At that moment, a small black dot appeared on the Time Sea in the distance. All the True Gods on the round platform immediately became nervous.
Their only purpose here was to wait for the Blind Tribe's invitation. Now, a small boat had appeared. They didn't know who it was …
The small black dot approached quickly. It was a young man from the Blind Tribe. From the time the boat appeared in the distance to the round plate, it only took him about ten breaths. When the boat got close, everyone held their breaths. Their faces were filled with hope.
"Whoosh!"
The young Blind Tribe member whistled and looked at Tang Xiuyuan.
"Me?" Tang Xiuyuan pointed at himself. He was pleasantly surprised. At that moment, he even forgot about the humiliation he had just suffered.
Then, he jumped up and onto the boat. Suddenly, he remembered something. He turned to Luo Zheng and laughed proudly. "Hahaha! A guy like you still hopes to be invited by the Blind Tribe? You can wait here for 10,000 years! "
Tang Xiuyuan was indeed very happy.
Luo Zheng just crossed his arms and looked at him indifferently. His expression didn't change at all.
As for the others, they looked envious. No matter what, Tang Xiuyuan had still been invited by the Blind Tribe …
Just as the boat left, someone pointed into the distance and said, "Another boat is coming!"
Everyone looked in the direction he was pointing and saw a large black dot on the surface of the sea. As the black dot continued to enlarge, it revealed a large ship!
The Blind Tribe usually had two types of boats. One was a small boat that could only seat one person. Usually, it was driven by a Blind Tribe member. The other type was a large boat that was dozens of feet long. When the Zhou family's floating island fell into the sea surface, they would use this large boat to send many of the Zhou family's disciples out.
"Why did they send a dragon bone boat here?"
"Don't tell me they want to take all of us!"
"You must be dreaming …"
These True Gods didn't think that the dragon bone boat was here to take people away. Given the Blind Tribe's personality, even Perfected True Gods didn't receive such treatment. The first batch of people to be taken away were Perfected True Gods from various big families. They also left on small boats.
But under everyone's watchful eyes, the dragonbone ship slowly approached, and on the deck of the ship stood an old Blind Tribesman.
"It's him." Luo Zheng recognized that this old Blind Tribe member was the ferryman when he entered the Sea of Time.
"Honorable guest, welcome to our Blind Tribe's Transit of the Sun Festival," the old Blind Tribe member said. At the same time, he made an inviting gesture to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and took two steps forward. The tip of his foot tapped lightly on the edge of the round plate, and his entire body flew up. He landed on the deck like a light bird.
The True Gods on the round plate were all in disbelief. They stared blankly at this scene, and their minds were in a mess.
"Who, who exactly is he!"
"How can he receive such treatment …"
"Only Saint Emperors are qualified to be welcomed by the dragon bone boat, right?"
Saint Emperors might be able to enjoy such treatment, but they were different from Perfected True Gods. Perfected True Gods weren't allowed to use space transmission on the Sea of Time, but Saint Emperors and quasi-sages were unimpeded. Therefore, they didn't need the Blind Tribe to personally welcome them.
Therefore, Luo Zheng was probably the first person to use a dragon bone boat to welcome people in the Transit of the Sun Festival.
"Return to the island!"
The old Blind Tribe member ordered loudly.
This kind of large boat required at least ten Blind Tribe members to steer. Naturally, its speed was much faster than ordinary small boats.
The small boat that Tang Xiuyuan was on had just left. In a short while, the two boats, one big and one small, were moving side by side. Luo Zheng stood in front of the big boat and looked down at Tang Xiuyuan, who was on the small boat.
Tang Xiuyuan sat in the middle of the boat and looked up at Luo Zheng. His mouth twitched unconsciously. His expression was similar to that of the True Gods on the round plate. He stared blankly at Luo Zheng. At this moment, he suddenly felt that he was incomparably small.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
The big boat moved away at an extremely fast speed, creating waves that pushed the small boat to the side.
Tang Xiuyuan couldn't help but ask the young Blind Tribe member who was rowing the boat, "Why can that guy ride the big boat? What are you Blind Tribe members thinking? Who exactly is he! "
The young Blind Tribe member who was rowing the boat shook his head and shrugged his shoulders.

Chapter 1896
Luo Zheng had never thought that he would be treated like this by the Blind Tribe.
The dragon bone ship was moving faster and faster. The huge floating islands on the Sea of Time gradually turned into small black dots and finally disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
The Blind Tribe old man did not say anything. He returned to the cabin and took out an exquisite tobacco pipe. Then, he poured some powder into it from a small pocket on his waist. The powder was made of an unknown material. It looked colorful, similar to the color of the Sea of Time.
After the Blind Tribe old man lit it, he took a deep breath. The smoke he exhaled was also colorful.
Luo Zheng noticed the smoke. His eyes flashed with a hint of surprise. The smoke actually emitted a faint time law.
"This is tobacco made from the Hallucination Grass in the Sea of Time. Take a puff and it will produce hallucination smoke. It will let you see an illusion of the past. Do you want a puff?" the Blind Tribe old man said as he handed the tobacco pipe to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not have the habit of smoking. However, he was very interested in the time law in this tobacco pipe.
Even after entering the floating island, Luo Zheng had almost never come into contact with a true god who cultivated the time laws. However, this old man from the Blind Tribe could emit the time laws with just a puff of smoke. This naturally made Luo Zheng very curious.
He took the tobacco pipe from the Blind Tribe old man and fiddled with it. Then, he took a light puff.
The moment he inhaled the hallucination smoke, Luo Zheng felt a tearing sensation in his throat. After the small time law dissipated, it caused small wounds in his body.
At the same time, the smoke flowed into these wounds and was absorbed by him.
Luo Zheng felt a wave of dizziness hit his head. The colorful smoke he exhaled rolled in circles in front of his eyes. A faint image appeared in the smoke.
Through the layer of smoke, Luo Zheng could vaguely see a huge sea turtle floating on the surface of the Sea of Time. This sea turtle was millions of miles long, and on its back was not a city but a continent!
Luo Zheng tried his best to see it clearly, but the smoke quickly drifted away. The illusion also quickly disappeared from Luo Zheng's eyes.
The Blind Tribe old man smiled faintly. "What did you see?"
"A sea turtle," Luo Zheng replied.
"You saw a cliff spirit on your first puff. Hehe, good luck." The Blind Tribe old man laughed.
"Cliff spirit?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"One of the largest true spirits in ancient legends. It's the mount of a Weapon Grandmaster," the Blind Tribe old man explained.
"Weapon Chi You?" Luo Zheng asked..
The Blind Tribe old man nodded.
At the mention of Chiyou, Luo Zheng's memories immediately floated to Hua Tianming. Hua Tianming's strength had improved greatly after obtaining Chiyou's treasure.
He had stepped into the God Domain before her, but she didn't know where he was now.
...........................................
"What happened to the turtle?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I don't know where it went. Maybe it was killed, or maybe it left the Realm of the Gods …" the Blind Tribe old man said.
Ever since Luo Zheng entered the Realm of the Gods, he had been trying his best to understand the history of the Realm of the Gods. In particular, he had read many ancient books in the Purple Soul Palace. He quickly discovered that some of the ancient history of the Realm of the Gods was incomplete.
Luo Zheng could not understand this.
The True Gods of the Realm of the Gods had unlimited lifespans. They existed for eternity. From the Ancient Era, the Middle Era, to the present, there were always a few True Gods who had survived. Under such circumstances, it was impossible for there to be a gap in history. However, when Luo Zheng tried to understand the history of the Realm of the Gods, he discovered that many things were incomplete. He felt like someone had deliberately erased them …
Afterward, Luo Zheng tried to smoke a few more times. The illusion he saw was different every time. Sometimes, it was an incomparably huge red bird. Sometimes, it was an incomplete image on a blank screen. Sometimes, it was an indistinguishable figure of a man or a woman.
According to the Blind Tribe old man, these images had all existed in the past. However, most of them were meaningless. Luo Zheng could not understand them, so he stopped trying.
What Luo Zheng did not know was that the colorful smoke he exhaled did not disappear after floating out of the keelboat. Instead, it kept circling the Sea of Time.
Not long after, a Blind Tribe member slowly approached with a small boat. This Blind Tribe member was holding a strangely shaped smoking pot. He made a strange gesture and gently opened the smoking pot, aiming it at the constantly circling illusory smoke. The smoke Luo Zheng exhaled transformed into a long snake and was sucked into the smoking pot. The Blind Tribe member glanced in the direction of the keelboat and quietly said a few words. Then, he quietly rowed the boat away …
Two hours later …
The keelboat finally arrived at its destination with Luo Zheng on board.
The Blind Tribe members lived on a group of islands. The black islands were densely packed, towering in the Sea of Time.
Some of the islands were only two or three feet wide, and there were small attics built on them. Under the constant beating of the Sea of Time, the attics could be swallowed up at any time.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng was amazed. The Blind Tribe members did not have strong cultivations. At least, the Blind Tribe members he saw were just ordinary Gods. However, the True Gods regarded the Sea of Time as the Sea of Death. In this environment, the Blind Tribe members lived with ease …
The keelboat passed by those small islands and finally sailed into a main island.
A wide port was built at the edge of the main island, and the True Gods on the floating islands were still gathered there.
"Why is there a keelboat here?" a True God asked.
"Maybe it's an important guest invited by the Blind Tribe?" another True God said.
The other True Gods also looked curious. Today was the Ling Sun Festival. The important guests invited by the Blind Tribe were the elites of the wealthy families on the thirty-six floating islands. No matter if it was a Great Perfection or a lower True God, they were all treated equally.
"Important guests? Can they be more important than us? "Mu Ning said proudly.
Mu Xuerong, who was standing behind Mu Ning, remained silent. It should be known that the people present included the Perfect True Gods of various large and powerful families. For example, Mu Xuerong, Tang Wan, and the others. There were also the clan leaders of various large floating islands, as well as Han Tianxiao and the others.
It could be said that, other than the Sages and Quasi-Sages, the strongest and most talented people of each wealthy family were gathered here.
This group of people could be said to represent the future of the Divine Realm. Who in the Divine Realm was more important than them?
However, when the keelboat slowly approached, many True Gods widened their eyes.
They saw a lower True God standing on the deck of the keelboat …
"Who is this guy?"
"Why is a lower True God being treated so well by the Blind Tribe members?"
"…"
The True Gods who were waiting here suddenly became noisy. Many of them lowered their heads and whispered to each other.
The Great Perfection True Gods quietly sized up Luo Zheng, seemingly thinking about something.
"It's this kid?" Han Tianxiao, who was standing at the back, had a strange look in his eyes. This was the disciple that Aunt Han Jiu had accepted into the Purple Soul Palace. Why would he be invited by the Blind Tribe members to participate in the Ling Sun Festival? More importantly, why would he be treated like this by the Blind Tribe?
"Ah! It's Luo Tianxing! "Han Biluo suddenly jumped up and laughed when she saw Luo Zheng.
"Biluo!" Han Tianxiao frowned and stopped her.
"Sister, it's really him," Han Chuyue said softly.
From a distance, Han Liusu's haggard eyes fell on Luo Zheng. She had a complicated expression. She thought that Luo Zheng might be invited by the Blind Tribe members, but she didn't expect them to value him so much …
Because of Bi Luo's reaction, the True Gods present immediately understood.

Chapter 1897
The dragon bone ship slowly approached the shore under everyone's gaze.
Luo Zheng jumped down from the ship, his gaze landing on Han Jia's group.
Han Chuyue gave him a playful smile, and Luo Zheng nodded. Finally, his gaze landed on Han Liusu.
Han Liusu's eyes met Luo Zheng's, and her eyebrows twitched, revealing a trace of annoyance. She then turned her head to look elsewhere …
It seems she's still angry. Luo Zheng smiled bitterly and found a place to stand.
Noticing her sister's expression, Han Chuyue fell into deep thought. Her guess was correct, but she didn't know what had happened between Luo Zheng and her sister.
Most of the True Gods' gazes were still focused on Luo Zheng, quietly guessing his origins.
"This kid doesn't seem to be from Han Jia's main family?"
"From the looks of it, he doesn't seem to be …"
After all, Luo Zheng didn't act like he was familiar with Han Tianxiao, Han Cangyan, and the others.
However, there were still people present who recognized Luo Zheng.
"It's this kid again. In such a short time, he's already become a lower True God," said a gloomy voice. It was Leng Linyue from the Leng family.
"Leng Linyue, you've seen this kid before?" Mu Ning asked doubtfully.
"Hmph, he's the kid who killed Xie Jiao. His name is Luo Tianxing. The injury on my forehead was caused by him! I didn't expect him to break through and condense a godhead so soon after entering the floating island! "Leng Linyue said coldly, a trace of killing intent in his eyes.
Even thinking back, Leng Linyue still had lingering fear. If he hadn't reacted quickly that day, he might have died at this kid's hands.
If it weren't for the Blind Tribe's Ling Ri, he might have already attacked.
"A prodigy can injure you? You're too careless," Mu Ning said indifferently.
Hearing Mu Ning's words, Leng Linyue wasn't convinced. His cultivation was a level higher than Mu Ning's, so how could he tolerate her judging him like this? He said, "You might not be his match. Xie Jiao's strength isn't much weaker than yours, but he still killed him!"
Mu Xuerong, who had been silent all this time, heard the conversation between her sister and Leng Linyue. A thought suddenly jumped in her mind.
Surnamed Luo? Han Jia's new genius?
With a thought, she glanced at Luo Zheng in the distance and asked, "This kid is just a Divine Warrior? He killed Xie Jiao. Didn't the Xie family find trouble with Han Jia? "
This matter with the Xie Family was not a small matter, but Mu Xuerong had always been in a hurry, and she had also been preparing for her battle with Yu Taibai, so there were many things that she did not have time to pay attention to. But now that she heard about it, she had a faint guess, and she naturally wanted to verify it.
"I did, but that brat seems to belong to the Purple Soul Palace. Aunt Han Jiu protected him." Leng Lin Yue indifferently said. When this matter was brought up, Leng Lin Yue had a belly full of anger.
"Purple Soul Palace? Did he just join the Leng Clan? "Mu Xuerong asked again.
Mu Xuerong had always been a woman of few words. As a Perfection Stage cultivator of the Mu Clan, she rarely paid attention to juniors like Leng Linyue. Leng Linyue found this strange and continued to answer, "I've investigated his background. He has indeed just joined the Purple Soul Palace …
He was able to kill Xie Jiao as a Proving Divinity Martial Artist and had the protection of Aunt Jiu …
Thinking of this, Leng Linyue's eyes suddenly lit up, and he immediately reevaluated Luo Zheng.
"Sister Xuerong, are you suspecting that brat is Anonymous?" he asked softly.
Leng Lin Yue was a middle true god. Although he had heard that there was an amazing anonymous person in the lower true god's Hall of Gods, he didn't take it to heart. He also didn't associate Luo Zheng with the anonymous person. Moreover, he had been busy winning points in the middle true god's Hall of Gods.
But now, because of the questions that Mu Xuerong had raised, he had suddenly understood everything …
Mu Ning's small mouth opened slightly, and her sharp eyes stared at Luo Zheng. She wanted to match Luo Zheng with the Anonymous in her heart.
"Most likely," Mu Xuerong said with a cold smile.
Of course, Leng Linyue did not know. She did not care whether Luo Zheng was Anonymous or not.
The Anonymous had only been chosen by the Dongfang family. As for the Dongfang family being able to bring Anonymous to their floating island, it had almost nothing to do with Mu Xuerong. She wanted to verify something else …
Some time ago, she had lost to Yu Taibai. At the same time, she had reported the news of Buzhou Spirit Mountain being closed to the Mu family to her father, Mu Haiji.
Mu Haiji had told her to pay attention to Han Jia, because of the four families that Yu Taibai had announced, Han Jia was most likely the Stowaways.
The Stowaways had only entered the divine territory two years ago. Now, an extremely monstrous Anonymous had appeared in Han Jia. She had previously wondered if the Anonymous did not dare to show his face in public because his identity was too sensitive.
Now, she was even sure that the Anonymous' surname was Luo!
Whether it was the time, the surname, or the talent, they all matched very well. Such a person definitely could not have appeared among the ordinary Gods …
At this moment, Mu Xuerong had a strong suspicion of Luo Zheng. This guy was very likely one of the two Stowaways. The rest was up to her to verify.
Although she had her suspicions, she did not show it on her face.
Today was the Blind Tribe's Soaring Sun Festival. She naturally could not capture Luo Zheng and torture him. Moreover, she only had her suspicions. During the Soaring Sun Festival, she had many opportunities to observe this brat. Once it was proven that he was one of the Stowaways, she would not hesitate to kill him.
On the floating island, Luo Zheng had indeed considered the possibility that his identity could be seen through and thus arouse suspicion.
However, after weighing the pros and cons, he felt that this possibility was not very high. Moreover, after the Soaring Sun Festival, he would immediately leave the floating island and head to the forbidden area.
He did not expect that Mu Xuerong had a conversation with Mu Haiji. Mu Haiji's judgment had deepened Mu Xuerong's vigilance, so as soon as Luo Zheng appeared, Mu Xuerong had locked onto him.
As everyone waited, the Blind Tribe members continued to row small boats to send people over.
After waiting for a while, six or seven Blind Tribe members walked over and cupped their hands towards the crowd. The leader of the Blind Tribe members said politely, "Today is the Soaring Sun Festival. We are very honored to be able to invite all the True Gods here. Everyone, please follow me …"
Under the guidance of the Blind Tribe members, all the True Gods left the harbor and headed towards the inner part of the island.
From the outside, the Blind Tribe looked like an extremely ordinary island. Looking at the surrounding environment, it was not much different from the environment of the ordinary Gods in the Divine Realm.
However, ever since they entered this island, they could feel a strong sense of the Law of Time emanating from almost everywhere. From the smallest plants to the largest rocks around the island …
The Sea of Time could tear everything apart, but this small island could resist the tearing force of the Sea of Time. This was originally very strange. The Blind Tribe's settlement was probably the only island group in the Sea of Time.
Under the guidance of the Blind Tribe members, everyone arrived at a flat and wide open space.

Chapter 1898
These nine pagodas were separated from each other by a distance of three meters. The patterns on the pagodas were complex and mysterious, emitting an extremely strong power of time law.
"These nine pagodas can be pieced together into one." Luo Zheng raised his head and carefully sized them up.
The patterns on each pagoda were broken, but the broken parts could be connected to another pagoda. In other words, if the nine pagodas were moved together, they could be pieced together to form a complete picture …
The Blind clansmen present were all worshipping the nine pagodas. The surrounding environment was solemn and peaceful.
"Everyone, please wait here. Be careful not to make any noise." A Blind clansman said in a low voice.
The True Divinities of the various large clans nodded and each found a place to sit down …
Taking advantage of this opportunity, Han Chuyue and Han Biluo who had always been by Han Liusu's side glanced at each other. Suddenly, like two nimble little swallows, they passed through the crowd and came to Luo Zheng's side.
Luo Zheng was in the middle of examining the nine pagodas when the two little girls suddenly appeared by his side. He was slightly taken aback and asked in a low voice, "What's wrong?"
"Quickly speak, what did you do to my big sister?" Han Chuyue lowered her voice. Her little face was filled with a serious expression, but her pair of lively eyes contained a trace of uncontrollable laughter.
"Big sister Liusu hasn't been happy these past two days." Han Biluo also said.
Under the questioning of the two little girls, Luo Zheng revealed a helpless smile.
He felt that what he did wasn't wrong. Han Liusu was the eldest princess of a large clan, but he was actually a fugitive who walked in the darkness. If he followed Han Liusu's wishes, it would only harm her.
"Quickly speak!" Han Chuyue fiercely stared at Luo Zheng and sternly said.
Looking at Han Chuyue's expression, Luo Zheng also knew that if he didn't give her an explanation today, she wouldn't let the matter rest. He could only helplessly shake his head and transmit his voice saying, "Very soon I will be leaving the floating island. Your big sister wanted to leave with me, but I refused."
Telling the truth to Han Chuyue was also good. At least she could persuade Han Liusu. Looking at Han Liusu's expression, Luo Zheng also felt distressed.
Hearing this, Han Chuyue's ridiculously large eyes blinked. She turned her head and carefully glanced at her sister. She tilted her little head and thought for a moment. That appearance was quite cute.
After thinking for a while, Han Chuyue raised her eyebrows and also transmitted her voice, "Why don't you agree? Isn't my sister good enough? She was willing to elope with you, but you actually rejected her so heartlessly! A man like you … "
Luo Zheng never thought that Han Chuyue would scold him to his face. He opened his mouth wide and stared blankly at Han Chuyue.
Han Biluo did not know what was going on, but she knew that the two of them were communicating through sound transmission. Thus, she asked with a displeased expression, "What are you two talking about?"
After Han Chuyue's reprimand, Luo Zheng opened his mouth and said: "You also know my background," he pointed to the ground, "Even if I am here, there is always danger."
Luo Zheng was very clear about his situation. As long as his identity was exposed, the Mu Clan would immediately kill him …
"My sister is not afraid of death," Han Chuyue continued to stare at Luo Zheng.
"Is there a sister like you …" Luo Zheng transmitted his voice, not knowing whether to laugh or cry.
Han Chuyue thought about it again. Just now her anger was completely due to her emotions taking over. Although she was already a Divine level martial artist, her body and mind were still that of a teenager. If she thought about it rationally, she also felt that her sister could not leave with Luo Zheng.
"Even if what you did was right, you still can't reject my sister," she finally said.
Luo Zheng also knew that there was no point in reasoning with this girl, so he simply did not speak.
But at this time, a group of blind people appeared on the other side of the flat square.
Ordinary blind people's clothing was very casual. Most of them were gray and brown cloth wrapped around their bodies. Many of the blind men were even naked for many years.
However, this group of blind people was wearing beautiful clothes. The surface of these clothes faintly had colorful patterns flowing slowly, and the power of time law emanated from them. It was unknown what kind of fabric was used to weave these clothes.
"These people … are the True Gods of the blind tribe?" Luo Zheng's gaze swept over them, and his eyes revealed a surprised expression.
The blind people that Luo Zheng had come into contact with along the way were all Gods. However, these blind people all had profound cultivation, and the aura they exuded was completely different from the ordinary blind people. The only thing they had in common was that these blind people were also "blind."
These blind people lined up along the two sides of the square. Their cultivation was also ranked from high to low. The outermost one was a lower True God, and the innermost one was an upper True God, as well as a Perfect True God!
In the center, a Perfect True God slowly walked out. This Perfect True God of the blind tribe glanced around, then closed his eyes tightly and said softly, "Today is the Blind Tribe's grand gathering. We respectfully welcome the arrival of the Holy Emperors of the floating islands!"
After saying this, the Perfect True God of the blind tribe placed his hands in front of his chest. This was the Blind Tribe's highest etiquette.
As soon as he said this, his voice drifted away …
"The Holy Emperors are also coming to participate?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned.
He originally thought that although the status of the blind tribe was special, it was impossible for them to invite all the Holy Emperors of the rich and powerful families, right? Perhaps they were an indispensable part of the Sea of Time, but ultimately, they were only ferrymen of the Sea of Time, a group of boatmen.
It seemed like his understanding was still shallow. The status of the blind tribe was higher than he had imagined.
"Whoosh …"
Suddenly, this island was surging with wind and clouds.
Countless auspicious clouds bloomed one after another, as though some strange treasure was about to be born. In the blink of an eye, a man wearing a yellow robe appeared in the sky above the island.
Flying was prohibited in the Sea of Time, so upper True Gods were unable to fly across the Sea of Time. Spatial shuttling was prohibited in the Sea of Time, so even Perfect True Gods were unable to use spatial transference. However, this man wearing a yellow robe was not restricted at all!
"Sage!"
Luo Zheng looked up at the man wearing a yellow robe and felt that extraordinary aura.
This was not the first time he had seen a Sage …
The first time he met a Sage was in the Immortal Mansion's library. When his master accepted him as a disciple, he met Gu Bei and Bei Sheng. Later, he met Jiu Sheng and Craftsman Sheng in the Forbidden Land of God Refinement. Finally, he met Mu Haiji and his father Luo Xiao.
However, the Sages he met did not appear in their true bodies. They were either soul imprints or incarnations that were projected into their inner worlds. As for Mu Haiji and Luo Xiao, they were just two souls.
After the man in the yellow robe appeared, the Perfect True God of the blind tribe bowed and said, "Welcome Sage Yi!"
"Sage Yi … Which family's Sage is he?" Luo Zheng quietly asked Han Chuyue who was beside him.
Han Chuyue seemed to be very dissatisfied with Luo Zheng. She glared at him and did not say anything …
However, Han Biluo covered her mouth and laughed, "It's the Holy Emperor of the Zhao family, Zhao Wuyi. Outsiders call him Sage Yi."
In the sky, Zhao Wuyi nodded. His figure disappeared in a flash and then appeared above the square, floating in the air.
Just as the Zhao Clan's Holy Will appeared, another cloud of scarlet mist rolled over from the horizon. When the scarlet mist stopped, a tall and sturdy man appeared within it.
The Perfect True God of the blind tribe bowed again and said politely, "Welcome Sage Lun!"
The tall and sturdy man's face was red and he just laughed loudly, "You're too polite, too polite." After saying that, Tang Lun also descended. With a light rub of his fingers, strands of flames intertwined and formed a huge flame throne floating in the air. Tang Lun sat on it.
This was Tang Lun's voice. Luo Zheng had heard it once in the Arena of Gods. Yu Taibai had forbidden the Tang family from entering Buzhou Spirit Mountain. The hot-tempered Tang Lun had argued with Yu Taibai.
The flame contained in the flame throne was very strange. After Luo Zheng took a look at it, his heart started to beat fast.
He knew that the flame was formed from the Fledgling Fire Supreme Dao. The Dao contained in it was shocking. However, Sages were able to control the Supreme Dao with ease. Even though it was floating more than ten feet above everyone's heads, there was no heat emitted. Otherwise, the entire island would have turned into a sea of fire.
Looking at Tang Lun, it seemed that he had also become a Sage with the Fledgling Fire Supreme Dao. Luo Zheng did not know how far Tang Lun had cultivated the Fledgling Fire Supreme Dao. If he used the Immeasurable Ruler to absorb the Dao in the flame, wouldn't he benefit immensely …

Chapter 1899
The perception of these Sages was ridiculously strong.
If Tang Lun were to discover something, he would be in deep trouble.
After Tang Lun appeared, the Sages of the other wealthy families appeared one after another. The Fang family, the Qin family, the Li family …
The Sages of the various wealthy families appeared one by one, including the Han family's Emperor Han Qing.
Starting from the fourth-ranked Leng family, these wealthy families only had one Sages.
However, it was different for the top three families.
For example, the Xuanyue family was divided into three factions: the Xuanyue Cult, the War God Temple, and the Heaven Spirit Pavilion. These three factions each had one Sage.
The Sword family had four black-robed elders, which meant they had four Sages …
"The second-ranked and third-ranked families already have three or four Sages. Their strength is indeed far beyond ordinary wealthy families. I wonder how many Sages the Dongfang family has …" Luo Zheng sighed as he looked up at the sky.
At that moment, the sky suddenly darkened, as if the world had lost all light. No one could see anything.
However, this lasted for a very short time. When everyone regained their vision, there was one more person in the sky …
This person wore a black robe, and his long hair flowed elegantly. He looked extraordinarily handsome, as if the masculinity of a man and the gentleness of a woman were fused into one.
"Respectfully greetings, Sage Jun." The Great Perfection stage Blind Tribe cupped his hands.
This time, before Luo Zheng could speak, Han Biluo explained to Luo Zheng, "Dongfang Chunjun, the Sage Emperor of the Dongfang family. He is considered the number one person in the Sage Hall."
Luo Zheng looked at Dongfang Chunjun and nodded slightly.
Han Chuyue, who had been silent all this time, couldn't help but send a voice transmission, "Actually, after my sister and I returned to the floating island, we investigated some things. Back then, your father lost to Dongfang Chunjun, which was why Mu Haiji had the chance to kill him."
Hearing Han Chuyue's words, Luo Zheng's expression was still calm. He only softly grunted in acknowledgment. At the same time, he stared at Dongfang Chunjun, as if wanting to firmly engrave this person in his mind.
He had already faintly guessed in his heart.
Luo Xiao, Gu Bei, and Jiu Sheng's influence in the Realm of the Gods wasn't something the Mu Family could deal with. If there was anyone who could destroy the Luo Family, it would be the Xuanyue Family, the Jian Family, and the Dongfang Family. Since Dongfang Chunjun was jackals of the same lair as Mu Haiji, he must have been involved in the matter back then.
When the last Saint, Dongfang Chunjun, appeared, another dozen or so people appeared at the same time. These people were the Secondary Saints of the various great clans.
Aristocratic families must have a saint overseeing them, but not every aristocratic family had a secondary saint.
After all, a Quasi-sage also had the opportunity to attack the sage realm and become a sage. This was very precious to the aristocratic families. Among the thirty-six aristocratic families, only half of them had their own Quasi-sage.
These secondary saints also swiftly returned to their positions. The secondary saints' positions were naturally one level lower than the saints'.
Aunt Hanjiu was also among them. When she arrived, she glanced around and saw Luo Zheng in the crowd. The corners of her mouth curled into a smile. This kid really wasn't afraid of death …
The saints and secondary saints were all present. The Great Perfection of the Blind Clan closed his eyes and asked, "Why don't I see the Supreme Saint?"
Tang Lun, the saint of the Tang Clan, laughed. "That old guy actually attacked Luo Xiao's universe not long ago and was punished by the Governing Slate. He's still facing the wall now!"
"Mu Haiji is still the same. He's too impulsive." The saint of the Qin Clan chuckled.
"Maybe he still can't forget what happened back then. That's why he's so anxious to get rid of Luo Xiao," another saint added.
Luo Zheng also noticed that these saints were all from the Aristocratic Alliance. They were the aristocratic families targeted by Yu Taibai. As for the saints of the other aristocratic families, they remained silent. For example, the saints of the Xuanyue Clan and the Sword Clan all had calm expressions on their faces.
"Firmament Saint only did what he was supposed to do. Since Luo Xiao's physical body has already fallen, there's no need for the Dayan Universe to exist. It's a good thing to eliminate it as soon as possible," Emperor Hanqing added.
Some of the saints had strange expressions on their faces. Leng Yao, the saint of the Leng Clan, chuckled. "Emperor Hanqing is right."
Emperor Hanqing was a legend in the divine realm. It seemed that he was determined to rely on the Dongfang Clan's Aristocratic Alliance …
Han Chuyue carefully glanced at Luo Zheng. Of course, she knew how Luo Zheng felt.
In truth, the members of the Han Clan were also upset when they heard Emperor Hanqing's words. The elites of the Han Clan had always been proud of their clan, but now their Saint Emperor was trying to curry favor with someone else. How would the elites of the other aristocratic families view them?
"Since the Supreme Saint can't come, then there's no need to wait." The Great Perfection saint of the Blind Tribe nodded. He turned and bowed towards the depths of the island. At the same time, all the members of the Blind Tribe also prostrated themselves on the ground.
"Lord Patriarch, please preside over the Ling Sun Festival …" the Great Perfection saint of the Blind Tribe said loudly.
The patriarch of the Blind Tribe?
Luo Zheng also looked in that direction, his heart filled with anticipation. Who was this person? Was he also a Saint? That was very likely. Otherwise, why would the Saints give the Blind Tribe face and participate in their clan's gathering? Because the Blind Tribe could ferry people across the Sea of Time?
If the aristocratic families could build portals to the various forbidden areas, then they could also build portals to various places in the divine realm. All they had to do was change the rules on the Governing Slate …
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw a woman in a white gown.
The woman walked barefoot, and her pace was extremely slow. As she got closer, Luo Zhengcai realized that she was wearing a strange-looking mask that completely covered her face.
But that wasn't the main point. When Luo Zheng saw this woman, he felt as if she didn't exist. When she slowly walked over, her figure was like a candle in the night wind, absent-minded.
It was a very strange feeling. It was as if she was shuttling back and forth in another space. One moment she disappeared, the next she reappeared. This repeated over and over again, giving off an illusory feeling.
What made Luo Zheng even more surprised was that when this woman appeared, almost all the Saints and Quasi-Sages stood up and bowed to her!
Weren't Saints the supreme existence in the divine realm?
From the looks of it, this woman's status was even higher than everyone else?
He was very puzzled. He turned to look at Han Chuyue and asked again, "What's the background of the Blind Tribe's patriarch? She's just a God. Why are all the Saints bowing to her?"
Han Chuyue smiled faintly. "Because the Blind Tribe's patriarch has the power to kill every living thing in the divine realm!"
"Every living thing? Even Saints? "Luo Zheng asked in surprise.
Han Chuyue nodded. "Yes, even Saints have the power to kill in her hands."
"How is that possible? She has the power to do so? "
Luo Zheng's face was full of disbelief. In his opinion, any Saint could wipe out the entire Blind Tribe with a wave of their hand.
"En." Han Chuyue had a matter-of-fact expression. "There are only three people here that she can't kill. In the entire universe, there are only seven or eight people that she can't kill. The others don't have the power to fight back in front of her."
"How did she do it?" Luo Zheng asked incredulously.
"Why don't you guess?" Han Chuyue winked and smiled mischievously.

Chapter 1900
"I can't guess." Luo Zheng shook his head helplessly. A divine ability that could kill everyone at will, Luo Zheng really couldn't imagine.
Han Chuyue looked at the white clothed woman and asked, "Don't you think her state is very strange?"
Luo Zheng nodded. The white clothed woman's figure seemed to be in a trance, as if she could disappear at any time.
"She doesn't really exist in this world. She lives in time." Han Chuyue transmitted her voice.
"Lives in time?" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. He found it hard to understand Han Chuyue's words.
Luo Zheng had seen the laws of time in the universe. But whether it was the laws of time used by warriors or the laws of time used by true gods, they could only delay the passage of time as much as possible. It seemed to be the simplest use of the laws of time …
Time, this kind of thing, was too mysterious and too difficult to grasp.
"Yes, the leader of the blind tribe can travel through the sea of time at will. She can return to any time period and change the past." Han Chuyue said, "In other words, she can turn back the time of the entire divine domain."
"What!" Luo Zheng's eyes opened wide. Although Luo Zheng felt that Han Chuyue's words were inconceivable, the attitude of the saints also explained the problem. "This kind of ability is too strong …"
"Do you understand now?" Han Chuyue said indifferently, "So in theory, she can kill anyone in the divine domain. But it's not without limits. Every time she uses time to turn back, she has to pay a big price."
These saints were indeed very strong now. Perhaps this woman wasn't their match.
But the white clothed woman could turn back time at any time. She could return to the past!
These saints had also gone through countless divine eras. After experiencing endless training and opportunities, they were able to become saints. She only needed to go back hundreds or even thousands of divine eras. At that time, these saints were just ordinary divine citizens. They were even infants that had just been born in the divine domain.
After the saints of that time period were killed, the current saints would also disappear …
This was the white clothed woman's powerful ability.
Although Luo Zheng felt that Han Chuyue's words were inconceivable, the attitude of the saints also explained everything.
The attitude of these peak existences in the divine domain towards the white clothed woman, there must be a reason.
"Is this an ability of the divine dao of time?" Luo Zheng still had some doubts. If this ability was obtained from the divine dao of time, it was too strong. Because there was no room to resist this ability.
No one could be born so strong..... If the divine dao of time was so powerful, then there was no need to cultivate any other divine dao.....
"Of course not." Han Chuyue shook her head. "The Divine Dao of Time does have the ability to turn back time, but it can only be used on oneself. Turning back to one's own body, she can turn back to the entire divine domain."
"How did she do it?" Luo Zheng continued to ask.
"Because the Sea of Time is also a great forbidden area, and she controlled the Sea of Time and obtained the 'Sword of Time of the Gods'. Her ability originates from this sword that controls the Sea of Time." Han Chuyue said.
. "The sea of time is a forbidden area …" Han Chuyue's answer surprised Luo Zheng again.
The sea of time was a forbidden area. For most true gods in the divine domain, it was a big secret. Many rich people didn't know about it. Almost all of them knew about it through the Ling Ri Festival.
It was also Han Chuyue's first time participating in the Ling Ri Festival. However, she and Han Liusu had a high status in the Han Family. It wasn't difficult for them to understand these things. Besides, it was the second time Han Cangyan was invited to the Ling Ri Festival. Some things would be told to the two sisters in advance.
"No wonder …" Luo Zheng nodded.
Previously, Luo Zheng was puzzled. According to the nature of the saints of the rich, they would have driven away the Blind Tribe in the sea of time or even killed them.
They had to rely on the Blind Tribe to leave the sea of time. The efficiency was so low. How could the rich tolerate it?
In the end, they had no choice …
In fact, when the rich first entered the sea of time, they did have conflicts with the Blind Tribe.
At first, there were already six rich families in the sea of time. Later, a new saint brought his family to establish a floating island and joined the sea of time.
At that time, this new Saint was also in high spirits. He could not tolerate this group of Blind Tribesmen and even killed fifty to sixty ferrymen of the Blind Tribesmen. At that time, the Six Great Clans did not express their opinions on this matter. They felt that the Blind Tribesmen were mysterious and strange, and it was better not to provoke them.
Unexpectedly, this saint from a rich family mysteriously disappeared one day. What disappeared was not only the saint himself, but also the universe he created.
Even if a huge universe completely collapsed, it would take thousands of years. It was a slow process. However, the saint and the universe he created disappeared overnight. This was too strange.
It was also at that time that the Blind Tribe invited the Sages of the Six Great Clans to the Feast of the Dark Sun. The Sages saw the Blind Tribe's leader for the first time. The masked female leader told the Sages that she was the one who killed the new Sage …
One had to know that after becoming a saint, there was a publicly acknowledged principle. As long as the universe was not destroyed, the saint wouldn't die. When the universe was formed, the saint's life would be connected with the universe. This female leader couldn't kill a saint silently.
Not only the female leader couldn't do it, but no one else could. Therefore, after Mu Haiji killed Luo Xiao, he wanted to launch another war against the Dayan Universe.
Although there was no Sage Hall at that time and there were no rules for the war between saints, the universe was so huge that even a saint couldn't destroy it in a short period of time.
However, this female leader easily proved her ability. On the day of the Ling Ri Festival, one of the saints carried a treasure sword with him. That treasure sword wasn't a top-grade item, but it was a treasure sword that the saint often used. Although it was useless now, he always carried it with him.
Not long after the female leader left the Ling Ri Festival, that saint's treasure sword disappeared. At the same time, that saint turned pale with fright.
When the Female Clan Leader appeared once again, the Sage was still staring blankly at the Female Clan Leader as he mumbled, "You, you …"
At this time, another treasure sword appeared in the female leader's hand. It was the saint's treasure sword.
The other Saints were puzzled. They didn't know what kind of trick the Saint was playing with the Blind Tribe's matriarch. Then, they found out that the Blind Tribe's matriarch had returned to forty epochs ago. At that time, the Saint was still a lower True God. The matriarch had snatched the sword away from him. The matriarch had even said to him that they would meet again forty epochs later.
Only then did the saints understand how powerful the female leader of the Blind Tribe was.
The immortality of saints seemed to be a joke in front of her.
Because of this, the Blind Tribe could safely sail their small boats on the Time Sea. No rich families dared to provoke this tribe.
"But you just said that she couldn't kill everyone?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Yes, I heard that she couldn't kill the saint emperor of the Dongfang family," Han Chuyue said. "I don't know what method Dongfang Chunjun used. He seemed to have hidden all traces of himself in the divine realm. That's why the female leader couldn't trace back to his past. As for what method Dongfang Chunjun used, I don't know. Anyway, she couldn't kill Dongfang Chunjun."
She had heard these stories from others. Naturally, she didn't know the details …
While Han Chu Yue was whispering, the matriarch of the Blind Tribe walked barefoot to the nine pagodas. She stretched out her slender hands and gently waved them to the sides. The nine pagodas began to slowly move towards the center. They leaned against each other and at the same time formed a mysterious star pattern.
There were a total of nine stars in the star pattern. Each star had a different color, and the star in the middle was light blue.
"This time, it's the transit of the Aqua Star," the female leader of the Blind Tribe said calmly.
After saying that, she stretched out her hand and gently patted the star …
